FILE: scripts\traceFamily8952.js

const fs = require("fs");

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const TARGETS = new Set([
    8948, 8949, 8950, 8951, 8952,
    8953, 8954, 8955, 8956, 8957,
    8958, 8959, 8960
]);

function hex(x, w = 8) {
    return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(w, "0");
}

let p = 8;

function u8() {
    return buf[p++];
}

function u32() {
    let r = 0;
    let s = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[p++];
        r |= (b & 0x7f) << s;

        if (!(b & 0x80))
            return r >>> 0;

        s += 7;
    }
}

function i32() {
    let r = 0;
    let s = 0;
    let b;

    while (true) {
        b = buf[p++];
        r |= (b & 0x7f) << s;
        s += 7;

        if (!(b & 0x80))
            break;
    }

    if (s < 32 && (b & 0x40))
        r |= (~0 << s);

    return r | 0;
}

function name() {
    const n = u32();
    const start = p;
    p += n;
    return Buffer.from(buf.subarray(start, p)).toString("utf8");
}

function skipMem() {
    u32();
    u32();
}

function skipBlockType() {
    const b = buf[p];

    if (
        b === 0x40 ||
        b === 0x7f ||
        b === 0x7e ||
        b === 0x7d ||
        b === 0x7c ||
        b === 0x7b
    ) {
        p++;
    } else {
        u32();
    }
}

function decode() {

    const start = p;
    const op = u8();

    const x = {
        start,
        op,
        name: "UNKNOWN"
    };

    switch (op) {

        case 0x00: x.name = "unreachable"; break;
        case 0x01: x.name = "nop"; break;

        case 0x02:
            x.name = "block";
            skipBlockType();
            break;

        case 0x03:
            x.name = "loop";
            skipBlockType();
            break;

        case 0x04:
            x.name = "if";
            skipBlockType();
            break;

        case 0x05:
            x.name = "else";
            break;

        case 0x0b:
            x.name = "end";
            break;

        case 0x0c:
            x.name = "br";
            x.index = u32();
            break;

        case 0x0d:
            x.name = "br_if";
            x.index = u32();
            break;

        case 0x0e: {
            x.name = "br_table";
            const n = u32();

            for (let i = 0; i < n; i++)
                u32();

            u32();
            break;
        }

        case 0x0f:
            x.name = "return";
            break;

        case 0x10:
            x.name = "call";
            x.functionIndex = u32();
            break;

        case 0x11:
            x.name = "call_indirect";
            x.typeIndex = u32();
            x.tableIndex = u32();
            break;

        case 0x1a:
            x.name = "drop";
            break;

        case 0x1b:
            x.name = "select";
            break;

        case 0x20:
            x.name = "local.get";
            x.local = u32();
            break;

        case 0x21:
            x.name = "local.set";
            x.local = u32();
            break;

        case 0x22:
            x.name = "local.tee";
            x.local = u32();
            break;

        case 0x23:
            x.name = "global.get";
            x.global = u32();
            break;

        case 0x24:
            x.name = "global.set";
            x.global = u32();
            break;

        // memory loads
        case 0x28:
            x.name = "i32.load";
            skipMem();
            break;

        case 0x29:
            x.name = "i64.load";
            skipMem();
            break;

        case 0x2a:
            x.name = "f32.load";
            skipMem();
            break;

        case 0x2b:
            x.name = "f64.load";
            skipMem();
            break;

        case 0x2c:
            x.name = "i32.load8_s";
            skipMem();
            break;

        case 0x2d:
            x.name = "i32.load8_u";
            skipMem();
            break;

        case 0x2e:
            x.name = "i32.load16_s";
            skipMem();
            break;

        case 0x2f:
            x.name = "i32.load16_u";
            skipMem();
            break;

        case 0x30:
            x.name = "i64.load8_s";
            skipMem();
            break;

        case 0x31:
            x.name = "i64.load8_u";
            skipMem();
            break;

        case 0x32:
            x.name = "i64.load16_s";
            skipMem();
            break;

        case 0x33:
            x.name = "i64.load16_u";
            skipMem();
            break;

        case 0x34:
            x.name = "i64.load32_s";
            skipMem();
            break;

        case 0x35:
            x.name = "i64.load32_u";
            skipMem();
            break;

        // stores
        case 0x36:
            x.name = "i32.store";
            skipMem();
            break;

        case 0x37:
            x.name = "i64.store";
            skipMem();
            break;

        case 0x38:
            x.name = "f32.store";
            skipMem();
            break;

        case 0x39:
            x.name = "f64.store";
            skipMem();
            break;

        case 0x3a:
            x.name = "i32.store8";
            skipMem();
            break;

        case 0x3b:
            x.name = "i32.store16";
            skipMem();
            break;

        case 0x3c:
            x.name = "i64.store8";
            skipMem();
            break;

        case 0x3d:
            x.name = "i64.store16";
            skipMem();
            break;

        case 0x3e:
            x.name = "i64.store32";
            skipMem();
            break;

        case 0x3f:
            x.name = "memory.size";
            u32();
            break;

        case 0x40:
            x.name = "memory.grow";
            u32();
            break;

        case 0x41:
            x.name = "i32.const";
            x.value = i32();
            break;

        case 0x42:
            x.name = "i64.const";

            while (true) {
                const b = u8();
                if (!(b & 0x80))
                    break;
            }
            break;

        case 0x43:
            x.name = "f32.const";
            p += 4;
            break;

        case 0x44:
            x.name = "f64.const";
            p += 8;
            break;

        case 0x45: x.name = "i32.eqz"; break;
        case 0x46: x.name = "i32.eq"; break;
        case 0x47: x.name = "i32.ne"; break;
        case 0x48: x.name = "i32.lt_s"; break;
        case 0x49: x.name = "i32.lt_u"; break;
        case 0x4a: x.name = "i32.gt_s"; break;
        case 0x4b: x.name = "i32.gt_u"; break;
        case 0x4c: x.name = "i32.le_s"; break;
        case 0x4d: x.name = "i32.le_u"; break;
        case 0x4e: x.name = "i32.ge_s"; break;
        case 0x4f: x.name = "i32.ge_u"; break;

        // i32
        case 0x67: x.name = "i32.clz"; break;
        case 0x68: x.name = "i32.ctz"; break;
        case 0x69: x.name = "i32.popcnt"; break;
        case 0x6a: x.name = "i32.add"; break;
        case 0x6b: x.name = "i32.sub"; break;
        case 0x6c: x.name = "i32.mul"; break;
        case 0x6d: x.name = "i32.div_s"; break;
        case 0x6e: x.name = "i32.div_u"; break;
        case 0x6f: x.name = "i32.rem_s"; break;
        case 0x70: x.name = "i32.rem_u"; break;
        case 0x71: x.name = "i32.and"; break;
        case 0x72: x.name = "i32.or"; break;
        case 0x73: x.name = "i32.xor"; break;
        case 0x74: x.name = "i32.shl"; break;
        case 0x75: x.name = "i32.shr_s"; break;
        case 0x76: x.name = "i32.shr_u"; break;
        case 0x77: x.name = "i32.rotl"; break;
        case 0x78: x.name = "i32.rotr"; break;

        // i64
        case 0x7c: x.name = "i64.add"; break;
        case 0x7d: x.name = "i64.sub"; break;
        case 0x7e: x.name = "i64.mul"; break;
        case 0x7f: x.name = "i64.div_s"; break;
        case 0x80: x.name = "i64.div_u"; break;
        case 0x81: x.name = "i64.rem_s"; break;
        case 0x82: x.name = "i64.rem_u"; break;
        case 0x83: x.name = "i64.and"; break;
        case 0x84: x.name = "i64.or"; break;
        case 0x85: x.name = "i64.xor"; break;
        case 0x86: x.name = "i64.shl"; break;
        case 0x87: x.name = "i64.shr_s"; break;
        case 0x88: x.name = "i64.shr_u"; break;

        case 0xa7:
            x.name = "i32.wrap_i64";
            break;

        case 0xac:
            x.name = "i64.extend_i32_s";
            break;

        case 0xad:
            x.name = "i64.extend_i32_u";
            break;

        case 0xd0:
            x.name = "ref.null";
            u8();
            break;

        case 0xd2:
            x.name = "ref.func";
            x.functionIndex = u32();
            break;

        case 0xfc: {
            x.name = "prefix_fc";
            x.subopcode = u32();

            if (x.subopcode === 8 || x.subopcode === 9) {
                u32();
                u32();
            }
            else if (x.subopcode >= 10 && x.subopcode <= 17) {
                u32();
            }

            break;
        }

        default:
            x.unknown = true;
            break;
    }

    x.end = p;

    return x;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// SECTIONS
// ------------------------------------------------------------

p = 8;

const sections = [];

while (p < buf.length) {

    const start = p;
    const id = u8();
    const size = u32();

    sections.push({
        id,
        start,
        payloadStart: p,
        payloadEnd: p + size
    });

    p += size;
}

const imp = sections.find(x => x.id === 2);
const fun = sections.find(x => x.id === 3);
const code = sections.find(x => x.id === 10);

let imported = 0;

if (imp) {

    p = imp.payloadStart;

    const n = u32();

    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {

        name();
        name();

        const kind = u8();

        if (kind === 0) {
            u32();
            imported++;
        }

        else if (kind === 1) {
            u8();
            const flags = u8();
            u32();
            if (flags & 1) u32();
        }

        else if (kind === 2) {
            const flags = u8();
            u32();
            if (flags & 1) u32();
        }

        else if (kind === 3) {
            u8();
            u8();
        }
    }
}

// Function types

p = fun.payloadStart;

const defined = u32();

const types = [];

for (let i = 0; i < defined; i++)
    types.push(u32());

function ftype(index) {

    if (index < imported)
        return "import";

    return types[index - imported];
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// SCAN CODE
// ------------------------------------------------------------

p = code.payloadStart;

const bodies = u32();

const matches = [];
const familyBodies = new Map();

for (let body = 0; body < bodies; body++) {

    const bodySize = u32();

    const bodyStart = p;
    const bodyEnd = p + bodySize;

    const func = imported + body;

    const localCount = u32();

    for (let i = 0; i < localCount; i++) {
        u32();
        u8();
    }

    const ins = [];

    while (p < bodyEnd) {

        const before = p;

        const x = decode();

        if (x.unknown) {

            p = bodyEnd;
            break;
        }

        ins.push(x);

        // Direct reference to family member
        if (
            x.name === "call" &&
            TARGETS.has(x.functionIndex)
        ) {

            matches.push({
                caller: func,
                type: ftype(func),
                target: x.functionIndex,
                address: x.start,
                context: ins.slice(
                    Math.max(0, ins.length - 12)
                )
            });
        }

        // ref.func reference
        if (
            x.name === "ref.func" &&
            TARGETS.has(x.functionIndex)
        ) {

            matches.push({
                caller: func,
                type: ftype(func),
                target: x.functionIndex,
                address: x.start,
                refFunc: true,
                context: ins.slice(
                    Math.max(0, ins.length - 12)
                )
            });
        }
    }

    p = bodyEnd;

    if (TARGETS.has(func))
        familyBodies.set(func, {
            body,
            type: ftype(func),
            start: bodyStart,
            end: bodyEnd,
            size: bodySize,
            instructions: ins
        });
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// FAMILY
// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FAMILY 8948..8960");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const f of [...TARGETS].sort((a,b) => a-b)) {

    const x = familyBodies.get(f);

    if (!x) {
        console.log(`FUNC ${f} : NOT FOUND`);
        continue;
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log(
        `FUNC ${f}  TYPE=${x.type}  BODY=${x.body}`
    );

    console.log(
        `BODY : ${hex(x.start)} .. ${hex(x.end)}`
    );

    console.log(
        `SIZE : ${x.size}`
    );

    for (const q of x.instructions) {

        let extra = "";

        if (q.local !== undefined)
            extra += ` local=${q.local}`;

        if (q.functionIndex !== undefined)
            extra += ` func=${q.functionIndex}`;

        if (q.typeIndex !== undefined)
            extra +=
                ` type=${q.typeIndex} table=${q.tableIndex}`;

        if (q.value !== undefined)
            extra += ` value=${q.value}`;

        console.log(
            `  ${hex(q.start)} ${q.name}${extra}`
        );
    }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// REFERENCES
// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("REFERENCES TO FAMILY");
console.log("============================================================");

if (matches.length === 0) {

    console.log(
        "NO DIRECT CALL / REF.FUNC REFERENCES FOUND"
    );

} else {

    for (const m of matches) {

        console.log("");
        console.log(
            `CALLER ${m.caller} TYPE=${m.type}`
        );

        console.log(
            `TARGET ${m.target}`
        );

        console.log(
            `AT     ${hex(m.address)}`
        );

        if (m.refFunc)
            console.log("KIND   : ref.func");

        else
            console.log("KIND   : direct call");

        console.log("CONTEXT:");

        for (const q of m.context) {

            let extra = "";

            if (q.local !== undefined)
                extra += ` local=${q.local}`;

            if (q.functionIndex !== undefined)
                extra += ` func=${q.functionIndex}`;

            if (q.typeIndex !== undefined)
                extra +=
                    ` type=${q.typeIndex} table=${q.tableIndex}`;

            if (q.value !== undefined)
                extra += ` value=${q.value}`;

            console.log(
                `  ${hex(q.start)} ${q.name}${extra}`
            );
        }
    }
}

console.log("");

FILE: scripts\traceFamilyBase8459.mjs

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGETS = new Set([
  8459, 8460, 8461, 8462,
  8465, 8466, 8467, 8468,
  8479, 8480
]);

function u(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x;
  do {
    x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 127) * 2 ** s;
    s += 7;
  } while (x & 128);
  return [v >>> 0, p];
}

function opName(op) {
  return {
    0x20: "local.get",
    0x21: "local.set",
    0x22: "local.tee",
    0x23: "global.get",
    0x28: "i32.load",
    0x29: "i64.load",
    0x36: "i32.store",
    0x41: "i32.const",
    0x10: "call",
    0x11: "call_indirect"
  }[op] || "";
}

let p = 8;
let codeStart = 0;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];
  let size;
  [size, p] = u(p);

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = p;
    break;
  }

  p += size;
}

let q = codeStart;
let count;
[count, q] = u(q);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FAMILY 8459..8480 BASE TRACE");
console.log("FOCUS : MEM[ MEM[L0] + 56 ]");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
  let size;
  [size, q] = u(q);

  const start = q;
  const end = q + size;
  const func = i + 91;

  if (!TARGETS.has(func)) {
    q = end;
    continue;
  }

  console.log("");
  console.log("############################################################");
  console.log("FUNC", func);
  console.log("BODY", "0x" + start.toString(16), "..", "0x" + end.toString(16));
  console.log("############################################################");

  // Print every local.get/set/tee and load/store/call around the body.
  for (let x = start; x < end; x++) {
    const op = b[x];

    if (
      op !== 0x20 &&
      op !== 0x21 &&
      op !== 0x22 &&
      op !== 0x28 &&
      op !== 0x29 &&
      op !== 0x36 &&
      op !== 0x10 &&
      op !== 0x11 &&
      op !== 0x41
    ) continue;

    let text = opName(op);

    if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
      const [v] = u(x + 1);
      text += " " + v;
    }
    else if (op === 0x41) {
      const [v] = u(x + 1);
      text += " " + v;
    }
    else if (op === 0x10) {
      const [v] = u(x + 1);
      text += " " + v;
    }
    else if (op === 0x11) {
      let v, z;
      [v, z] = u(x + 1);
      let t;
      [t] = u(z);
      text += " type=" + v + " table=" + t;
    }
    else {
      // Show immediate alignment/offset bytes only.
      let align, z;
      [align, z] = u(x + 1);
      let offset;
      [offset] = u(z);
      text += " offset=" + offset;
    }

    console.log(
      "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0"),
      text
    );
  }

  q = end;
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceFamilyCallers.js
====================================================================================================
const fs = require("fs");

const FILE =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const IMPORTS = 83;

const TARGETS = new Set([
    8948, 8949, 8950, 8951, 8952,
    8953, 8954, 8955, 8956
]);

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(FILE);

function uleb(p) {
    let v = 0, s = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[p++];
        v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;

        if (!(b & 0x80))
            break;

        s += 7;
    }

    return {
        value: v >>> 0,
        next: p
    };
}

function sleb(p) {
    let v = 0, s = 0, b;

    while (true) {
        b = wasm[p++];
        v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;
        s += 7;

        if (!(b & 0x80))
            break;
    }

    if (s < 32 && (b & 0x40))
        v |= (~0 << s);

    return {
        value: v | 0,
        next: p
    };
}

function findSection(type) {

    let p = 8;

    while (p < wasm.length) {

        const t = wasm[p++];

        const z = uleb(p);
        p = z.next;

        if (t === type) {
            return {
                start: p,
                end: p + z.value
            };
        }

        p += z.value;
    }

    return null;
}

function decodeBody(start, end) {

    let p = start;

    const lg = uleb(p);
    p = lg.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < lg.value; i++) {
        const n = uleb(p);
        p = n.next;
        p++;
    }

    const ins = [];

    while (p < end) {

        const addr = p;
        const op = wasm[p++];

        let text =
            `opcode_0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;

        switch (op) {

            case 0x20: {
                const x = uleb(p);
                p = x.next;
                text = `local.get ${x.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x21: {
                const x = uleb(p);
                p = x.next;
                text = `local.set ${x.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x22: {
                const x = uleb(p);
                p = x.next;
                text = `local.tee ${x.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x41: {
                const x = sleb(p);
                p = x.next;
                text = `i32.const ${x.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x10: {
                const x = uleb(p);
                p = x.next;
                text = `call ${x.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x11: {
                const t = uleb(p);
                p = t.next;

                const tab = uleb(p);
                p = tab.next;

                text =
                    `call_indirect type=${t.value} table=${tab.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x28: {
                const a = uleb(p);
                p = a.next;

                const o = uleb(p);
                p = o.next;

                text =
                    `i32.load align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x29: {
                const a = uleb(p);
                p = a.next;

                const o = uleb(p);
                p = o.next;

                text =
                    `i64.load align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x2d: {
                const a = uleb(p);
                p = a.next;

                const o = uleb(p);
                p = o.next;

                text =
                    `i32.load8_u align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x36: {
                const a = uleb(p);
                p = a.next;

                const o = uleb(p);
                p = o.next;

                text =
                    `i32.store align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x37: {
                const a = uleb(p);
                p = a.next;

                const o = uleb(p);
                p = o.next;

                text =
                    `i64.store align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x23: {
                const x = uleb(p);
                p = x.next;
                text = `global.get ${x.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x24: {
                const x = uleb(p);
                p = x.next;
                text = `global.set ${x.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x45:
                text = "i32.eqz";
                break;

            case 0x46:
                text = "i32.eq";
                break;

            case 0x47:
                text = "i32.ne";
                break;

            case 0x6a:
                text = "i32.add";
                break;

            case 0x6b:
                text = "i32.sub";
                break;

            case 0x1a:
                text = "drop";
                break;

            case 0x04:
                text = "if";
                break;

            case 0x05:
                text = "else";
                break;

            case 0x0b:
                text = "end";
                break;

            case 0x0c: {
                const x = uleb(p);
                p = x.next;
                text = `br ${x.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x0d: {
                const x = uleb(p);
                p = x.next;
                text = `br_if ${x.value}`;
                break;
            }
        }

        ins.push({
            addr,
            text
        });
    }

    return ins;
}

function getBodies() {

    const sec = findSection(10);

    if (!sec)
        throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");

    let p = sec.start;

    const n = uleb(p);
    p = n.next;

    const bodies = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {

        const z = uleb(p);
        p = z.next;

        const start = p;
        const end = p + z.value;

        bodies.push({
            body: i,
            func: IMPORTS + i,
            start,
            end
        });

        p = end;
    }

    return bodies;
}

function hx(x) {
    return "0x" +
        x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

const bodies = getBodies();

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DISPATCH FAMILY 8948..8956 CALLER SCAN");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

const hits = new Map();

for (const b of bodies) {

    const ins = decodeBody(b.start, b.end);

    for (let i = 0; i < ins.length; i++) {

        const m =
            ins[i].text.match(/^call (\d+)$/);

        if (!m)
            continue;

        const target = Number(m[1]);

        if (!TARGETS.has(target))
            continue;

        if (!hits.has(target))
            hits.set(target, []);

        hits.get(target).push({
            func: b.func,
            addr: ins[i].addr,
            ins,
            index: i
        });
    }
}

for (const target of [...TARGETS].sort((a,b) => a-b)) {

    const list = hits.get(target) || [];

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(`TARGET FUNC ${target}`);
    console.log("DIRECT CALLERS :", list.length);
    console.log("============================================================");

    for (const h of list) {

        console.log("");
        console.log(
            `CALLER FUNC ${h.func} @ ${hx(h.addr)}`
        );

        const from = Math.max(0, h.index - 6);
        const to = Math.min(
            h.ins.length,
            h.index + 5
        );

        for (let i = from; i < to; i++) {

            const mark =
                i === h.index ? ">>" : "  ";

            console.log(
                `${mark} ${hx(h.ins[i].addr)} ${h.ins[i].text}`
            );
        }
    }
}

/*

 *
 * Search specifically for call_indirect type=4.
 * This is important because FUNC 8952 itself is reached
 * through an indirect dispatch chain.
 */

console.log("");

for (const b of bodies) {

    const ins = decodeBody(b.start, b.end);

    for (let i = 0; i < ins.length; i++) {

        if (
            ins[i].text !==
            "call_indirect type=4 table=0"
        )
            continue;

        indirectCount++;

        console.log("");

        const to = Math.min(
            ins.length,
            i + 3
        );

        for (let j = from; j < to; j++) {

            const mark =
                j === i ? ">>" : "  ";

            console.log(
                `${mark} ${hx(ins[j].addr)} ${ins[j].text}`
            );
        }
    }
}

FILE: scripts\traceFirstLocal0Tee6618.js

const fs = require("fs");

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm"
);

const START = 0x0022ccd0;
const END   = 0x0022ce7c;

function u32(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];

    v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;

    if (!(b & 0x80))
      return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };

    s += 7;
  }
}

function i32(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];

    v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;

    if (!(b & 0x80)) {
      if (s < 32 && (b & 0x40))
        v |= (~0 << s);

      return { value: v | 0, next: p };
    }
  }
}

function hx(x) {
  return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TRACE FIRST LOCAL0 TEE - FUNC 6618");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`RANGE : ${hx(START)} .. ${hx(END)}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let p = START;
const stack = [];

function push(x) {
  stack.push(x);
}

function pop() {
  return stack.length ? stack.pop() : "<?>"; 
}

while (p < END) {

  const off = p;
  const op = wasm[p++];

  // local.get
  if (op === 0x20) {

    const x = u32(p);
    p = x.next;

    push(`local${x.value}`);

    console.log(
      `${hx(off)} local.get ${x.value}`
    );

    continue;
  }

  // local.set
  if (op === 0x21) {

    const x = u32(p);
    p = x.next;

    const e = pop();

    console.log(
      `${hx(off)} local.set ${x.value} = ${e}`
    );

    continue;
  }

  // local.tee
  if (op === 0x22) {

    const x = u32(p);
    p = x.next;

    const e = pop();

    console.log(
      `${hx(off)} local.tee ${x.value} = ${e}`
    );

    push(e);

    if (x.value === 0) {

      console.log("");
      console.log("************************************************************");
      console.log("FIRST LOCAL0 TEE");
      console.log("************************************************************");
      console.log(`ADDRESS : ${hx(off)}`);
      console.log(`LOCAL0  : ${e}`);
      console.log("************************************************************");
      console.log("");
    }

    continue;
  }

  // i32.const
  if (op === 0x41) {

    const x = i32(p);
    p = x.next;

    push(String(x.value));

    console.log(
      `${hx(off)} i32.const ${x.value}`
    );

    continue;
  }

  // i32.load
  if (op === 0x28) {

    const align = u32(p);
    p = align.next;

    const offx = u32(p);
    p = offx.next;

    const addr = pop();

    const e =
      offx.value === 0
        ? `MEM[${addr}]`
        : `MEM[${addr}+${offx.value}]`;

    push(e);

    console.log(
      `${hx(off)} i32.load offset=${offx.value} : ${addr} -> ${e}`
    );

    continue;
  }

  // i32.add
  if (op === 0x6a) {

    const b = pop();
    const a = pop();

    const e = `(${a}+${b})`;

    push(e);

    console.log(
      `${hx(off)} i32.add -> ${e}`
    );

    continue;
  }

  // i32.sub
  if (op === 0x6b) {

    const b = pop();
    const a = pop();

    const e = `(${a}-${b})`;

    push(e);

    console.log(
      `${hx(off)} i32.sub -> ${e}`
    );

    continue;
  }

  // i32.eqz
  if (op === 0x45) {

    const a = pop();

    push(`(${a}==0)`);

    console.log(
      `${hx(off)} i32.eqz`
    );

    continue;
  }

  // call
  if (op === 0x10) {

    const x = u32(p);
    p = x.next;

    console.log(
      `${hx(off)} CALL ${x.value}`
    );

    push(`RET[${x.value}]`);

    continue;
  }

  // br_if
  if (op === 0x0d) {

    const x = u32(p);
    p = x.next;

    const cond = pop();

    console.log(
      `${hx(off)} br_if ${x.value} [${cond}]`
    );

    continue;
  }

  // br
  if (op === 0x0c) {

    const x = u32(p);
    p = x.next;

    console.log(
      `${hx(off)} br ${x.value}`
    );

    continue;
  }

  console.log(
    `${hx(off)} opcode 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")}`
  );
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("STACK AT END");
console.log("============================================================");

stack.forEach((x, i) => {
  console.log(`[${i}] ${x}`);
});

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END");
console.log("============================================================");

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const START = 0x964d1;
const END   = 0x965c7;

function hx(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(6, "0");
}

function uleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = buf[p++];
    v += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(b & 0x80))
      break;

    shift += 7;
  }

  return { value: v, next: p };
}

function sleb32(p: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b = 0;

  while (true) {
    b = buf[p++];
    v |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if (!(b & 0x80))
      break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
    v |= (~0 << shift);

  return { value: v | 0, next: p };
}

function memarg(p: number) {
  const a = uleb(p);
  const o = uleb(a.next);

  return {
    align: a.value,
    offset: o.value,
    next: o.next
  };
}

function fcInstruction(p: number) {
  const sub = uleb(p);
  let next = sub.next;

  /*
   * Bulk-memory / reference instructions.
   * Decode the immediate according to subopcode.
   */

  switch (sub.value) {

    // memory.init
    case 8: {
      const data = uleb(next);
      next = data.next;

      const mem = uleb(next);
      next = mem.next;

      return {
        text: `memory.init data=${data.value} mem=${mem.value}`,
        next
      };
    }

    // data.drop
    case 9: {
      const data = uleb(next);

      return {
        text: `data.drop ${data.value}`,
        next: data.next
      };
    }

    // memory.copy
    case 10: {
      const dst = uleb(next);
      const src = uleb(dst.next);

      return {
        text: `memory.copy dst=${dst.value} src=${src.value}`,
        next: src.next
      };
    }

    // memory.fill
    case 11: {
      const mem = uleb(next);

      return {
        text: `memory.fill mem=${mem.value}`,
        next: mem.next
      };
    }

    default:
      return {
        text: `0xfc subopcode=${sub.value}`,
        next
      };
  }
}

while (p < END) {

  const at = p;
  const op = buf[p++];

  let name = `UNKNOWN(0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")})`;

  try {

    switch (op) {

      case 0x20: {
        const r = uleb(p);
        p = r.next;
        name = `local.get ${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x21: {

        p = r.next;

        p = r.next;
        name = `local.tee ${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x23: {
        const r = uleb(p);
        p = r.next;
        name = `global.get ${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x24: {
        const r = uleb(p);
        p = r.next;
        name = `global.set ${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x41: {
        const r = sleb32(p);
        p = r.next;
        name = `i32.const ${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x42: {
        const r = uleb(p);
        p = r.next;
        name = `i64.const ${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x29: {
        const m = memarg(p);
        p = m.next;
        name =
          `i64.load align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x37: {
        const m = memarg(p);
        p = m.next;
        name =
          `i64.store align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x28: {
        const m = memarg(p);
        p = m.next;
        name =
          `i32.load align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x36: {
        const m = memarg(p);
        p = m.next;
        name =
          `i32.store align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x6a:
        name = "i32.add";
        break;

      case 0x6b:
        name = "i32.sub";
        break;

      case 0x74:
        name = "i32.shl";
        break;

      case 0x7c:
        name = "i64.add";
        break;

      case 0x7d:
        name = "i64.sub";
        break;

      case 0x7e:
        name = "i64.mul";
        break;

      case 0x03:
        name = "loop";
        break;

      case 0x02:
        name = "block";
        break;

      case 0x0b:
        name = "end";
        break;

      case 0x0c: {
        const r = uleb(p);
        p = r.next;
        name = `br ${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x0d: {
        const r = uleb(p);
        p = r.next;
        name = `br_if ${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x10: {
        const r = uleb(p);
        p = r.next;
        name = `call ${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x1a:
        name = "drop";
        break;

      case 0xfc: {
        const r = fcInstruction(p);
        p = r.next;
        name = r.text;
        break;
      }

      default:
        break;
    }

  } catch (e) {
    name += " [DECODE ERROR]";
    p = at + 1;
  }

  console.log(`${hx(at)}  ${name}`);
}

import fs from "fs";

const file =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

const TARGET = 104;

function readULEB(p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };

    s += 7;
  }
}

function readSLEB(p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;

    if (!(x & 0x80)) {
      if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40))
        v |= (~0 << (s + 7));

      return { value: v | 0, next: p };
    }

    s += 7;
  }
}

function hex(x: number) {
  return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

// CODE section
let p = 8;
let codeStart = -1;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];

  const sz = readULEB(p);
  p = sz.next;

  const end = p + sz.value;

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = p;
    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

if (codeStart < 0)
  throw new Error("CODE section not found");

let q = codeStart;
const count = readULEB(q);
q = count.next;

const importedCount = 83;

let bodyStart = -1;
let bodyEnd = -1;

for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {

  const sz = readULEB(q);
  q = sz.next;

  const fn = importedCount + i;

  if (fn === TARGET) {
    bodyStart = q;
    bodyEnd = q + sz.value;
    break;
  }

  q += sz.value;
}

if (bodyStart < 0)
  throw new Error("FUNC104 not found");

console.log("FUNC 104 EXACT TRACE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`BODY : ${hex(bodyStart)} .. ${hex(bodyEnd)}`);
console.log(`SIZE : ${bodyEnd - bodyStart}`);
console.log("============================================================");

let x = bodyStart;

while (x < bodyEnd) {

  const a = x;
  const op = b[x++];

  let s = "";

  switch (op) {

    case 0x00: s = "unreachable"; break;
    case 0x01: s = "nop"; break;

    case 0x02:
      s = "block";
      x++;
      break;

    case 0x03:
      s = "loop";
      x++;
      break;

    case 0x04:
      s = "if";
      x++;
      break;

    case 0x05:
      s = "else";
      break;

    case 0x0b:
      s = "end";
      break;

    case 0x0c: {
      const r = readULEB(x);
      x = r.next;
      s = `br ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x0d: {
      const r = readULEB(x);
      x = r.next;
      s = `br_if ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x0f:
      s = "return";
      break;

    case 0x10: {
      const r = readULEB(x);
      x = r.next;
      s = `CALL FUNC ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x11: {
      const t = readULEB(x);
      x = t.next;

      const tab = readULEB(x);
      x = tab.next;

      s = `CALL_INDIRECT type=${t.value} table=${tab.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x1a:
      s = "drop";
      break;

    case 0x20: {
      const r = readULEB(x);
      x = r.next;
      s = `local.get ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x21: {
      const r = readULEB(x);
      x = r.next;
      s = `local.set ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x22: {
      const r = readULEB(x);
      x = r.next;
      s = `local.tee ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x23: {
      const r = readULEB(x);
      x = r.next;
      s = `global.get ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x24: {
      const r = readULEB(x);
      x = r.next;
      s = `global.set ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x28: {
      const a1 = readULEB(x);
      x = a1.next;

      const off = readULEB(x);
      x = off.next;

      s = `i32.load align=${a1.value} offset=${off.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x36: {
      const a1 = readULEB(x);
      x = a1.next;

      const off = readULEB(x);
      x = off.next;

      s = `i32.store align=${a1.value} offset=${off.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x41: {
      const r = readSLEB(x);
      x = r.next;
      s = `i32.const ${r.value} (${hex(r.value >>> 0)})`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x42: {
      const r = readSLEB(x);
      x = r.next;
      s = `i64.const ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x45: s = "i32.eqz"; break;
    case 0x46: s = "i32.eq"; break;
    case 0x47: s = "i32.ne"; break;
    case 0x48: s = "i32.lt_s"; break;
    case 0x49: s = "i32.lt_u"; break;
    case 0x4a: s = "i32.gt_s"; break;
    case 0x4b: s = "i32.gt_u"; break;
    case 0x4c: s = "i32.le_s"; break;
    case 0x4d: s = "i32.le_u"; break;
    case 0x4e: s = "i32.ge_s"; break;
    case 0x4f: s = "i32.ge_u"; break;

    case 0x6a: s = "i32.add"; break;
    case 0x6b: s = "i32.sub"; break;
    case 0x71: s = "i32.and"; break;
    case 0x72: s = "i32.or"; break;
    case 0x73: s = "i32.xor"; break;

    default:
      s = `opcode 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
      break;
  }

  console.log(`${hex(a)}  ${s}`);
}

FILE: scripts\traceFunc104Returns.mjs

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function readULEB(p) {
  let v = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let n = 0;

  while (p < b.length) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
    n++;

    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { value: v >>> 0, len: n };

    shift += 7;
    if (shift > 35) return null;
  }

  return null;
}

function hex(v) {
  return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function findCode() {
  let p = 8;
  let imported = 0;

  while (p < b.length) {
    const sid = b[p++];
    const sr = readULEB(p);
    if (!sr) break;

    const ss = sr.value;
    const st = p + sr.len;
    const en = st + ss;

    if (sid === 2) {
      let q = st;
      const r = readULEB(q);
      q += r.len;

      for (let i = 0; i < r.value; i++) {
        const ml = readULEB(q); q += ml.len;
        const fl = readULEB(q); q += fl.len;
        const kl = readULEB(q); q += kl.len;

        if (kl.value === 0) {
          const t = readULEB(q);
          q += t.len;
          imported++;
        } else if (kl.value === 1) {
          q++;
          const f = readULEB(q); q += f.len;
          const a = readULEB(q); q += a.len;
          if (f.value & 1) {
            const m = readULEB(q); q += m.len;
          }
        } else if (kl.value === 2) {
          const f = readULEB(q); q += f.len;
          const a = readULEB(q); q += a.len;
          if (f.value & 1) {
            const m = readULEB(q); q += m.len;
          }
        } else if (kl.value === 3) {
          q += 2;
        }
      }
    }

    if (sid === 10) {
      let q = st;
      const r = readULEB(q);
      q += r.len;

      const funcs = [];

      for (let i = 0; i < r.value; i++) {
        const z = readULEB(q);
        q += z.len;

        funcs.push({
          func: imported + i,
          start: q,
          end: q + z.value
        });

        q += z.value;
      }

      return funcs;
    }

    p = en;
  }

  return [];
}

const funcs = findCode();
const fn = funcs.find(x => x.func === 104);

console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log("FUNC 104 RETURN ANALYSIS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("=".repeat(72));

if (!fn) {
  console.log("FUNC 104 NOT FOUND");
  process.exit(0);
}

console.log(`BODY : ${hex(fn.start)} .. ${hex(fn.end)}`);
console.log("-".repeat(72));

let p = fn.start;

while (p < fn.end) {
  const at = p;
  const op = b[p++];

  let out = `${hex(at)}  ${op.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")}`;

  if (op === 0x41) {
    const r = readULEB(p);
    if (!r) break;

    out += `  i32.const ${r.value} (${hex(r.value)})`;
    p += r.len;
  }

  else if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
    const r = readULEB(p);
    if (!r) break;

    const n =
      op === 0x20 ? "local.get" :
      op === 0x21 ? "local.set" :
      "local.tee";

    out += `  ${n} ${r.value}`;
    p += r.len;
  }

  else if (op === 0x28 || op === 0x36) {
    const align = b[p++];
    const r = readULEB(p);
    if (!r) break;

    out +=
      `  ${op === 0x28 ? "i32.load" : "i32.store"}` +
      ` align=${align} offset=${r.value}`;

    p += r.len;
  }

  else if (op === 0x10) {
    const r = readULEB(p);
    if (!r) break;

    out += `  call ${r.value}`;
    p += r.len;
  }

  else if (op === 0x11) {
    const t = readULEB(p);
    p += t.len;

    const tab = readULEB(p);
    p += tab.len;

    out += `  call_indirect type=${t.value} table=${tab.value}`;
  }

  else if (op === 0x0f) {
    out += "  return";
  }

  else if (op === 0x0b) {
    out += "  end";
  }

  else if (op === 0x6a) {
    out += "  i32.add";
  }

  else if (op === 0x6b) {
    out += "  i32.sub";
  }

  else if (op === 0x45) {
    out += "  i32.eqz";
  }

  else {
    out += `  opcode=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")}`;
  }

  console.log(out);
}

FILE: scripts\traceFunc106To2431.mjs

import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const IMPORTED = 83;

const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function uleb(pos) {
  let v = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << shift;

    if (!(x & 0x80)) {
      return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function findCodeSection() {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < b.length) {
    const id = b[p++];
    const s = uleb(p);
    p = s.next;

    if (id === 10) {
      return {
        start: p,
        end: p + s.value
      };
    }

    p += s.value;
  }

  throw new Error("CODE section not found");
}

function skipBlockType(p) {
  const x = b[p];

  if (
    x === 0x40 ||
    x === 0x7f ||
    x === 0x7e ||
    x === 0x7d ||
    x === 0x7c ||
    x === 0x7b
  ) {
    return p + 1;
  }

  return uleb(p).next;
}

function decodeBody(start, end) {
  let p = start;
  const out = [];

  while (p < end) {
    const addr = p;
    const op = b[p++];

    const x = {
      addr,
      op,
      name: `0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
    };

    switch (op) {
      case 0x02:
        x.name = "BLOCK";
        p = skipBlockType(p);
        break;

      case 0x03:
        x.name = "LOOP";
        p = skipBlockType(p);
        break;

      case 0x04:
        x.name = "IF";
        p = skipBlockType(p);
        break;

      case 0x05:
        x.name = "ELSE";
        break;

      case 0x0b:
        x.name = "END";
        break;

      case 0x0c:
        x.name = "BR";
        x.value = uleb(p).value;
        p = uleb(p).next;
        break;

      case 0x0d: {
        x.name = "BR_IF";
        const q = uleb(p);
        x.value = q.value;
        p = q.next;
        break;
      }

      case 0x0f:
        x.name = "RETURN";
        break;

      case 0x10: {
        x.name = "CALL";
        const q = uleb(p);
        x.value = q.value;
        p = q.next;
        break;
      }

      case 0x11: {
        x.name = "CALL_INDIRECT";

        const t = uleb(p);
        p = t.next;

        const tab = uleb(p);
        p = tab.next;

        x.typeidx = t.value;
        x.tableidx = tab.value;
        break;
      }

      case 0x20: {
        x.name = "LOCAL.GET";
        const q = uleb(p);
        x.value = q.value;
        p = q.next;
        break;
      }

      case 0x21: {
        x.name = "LOCAL.SET";
        const q = uleb(p);
        x.value = q.value;
        p = q.next;
        break;
      }

      case 0x22: {
        x.name = "LOCAL.TEE";
        const q = uleb(p);
        x.value = q.value;
        p = q.next;
        break;
      }

      case 0x23: {
        x.name = "GLOBAL.GET";
        const q = uleb(p);
        x.value = q.value;
        p = q.next;
        break;
      }

      case 0x24: {
        x.name = "GLOBAL.SET";
        const q = uleb(p);
        x.value = q.value;
        p = q.next;
        break;
      }

      case 0x28:
      case 0x29:
      case 0x2a:
      case 0x2b:
      case 0x2c:
      case 0x2d:
      case 0x2e:
      case 0x2f:
      case 0x30:
      case 0x31:
      case 0x32:
      case 0x33:
      case 0x34:
      case 0x35:
      case 0x36:
      case 0x37:
      case 0x38:
      case 0x39:
      case 0x3a:
      case 0x3b:
      case 0x3c:
      case 0x3d:
      case 0x3e:
      case 0x3f:
      case 0x40: {
        const a = uleb(p);
        p = a.next;

        const o = uleb(p);
        p = o.next;

        x.name = op <= 0x35 ? "LOAD" : "STORE";
        x.align = a.value;
        x.offset = o.value;
        break;
      }

      case 0x41: {
        x.name = "I32.CONST";
        const q = uleb(p);
        x.value = q.value;
        p = q.next;
        break;
      }

      default:
        break;
    }

    out.push(x);
  }

  return out;
}

function getFunction(funcIndex) {
  const code = findCodeSection();

  let p = code.start;
  const count = uleb(p);
  p = count.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const size = uleb(p);
    p = size.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + size.value;
    const index = IMPORTED + i;

    if (index === funcIndex) {
      return {
        index,
        start,
        end,
        size: size.value,
        instructions: decodeBody(start, end)
      };
    }

    p = end;
  }

  throw new Error(`FUNC ${funcIndex} not found`);
}

function printFunction(fn) {
  console.log(
    `FUNCTION ${fn.index}  BODY 0x${fn.start.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} .. 0x${fn.end.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
  );

  for (const x of fn.instructions) {
    const a = "0x" + x.addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");

    let s = `${a}  ${x.name}`;

    if (x.value !== undefined) {
      s += ` ${x.value}`;
    }

    if (x.offset !== undefined) {
      s += ` offset=${x.offset}`;
    }

    if (x.typeidx !== undefined) {
      s += ` type=${x.typeidx} table=${x.tableidx}`;
    }

    console.log(s);
  }
}

const f106 = getFunction(106);

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const START = 0x0000d3f0;
const END   = 0x0000d425;

const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function uleb(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[pos++];
    value += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return { value, next: pos };

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function sleb(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let byte;

  do {
    byte = b[pos++];
    value |= (byte & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;
  } while (byte & 0x80);

  if (shift < 32 && (byte & 0x40))
    value |= (~0 << shift);

  return { value, next: pos };
}

function readInstruction(p) {

  const start = p;
  const op = b[p++];

  let text = `0x${start.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  `;

  switch (op) {

    case 0x20: {
      const q = uleb(p);
      p = q.next;
      text += `LOCAL.GET ${q.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x21: {
      const q = uleb(p);
      p = q.next;
      text += `LOCAL.SET ${q.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x22: {
      const q = uleb(p);
      p = q.next;
      text += `LOCAL.TEE ${q.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x28: {
      const a = uleb(p);
      p = a.next;
      const o = uleb(p);
      p = o.next;
      text += `I32.LOAD align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x29: {
      const a = uleb(p);
      p = a.next;
      const o = uleb(p);
      p = o.next;
      text += `I64.LOAD align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x36: {
      const a = uleb(p);
      p = a.next;
      const o = uleb(p);
      p = o.next;
      text += `I32.STORE align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x41: {
      const q = sleb(p);
      p = q.next;
      text += `I32.CONST ${q.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x42: {
      const q = sleb(p);
      p = q.next;
      text += `I64.CONST ${q.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x6a:
      text += "I32.ADD";
      break;

    case 0x6b:
      text += "I32.SUB";
      break;

    case 0x10: {
      const q = uleb(p);
      p = q.next;
      text += `CALL FUNC ${q.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x11: {
      const q = uleb(p);
      p = q.next;
      const table = uleb(p);
      p = table.next;
      text += `CALL_INDIRECT type=${q.value} table=${table.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x1a:
      text += "DROP";
      break;

    case 0x0b:
      text += "END";
      break;

    case 0x0f:
      text += "RETURN";
      break;

    default:
      text += `OPCODE 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
      break;
  }

  return { next: p, text };
}

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET = 107;
const IMPORTS = 83;

function uleb(pos) {
    let v = 0, shift = 0, p = pos;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[p++];
        v += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if (!(b & 0x80)) {
            return { value: v, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function hx(v) {
    return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

let pos = 8;

while (pos < wasm.length) {

    const section = wasm[pos++];

    const s = uleb(pos);
    pos = s.next;

    const end = pos + s.value;

    if (section === 10) {

        let p = pos;

        const c = uleb(p);
        p = c.next;

        for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < c.value; ordinal++) {

            const b = uleb(p);
            const size = b.value;
            p = b.next;

            const start = p;
            const bodyEnd = p + size;

            const func = IMPORTS + ordinal;

            if (func === TARGET) {

                console.log("=".repeat(72));
                console.log("FUNC 107 LOCAL 1 TRACE");
                console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
                console.log("=".repeat(72));
                console.log(`BODY : ${hx(start)} .. ${hx(bodyEnd)}`);
                console.log(`SIZE : ${size}`);
                console.log("-".repeat(72));

                let q = start;

                while (q < bodyEnd) {

                    const op = wasm[q];

                    // local.get
                    if (op === 0x20) {
                        const r = uleb(q + 1);

                        if (r.value === 1) {
                            console.log(
                                `${hx(q)}  >>> LOCAL.GET 1`
                            );
                        }

                        q = r.next;
                        continue;
                    }

                    // local.set
                    if (op === 0x21) {
                        const r = uleb(q + 1);

                        if (r.value === 1) {
                            console.log(
                                `${hx(q)}  >>> LOCAL.SET 1`
                            );
                        }

                        q = r.next;
                        continue;
                    }

                    // local.tee
                    if (op === 0x22) {
                        const r = uleb(q + 1);

                        if (r.value === 1) {
                            console.log(
                                `${hx(q)}  >>> LOCAL.TEE 1`
                            );
                        }

                        q = r.next;
                        continue;
                    }

                    // CALL
                    if (op === 0x10) {
                        const r = uleb(q + 1);

                        console.log(
                            `${hx(q)}  CALL FUNC ${r.value}`
                        );

                        q = r.next;
                        continue;
                    }

                    // RETURN
                    if (op === 0x0f) {
                        console.log(
                            `${hx(q)}  RETURN`
                        );
                        q++;
                        continue;
                    }

                    q++;
                }

                console.log("-".repeat(72));
                console.log(
                    "FOCUS: آیا LOCAL 1 بعد از خروجی FUNC 122 بازنویسی می‌شود؟"
                );
                console.log("=".repeat(72));

                process.exit(0);
            }

            p = bodyEnd;
        }
    }

    pos = end;
}

FILE: scripts\traceFunc107Raw.mjs

import fs from "fs";

const wasmPath = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const TARGET_FUNC = 107;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

function hex(v, width = 8) {
    return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function readULEB(buf, pos) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * (2 ** shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }

    throw new Error("ULEB128 overflow");
}

console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log(`FUNC ${TARGET_FUNC} RAW BODY`);
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("=".repeat(72));

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// WASM HEADER
// ------------------------------------------------------------
if (
    wasm[0] !== 0x00 ||
    wasm[1] !== 0x61 ||
    wasm[2] !== 0x73 ||
    wasm[3] !== 0x6d
) {
    throw new Error("Invalid WASM header");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// IMPORTED FUNCTION COUNT
// ------------------------------------------------------------
let pos = 8;
let importedFunctions = 0;

while (pos < wasm.length) {

    const sectionId = wasm[pos++];

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(wasm, pos);
    const sectionSize = sizeInfo.value;
    pos = sizeInfo.next;

    const sectionStart = pos;
    const sectionEnd = pos + sectionSize;

    if (sectionId === 2) {

        let p = sectionStart;

        const countInfo = readULEB(wasm, p);
        const importCount = countInfo.value;
        p = countInfo.next;

        for (let i = 0; i < importCount; i++) {

            let r = readULEB(wasm, p);
            p = r.next + r.value;

            r = readULEB(wasm, p);
            p = r.next + r.value;

            const kind = wasm[p++];

            if (kind === 0) {

                importedFunctions++;

                r = readULEB(wasm, p);
                p = r.next;
            }
            else if (kind === 1) {

                p++;

                const flags = wasm[p++];

                r = readULEB(wasm, p);
                p = r.next;

                if (flags & 1) {
                    r = readULEB(wasm, p);
                    p = r.next;
                }
            }
            else if (kind === 2) {

                const flags = wasm[p++];

                r = readULEB(wasm, p);
                p = r.next;

                if (flags & 1) {
                    r = readULEB(wasm, p);
                    p = r.next;
                }
            }
            else if (kind === 3) {
                p += 2;
            }
            else if (kind === 4) {

                p++;

                r = readULEB(wasm, p);
                p = r.next;
            }
        }
    }

    pos = sectionEnd;
}

console.log(`IMPORTED FUNCTION COUNT : ${importedFunctions}`);
console.log();

// FIND TARGET FUNCTION

pos = 8;

while (pos < wasm.length) {

    const sectionId = wasm[pos++];

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(wasm, pos);
    const sectionSize = sizeInfo.value;
    pos = sizeInfo.next;

    const sectionStart = pos;
    const sectionEnd = pos + sectionSize;

    if (sectionId === 10) {

        let p = sectionStart;

        const countInfo = readULEB(wasm, p);
        const bodyCount = countInfo.value;
        p = countInfo.next;

        for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < bodyCount; ordinal++) {

            const bodySizeInfo = readULEB(wasm, p);
            const bodySize = bodySizeInfo.value;
            p = bodySizeInfo.next;

            const bodyStart = p;
            const bodyEnd = p + bodySize;

            const functionIndex =
                importedFunctions + ordinal;

            if (functionIndex === TARGET_FUNC) {

                console.log("TARGET FOUND");
                console.log("-".repeat(72));
                console.log(`FUNCTION INDEX : ${functionIndex}`);
                console.log(`CODE ORDINAL   : ${ordinal}`);
                console.log(`BODY           : ${hex(bodyStart)} .. ${hex(bodyEnd)}`);
                console.log(`BODY SIZE      : ${bodySize}`);
                console.log("-".repeat(72));
                console.log();

                // ------------------------------------------------
                // RAW BODY
                // ------------------------------------------------
                for (let a = bodyStart; a < bodyEnd; a += 16) {

                    const lineEnd =
                        Math.min(a + 16, bodyEnd);

                    const bytes = [];

                    for (let b = a; b < lineEnd; b++) {
                        bytes.push(
                            wasm[b]
                                .toString(16)
                                .padStart(2, "0")
                        );
                    }

                    console.log(
                        `${hex(a)}  ${bytes.join(" ")}`
                    );
                }

                console.log();
                console.log("=".repeat(72));
                console.log("DONE");
                console.log("=".repeat(72));

                process.exit(0);
            }

            p = bodyEnd;
        }
    }

    pos = sectionEnd;
}

console.log(`FUNC ${TARGET_FUNC} NOT FOUND`);

import fs from "fs";

const wasmPath = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const TARGET_FUNC = 107;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

function hex(v, width = 8) {
    return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function readULEB(buf, pos) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * (2 ** shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }

    throw new Error("ULEB128 overflow");
}

function readSLEB(buf, pos) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {

            if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
                value |= (~0 << shift);
            }

            return {
                value: value | 0,
                next: p
            };
        }
    }

    throw new Error("SLEB128 overflow");
}

console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log(`FUNC ${TARGET_FUNC} RETURN / DATA FLOW`);
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("=".repeat(72));

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// WASM HEADER
// ------------------------------------------------------------
if (
    wasm[0] !== 0x00 ||
    wasm[1] !== 0x61 ||
    wasm[2] !== 0x73 ||
    wasm[3] !== 0x6d
) {
    throw new Error("Invalid WASM");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// IMPORT COUNT
// ------------------------------------------------------------
let pos = 8;
let importedFunctions = 0;

while (pos < wasm.length) {

    const sectionId = wasm[pos++];

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(wasm, pos);
    pos = sizeInfo.next;

    const sectionSize = sizeInfo.value;
    const sectionStart = pos;
    const sectionEnd = sectionStart + sectionSize;

    if (sectionId === 2) {

        let p = sectionStart;

        const c = readULEB(wasm, p);
        const count = c.value;
        p = c.next;

        for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {

            let r = readULEB(wasm, p);
            p = r.next + r.value;

            r = readULEB(wasm, p);
            p = r.next + r.value;

            const kind = wasm[p++];

            if (kind === 0) {

                importedFunctions++;

                r = readULEB(wasm, p);
                p = r.next;
            }
            else if (kind === 1) {

                p++;

                const flags = wasm[p++];

                r = readULEB(wasm, p);
                p = r.next;

                if (flags & 1) {
                    r = readULEB(wasm, p);
                    p = r.next;
                }
            }
            else if (kind === 2) {

                const flags = wasm[p++];

                r = readULEB(wasm, p);
                p = r.next;

                if (flags & 1) {
                    r = readULEB(wasm, p);
                    p = r.next;
                }
            }
            else if (kind === 3) {
                p += 2;
            }
            else if (kind === 4) {

                p++;

                r = readULEB(wasm, p);
                p = r.next;
            }
        }
    }

    pos = sectionEnd;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// FIND FUNC 107
// ------------------------------------------------------------
pos = 8;

while (pos < wasm.length) {

    const sectionId = wasm[pos++];

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(wasm, pos);
    pos = sizeInfo.next;

    const sectionSize = sizeInfo.value;
    const sectionStart = pos;
    const sectionEnd = sectionStart + sectionSize;

    if (sectionId === 10) {

        let p = sectionStart;

        const countInfo = readULEB(wasm, p);
        const bodyCount = countInfo.value;
        p = countInfo.next;

        for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < bodyCount; ordinal++) {

            const bodyInfo = readULEB(wasm, p);
            const bodySize = bodyInfo.value;
            p = bodyInfo.next;

            const bodyStart = p;
            const bodyEnd = p + bodySize;

            const functionIndex =
                importedFunctions + ordinal;

            if (functionIndex === TARGET_FUNC) {

                console.log("TARGET FOUND");
                console.log("-".repeat(72));
                console.log(`FUNCTION INDEX : ${functionIndex}`);
                console.log(`CODE ORDINAL   : ${ordinal}`);
                console.log(`BODY           : ${hex(bodyStart)} .. ${hex(bodyEnd)}`);
                console.log(`BODY SIZE      : ${bodySize}`);
                console.log("-".repeat(72));
                console.log();

                // ------------------------------------------------
                // CALLS
                // ------------------------------------------------
                console.log("CALLS");
                console.log("-".repeat(72));

                let q = bodyStart;

                while (q < bodyEnd) {

                    const opcode = wasm[q];

                    if (opcode === 0x10) {

                        const r = readULEB(wasm, q + 1);

                        console.log(
                            `${hex(q)}  CALL FUNC ${r.value}`
                        );

                        q = r.next;
                        continue;
                    }

                    q++;
                }

                console.log();

                // ------------------------------------------------
                // MEMORY OPERATIONS
                // ------------------------------------------------
                console.log("MEMORY LOAD / STORE");
                console.log("-".repeat(72));

                q = bodyStart;

                while (q < bodyEnd) {

                    const opcode = wasm[q];

                    // i32.load
                    if (opcode === 0x28) {

                        const align = readULEB(wasm, q + 1);
                        const offset = readULEB(wasm, align.next);

                        console.log(
                            `${hex(q)}  i32.load ` +
                            `align=${align.value} ` +
                            `offset=${hex(offset.value, 4)}`
                        );

                        q = offset.next;
                        continue;
                    }

                    // i64.load
                    if (opcode === 0x29) {

                        const align = readULEB(wasm, q + 1);
                        const offset = readULEB(wasm, align.next);

                        console.log(
                            `${hex(q)}  i64.load ` +
                            `align=${align.value} ` +
                            `offset=${hex(offset.value, 4)}`
                        );

                        q = offset.next;
                        continue;
                    }

                    // i32.store
                    if (opcode === 0x36) {

                        const align = readULEB(wasm, q + 1);
                        const offset = readULEB(wasm, align.next);

                        console.log(
                            `${hex(q)}  i32.store ` +
                            `align=${align.value} ` +
                            `offset=${hex(offset.value, 4)}`
                        );

                        q = offset.next;
                        continue;
                    }

                    // i64.store
                    if (opcode === 0x37) {

                        const align = readULEB(wasm, q + 1);
                        const offset = readULEB(wasm, align.next);

                        console.log(
                            `${hex(q)}  i64.store ` +
                            `align=${align.value} ` +
                            `offset=${hex(offset.value, 4)}`
                        );

                        q = offset.next;
                        continue;
                    }

                    q++;
                }

                console.log();

                // ------------------------------------------------
                // GLOBAL ACCESS
                // ------------------------------------------------
                console.log("GLOBAL ACCESS");
                console.log("-".repeat(72));

                q = bodyStart;

                let globalCount = 0;

                while (q < bodyEnd) {

                    const opcode = wasm[q];

                    if (opcode === 0x23 || opcode === 0x24) {

                        const r = readULEB(wasm, q + 1);

                        console.log(
                            `${hex(q)}  ` +
                            `${opcode === 0x23 ? "GLOBAL.GET" : "GLOBAL.SET"} ` +
                            `${r.value}`
                        );

                        globalCount++;

                        q = r.next;
                        continue;
                    }

                    q++;
                }

                if (globalCount === 0) {
                    console.log("NO GLOBAL ACCESS");
                }

                console.log();

                // ------------------------------------------------
                // RETURN / END
                // ------------------------------------------------
                console.log("RETURN-RELATED INSTRUCTIONS");
                console.log("-".repeat(72));

                q = bodyStart;

                while (q < bodyEnd) {

                    const opcode = wasm[q];

                    if (opcode === 0x0f) {

                        console.log(
                            `${hex(q)}  RETURN`
                        );
                    }

                    if (opcode === 0x0b) {

                        console.log(
                            `${hex(q)}  END`
                        );
                    }

                    q++;
                }

                console.log();
                console.log("=".repeat(72));
                console.log("DONE");
                console.log("=".repeat(72));

                process.exit(0);
            }

            p = bodyEnd;
        }
    }

    pos = sectionEnd;
}

console.log(`FUNC ${TARGET_FUNC} NOT FOUND`);

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const IMPORTED = 83;
const TARGET_FUNC = 107;

const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function uleb(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[pos++];
    value += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(x & 0x80)) {
      return { value, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function findCode() {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < b.length) {
    const id = b[p++];

    const s = uleb(p);
    p = s.next;

    if (id === 10) {
      return {
        start: p,
        end: p + s.value
      };
    }

    p += s.value;
  }

  throw new Error("CODE section not found");
}

function getFunction(index) {
  const code = findCode();

  let p = code.start;

  const count = uleb(p);
  p = count.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {

    const size = uleb(p);
    p = size.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + size.value;

    if (IMPORTED + i === index) {
      return { start, end, size: size.value };
    }

    p = end;
  }

  throw new Error(`FUNC ${index} not found`);
}

const fn = getFunction(TARGET_FUNC);

const returns = [];

for (let p = fn.start; p < fn.end; p++) {
  if (b[p] === 0x0f) {
    returns.push(p);
  }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 107 RETURN LOCAL2 / LOCAL1 SOURCE TRACE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(
  `BODY : 0x${fn.start.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} .. 0x${fn.end
    .toString(16)
    .padStart(8, "0")}`
);

console.log(`SIZE : ${fn.size}`);
console.log(`RETURNS : ${returns.length}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (const ret of returns) {

  console.log("");
  console.log(
    `RETURN @ 0x${ret.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
  );

  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  const from = Math.max(fn.start, ret - 80);

  for (let p = from; p < ret; p++) {

    const op = b[p];

    if (op === 0x20) {
      const q = uleb(p + 1);

      console.log(
        `0x${p.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  LOCAL.GET ${q.value}`
      );
    }

    else if (op === 0x21) {
      const q = uleb(p + 1);

      console.log(
        `0x${p.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  LOCAL.SET ${q.value}`
      );
    }

    else if (op === 0x22) {
      const q = uleb(p + 1);

      console.log(
        `0x${p.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  LOCAL.TEE ${q.value}`
      );
    }

    else if (op === 0x23) {
      const q = uleb(p + 1);

      console.log(
        `0x${p.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  GLOBAL.GET ${q.value}`
      );
    }

    else if (op === 0x24) {
      const q = uleb(p + 1);

      console.log(
        `0x${p.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  GLOBAL.SET ${q.value}`
      );
    }

    else if (op === 0x41) {
      const q = uleb(p + 1);

      console.log(
        `0x${p.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  I32.CONST ${q.value} (0x${q.value.toString(16)})`
      );
    }

    else if (op === 0x28) {
      const a = uleb(p + 1);
      const o = uleb(a.next);

      console.log(
        `0x${p.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  I32.LOAD align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`
      );
    }

    else if (op === 0x36) {
      const a = uleb(p + 1);
      const o = uleb(a.next);

      console.log(
        `0x${p.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  I32.STORE align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`
      );
    }

    else if (op === 0x10) {
      const q = uleb(p + 1);

      console.log(
        `0x${p.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  CALL FUNC ${q.value}`
      );
    }
  }

  console.log(
    `0x${ret.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  RETURN`
  );
}
