import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const IMPORTED = 83;
const TARGET_FUNC = 107;

const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function uleb(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[pos++];

    value += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(x & 0x80)) {
      return { value, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function findCodeSection() {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < b.length) {
    const id = b[p++];

    const s = uleb(p);
    p = s.next;

    if (id === 10) {
      return {
        start: p,
        end: p + s.value
      };
    }

    p += s.value;
  }

  throw new Error("CODE section not found");
}

function getFunction(index) {
  const code = findCodeSection();

  let p = code.start;

  const count = uleb(p);
  p = count.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const size = uleb(p);
    p = size.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + size.value;

    const funcIndex = IMPORTED + i;

    if (funcIndex === index) {
      return { start, end, size: size.value };
    }

    p = end;
  }

  throw new Error(`FUNC ${index} not found`);
}

  const to = Math.min(fn.end, r + 8);

    const end = Math.min(p + 16, to);

    for (let q = p; q < end; q++) {

        b[q].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")

    console.log(

    );
  }

  console.log("");
  console.log("LAST LOCAL OPERATIONS BEFORE RETURN:");

  const from2 = Math.max(fn.start, r - 16);

  for (let p = from2; p < r; p++) {

    const op = b[p];

    if (op === 0x20) {
      const q = uleb(p + 1);

      console.log(
        `0x${p.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  LOCAL.GET ${q.value}`
      );
    }

    if (op === 0x21) {
      const q = uleb(p + 1);

      console.log(
        `0x${p.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  LOCAL.SET ${q.value}`
      );
    }

    if (op === 0x22) {
      const q = uleb(p + 1);

      console.log(
        `0x${p.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  LOCAL.TEE ${q.value}`
      );
    }
  }
}

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const START = 0x0000d3eb;
const END   = 0x0000d3f5;

const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function uleb(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[pos++];

    value += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(x & 0x80)) {
      return { value, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readInstruction(p) {

  const start = p;
  const op = b[p++];

  let text = `0x${start.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  `;

  switch (op) {

    case 0x20: {
      const q = uleb(p);
      p = q.next;
      text += `LOCAL.GET ${q.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x21: {
      const q = uleb(p);
      p = q.next;
      text += `LOCAL.SET ${q.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x22: {
      const q = uleb(p);
      p = q.next;
      text += `LOCAL.TEE ${q.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x28: {
      const a = uleb(p);
      p = a.next;

      const o = uleb(p);
      p = o.next;

      text += `I32.LOAD align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x36: {
      const a = uleb(p);
      p = a.next;

      const o = uleb(p);
      p = o.next;

      text += `I32.STORE align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x41: {
      const q = uleb(p);
      p = q.next;

      text += `I32.CONST ${q.value} (0x${q.value.toString(16)})`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x10: {
      const q = uleb(p);
      p = q.next;

      text += `CALL FUNC ${q.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x6a:
      text += "I32.ADD";
      break;

    default:
      text += `OPCODE 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
      break;
  }

  return { next: p, text };
}

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const START = 0x0000d379;
const END   = 0x0000d3f7;

const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function uleb(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[pos++];

    value += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return { value, next: pos };

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function sleb(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let x;

  do {
    x = b[pos++];
    value |= (x & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;
  } while (x & 0x80);

  if (shift < 32 && (x & 0x40))
    value |= (~0 << shift);

  return { value, next: pos };
}

function ins(p) {

  const addr = p;
  const op = b[p++];

  let s =
    `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  `;

  if (op === 0x20) {
    const x = uleb(p);
    p = x.next;
    s += `LOCAL.GET ${x.value}`;

  } else if (op === 0x21) {
    const x = uleb(p);
    p = x.next;
    s += `LOCAL.SET ${x.value}`;

  } else if (op === 0x22) {
    const x = uleb(p);
    p = x.next;
    s += `LOCAL.TEE ${x.value}`;

  } else if (op === 0x28) {
    const a = uleb(p);
    p = a.next;

    const o = uleb(p);
    p = o.next;

    s += `I32.LOAD align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;

  } else if (op === 0x36) {
    const a = uleb(p);
    p = a.next;

    const o = uleb(p);
    p = o.next;

    s += `I32.STORE align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;

  } else if (op === 0x41) {
    const x = sleb(p);
    p = x.next;

    s += `I32.CONST ${x.value}`;

  } else if (op === 0x10) {
    const x = uleb(p);
    p = x.next;

    s += `CALL FUNC ${x.value}`;

  } else if (op === 0x11) {
    const t = uleb(p);
    p = t.next;

    const tab = uleb(p);
    p = tab.next;

    s += `CALL_INDIRECT type=${t.value} table=${tab.value}`;

  } else if (op === 0x6a) {
    s += "I32.ADD";

  } else if (op === 0x6b) {
    s += "I32.SUB";

  } else if (op === 0x45) {
    s += "I32.EQZ";

  } else if (op === 0x47) {
    s += "I32.NE";

  } else if (op === 0x04) {
    const x = b[p++];
    s += `IF type=0x${x.toString(16)}`;

  } else if (op === 0x02) {
    const x = b[p++];
    s += `BLOCK type=0x${x.toString(16)}`;

  } else if (op === 0x03) {
    const x = b[p++];
    s += `LOOP type=0x${x.toString(16)}`;

  } else if (op === 0x0c) {
    const x = uleb(p);
    p = x.next;
    s += `BR ${x.value}`;

  } else if (op === 0x0d) {
    const x = uleb(p);
    p = x.next;
    s += `BR_IF ${x.value}`;

  } else if (op === 0x1a) {
    s += "DROP";

  } else if (op === 0x0b) {
    s += "END";

  } else {
    s += `OPCODE 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
  }

  return { next: p, text: s };
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 107 STACK SOURCE FOR FUNC106");

console.log("============================================================");

console.log(
  `RANGE : 0x${START.toString(16)} .. 0x${END.toString(16)}`
);

while (p < END) {

  const x = ins(p);

  console.log(x.text);

  p = x.next;

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const IMPORTED = 83;
const FUNC107 = 107;
const FUNC106 = 106;

const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function uleb(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[pos++];
    value += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(x & 0x80)) {
      return { value, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function findCode() {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < b.length) {
    const id = b[p++];

    const s = uleb(p);
    p = s.next;

    if (id === 10) {
      return { start: p, end: p + s.value };
    }

    p += s.value;
  }

  throw new Error("CODE section not found");
}

function getFunction(index) {
  const code = findCode();

  let p = code.start;

  const count = uleb(p);
  p = count.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {

    const size = uleb(p);
    p = size.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + size.value;

    if (IMPORTED + i === index) {
      return { start, end, size: size.value };
    }

    p = end;
  }

  throw new Error(`FUNC ${index} not found`);
}

const fn = getFunction(FUNC107);

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET = 107;
const IMPORTS = 83;

function uleb(pos) {
    let v = 0, s = 0, p = pos;
    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[p++];
        v += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
        if (!(b & 0x80)) return { value: v, next: p };
        s += 7;
    }
}

function hx(v) {
    return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

let pos = 8;

while (pos < wasm.length) {

    const section = wasm[pos++];

    const si = uleb(pos);
    pos = si.next;

    const end = pos + si.value;

    if (section === 10) {

        let p = pos;

        const ci = uleb(p);
        const count = ci.value;
        p = ci.next;

        for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < count; ordinal++) {

            const bi = uleb(p);
            const size = bi.value;
            p = bi.next;

            const start = p;
            const bodyEnd = p + size;

            const func = IMPORTS + ordinal;

            if (func === TARGET) {

                console.log("=".repeat(72));
                console.log("FUNC 107 VALUE FLOW");
                console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
                console.log("=".repeat(72));
                console.log(`FUNCTION : ${func}`);
                console.log(`BODY     : ${hx(start)} .. ${hx(bodyEnd)}`);
                console.log(`SIZE     : ${size}`);
                console.log("-".repeat(72));

                let q = start;

                while (q < bodyEnd) {

                    const op = wasm[q];

                    // local.get
                    if (op === 0x20) {
                        const r = uleb(q + 1);
                        console.log(
                            `${hx(q)}  local.get ${r.value}`
                        );
                        q = r.next;
                        continue;
                    }

                    // local.set
                    if (op === 0x21) {
                        const r = uleb(q + 1);
                        console.log(
                            `${hx(q)}  local.set ${r.value}`
                        );
                        q = r.next;
                        continue;
                    }

                    // local.tee
                    if (op === 0x22) {
                        const r = uleb(q + 1);
                        console.log(
                            `${hx(q)}  local.tee ${r.value}`
                        );
                        q = r.next;
                        continue;
                    }

                    // call
                    if (op === 0x10) {
                        const r = uleb(q + 1);
                        console.log(
                            `${hx(q)}  CALL FUNC ${r.value}`
                        );
                        q = r.next;
                        continue;
                    }

                    // return
                    if (op === 0x0f) {
                        console.log(
                            `${hx(q)}  RETURN`
                        );
                        q++;
                        continue;
                    }

                    // global.get
                    if (op === 0x23) {
                        const r = uleb(q + 1);
                        console.log(
                            `${hx(q)}  GLOBAL.GET ${r.value}`
                        );
                        q = r.next;
                        continue;
                    }

                    // global.set
                    if (op === 0x24) {
                        const r = uleb(q + 1);
                        console.log(
                            `${hx(q)}  GLOBAL.SET ${r.value}`
                        );
                        q = r.next;
                        continue;
                    }

                    // i32.const
                    if (op === 0x41) {
                        const r = uleb(q + 1);
                        console.log(
                            `${hx(q)}  i32.const ${r.value}`
                        );
                        q = r.next;
                        continue;
                    }

                    q++;
                }

                console.log("=".repeat(72));
                console.log("DONE");
                console.log("=".repeat(72));

                process.exit(0);
            }

            p = bodyEnd;
        }
    }

    pos = end;
}

import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

// Known FUNC 122 body from previous analysis
const START = 0x1226d;
const END   = 0x1295b;

const TARGETS = new Set([
  0x242bd,
  0x242d1,
]);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 122 ARG2 DATAFLOW");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("WASM SIZE :", buf.length);
console.log("FUNC 122  :", `0x${START.toString(16)} .. 0x${END.toString(16)}`);
console.log("TARGETS   : 0x242bd / 0x242d1");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

function readULEB(pos: number) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (p < buf.length) {
    const b = buf[p++];
    result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
    shift += 7;
  }

  return { value: result >>> 0, next: p };
}

function readSLEB(pos: number) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;
  let b = 0;

  while (p < buf.length) {
    b = buf[p++];
    result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;
    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    result |= (~0 << shift);
  }

  return { value: result | 0, next: p };
}

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(6, "0");
}

function dumpAround(pos: number, radius = 12) {
  const a = Math.max(START, pos - radius);
  const b = Math.min(END, pos + radius);

  const bytes: string[] = [];

  for (let i = a; i < b; i++) {
    bytes.push(buf[i].toString(16).padStart(2, "0"));
  }

  console.log(
    `  ${hex(a)} .. ${hex(b)} : ${bytes.join(" ")}`
  );
}

console.log("\nSCANNING CONSTANTS INSIDE FUNC 122");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let p = START;
let count = 0;

while (p < END) {
  const opPos = p;
  const op = buf[p++];

  // i32.const
  if (op === 0x41) {
    const r = readSLEB(p);
    p = r.next;

    const value = r.value >>> 0;

    if (TARGETS.has(value)) {
      console.log("");
      console.log("############################################################");
      console.log("MATCH i32.const");
      console.log("############################################################");
      console.log("OFFSET :", hex(opPos));
      console.log("VALUE  :", hex(value));
      dumpAround(opPos);
      count++;
    }

    continue;
  }

  // local.get
  if (op === 0x20) {
    const r = readULEB(p);
    p = r.next;
    continue;
  }

  // local.set / local.tee
  if (op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
    const r = readULEB(p);
    p = r.next;
    continue;
  }

  // i32.load / load8 / load16 etc.
  if (
    op === 0x28 ||
    op === 0x2c ||
    op === 0x2d ||
    op === 0x2e ||
    op === 0x2f
  ) {
    const align = readULEB(p);
    p = align.next;

    const offset = readULEB(p);
    p = offset.next;

    console.log(
      `MEM LOAD @ ${hex(opPos)}  align=${align.value} offset=${hex(offset.value)}`
    );

    continue;
  }

  // i32.store variants
  if (
    op === 0x36 ||
    op === 0x3a ||
    op === 0x3b ||
    op === 0x3c ||
    op === 0x3e
  ) {
    const align = readULEB(p);
    p = align.next;

    const offset = readULEB(p);
    p = offset.next;

    console.log(
      `MEM STORE @ ${hex(opPos)}  align=${align.value} offset=${hex(offset.value)}`
    );

    continue;
  }

  // call
  if (op === 0x10) {
    const r = readULEB(p);
    p = r.next;

    console.log(
      `CALL @ ${hex(opPos)} -> FUNC ${r.value}`
    );

    continue;
  }

  // br / br_if
  if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
    const r = readULEB(p);
    p = r.next;
    continue;
  }

  // block / loop / if
  if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
    if (p < END) p++;
    continue;
  }

  // common no-immediate opcodes
}

console.log("\n------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("MATCH COUNT :", count);
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function readU32LE(p: number) {
  return (
    wasm[p] |
    (wasm[p + 1] << 8) |
    (wasm[p + 2] << 16) |
    (wasm[p + 3] << 24)
  ) >>> 0;
}

function readLeb(pos: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, p = pos;
  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
    s += 7;
  }
  return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
}

// Locate code section
let p = 8;
let codeStart = 0;
let codeSize = 0;

while (p < wasm.length) {
  const id = wasm[p++];
  let r = readLeb(p);
  const size = r.value;
  p = r.next;

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = p;
    codeSize = size;
    break;
  }

  p += size;
}

let q = codeStart;
const bodyCount = readLeb(q);
q = bodyCount.next;

const target = 122;

for (let i = 0; i < bodyCount.value; i++) {
  const bodySizeR = readLeb(q);
  const bodySize = bodySizeR.value;
  q = bodySizeR.next;

  const bodyStart = q;
  const bodyEnd = q + bodySize;

  if (i !== target) {
    q = bodyEnd;
    continue;
  }

  const localR = readLeb(q);
  q = localR.next;

  for (let j = 0; j < localR.value; j++) {
    const n = readLeb(q);
    q = n.next;
    q += 1;
  }

  const codeStartFunc = q;
  const codeEndFunc = bodyEnd - 1;

  console.log("FUNC 122 ARGUMENT TRACE");
  console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
  console.log("============================================================");

  for (let x = codeStartFunc; x < codeEndFunc; x++) {
    if (wasm[x] !== 0x10) continue;

    const r = readLeb(x + 1);

    if (r.value !== 87) continue;

    console.log("");
    console.log(`CALL FUNC 87 @ 0x${x.toString(16).padStart(4, "0")}`);
    console.log("PRECEDING BYTES:");

    const from = Math.max(codeStartFunc, x - 18);

    for (let k = from; k < x; k++) {
      console.log(
        `  0x${k.toString(16).padStart(6, "0")}  ${wasm[k].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
      );
    }
  }

  console.log("");
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log("DONE");
  break;
}

import fs from "fs";

const file = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

let p = 8;
let types: any[] = [];
let imported = 0;
let funcTypes: number[] = [];
let bodies: Buffer[] = [];

function u32() {
  let n = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    n |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return n >>> 0;
    s += 7;
  }
}

function vu(buf: Buffer, q: {v:number}) {
  let n=0,s=0;
  while(true){
    const x=buf[q.v++];
    n |= (x&127)<<s;
    if(!(x&128)) return n>>>0;
    s+=7;
  }
}

function s64(buf: Buffer, q:{v:number}) {
  let n=0n,s=0n;
  while(true){
    const x=buf[q.v++];
    n |= BigInt(x&127)<<s;
    if(!(x&128)){
      if(s<63n && (x&64)) n |= (-1n) << (s+7n);
      return n;
    }
    s+=7n;
  }
}

function sec() {
  const id=b[p++];
  const size=u32();
  const start=p, end=p+size;
  return {id,start,end};
}

while(p<b.length){
  const s=sec();
  const q=s.start;

  if(s.id===1){
    const qq={v:q};
    const count=vu(b,qq);
    for(let i=0;i<count;i++){
      if(b[qq.v++]!==0x60) throw Error("bad type");
      const np=vu(b,qq);
      const params=[...b.subarray(qq.v,qq.v+np)];
      qq.v+=np;
      const nr=vu(b,qq);
      const results=[...b.subarray(qq.v,qq.v+nr)];
      qq.v+=nr;
      types.push({params,results});
    }
  }

  if(s.id===2){
    const qq={v:q};
    const count=vu(b,qq);
    for(let i=0;i<count;i++){
      vu(b,qq);
      vu(b,qq);
      const kind=b[qq.v++];
      if(kind===0){
        vu(b,qq);
        imported++;
      } else if(kind===1){
        qq.v++; vu(b,qq);
        const lim=b[qq.v++];
        vu(b,qq);
        if(lim===1) vu(b,qq);
      } else if(kind===2){
        qq.v++;
      } else if(kind===3){
        qq.v++; qq.v++;
      }
    }
  }

  if(s.id===3){
    const qq={v:q};
    const count=vu(b,qq);
    for(let i=0;i<count;i++) funcTypes.push(vu(b,qq));
  }

  if(s.id===10){
    const qq={v:q};
    const count=vu(b,qq);
    for(let i=0;i<count;i++){
      const sz=vu(b,qq);
      const st=qq.v;
      const end=st+sz;
      const localCount=vu(b,qq);
      for(let j=0;j<localCount;j++){
        vu(b,qq);
        qq.v++;
      }
      bodies.push(b.subarray(st,end));
      qq.v=end;
    }
  }

  p=s.end;
}

const target=122;
const codeIndex=target-imported;
const body=bodies[codeIndex];

if(!body) throw Error("FUNC 122 body not found");

const typeIndex=funcTypes[codeIndex];
const sig=types[typeIndex];

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 122 -> EXACT CALL 87 ARGUMENT TRACE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 122 TYPE :", sig.params.map((x:number)=>x===0x7f?"i32":x===0x7e?"i64":"0x"+x.toString(16)).join(", "));
console.log("BODY SIZE     :", body.length);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

const q={v:0};
const localCount=vu(body,q);

for(let i=0;i<localCount;i++){
  vu(body,q);
  q.v++;
}

const locals:string[]=[];
for(let i=0;i<localCount;i++) locals.push(`local${i}`);

const stack:string[]=[];

function pop(n=1){
  const a:string[]=[];
  while(n--) a.unshift(stack.pop() ?? "?");
  return a;
}

function push(x:string){ stack.push(x); }

function hex(n:number){
  return "0x"+n.toString(16).padStart(8,"0");
}

while(q.v<body.length){
  const at=q.v;
  const op=body[q.v++];

  if(op===0x0b || op===0x05){
    continue;
  }

  if(op===0x20){
    const i=vu(body,q);
    push(`L${i}`);
    continue;
  }

  if(op===0x21){
    const i=vu(body,q);
    const a=pop()[0];
    continue;
  }

  if(op===0x22){
    const i=vu(body,q);
    const a=pop()[0];
    push(a);
    continue;
  }

  if(op===0x41){
    const n=vu(body,q);
    push(`${n}`);
    continue;
  }

  if(op===0x42){
    const n=s64(body,q);
    push(`${n}i64`);
    continue;
  }

  if(op===0x28){
    vu(body,q); vu(body,q);
    const a=pop()[0];
    push(`i32.load(${a})`);
    continue;
  }

  if(op===0x29){
    vu(body,q); vu(body,q);
    const a=pop()[0];
    push(`i64.load(${a})`);
    continue;
  }

  if(op===0x36){
    vu(body,q); vu(body,q);
    const [v,a]=pop(2);
    continue;
  }

  if(op===0x37){
    vu(body,q); vu(body,q);
    const [v,a]=pop(2);
    continue;
  }

  if(op===0x6a){
    const [a,b]=pop(2);
    push(`(${a}+${b})`);
    continue;
  }

  if(op===0x6b){
    const [a,b]=pop(2);
    push(`(${a}-${b})`);
    continue;
  }

  if(op===0x7c){
    const [a,b]=pop(2);
    push(`(${a}+${b})i64`);
    continue;
  }

  if(op===0x7d){
    const [a,b]=pop(2);
    push(`(${a}-${b})i64`);
    continue;
  }

  if(op===0x7e){
    const [a,b]=pop(2);
    push(`(${a}*${b})i64`);
    continue;
  }

  if(op===0x83){
    const [a,b]=pop(2);
    push(`(${a}&${b})i64`);
    continue;
  }

  if(op===0x84){
    const [a,b]=pop(2);
    push(`(${a}|${b})i64`);
    continue;
  }

  if(op===0x85){
    const [a,b]=pop(2);
    push(`(${a}^${b})i64`);
    continue;
  }

  if(op===0x86){
    const [a,b]=pop(2);
    push(`(${a}<<${b})i64`);
    continue;
  }

  if(op===0x47){
    const [a,b]=pop(2);
    push(`(${a}!=${b})`);
    continue;
  }

  if(op===0x45){
    const a=pop()[0];
    push(`(${a}==0)`);
    continue;
  }

  if(op===0x1a){
    pop();
    continue;
  }

  if(op===0x10){
    const fn=vu(body,q);

    if(fn===87){
      const args=pop(5);

      console.log("");
      console.log(`CALL 87 @ ${hex(at)}`);
      console.log("  ARG1 i32 :",args[0]);
      console.log("  ARG2 i64 :",args[1]);
      console.log("  ARG3 i64 :",args[2]);
      console.log("  ARG4 i64 :",args[3]);
      console.log("  ARG5 i64 :",args[4]);
      console.log("  STACK    :",stack.length);
      console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
    } else {
      const t=funcTypes[fn-imported];
      const n=t?.params?.length ?? 0;
      const args=pop(n);
      if(t?.results?.length) push(`CALL${fn}()`);
    }
    continue;
  }

  // block / loop / if
  if(op===0x02 || op===0x03 || op===0x04){
    q.v++;
    continue;
  }

  // br / br_if
  if(op===0x0c || op===0x0d){
    vu(body,q);
    if(op===0x0d) pop();
    continue;
  }

  // i64 extend/truncate/common conversions
  if(op===0xa7 || op===0xac || op===0xad || op===0xc4){
    const a=pop()[0];
    push(`conv(${a})`);
    continue;
  }

  console.log(`UNKNOWN ${hex(at)} opcode=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")}`);
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

import fs from "fs";

// FUNC 122 از خروجی قبلی:
// CODE INDEX = 39
const codeIndex = 39;

// ساده‌ترین decoder برای استخراج body
function readU32Leb(buf: Buffer, pos: number) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  while (true) {
    const b = buf[pos++];
    result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
    shift += 7;
  }
  return { value: result >>> 0, next: pos };
}

// پیدا کردن Code section
let p = 8;
let codeStart = -1;
let codeEnd = -1;

while (p < wasm.length) {
  const id = wasm[p++];
  const sz = readU32Leb(wasm, p);
  const sectionSize = sz.value;
  p = sz.next;

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = p;
    codeEnd = p + sectionSize;
    break;
  }

  p += sectionSize;
}

if (codeStart < 0) {
  throw new Error("Code section not found");
}

let q = codeStart;
const count = readU32Leb(wasm, q);
q = count.next;

let bodyStart = -1;
let bodyEnd = -1;

for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
  const r = readU32Leb(wasm, q);
  const bodySize = r.value;
  q = r.next;

  if (i === codeIndex) {
    bodyStart = q;
    bodyEnd = q + bodySize;
    break;
  }

  q += bodySize;
}

if (bodyStart < 0) {
  throw new Error(`FUNC ${codeIndex} body not found`);
}

const body = wasm.subarray(bodyStart, bodyEnd);

console.log(`BODY SIZE  : ${body.length}`);

      .toString(16)
      .padStart(2, "0")}`
  );
}

// CALL 87 در body offset 0x4b

if (body[callOffset] !== 0x10) {
  console.log(

      callOffset

  );

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const START = 0x1226d;
const END   = 0x1295b;

function uleb(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (p < buf.length) {
    const b = buf[p++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
    shift += 7;
  }

  return { value: value >>> 0, next: p };
}

function sleb(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (p < buf.length) {
    b = buf[p++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
    value |= ~0 << shift;

  return { value: value | 0, next: p };
}

function hx(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(6, "0");
}

function decode(pos: number) {
  const start = pos;
  const op = buf[pos++];

  const r = (name: string, value?: number) => ({
    start,
    end: pos,
    name,
    value,
  });

  switch (op) {
    case 0x20: {
      const x = uleb(pos);
      pos = x.next;
      return r("local.get", x.value);
    }

    case 0x21: {
      const x = uleb(pos);
      pos = x.next;
      return r("local.set", x.value);
    }

    case 0x22: {
      const x = uleb(pos);
      pos = x.next;
      return r("local.tee", x.value);
    }

    case 0x41: {
      const x = sleb(pos);
      pos = x.next;
      return r("i32.const", x.value);
    }

    case 0x10: {
      const x = uleb(pos);
      pos = x.next;
      return r("call", x.value);
    }

    case 0x0c: {
      const x = uleb(pos);
      pos = x.next;
      return r("br", x.value);
    }

    case 0x0d: {
      const x = uleb(pos);
      pos = x.next;
      return r("br_if", x.value);
    }

    case 0x02:
      if (pos < END) pos++;
      return r("block");

    case 0x03:
      if (pos < END) pos++;
      return r("loop");

    case 0x04:
      if (pos < END) pos++;
      return r("if");

    default:
      return r("opcode_" + op.toString(16));
  }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 122 -> CALL 87 ARGUMENT TRACE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("WASM SIZE :", buf.length);
console.log("FUNC 122  :", `${hx(START)} .. ${hx(END)}`);
console.log("TARGET    : CALL FUNC 87");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

const instructions: any[] = [];

let p = START;

while (p < END) {
  const ins = decode(p);

  if (ins.end <= p)
    break;

  instructions.push(ins);
  p = ins.end;
}

console.log("\nCALL 87 LOCATIONS");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let found = 0;

for (let i = 0; i < instructions.length; i++) {
  const ins = instructions[i];

  if (ins.name !== "call" || ins.value !== 87)
    continue;

  found++;

  console.log("");
  console.log("############################################################");
  console.log(`CALL 87 #${found} @ ${hx(ins.start)}`);
  console.log("############################################################");

  const from = Math.max(0, i - 12);
  const to = Math.min(instructions.length, i + 1);

  console.log("PREVIOUS INSTRUCTIONS");
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  for (let j = from; j < to; j++) {
    const x = instructions[j];

    let extra = "";

    if (
      x.name === "i32.const" &&
      (x.value === 0x242bd || x.value === 0x242d1)
    ) {
      extra = "  <<< TARGET CONSTANT";
    }

    if (x.name === "local.get")
      extra = `  <<< local ${x.value}`;

    if (x.name === "local.tee")
      extra = `  <<< local.tee ${x.value}`;

    console.log(
      `${hx(x.start)}  ${x.name}` +
      (x.value !== undefined ? ` ${x.value}` : "") +
      extra
    );
  }

  console.log("");
  console.log("STACK-NEAR-CALL INTERPRETATION");
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  const recent = instructions.slice(from, i);

  for (const x of recent) {
    if (x.name === "i32.const") {
      console.log(
        `${hx(x.start)}  PUSH CONST  ${x.value} (${hx(x.value >>> 0)})`
      );
    }

    else if (x.name === "local.get") {
      console.log(
        `${hx(x.start)}  PUSH LOCAL  ${x.value}`
      );
    }

    else if (x.name === "local.tee") {
      console.log(
        `${hx(x.start)}  LOCAL.TEE   ${x.value}`
      );
    }

    else if (x.name === "call") {
      console.log(
        `${hx(x.start)}  CALL        FUNC ${x.value}`
      );
    }
  }

  console.log("");
}

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("TOTAL CALL 87 :", found);
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET = 122;
const IMPORTS = 83;

function uleb(pos) {
    let v = 0, shift = 0, p = pos;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[p++];
        v += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if (!(b & 0x80)) {
            return { value: v, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function hx(v) {
    return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

let pos = 8;

while (pos < wasm.length) {

    const section = wasm[pos++];

    const s = uleb(pos);
    pos = s.next;

    const end = pos + s.value;

    if (section === 10) {

        let p = pos;

        const c = uleb(p);
        p = c.next;

        for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < c.value; ordinal++) {

            const b = uleb(p);
            const size = b.value;
            p = b.next;

            const start = p;
            const bodyEnd = p + size;

            const func = IMPORTS + ordinal;

            if (func === TARGET) {

                console.log("=".repeat(72));

                console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
                console.log("=".repeat(72));

                console.log(`SIZE     : ${size}`);
                console.log("-".repeat(72));

                let q = start;

                while (q < bodyEnd) {

                    const op = wasm[q];

                    // local.get
                    if (op === 0x20) {
                        const r = uleb(q + 1);

                        console.log(
                            `${hx(q)}  local.get ${r.value}`
                        );

                        q = r.next;
                        continue;
                    }

                    // local.set
                    if (op === 0x21) {

                        console.log(
                            `${hx(q)}  local.set ${r.value}`
                        );

                        q = r.next;
                        continue;
                    }

                    // local.tee
                    if (op === 0x22) {
                        const r = uleb(q + 1);

                        console.log(
                            `${hx(q)}  local.tee ${r.value}`
                        );

                        q = r.next;
                        continue;
                    }

                    // i32.const
                    if (op === 0x41) {
                        const r = uleb(q + 1);

                        console.log(
                            `${hx(q)}  i32.const ${r.value} (0x${r.value.toString(16)})`
                        );

                        q = r.next;
                        continue;
                    }

                    // call
                    if (op === 0x10) {
                        const r = uleb(q + 1);

                        console.log(
                            `${hx(q)}  CALL FUNC ${r.value}`
                        );

                        q = r.next;
                        continue;
                    }

                    // call_indirect
                    if (op === 0x11) {
                        const r = uleb(q + 1);

                        console.log(
                            `${hx(q)}  CALL_INDIRECT type=${r.value}`
                        );

                        q = r.next + 1;
                        continue;
                    }

                    // global.get
                    if (op === 0x23) {
                        const r = uleb(q + 1);

                        console.log(
                            `${hx(q)}  GLOBAL.GET ${r.value}`
                        );

                        q = r.next;
                        continue;
                    }

                    // global.set
                    if (op === 0x24) {
                        const r = uleb(q + 1);

                        console.log(
                            `${hx(q)}  GLOBAL.SET ${r.value}`
                        );

                        q = r.next;
                        continue;
                    }

                    // return
                    if (op === 0x0f) {
                        console.log(
                            `${hx(q)}  RETURN`
                        );
                        q++;
                        continue;
                    }

                    q++;
                }

                console.log("-".repeat(72));
                console.log("IMPORTANT CALL SITE IN FUNC 107:");
                console.log("  arg0 = 328");
                console.log("  arg1 = 139014");
                console.log("  arg2 = 324");

    pos = end;

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const START = 0x001226d;
const END   = 0x001295b;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 122 - CALL SITE TRACE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

for (let p = START; p < END; p++) {
  const op = wasm[p];

  // call
  if (op === 0x10) {
    let q = p + 1;
    let func = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (q < END) {
      const b = wasm[q++];
      func |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

      if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
      shift += 7;
      if (shift > 35) break;
    }

    const from = Math.max(START, p - 24);
    const to = Math.min(END, q + 12);

    const raw = [...wasm.subarray(from, to)]
      .map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
      .join(" ");

    console.log("");
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
    console.log(`CALL OFFSET : 0x${p.toString(16)}`);
    console.log(`TARGET FUNC : ${func >>> 0}`);
    console.log(`RAW         : ${raw}`);
  }
}

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const START = 0x0000eec5;
const END   = 0x0000ef44;

function uleb(pos) {
    let v = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];

        v += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if (!(b & 0x80))
            return { value: v, next: pos };

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function hex(v, n = 8) {
    return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(n, "0");
}

    switch (op) {

        case 0x02:
        case 0x03:
        case 0x04: {
            const x = wasm[p++];
            const name =
                op === 0x02 ? "BLOCK" :
                op === 0x03 ? "LOOP"  : "IF";

            console.log(
                `${hex(at)} ${name} type=${hex(x,2)}`
            );
            break;
        }

        case 0x05:
            console.log(`${hex(at)} ELSE`);
            break;

        case 0x0b:
            console.log(`${hex(at)} END`);
            break;

        case 0x0c: {
            const x = uleb(p);
            p = x.next;
            console.log(`${hex(at)} BR depth=${x.value}`);
            break;
        }

        case 0x0d: {
            const x = uleb(p);
            p = x.next;
            console.log(`${hex(at)} BR_IF depth=${x.value}`);
            break;
        }

        case 0x0f:
            console.log(`${hex(at)} RETURN`);
            break;

        case 0x20: {
            const x = uleb(p);
            p = x.next;
            console.log(`${hex(at)} local.get ${x.value}`);
            break;
        }

        case 0x21: {
            const x = uleb(p);
            p = x.next;
            console.log(`${hex(at)} local.set ${x.value}`);
            break;
        }

        case 0x22: {
            const x = uleb(p);
            p = x.next;
            console.log(`${hex(at)} local.tee ${x.value}`);
            break;
        }

        case 0x41: {
            const b0 = wasm[p++];
            let value = b0 & 0x7f;
            let shift = 7;

            if (b0 & 0x80) {
                while (true) {
                    const b = wasm[p++];
                    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

                    if (!(b & 0x80))
                        break;

                    shift += 7;
                }
            }

            console.log(
                `${hex(at)} i32.const ${value >>> 0} (${hex(value >>> 0)})`
            );
            break;
        }

        case 0x10: {
            const x = uleb(p);
            p = x.next;
            console.log(`${hex(at)} CALL FUNC ${x.value}`);
            break;
        }

        case 0x11: {
            const t = uleb(p);
            p = t.next;

            const table = uleb(p);
            p = table.next;

            console.log(
                `${hex(at)} CALL_INDIRECT type=${t.value} table=${table.value}`
            );
            break;
        }

        case 0x45:
            console.log(`${hex(at)} i32.eqz`);
            break;

        case 0x46:
            console.log(`${hex(at)} i32.eq`);
            break;

        case 0x47:
            console.log(`${hex(at)} i32.ne`);
            break;

        case 0x72:
            console.log(`${hex(at)} i32.shr_u`);
            break;

        case 0x28: {
            const a = uleb(p);
            p = a.next;

            const o = uleb(p);
            p = o.next;

            console.log(
                `${hex(at)} i32.load align=${a.value} offset=${hex(o.value)}`
            );
            break;
        }

        case 0x2d: {
            const a = uleb(p++);
            const o = uleb(p);
            p = o.next;

            console.log(
                `${hex(at)} i32.load8_u align=${a.value} offset=${hex(o.value)}`
            );
            break;
        }

        case 0x36: {
            const a = uleb(p);
            p = a.next;

            const o = uleb(p);
            p = o.next;

            console.log(
                `${hex(at)} i32.store align=${a.value} offset=${hex(o.value)}`
            );
            break;
        }

        default:
            console.log(`${hex(at)} OPCODE ${hex(op,2)}`);
            break;
    }
}

console.log();
console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log("RETURN FLOW SUMMARY");
console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log();
console.log("SELF-CALL RESULT:");
console.log("  local3 = CALL_INDIRECT(...)");
console.log();
console.log("THEN:");
console.log("  local.get 3");
console.log("  br_if depth=0");
console.log();
console.log("Therefore:");
console.log("  return != 0  -> immediate outer-block exit");
console.log("  return == 0  -> continue to FUNC259 / FUNC85 / FUNC84 / FUNC86");
console.log();
console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log("DONE");
console.log("=".repeat(72));

FILE: scripts\traceFunc122Runtime.ts

import fs from "fs";

const file =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

const START = 0x0000eec5;
const END   = 0x0000ef44;

const ARG0 = 32;
const ARG1 = 0x242d1;
const ARG2 = 902;

function readULEB(p: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };

    s += 7;
  }
}

function readSLEB(p: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;

    if (!(x & 0x80)) {
      if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40))
        v |= (~0 << (s + 7));

      return { value: v | 0, next: p };
    }

    s += 7;
  }
}

function hex(x: number) {
  return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

console.log("FUNC 122 RUNTIME-PATH SYMBOLIC TRACE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`ARGS : ${ARG0}, ${hex(ARG1)}, ${ARG2}`);
console.log(`RANGE: ${hex(START)} .. ${hex(END)}`);
console.log("============================================================");

let p = START;

while (p < END) {

  const a = p;
  const op = b[p++];

  let s = "";

  switch (op) {

    case 0x00:
      s = "unreachable";
      break;

    case 0x01:
      s = "nop";
      break;

    case 0x02:
      s = "block";
      p++;
      break;

    case 0x03:
      s = "loop";
      p++;
      break;

    case 0x04:
      s = "if";
      p++;
      break;

    case 0x05:
      s = "else";
      break;

    case 0x0b:
      s = "end";
      break;

    case 0x0c: {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      p = r.next;
      s = `br ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x0d: {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      p = r.next;
      s = `br_if ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x0f:
      s = "return";
      break;

    case 0x10: {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      p = r.next;
      s = `CALL FUNC ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x11: {
      const t = readULEB(p);
      p = t.next;

      const tab = readULEB(p);
      p = tab.next;

      s = `CALL_INDIRECT type=${t.value} table=${tab.value}`;

      if (t.value === 3 && tab.value === 0) {
        s += "  <-- IMPORTANT INDIRECT CALL";
        s += " | selector = local3";
      }

      break;
    }

    case 0x1a:
      s = "drop";
      break;

    case 0x20: {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      p = r.next;

      const names: Record<number, string> = {
        0: `local0=${ARG0}`,
        1: `local1=${hex(ARG1)}`,
        2: `local2=${ARG2}`,
        3: "local3=<runtime>",
      };

      s = names[r.value] ?? `local.get ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x21: {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      p = r.next;
      s = `local.set ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x22: {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      p = r.next;
      s = `local.tee ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x28: {
      const al = readULEB(p);
      p = al.next;

      const off = readULEB(p);
      p = off.next;

      s = `i32.load align=${al.value} offset=${off.value}`;

      if (a === 0x0000eed3) {
        s += "  <-- MEM[0x19534C]";
      }

      break;
    }

    case 0x36: {
      const al = readULEB(p);
      p = al.next;

      const off = readULEB(p);
      p = off.next;

      s = `i32.store align=${al.value} offset=${off.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x41: {
      const r = readSLEB(p);
      p = r.next;

      s = `i32.const ${r.value} (${hex(r.value >>> 0)})`;

      if (r.value === 4788)
        s += "  <-- TABLE[4788] = FUNC122";

      if ((r.value >>> 0) === 0x19534c)
        s += "  <-- ADDRESS 0x19534C";

      break;
    }

    case 0x45:
      s = "i32.eqz";
      break;

    case 0x46:
      s = "i32.eq";
      break;

    case 0x47:
      s = "i32.ne";
      break;

    case 0x6a:
      s = "i32.add";
      break;

    case 0x6b:
      s = "i32.sub";
      break;

    default:
      s = `opcode 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
      break;
  }

  console.log(`${hex(a)}  ${s}`);
}

console.log("\n============================================================");
console.log("STATIC SELECTOR RESULT");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("MEM[0x19534C] = 4788");
console.log("TABLE[4788]   = FUNC122");
console.log("Therefore indirect target = FUNC122");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const START = 0x0000eec5;
const END   = 0x0000ef44;

function uleb(pos) {
    let v = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];

        v += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            return { value: v, next: pos };

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function hex(v, n = 8) {
    return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(n, "0");
}

console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log("FUNC 122 SELF CALL ARGUMENT TRACE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log();

console.log(`BODY : ${hex(START)} .. ${hex(END)}`);
console.log();

let p = START;

while (p < END) {

    const op = wasm[p];
    const at = p++;

    switch (op) {

        case 0x20: { // local.get
            const x = uleb(p);
            p = x.next;
            console.log(
                `${hex(at)}  local.get ${x.value}`
            );
            break;
        }

        case 0x21: { // local.set
            const x = uleb(p);
            p = x.next;
            console.log(
                `${hex(at)}  local.set ${x.value}`
            );
            break;
        }

        case 0x22: { // local.tee
            const x = uleb(p);
            p = x.next;
            console.log(
                `${hex(at)}  local.tee ${x.value}`
            );
            break;
        }

        case 0x41: { // i32.const
            const x = uleb(p);
            p = x.next;
            console.log(
                `${hex(at)}  i32.const ${x.value} (${hex(x.value)})`
            );
            break;
        }

        case 0x10: { // call
            const x = uleb(p);
            p = x.next;
            console.log(
                `${hex(at)}  CALL FUNC ${x.value}`
            );
            break;
        }

        case 0x11: { // call_indirect
            const type = uleb(p);
            p = type.next;

            const table = uleb(p);
            p = table.next;

            console.log(
                `${hex(at)}  CALL_INDIRECT type=${type.value} table=${table.value}`
            );

            console.log();
            console.log("^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^");
            console.log("SELF-DISPATCH POINT");
            console.log("^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^");
            console.log();

            break;
        }

        case 0x0f:
            console.log(`${hex(at)}  RETURN`);
            break;

        case 0x0b:
            console.log(`${hex(at)}  END`);
            break;

        default:
            break;
    }
}

    );

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceFunc1974Callers.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const wasmPath = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const TARGET_FUNC = 1974;

const CODE_START = 0x00006e7d;
const CODE_END   = 0x00324ef5;

const TARGETS = new Set([
  0x0019534c,
  0x00195350,
  0x00195354,
  0x00195358,
]);

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function uleb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return {
        value: value >>> 0,
        next: pos,
      };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readU32LE(pos: number) {
  return (
    wasm[pos] |
    (wasm[pos + 1] << 8) |
    (wasm[pos + 2] << 16) |
    (wasm[pos + 3] << 24)
  ) >>> 0;
}

function isI32Const(pos: number) {
  return wasm[pos] === 0x41;
}

function readSleb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let byte = 0;

  while (true) {
    byte = wasm[pos++];
    value |= (byte & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (byte & 0x40)) {
    value |= (~0 << shift);
  }

  return {
    value: value | 0,
    next: pos,
  };
}

function skipImmediate(op: number, p: number): number {
  switch (op) {

    // block / loop / if
    case 0x02:
    case 0x03:
    case 0x04:
      return p + 1;

    // br / br_if
    case 0x0c:
    case 0x0d: {
      return uleb(p).next;
    }

    // call
    case 0x10: {
      return uleb(p).next;
    }

    // call_indirect
    case 0x11: {
      const a = uleb(p);
      return a.next + 1;
    }

    // local.get / set / tee
    case 0x20:
    case 0x21:
    case 0x22:
      return uleb(p).next;

    // global.get / set
    case 0x23:
    case 0x24:
      return uleb(p).next;

    // i32.load
    case 0x28: {
      const a = uleb(p);
      return uleb(a.next).next;
    }

    // i32.store
    case 0x36: {
      const a = uleb(p);
      return uleb(a.next).next;
    }

    // i32.const
    case 0x41:
      return readSleb(p).next;

    // memory.copy
    case 0xfc: {
      const sub = uleb(p);
      if (sub.value === 10) {
        return sub.next + 2;
      }
      return sub.next;
    }

    // memory.fill
    case 0xfc: {
      return p;
    }

    default:
      return p;
  }
}

type Hit = {
  pc: number;
  caller: number;
  args: string[];
};

console.log("TRACE FUNC1974 CALLERS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("=".repeat(60));
console.log(`TARGET FUNC = ${TARGET_FUNC}`);
console.log(`CODE = ${hex(CODE_START)} .. ${hex(CODE_END)}`);
console.log();

const hits: Hit[] = [];

let p = CODE_START;
let currentFunc = -1;

while (p < CODE_END - 4) {

  const start = p;
  const op = wasm[p++];

  // Function boundaries cannot be reliably reconstructed from raw bytes
  // without the code-section body table, so we identify direct calls first.
  if (op === 0x10) {
    const x = uleb(p);
    p = x.next;

    if (x.value === TARGET_FUNC) {

      const args: string[] = [];

      // Inspect preceding ~20 bytes/instructions.
      let q = Math.max(CODE_START, start - 80);

      while (q < start) {
        const a = q;
        const o = wasm[q++];

        if (o === 0x20) {
          const x2 = uleb(q);
          q = x2.next;
          args.push(`local${x2.value} @ ${hex(a)}`);
          continue;
        }

        if (o === 0x41) {
          const x2 = readSleb(q);
          q = x2.next;
          args.push(`CONST(${hex(x2.value >>> 0)}) @ ${hex(a)}`);
          continue;
        }

        if (o === 0x23) {
          const x2 = uleb(q);
          q = x2.next;
          args.push(`global${x2.value} @ ${hex(a)}`);
          continue;
        }

        if (o === 0x28) {
          const x2 = uleb(q);
          q = x2.next;
          const x3 = uleb(q);
          q = x3.next;
          args.push(
            `i32.load(offset=${hex(x3.value)}) @ ${hex(a)}`
          );
          continue;
        }

        if (o === 0x21 || o === 0x22 || o === 0x24) {
          const x2 = uleb(q);
          q = x2.next;
          continue;
        }

        q = skipImmediate(o, q);
      }

      hits.push({
        pc: start,
        caller: currentFunc,
        args,
      });

      console.log("-".repeat(60));
      console.log(`CALL FUNC1974 @ ${hex(start)}`);
      console.log(`RAW PRECEDING ARGUMENT CANDIDATES:`);

      for (const a of args.slice(-12)) {
        console.log(`  ${a}`);
      }

      // Show exact bytes around call.
      const lo = Math.max(CODE_START, start - 32);
      const hi = Math.min(CODE_END, start + 8);

      console.log();
      console.log(`RAW ${hex(lo)} .. ${hex(hi)}`);

      for (let z = lo; z < hi; z += 16) {
        const end = Math.min(hi, z + 16);

        const bytes: string[] = [];

        for (let k = z; k < end; k++) {
          bytes.push(
            wasm[k].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
          );
        }

        console.log(
          `${hex(z)}  ${bytes.join(" ")}`
        );
      }

      // Check whether the immediately preceding bytes contain
      // a target selector constant.
      for (let z = Math.max(CODE_START, start - 40); z < start; z++) {
        if (!isI32Const(z)) continue;

        const x2 = readSleb(z + 1);

        if (TARGETS.has(x2.value >>> 0)) {
          console.log();
          console.log(
            `>>> TARGET CONSTANT BEFORE CALL = ${hex(x2.value >>> 0)}`
          );
          console.log(
            `>>> CONST PC = ${hex(z)}`
          );
        }
      }
    }

    continue;
  }

  // Keep scanner moving.
  p = skipImmediate(op, p);
}

console.log();
console.log("=".repeat(60));
console.log("SUMMARY");
console.log(`DIRECT CALLS TO FUNC1974 = ${hits.length}`);
console.log();

if (hits.length === 0) {
  console.log("NO DIRECT CALLS FOUND");
  console.log("FUNC1974 MAY BE REACHED INDIRECTLY OR EXPORTED.");
}

console.log();
console.log("TARGET ADDRESSES:");
for (const x of TARGETS) {
  console.log(`  ${hex(x)}`);
}

console.log();
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("NO WASM EXECUTION");

FILE: scripts\traceFunc2034Raw.mjs

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET = 2034;
const IMPORTED = 83;

function readULEB(pos) {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[pos++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return { value: v >>> 0, next: pos };

    s += 7;
  }
}

function hx(v) {
  return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

let p = 8;
let codeStart = -1;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];

  const s = readULEB(p);
  p = s.next;

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = p;
    break;
  }

  p += s.value;
}

let q = codeStart;
const count = readULEB(q);
q = count.next;

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < count.value; ordinal++) {

  const size = readULEB(q);
  q = size.next;

  const start = q;
  const end = q + size.value;

  const globalIndex = ordinal + IMPORTED;

  if (globalIndex === TARGET) {

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("FUNC 2034 RAW BODY");
    console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(`GLOBAL FUNC : ${globalIndex}`);
    console.log(`CODE ORDINAL: ${ordinal}`);
    console.log(`BODY        : ${hx(start)} .. ${hx(end)}`);
    console.log(`SIZE        : ${end - start}`);
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    for (let pos = start; pos < end; pos += 16) {
      const stop = Math.min(pos + 16, end);

      let line = "";
      for (let x = pos; x < stop; x++) {
        line += b[x].toString(16).padStart(2, "0") + " ";
      }

      console.log(`${hx(pos)}  ${line}`);
    }

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("DONE");
    console.log("============================================================");

    break;
  }

  q = end;
}

FILE: scripts\traceFunc2055.mjs

import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const TARGET = 2055;

function uleb(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return { value: v, next: p };

    s += 7;
  }
}

function sleb(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x;

  do {
    x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;
  } while (x & 0x80);

  if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40))
    v |= (~0 << s);

  return { value: v, next: p };
}

function sections() {

  let p = 8;
  let functionSection = null;
  let codeSection = null;

  while (p < b.length) {

    const id = b[p++];

    const z = uleb(p);
    const size = z.value;
    p = z.next;

    if (id === 3)
      functionSection = {
        start: p,
        end: p + size
      };

    if (id === 10)
      codeSection = {
        start: p,
        end: p + size
      };

    p += size;
  }

  return { functionSection, codeSection };
}

const { functionSection, codeSection } = sections();

if (!functionSection || !codeSection) {
  console.log("FUNCTION/CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");
  process.exit(1);
}

/*
 * Function Section:
 *
 * vector of type indices for DEFINED functions only.
 * Function index = imported_count + code_ordinal.
 *
 * Therefore first obtain imported_count from Import Section.
 */

function getImportedFunctionCount() {

  let p = 8;

  while (p < b.length) {

    const id = b[p++];

    const z = uleb(p);
    const size = z.value;
    p = z.next;

    if (id !== 2) {
      p += size;
      continue;
    }

    const end = p + size;

    const c = uleb(p);
    p = c.next;

    let count = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < c.value; i++) {

      const ml = uleb(p);
      p = ml.next + ml.value;

      const nl = uleb(p);
      p = nl.next + nl.value;

      const kind = b[p++];

      if (kind === 0) {
        uleb(p);
        p = uleb(p).next;
        count++;
      }

      else if (kind === 1) {
        p += 4;
      }

      else if (kind === 2) {
        const fl = uleb(p);
        p = fl.next + fl.value;
      }

      else if (kind === 3) {
        p += 2;
      }
    }

    return count;
  }

  return 0;
}

const importedCount = getImportedFunctionCount();

const ordinal = TARGET - importedCount;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 2055 EXACT BODY");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`FUNCTION INDEX : ${TARGET}`);
console.log(`IMPORTED COUNT : ${importedCount}`);
console.log(`CODE ORDINAL   : ${ordinal}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

if (ordinal < 0) {
  console.log("ERROR: TARGET RESOLVED AS IMPORTED");
  process.exit(1);
}

/*
 * Function Section validation
 */

let fp = functionSection.start;

const fc = uleb(fp);
fp = fc.next;

if (ordinal >= fc.value) {
  console.log("ERROR: CODE ORDINAL OUT OF RANGE");
  console.log(`DEFINED FUNCTION COUNT : ${fc.value}`);
  process.exit(1);
}

let targetType = null;

for (let i = 0; i <= ordinal; i++) {

  const x = uleb(fp);
  fp = x.next;

  if (i === ordinal)
    targetType = x.value;
}

console.log(`TYPE INDEX     : ${targetType}`);

 * Locate body in Code Section

let cp = codeSection.start;

const cc = uleb(cp);
cp = cc.next;

if (ordinal >= cc.value) {
  console.log("ERROR: CODE ORDINAL EXCEEDS CODE COUNT");
  process.exit(1);
}

let body = null;

for (let i = 0; i < cc.value; i++) {

  const s = uleb(cp);
  const size = s.value;
  cp = s.next;

  const start = cp;
  const end = cp + size;

  if (i === ordinal) {
    body = { start, end };
    break;
  }

  cp = end;
}

if (!body) {
  console.log("BODY NOT FOUND");
  process.exit(1);
}

console.log(
  `BODY           : 0x${body.start.toString(16)} .. 0x${body.end.toString(16)}`
);

function decode(p) {

  const addr = p;
  const op = b[p++];

  let text =
    `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  `;

  switch (op) {

    case 0x01:
      text += "NOP";
      break;

    case 0x02:
      text += `BLOCK type=0x${b[p++].toString(16)}`;
      break;

    case 0x03:
      text += `LOOP type=0x${b[p++].toString(16)}`;
      break;

    case 0x04:
      text += `IF type=0x${b[p++].toString(16)}`;
      break;

    case 0x05:
      text += "ELSE";
      break;

    case 0x0b:
      text += "END";
      break;

    case 0x0c: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;
      text += `BR ${x.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x0d: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;
      text += `BR_IF ${x.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x0f:
      text += "RETURN";
      break;

    case 0x10: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;
      text += `CALL FUNC ${x.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x11: {
      const t = uleb(p);
      p = t.next;

      const tab = uleb(p);
      p = tab.next;

      text +=
        `CALL_INDIRECT type=${t.value} table=${tab.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x1a:
      text += "DROP";
      break;

    case 0x1b:
      text += "SELECT";
      break;

    case 0x20: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;
      text += `LOCAL.GET ${x.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x21: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;
      text += `LOCAL.SET ${x.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x22: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;
      text += `LOCAL.TEE ${x.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x23: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;
      text += `GLOBAL.GET ${x.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x24: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;
      text += `GLOBAL.SET ${x.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x28: {
      const a = uleb(p);
      p = a.next;

      const o = uleb(p);
      p = o.next;

      text +=
        `I32.LOAD align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x2d: {
      const a = uleb(p);
      p = a.next;

      const o = uleb(p);
      p = o.next;

      text +=
        `I32.LOAD8_U align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x36: {
      const a = uleb(p);
      p = a.next;

      const o = uleb(p);
      p = o.next;

      text +=
        `I32.STORE align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x3a: {
      const a = uleb(p);
      p = a.next;

      const o = uleb(p);
      p = o.next;

      text +=
        `I32.STORE8 align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x41: {
      const x = sleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      text += `I32.CONST ${x.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x42: {
      const x = sleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      text += `I64.CONST ${x.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x45:
      text += "I32.EQZ";
      break;

    case 0x46:
      text += "I32.EQ";
      break;

    case 0x47:
      text += "I32.NE";
      break;

    case 0x48:
      text += "I32.LT_S";
      break;

    case 0x49:
      text += "I32.LT_U";
      break;

    case 0x4a:
      text += "I32.GT_S";
      break;

    case 0x4b:
      text += "I32.GT_U";
      break;

    case 0x6a:
      text += "I32.ADD";
      break;

    case 0x6b:
      text += "I32.SUB";
      break;

    case 0x71:
      text += "I32.AND";
      break;

    case 0x72:
      text += "I32.OR";
      break;

    case 0x73:
      text += "I32.XOR";
      break;

    case 0x74:
      text += "I32.SHL";
      break;

    case 0x75:
      text += "I32.SHR_S";
      break;

    case 0x76:
      text += "I32.SHR_U";
      break;

    default:
      text +=
        `OPCODE 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")}`;
      break;
  }

  return { next:p, text };
}

let p = body.start;

while (p < body.end) {

  const x = decode(p);

  console.log(x.text);

  p = x.next;
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("GLOBAL0 / SELECTOR REFERENCE SCAN");
console.log("============================================================");

for (let p = body.start; p < body.end; p++) {

  if (b[p] === 0x23 || b[p] === 0x24) {

    const x = uleb(p + 1);

    if (x.value === 0)
      console.log(
        `GLOBAL0 ${b[p] === 0x23 ? "GET" : "SET"} @ 0x${p.toString(16)}`
      );
  }

  if (b[p] === 0x41) {

    const x = sleb(p + 1);

    if (
      x.value === 0x19534c ||
      x.value === 0x250fb8 ||
      x.value === 0x121e7b4
    ) {
      console.log(
        `CONST MATCH @ 0x${p.toString(16)} : ` +
        `${x.value} (0x${(x.value >>> 0).toString(16)})`
      );
    }
  }
}

import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const START = 0x93c1b;
const END   = 0x93c9e;
const TARGET = 0x93c7b;

function uleb(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { value:v, next:p };
    s += 7;
  }
}

function sleb(p) {
  let v=0,s=0,x;
  do {
    x=b[p++];
    v |= (x&0x7f)<<s;
    s+=7;
  } while(x&0x80);
  if(s<32 && (x&0x40)) v|=(~0<<s);
  return {value:v,next:p};
}

function dec(p) {
  const a=p, op=b[p++];

  switch(op) {
    case 0x20:{const x=uleb(p);return {next:x.next,s:`0x${a.toString(16)} LOCAL.GET ${x.value}`};}
    case 0x21:{const x=uleb(p);return {next:x.next,s:`0x${a.toString(16)} LOCAL.SET ${x.value}`};}
    case 0x22:{const x=uleb(p);return {next:x.next,s:`0x${a.toString(16)} LOCAL.TEE ${x.value}`};}
    case 0x41:{const x=sleb(p);return {next:x.next,s:`0x${a.toString(16)} I32.CONST ${x.value}`};}
    case 0x10:{const x=uleb(p);return {next:x.next,s:`0x${a.toString(16)} CALL ${x.value}`};}
    case 0x11:{
      const t=uleb(p); p=t.next;
      const tb=uleb(p); p=tb.next;
      return {next:p,s:`0x${a.toString(16)} CALL_INDIRECT type=${t.value} table=${tb.value}`};
    }
    case 0x28:{
      const al=uleb(p);p=al.next;
      const of=uleb(p);p=of.next;
      return {next:p,s:`0x${a.toString(16)} I32.LOAD offset=${of.value}`};
    }
    case 0x6a:return {next:p,s:`0x${a.toString(16)} I32.ADD`};
    case 0x6b:return {next:p,s:`0x${a.toString(16)} I32.SUB`};
    case 0x45:return {next:p,s:`0x${a.toString(16)} I32.EQZ`};
    case 0x46:return {next:p,s:`0x${a.toString(16)} I32.EQ`};
    case 0x47:return {next:p,s:`0x${a.toString(16)} I32.NE`};
    case 0x48:return {next:p,s:`0x${a.toString(16)} I32.LT_S`};
    case 0x4a:return {next:p,s:`0x${a.toString(16)} I32.GT_S`};
    case 0x4c:return {next:p,s:`0x${a.toString(16)} I32.LE_S`};
    case 0x0b:return {next:p,s:`0x${a.toString(16)} END`};
    case 0x0d:{const x=uleb(p);return {next:x.next,s:`0x${a.toString(16)} BR_IF ${x.value}`};}
    case 0x1a:return {next:p,s:`0x${a.toString(16)} DROP`};
    default:return {next:p,s:`0x${a.toString(16)} OP 0x${op.toString(16)}`};
  }
}

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const START = 0x93d80;
const END   = 0x93ec0;

function readULEB(p) {
  let v = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let n = 0;

  while (p < b.length) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
    n++;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return { value: v >>> 0, len: n };

    shift += 7;
    if (shift > 35) return null;
  }

  return null;
}

function hx(v) {
  return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log("FUNC 2057 LOCAL1 DATAFLOW");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log(`RANGE : ${hx(START)} .. ${hx(END)}`);
console.log("FOCUS : LOCAL 1 / STORE DESTINATION");
console.log("-".repeat(72));

let p = START;

while (p < END) {
  const at = p;
  const op = b[p++];

  let extra = "";

  switch (op) {

    case 0x20:
    case 0x21:
    case 0x22: {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      if (!r) break;

      extra = ` local=${r.value}`;
      p += r.len;

      if (r.value === 1) {
        console.log(
          `${hx(at)}  ${b[at].toString(16).padStart(2,"0")}  ` +
          `${op === 0x20 ? "local.get" :
             op === 0x21 ? "local.set" : "local.tee"} 1  <-- FOCUS`
        );
      }

      break;
    }

    case 0x41: {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      if (!r) break;

      extra = ` ${r.value} (${hx(r.value)})`;
      p += r.len;
      break;
    }

    case 0x10: {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      if (!r) break;

      extra = ` call ${r.value}`;
      p += r.len;
      break;
    }

    case 0x28:
    case 0x36: {
      const align = b[p++];

      const r = readULEB(p);
      if (!r) break;

      extra = ` align=${align} offset=${r.value}`;
      p += r.len;

      if (op === 0x36) {
        console.log(
          `${hx(at)}  i32.store${extra}  <-- STORE`
        );
      }

      break;
    }

    case 0x02:
    case 0x03:
    case 0x04: {
      const bt = b[p++];
      extra = ` blocktype=0x${bt.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x0c:
    case 0x0d: {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      if (!r) break;

      extra = ` ${r.value}`;
      p += r.len;
      break;
    }

    default:
      break;
  }

  if (
    op !== 0x20 &&
    op !== 0x21 &&
    op !== 0x22 &&
    op !== 0x36
  ) {
    console.log(
      `${hx(at)}  ${b[at].toString(16).padStart(2,"0")}  opcode${extra}`
    );
  }
}

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const START = 0x93e60;
const END   = 0x93eb0;

function readULEB(p) {
  let v = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let n = 0;

  while (p < b.length) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
    n++;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return { value: v >>> 0, len: n };

    shift += 7;
    if (shift > 35) return null;
  }

  return null;
}

function hex(v, n = 8) {
  return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(n, "0");
}

function opName(op) {
  const m = {
    0x00: "unreachable",
    0x01: "nop",
    0x02: "block",
    0x03: "loop",
    0x04: "if",
    0x05: "else",
    0x0b: "end",
    0x0c: "br",
    0x0d: "br_if",
    0x0e: "br_table",
    0x0f: "return",

    0x10: "call",
    0x11: "call_indirect",

    0x20: "local.get",
    0x21: "local.set",
    0x22: "local.tee",

    0x28: "i32.load",
    0x2d: "i32.load8_u",

    0x36: "i32.store",
    0x3a: "i32.store8",

    0x41: "i32.const",

    0x45: "i32.eqz",
    0x47: "i32.lt_u",

    0x6a: "i32.add",
    0x6b: "i32.sub",
    0x76: "i32.shr_u"
  };

  return m[op] ?? `opcode=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

console.log("FUNC 2057 TARGET / STORE TRACE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log(`RANGE : ${hex(START)} .. ${hex(END)}`);
console.log("TARGET: 0x0019534C");
console.log("-".repeat(72));

let p = START;

while (p < END) {
  const at = p;
  const op = b[p++];

  let extra = "";

  switch (op) {

    case 0x41: {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      if (!r) break;

      extra = ` ${r.value} (${hex(r.value)})`;
      p += r.len;
      break;
    }

    case 0x20:
    case 0x21:
    case 0x22:
    case 0x0c:
    case 0x0d:
    case 0x10: {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      if (!r) break;

      extra = ` ${r.value}`;
      p += r.len;
      break;
    }

    case 0x28:
    case 0x2d:
    case 0x36:
    case 0x3a: {
      const align = b[p++];

      const r = readULEB(p);
      if (!r) break;

      extra = ` align=${align} offset=${r.value}`;
      p += r.len;

      if (op === 0x36 || op === 0x3a) {
        console.log(
          `STORE @ ${hex(at)} ${opName(op)}${extra}`
        );
      }

      break;
    }

    case 0x11: {
      const type = readULEB(p);
      if (!type) break;
      p += type.len;

      const table = readULEB(p);
      if (!table) break;
      p += table.len;

      extra = ` type=${type.value} table=${table.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x02:
    case 0x03:
    case 0x04: {
      const blockType = b[p++];
      extra = ` blocktype=0x${blockType.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
      break;
    }

    default:
      break;
  }

  console.log(
    `${hex(at)}  ${b[at].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}  ${opName(op)}${extra}`
  );
}

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET = 2075;
const IMPORTED = 83;

const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function uleb(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { value: v, next: p };
    s += 7;
  }
}

function sleb(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x;
  do {
    x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;
  } while (x & 0x80);

  if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40))
    v |= (~0 << s);

  return { value: v, next: p };
}

/* --------------------------------------------------------- */
/* FIND SECTIONS                                              */
/* --------------------------------------------------------- */

let p = 8;
let funcSec = null;
let codeSec = null;

while (p < b.length) {

  const id = b[p++];

  const z = uleb(p);
  const size = z.value;
  p = z.next;

  if (id === 3)
    funcSec = { start: p, end: p + size };

  if (id === 10)
    codeSec = { start: p, end: p + size };

  p += size;
}

/* --------------------------------------------------------- */
/* FUNCTION BODY                                              */
/* --------------------------------------------------------- */

const ordinal = TARGET - IMPORTED;

let fp = funcSec.start;
const fc = uleb(fp);
fp = fc.next;

let typeIndex = null;

for (let i = 0; i <= ordinal; i++) {
  const x = uleb(fp);
  fp = x.next;

  if (i === ordinal)
    typeIndex = x.value;
}

let cp = codeSec.start;
const cc = uleb(cp);
cp = cc.next;

let body = null;

for (let i = 0; i < cc.value; i++) {

  const z = uleb(cp);
  const size = z.value;
  cp = z.next;

  const start = cp;
  const end = cp + size;

  if (i === ordinal) {
    body = { start, end };
    break;
  }

  cp = end;
}
