import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const START = 0x00095b55;
const END   = 0x00095b83;

function uleb(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { value:v, next:p };
    s += 7;
  }
}

function sleb(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x;
  do {
    x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;
  } while (x & 0x80);

  if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40))
    v |= (~0 << s);

  return { value:v, next:p };
}

function decode(p) {
  const addr = p;
  const op = b[p++];

  let t = `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")}  `;

  switch(op) {

    case 0x20: {
      const x = uleb(p); p=x.next;
      t += `LOCAL.GET ${x.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x21: {
      const x = uleb(p); p=x.next;
      t += `LOCAL.SET ${x.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x22: {
      const x = uleb(p); p=x.next;
      t += `LOCAL.TEE ${x.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x28: {
      const a=uleb(p); p=a.next;
      const o=uleb(p); p=o.next;
      t += `I32.LOAD align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x36: {
      const a=uleb(p); p=a.next;
      const o=uleb(p); p=o.next;
      t += `I32.STORE align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x41: {
      const x=sleb(p); p=x.next;
      t += `I32.CONST ${x.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x10: {
      const x=uleb(p); p=x.next;
      t += `CALL FUNC ${x.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x1b:
      t += "SELECT";
      break;

    case 0x6a:
      t += "I32.ADD";
      break;

    case 0x0b:
      t += "END";
      break;

    default:
      t += `OPCODE 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")}`;
  }

  return {next:p,text:t};
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 2088 → FUNC106 ARGUMENT TRACE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

let p=START;

while(p<END) {
  const x=decode(p);
  console.log(x.text);
  p=x.next;
}

import fs from "fs";

const wasmPath = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const TARGET_FUNC = 2088;
const TARGET_GLOBAL = 0;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

function hex(v, width = 8) {
    return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function readULEB(buf, pos) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * (2 ** shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }

    throw new Error("ULEB128 overflow");
}

console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log(`FUNC ${TARGET_FUNC} GLOBAL ${TARGET_GLOBAL} TRACE`);
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("=".repeat(72));

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// WASM HEADER
// ------------------------------------------------------------
if (
    wasm[0] !== 0x00 ||
    wasm[1] !== 0x61 ||
    wasm[2] !== 0x73 ||
    wasm[3] !== 0x6d
) {
    throw new Error("Invalid WASM header");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// IMPORTED FUNCTION COUNT
// ------------------------------------------------------------
let pos = 8;
let importedFunctions = 0;

while (pos < wasm.length) {

    const sectionId = wasm[pos++];

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(wasm, pos);
    const sectionSize = sizeInfo.value;
    pos = sizeInfo.next;

    const start = pos;
    const end = start + sectionSize;

    if (sectionId === 2) {

        let p = start;

        const c = readULEB(wasm, p);
        const count = c.value;
        p = c.next;

        for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {

            let r = readULEB(wasm, p);
            p = r.next + r.value;

            r = readULEB(wasm, p);
            p = r.next + r.value;

            const kind = wasm[p++];

            if (kind === 0) {
                importedFunctions++;

                r = readULEB(wasm, p);
                p = r.next;
            }
            else if (kind === 1) {
                p += 1;

                const flags = wasm[p++];

                r = readULEB(wasm, p);
                p = r.next;

                if (flags & 1) {
                    r = readULEB(wasm, p);
                    p = r.next;
                }
            }
            else if (kind === 2) {

                const flags = wasm[p++];

                r = readULEB(wasm, p);
                p = r.next;

                if (flags & 1) {
                    r = readULEB(wasm, p);
                    p = r.next;
                }
            }
            else if (kind === 3) {
                p += 2;
            }
            else if (kind === 4) {
                p++;

                r = readULEB(wasm, p);
                p = r.next;
            }
        }
    }

    pos = end;
}

console.log(`IMPORTED FUNCTION COUNT : ${importedFunctions}`);
console.log();

while (pos < wasm.length) {

    const sectionSize = sizeInfo.value;
    pos = sizeInfo.next;

    const start = pos;
    const end = start + sectionSize;

    if (sectionId === 10) {

        let p = start;

        const c = readULEB(wasm, p);
        const bodyCount = c.value;
        p = c.next;

        for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < bodyCount; ordinal++) {

            const sizeInfo2 = readULEB(wasm, p);
            const bodySize = sizeInfo2.value;

                console.log(`BODY           : ${hex(bodyStart)} .. ${hex(bodyEnd)}`);
                console.log(`BODY SIZE      : ${bodySize}`);
                console.log("-".repeat(72));
                console.log();

                // ------------------------------------------------
                // SCAN GLOBAL.GET / GLOBAL.SET
                // ------------------------------------------------
                console.log("GLOBAL 0 ACCESS");
                console.log("-".repeat(72));

                let q = bodyStart;

                while (q < bodyEnd) {

                    const opcode = wasm[q];

                    // global.get
                    if (opcode === 0x23) {

                        const r = readULEB(wasm, q + 1);

                        if (r.value === TARGET_GLOBAL) {
                            console.log(
                                `${hex(q)}  GLOBAL.GET ${TARGET_GLOBAL}`
                            );
                        }

                        q = r.next;
                        continue;
                    }

                    // global.set
                    if (opcode === 0x24) {

                        const r = readULEB(wasm, q + 1);

                        if (r.value === TARGET_GLOBAL) {
                            console.log(
                                `${hex(q)}  GLOBAL.SET ${TARGET_GLOBAL}`
                            );
                        }

                        q = r.next;
                        continue;
                    }

                    // call
                    if (opcode === 0x10) {

                        const r = readULEB(wasm, q + 1);

                        console.log(
                            `${hex(q)}  CALL FUNC ${r.value}`
                        );

                        q = r.next;
                        continue;
                    }

                    // local.get
                    if (opcode === 0x20) {

                        const r = readULEB(wasm, q + 1);

                        console.log(
                            `${hex(q)}  local.get ${r.value}`
                        );

                        q = r.next;
                        continue;
                    }

                    // local.set
                    if (opcode === 0x21) {

                        const r = readULEB(wasm, q + 1);

                        console.log(
                            `${hex(q)}  local.set ${r.value}`
                        );

                        q = r.next;
                        continue;
                    }

                    // local.tee
                    if (opcode === 0x22) {

                        const r = readULEB(wasm, q + 1);

                        console.log(
                            `${hex(q)}  local.tee ${r.value}`
                        );

                        q = r.next;
                        continue;
                    }

                    q++;
                }

                console.log();
                console.log("=".repeat(72));
                console.log("DONE");
                console.log("=".repeat(72));

                process.exit(0);
            }

            p = bodyEnd;
        }
    }

    pos = end;
}

function readULEB(buf, pos) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * (2 ** shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }

    throw new Error("ULEB128 overflow");
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceFunc2088To106.mjs
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const IMPORTED = 83;
const TARGET_FUNC = 2088;
const TARGET_CALL = 106;

const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function uleb(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    value |= (x & 0x7f) << shift;

    if (!(x & 0x80)) {
      return { value: value >>> 0, next: p };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function findCodeSection() {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < b.length) {
    const id = b[p++];
    const s = uleb(p);
    p = s.next;

    if (id === 10) {
      return {
        start: p,
        end: p + s.value
      };
    }

    p += s.value;
  }

  throw new Error("CODE section not found");
}

function readBlockType(p) {
  const x = b[p];

  if (
    x === 0x40 ||
    x === 0x7f ||
    x === 0x7e ||
    x === 0x7d ||
    x === 0x7c ||
    x === 0x7b
  ) {
    return p + 1;
  }

  return uleb(p).next;
}

function decode(start, end) {
  const ins = [];
  let p = start;

  while (p < end) {
    const addr = p;
    const op = b[p++];

    const x = {
      addr,
      op,
      name: `0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
    };

    switch (op) {

      case 0x02:
        x.name = "BLOCK";
        p = readBlockType(p);
        break;

      case 0x03:
        x.name = "LOOP";
        p = readBlockType(p);
        break;

      case 0x04:
        x.name = "IF";
        p = readBlockType(p);
        break;

      case 0x05:
        x.name = "ELSE";
        break;

      case 0x0b:
        x.name = "END";
        break;

      case 0x0c: {
        const q = uleb(p);
        x.name = "BR";
        x.value = q.value;
        p = q.next;
        break;
      }

      case 0x0d: {
        const q = uleb(p);
        x.name = "BR_IF";
        x.value = q.value;
        p = q.next;
        break;
      }

      case 0x0f:
        x.name = "RETURN";
        break;

      case 0x10: {
        const q = uleb(p);
        x.name = "CALL";
        x.value = q.value;
        p = q.next;
        break;
      }

      case 0x11: {
        const t = uleb(p);
        p = t.next;

        const table = uleb(p);
        p = table.next;

        x.name = "CALL_INDIRECT";
        x.typeidx = t.value;
        x.tableidx = table.value;
        break;
      }

      case 0x20: {
        const q = uleb(p);
        x.name = "LOCAL.GET";
        x.value = q.value;
        p = q.next;
        break;
      }

      case 0x21: {
        const q = uleb(p);
        x.name = "LOCAL.SET";
        x.value = q.value;
        p = q.next;
        break;
      }

      case 0x22: {
        const q = uleb(p);
        x.name = "LOCAL.TEE";
        x.value = q.value;
        p = q.next;
        break;
      }

      case 0x23: {
        const q = uleb(p);
        x.name = "GLOBAL.GET";
        x.value = q.value;
        p = q.next;
        break;
      }

      case 0x24: {
        const q = uleb(p);
        x.name = "GLOBAL.SET";
        x.value = q.value;
        p = q.next;
        break;
      }

      case 0x28:
      case 0x29:
      case 0x2a:
      case 0x2b:
      case 0x2c:
      case 0x2d:
      case 0x2e:
      case 0x2f:
      case 0x30:
      case 0x31:
      case 0x32:
      case 0x33:
      case 0x34:
      case 0x35:
      case 0x36:
      case 0x37:
      case 0x38:
      case 0x39:
      case 0x3a:
      case 0x3b:
      case 0x3c:
      case 0x3d:
      case 0x3e:
      case 0x3f:
      case 0x40: {
        const a = uleb(p);
        p = a.next;

        const o = uleb(p);
        p = o.next;

        x.name = op <= 0x35 ? "LOAD" : "STORE";
        x.align = a.value;
        x.offset = o.value;
        break;
      }

      case 0x41: {
        const q = uleb(p);
        x.name = "I32.CONST";
        x.value = q.value;
        p = q.next;
        break;
      }

      case 0x42: {
        const q = uleb(p);
        x.name = "I64.CONST";
        x.value = q.value;
        p = q.next;
        break;
      }

      default:
        break;
    }

    ins.push(x);
  }

  return ins;
}

function getFunction(index) {
  const code = findCodeSection();

  let p = code.start;
  const count = uleb(p);
  p = count.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const size = uleb(p);
    p = size.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + size.value;

    const funcIndex = IMPORTED + i;

    if (funcIndex === index) {
      return {
        index: funcIndex,
        start,
        end,
        size: size.value,
        ins: decode(start, end)
      };
    }

    p = end;
  }

  throw new Error(`FUNC ${index} not found`);
}

const fn = getFunction(TARGET_FUNC);

  .map((x, i) => ({ x, i }))
  .filter(z => z.x.name === "CALL" && z.x.value === TARGET_CALL);

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm"
);

const TARGET = 2161;

function uleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x = 0;
  do {
    x = wasm[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;
  } while (x & 0x80);
  return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
}

function sleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x = 0;
  do {
    x = wasm[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;
  } while (x & 0x80);

  if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40)) v |= (~0 << s);

  return { value: v | 0, next: p };
}

function section(id: number) {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const sid = wasm[p++];
    const z = uleb(p);
    p = z.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + z.value;

    if (sid === id) return { start, end };

    p = end;
  }

  throw new Error(`Section ${id} not found`);
}

function importedFunctionCount() {
  const s = section(2);
  let p = s.start;

  const c = uleb(p);
  p = c.next;

  let n = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < c.value; i++) {
    const ml = uleb(p);
    p = ml.next + ml.value;

    const nl = uleb(p);
    p = nl.next + nl.value;

    const kind = wasm[p++];

    if (kind === 0) {
      n++;
      p = uleb(p).next;
    } else if (kind === 1) {
      p += 4;
      if (wasm[p - 1] & 1) p = uleb(p).next;
    } else if (kind === 2) {
      const flags = wasm[p + 1];
      p += 2;
      p = uleb(p).next;
      if (flags & 1) p = uleb(p).next;
    } else if (kind === 3) {
      p++;
      p = uleb(p).next;
    }
  }

  return n;
}

function getBody(funcIndex: number) {
  const imported = importedFunctionCount();
  const ordinal = funcIndex - imported;

  const fs = section(3);
  let p = fs.start;

  const fc = uleb(p);
  p = fc.next;

  const types: number[] = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < fc.value; i++) {
    const x = uleb(p);
    p = x.next;
    types.push(x.value);
  }

  const cs = section(10);
  p = cs.start;

  const cc = uleb(p);
  p = cc.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < cc.value; i++) {
    const z = uleb(p);
    p = z.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + z.value;

    if (i === ordinal) {
      return { start, end, type: types[ordinal] };
    }

    p = end;
  }

  throw new Error("FUNC body not found");
}

const body = getBody(TARGET);

const names: Record<number, string> = {
  0x00: "unreachable",
  0x01: "nop",
  0x02: "block",
  0x03: "loop",
  0x04: "if",
  0x05: "else",
  0x0b: "end",
  0x0c: "br",
  0x0d: "br_if",
  0x0f: "return",

  0x10: "CALL",
  0x11: "CALL_INDIRECT",

  0x1a: "drop",
  0x1b: "select",

  0x20: "local.get",
  0x21: "local.set",
  0x22: "local.tee",

  0x23: "global.get",
  0x24: "global.set",

  0x28: "i32.load",
  0x29: "i64.load",
  0x2c: "i32.load8_s",
  0x2d: "i32.load8_u",
  0x36: "i32.store",
  0x3a: "i32.store8",

  0x41: "i32.const",
  0x42: "i64.const",

  0x45: "i32.eqz",
  0x46: "i32.eq",
  0x47: "i32.ne",

  0x6a: "i32.add",
  0x6b: "i32.sub",
  0x6c: "i32.mul",
  0x6f: "i32.rem_s",
  0x70: "i32.rem_u",
  0x71: "i32.and",
  0x72: "i32.or",
  0x73: "i32.xor",
  0x74: "i32.shl",
  0x75: "i32.shr_s",
  0x76: "i32.shr_u"
};

function memarg(pos: number) {
  const a = uleb(pos);
  const o = uleb(a.next);

  return {
    next: o.next,
    align: a.value,
    offset: o.value
  };
}

while (p < body.end) {
  const addr = p;
  const op = wasm[p++];

  if (op === 0x10) {
    const x = uleb(p);
    p = x.next;

    console.log(
      `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  CALL FUNC ${x.value}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x11) {
    const x = uleb(p);
    p = x.next;

    const table = wasm[p++];

    console.log(
      `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  CALL_INDIRECT type=${x.value} table=${table}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  if (
    op === 0x28 ||
    op === 0x29 ||
    op === 0x2c ||
    op === 0x2d ||
    op === 0x36 ||
    op === 0x3a
  ) {
    const m = memarg(p);
    p = m.next;

    console.log(
      `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${
        names[op]
      } align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
    const bt = wasm[p++];

    console.log(
      `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${
        names[op]
      } type=0x${bt.toString(16)}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
    const x = uleb(p);
    p = x.next;

    console.log(
      `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${
        names[op]
      } ${x.value}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
    const x = uleb(p);
    p = x.next;

    console.log(
      `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${
        names[op]
      } ${x.value}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x23 || op === 0x24) {
    const x = uleb(p);
    p = x.next;

    console.log(
      `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${
        names[op]
      } ${x.value}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x41) {
    const x = sleb(p);
    p = x.next;

    console.log(
      `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  i32.const ${x.value} (0x${(
        x.value >>> 0
      )
        .toString(16)
        .padStart(8, "0")})`
    );
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x42) {
    const x = sleb(p);
    p = x.next;

    console.log(
      `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  i64.const ${x.value}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  console.log(
    `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${
      names[op] ?? `opcode 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
    }`
  );
}

import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const TARGET = 2431;
const IMPORTED = 83;
const TARGET_ADDR = 0x000bfc7b;

const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function uleb(pos) {
  let v = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << shift;

    if (!(x & 0x80)) {
      return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function findCodeSection() {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < b.length) {
    const id = b[p++];
    const sz = uleb(p);
    p = sz.next;

    if (id === 10) {
      return {
        start: p,
        end: p + sz.value
      };
    }

    p += sz.value;
  }

  throw new Error("CODE section not found");
}

function skipBlockType(p) {
  const x = b[p];

  if (
    x === 0x40 ||
    x === 0x7f ||
    x === 0x7e ||
    x === 0x7d ||
    x === 0x7c ||
    x === 0x7b
  ) {
    return p + 1;
  }

  return uleb(p).next;
}

function decodeBody(start, end) {
  let p = start;
  const out = [];

  while (p < end) {
    const addr = p;
    const op = b[p++];

    let item = {
      addr,
      op,
      name: `0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
    };

    switch (op) {
      case 0x02:
        p = skipBlockType(p);
        item.name = "BLOCK";
        break;

      case 0x03:
        p = skipBlockType(p);
        item.name = "LOOP";
        break;

      case 0x04:
        p = skipBlockType(p);
        item.name = "IF";
        break;

      case 0x05:
        item.name = "ELSE";
        break;

      case 0x0b:
        item.name = "END";
        break;

      case 0x0c: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;
        item.name = "BR";
        item.value = x.value;
        break;
      }

      case 0x0d: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;
        item.name = "BR_IF";
        item.value = x.value;
        break;
      }

      case 0x0f:
        item.name = "RETURN";
        break;

      case 0x10: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;
        item.name = "CALL";
        item.value = x.value;
        break;
      }

      case 0x11: {
        const t = uleb(p);
        p = t.next;

        const table = uleb(p);
        p = table.next;

        item.name = "CALL_INDIRECT";
        item.typeidx = t.value;
        item.tableidx = table.value;
        break;
      }

      case 0x20: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;
        item.name = "LOCAL.GET";
        item.value = x.value;
        break;
      }

      case 0x21: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;
        item.name = "LOCAL.SET";
        item.value = x.value;
        break;
      }

      case 0x22: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;
        item.name = "LOCAL.TEE";
        item.value = x.value;
        break;
      }

      case 0x23: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;
        item.name = "GLOBAL.GET";
        item.value = x.value;
        break;
      }

      case 0x24: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;
        item.name = "GLOBAL.SET";
        item.value = x.value;
        break;
      }

      case 0x28:
      case 0x29:
      case 0x2a:
      case 0x2b:
      case 0x2c:
      case 0x2d:
      case 0x2e:
      case 0x2f:
      case 0x30:
      case 0x31:
      case 0x32:
      case 0x33:
      case 0x34:
      case 0x35:
      case 0x36:
      case 0x37:
      case 0x38:
      case 0x39:
      case 0x3a:
      case 0x3b:
      case 0x3c:
      case 0x3d:
      case 0x3e:
      case 0x3f:
      case 0x40: {
        const align = uleb(p);
        p = align.next;

        const offset = uleb(p);
        p = offset.next;

        item.name = op <= 0x35 ? "LOAD" : "STORE";
        item.align = align.value;
        item.offset = offset.value;
        break;
      }

      case 0x41: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;
        item.name = "I32.CONST";
        item.value = x.value;
        break;
      }

      case 0x42: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;
        item.name = "I64.CONST";
        item.value = x.value;
        break;
      }

      default:
        break;
    }

    out.push(item);
  }

  return out;
}

const code = findCodeSection();

let p = code.start;
const functionCount = uleb(p);
p = functionCount.next;

let target = null;

for (let i = 0; i < functionCount.value; i++) {
  const bodySize = uleb(p);
  p = bodySize.next;

  const bodyStart = p;
  const bodyEnd = p + bodySize.value;
  const funcIndex = IMPORTED + i;

  if (funcIndex === TARGET) {
    target = {
      funcIndex,
      bodyStart,
      bodyEnd,
      size: bodySize.value
    };
    break;
  }

  p = bodyEnd;
}

  throw new Error(`FUNC ${TARGET} not found`);

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const START = 0x00014308;
const END   = 0x00014420;

function uleb(pos) {
    let v = 0, shift = 0;
    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];
        v += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
        if (!(b & 0x80)) return { value: v, next: pos };
        shift += 7;
    }
}

function hex(v) {
    return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log("FUNC 259 ENTRY / GLOBAL 0 FLOW");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log();

console.log(`RANGE : ${hex(START)} .. ${hex(END)}`);
console.log();

let p = START;

while (p < END) {
    const at = p;
    const op = wasm[p++];

    switch (op) {

        case 0x23: {
            const g = uleb(p);
            p = g.next;
            console.log(`${hex(at)} GLOBAL.GET ${g.value}`);
            break;
        }

        case 0x24: {
            const g = uleb(p);
            p = g.next;
            console.log(`${hex(at)} GLOBAL.SET ${g.value}`);
            break;
        }

        case 0x10: {
            const f = uleb(p);
            p = f.next;
            console.log(`${hex(at)} CALL FUNC ${f.value}`);
            break;
        }

        case 0x20: {
            const l = uleb(p);
            p = l.next;
            console.log(`${hex(at)} LOCAL.GET ${l.value}`);
            break;
        }

        case 0x21: {
            const l = uleb(p);
            p = l.next;
            console.log(`${hex(at)} LOCAL.SET ${l.value}`);
            break;
        }

        case 0x22: {
            const l = uleb(p);
            p = l.next;
            console.log(`${hex(at)} LOCAL.TEE ${l.value}`);
            break;
        }

        case 0x41: {
            const x = uleb(p);
            p = x.next;
            console.log(`${hex(at)} I32.CONST ${x.value} (${hex(x.value)})`);
            break;
        }

        case 0x45:
            console.log(`${hex(at)} I32.EQZ`);
            break;

        case 0x47:
            console.log(`${hex(at)} I32.NE`);
            break;

        case 0x4d:
            console.log(`${hex(at)} I32.LT_U`);
            break;

        case 0x6a:
            console.log(`${hex(at)} I32.ADD`);
            break;

        case 0x6b:
            console.log(`${hex(at)} I32.SUB`);
            break;

        case 0x71:
            console.log(`${hex(at)} I32.AND`);
            break;

        case 0x72:
            console.log(`${hex(at)} I32.SHR_U`);
            break;

        case 0x28: {
            const a = uleb(p);
            p = a.next;
            const o = uleb(p);
            p = o.next;
            console.log(
                `${hex(at)} I32.LOAD align=${a.value} offset=${hex(o.value)}`
            );
            break;
        }

        case 0x2d: {
            const a = uleb(p);
            p = a.next;
            const o = uleb(p);
            p = o.next;
            console.log(
                `${hex(at)} I32.LOAD8_U align=${a.value} offset=${hex(o.value)}`
            );
            break;
        }

        case 0x0c: {
            const d = uleb(p);
            p = d.next;
            console.log(`${hex(at)} BR depth=${d.value}`);
            break;
        }

        case 0x0d: {
            const d = uleb(p);
            p = d.next;
            console.log(`${hex(at)} BR_IF depth=${d.value}`);
            break;
        }

        case 0x02:
            console.log(`${hex(at)} BLOCK`);
            break;

        case 0x03:
            console.log(`${hex(at)} LOOP`);
            break;

        case 0x04:
            console.log(`${hex(at)} IF`);
            break;

        case 0x0b:
            console.log(`${hex(at)} END`);
            break;

        case 0x0f:
            console.log(`${hex(at)} RETURN`);
            break;

        case 0x1a:
            console.log(`${hex(at)} DROP`);
            break;

        case 0x36: {
            const a = uleb(p);
            p = a.next;
            const o = uleb(p);
            p = o.next;
            console.log(
                `${hex(at)} I32.STORE align=${a.value} offset=${hex(o.value)}`
            );
            break;
        }

        default:
            console.log(`${hex(at)} OPCODE 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")}`);
            break;
    }

import fs from "fs";

const file =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

const TARGET = 259;

function readULEB(p: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };

    s += 7;
  }
}

function readSLEB(p: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;

    if (!(x & 0x80)) {
      if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40))
        v |= (~0 << (s + 7));

      return { value: v | 0, next: p };
    }

    s += 7;
  }
}

function hex(x: number) {
  return "0x" + (x >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

// Find CODE section
let p = 8;
let codeStart = -1;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];

  const sz = readULEB(p);
  p = sz.next;

  const end = p + sz.value;

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = p;
    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

if (codeStart < 0)
  throw new Error("CODE section not found");

let q = codeStart;

const count = readULEB(q);
q = count.next;

const importedCount = 83;

let bodyStart = -1;
let bodyEnd = -1;

for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {

  const sz = readULEB(q);
  q = sz.next;

  const fn = importedCount + i;

  if (fn === TARGET) {
    bodyStart = q;
    bodyEnd = q + sz.value;
    break;
  }

  q += sz.value;
}

if (bodyStart < 0)
  throw new Error("FUNC259 not found");

console.log("FUNC 259 RETURN / STATE TRACE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`BODY : ${hex(bodyStart)} .. ${hex(bodyEnd)}`);
console.log(`SIZE : ${bodyEnd - bodyStart}`);
console.log("FOCUS : ARG0 / MEM[0x250FB8] / CALLS / RETURN");
console.log("============================================================");

let x = bodyStart;

while (x < bodyEnd) {

  const addr = x;
  const op = b[x++];

  let s = "";

  switch (op) {

    case 0x00:
      s = "unreachable";
      break;

    case 0x01:
      s = "nop";
      break;

    case 0x02:
      s = "block";
      x++;
      break;

    case 0x03:
      s = "loop";
      x++;
      break;

    case 0x04:
      s = "if";
      x++;
      break;

    case 0x05:
      s = "else";
      break;

    case 0x0b:
      s = "end";
      break;

    case 0x0c: {
      const r = readULEB(x);
      x = r.next;
      s = `br ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x0d: {
      const r = readULEB(x);
      x = r.next;
      s = `br_if ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x0f:
      s = "return";
      break;

    case 0x10: {
      const r = readULEB(x);
      x = r.next;
      s = `CALL FUNC ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x11: {
      const t = readULEB(x);
      x = t.next;

      const tab = readULEB(x);
      x = tab.next;

      s = `CALL_INDIRECT type=${t.value} table=${tab.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x1a:
      s = "drop";
      break;

    case 0x20: {
      const r = readULEB(x);
      x = r.next;

      s = `local.get ${r.value}`;

      if (r.value === 0)
        s += "  <-- ARG0";

      break;
    }

    case 0x21: {
      const r = readULEB(x);
      x = r.next;
      s = `local.set ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x22: {
      const r = readULEB(x);
      x = r.next;
      s = `local.tee ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x23: {
      const r = readULEB(x);
      x = r.next;
      s = `global.get ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x24: {
      const r = readULEB(x);
      x = r.next;
      s = `global.set ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x28: {
      const al = readULEB(x);
      x = al.next;

      const off = readULEB(x);
      x = off.next;

      s = `i32.load align=${al.value} offset=${off.value}`;

      break;
    }

    case 0x2d: {
      const al = readULEB(x);
      x = al.next;

      const off = readULEB(x);
      x = off.next;

      s = `i32.load8_u align=${al.value} offset=${off.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x36: {
      const al = readULEB(x);
      x = al.next;

      const off = readULEB(x);
      x = off.next;

      s = `i32.store align=${al.value} offset=${off.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x3a: {
      const al = readULEB(x);
      x = al.next;

      const off = readULEB(x);
      x = off.next;

      s = `i32.store8 align=${al.value} offset=${off.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x41: {
      const r = readSLEB(x);
      x = r.next;

      s = `i32.const ${r.value} (${hex(r.value)})`;

      if ((r.value >>> 0) === 0x250fb8)
        s += "  <-- MEM[0x250FB8]";

      if ((r.value >>> 0) === 0x25100c)
        s += "  <-- MEM[0x25100C]";

      break;
    }

    case 0x42: {
      const r = readSLEB(x);
      x = r.next;
      s = `i64.const ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x45:
      s = "i32.eqz";
      break;

    case 0x46:
      s = "i32.eq";
      break;

    case 0x47:
      s = "i32.ne";
      break;

    case 0x48:
      s = "i32.lt_s";
      break;

    case 0x49:
      s = "i32.lt_u";
      break;

    case 0x4a:
      s = "i32.gt_s";
      break;

    case 0x4b:
      s = "i32.gt_u";
      break;

    case 0x4c:
      s = "i32.le_s";
      break;

    case 0x4d:
      s = "i32.le_u";
      break;

    case 0x4e:
      s = "i32.ge_s";
      break;

    case 0x4f:
      s = "i32.ge_u";
      break;

    case 0x6a:
      s = "i32.add";
      break;

    case 0x6b:
      s = "i32.sub";
      break;

    case 0x70:
      s = "i32.rotl";
      break;

    case 0x71:
      s = "i32.and";
      break;

    case 0x72:
      s = "i32.or";
      break;

    case 0x73:
      s = "i32.xor";
      break;

    case 0x74: {
      const r = readULEB(x);
      x = r.next;
      s = `i32.shl ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x75: {
      const r = readULEB(x);
      x = r.next;
      s = `i32.shr_s ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x76: {
      const r = readULEB(x);
      x = r.next;
      s = `i32.shr_u ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x77: {
      const r = readULEB(x);
      x = r.next;
      s = `i32.rotl ${r.value}`;

      x = r.next;

    default:
      s = `opcode 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;

FILE: scripts\traceFunc265Return.ts

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function readULEB(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    value += (x & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

    if ((x & 0x80) === 0) break;

    shift += 7;
  }

  return { value, next: p };
}

function readSLEB(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    value |= (x & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((x & 0x80) === 0) {
      if (shift < 32 && (x & 0x40)) {
        value |= (~0 << shift);
      }
      break;
    }
  }

  return { value, next: p };
}

function readSections() {
  let p = 8;
  const sections: any[] = [];

  while (p < b.length) {
    const id = b[p++];

    const r = readULEB(p);
    p = r.next;

    sections.push({
      id,
      start: p,
      end: p + r.value
    });

    p += r.value;
  }

  return sections;
}

const sections = readSections();

const funcSection = sections.find(x => x.id === 3);
const codeSection = sections.find(x => x.id === 10);

if (!funcSection || !codeSection) {
  throw new Error("Sections not found");
}

const importedFunctions = 83;
const targetFunc = 265;
const ordinal = targetFunc - importedFunctions;

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// FUNCTION TYPES
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let p = funcSection.start;

const fc = readULEB(p);
p = fc.next;

const types: number[] = [];

for (let i = 0; i < fc.value; i++) {
  const r = readULEB(p);
  types.push(r.value);
  p = r.next;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// FIND BODY
// ------------------------------------------------------------

p = codeSection.start;

const bc = readULEB(p);
p = bc.next;

let start = -1;
let end = -1;

for (let i = 0; i < bc.value; i++) {
  const r = readULEB(p);
  const size = r.value;
  p = r.next;

  const bodyStart = p;
  const bodyEnd = p + size;

  if (i === ordinal) {
    start = bodyStart;
    end = bodyEnd;
    break;
  }

  p = bodyEnd;
}

if (start < 0) {
  throw new Error("FUNC265 not found");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC265 RETURN TRACE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(`BODY 0x${start.toString(16)} .. 0x${(end - 1).toString(16)}`);
console.log(`SIZE = ${end - start}`);
console.log("");

console.log("RETURN / BLOCK-END CANDIDATES");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (let q = start; q < end; q++) {

  const op = b[q];

  if (op === 0x0f) {
    console.log(
      `RETURN @ 0x${q.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
    );

    const from = Math.max(start, q - 20);
    const to = Math.min(end, q + 8);

    for (let x = from; x < to; x++) {
      console.log(
        `  0x${x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${b[x]
          .toString(16)
          .padStart(2, "0")}`
      );
    }

    console.log("");
  }

  if (op === 0x0b) {
    if (q >= end - 12) {
      console.log(
        `FINAL END @ 0x${q.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
      );

      const from = Math.max(start, q - 30);

      for (let x = from; x < end; x++) {
        console.log(
          `  0x${x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${b[x]
            .toString(16)
            .padStart(2, "0")}`
        );
      }
    }
  }
}

console.log("");

console.log("NO WASM EXECUTION");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET_START = 0x195340;
const TARGET_END   = 0x195364;

function readULEB(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];
        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            return { value, next: pos };

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function hex(v: number) {
    return "0x" + (v >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function signed(v: number) {
    return v >>> 0;
}

const TARGET_ORDINAL = 3250;

let p = 8;
let codeStart = -1;
let codeEnd = -1;

while (p < wasm.length) {
    const id = wasm[p++];

    const s = readULEB(p);
    p = s.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + s.value;

    if (id === 10) {
        codeStart = start;
        codeEnd = end;
        break;
    }

    p = end;
}

if (codeStart < 0)
    throw new Error("CODE section not found");

let q = codeStart;

const fc = readULEB(q);
const functionCount = fc.value;
q = fc.next;

let bodyStart = -1;
let bodyEnd = -1;

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < functionCount; ordinal++) {

    const bs = readULEB(q);
    q = bs.next;

    const start = q;
    const end = q + bs.value;

    if (ordinal === TARGET_ORDINAL) {
        bodyStart = start;
        bodyEnd = end;
        break;
    }

    q = end;
}

if (bodyStart < 0)
    throw new Error("Target function not found");

console.log("FUNC ORDINAL =", TARGET_ORDINAL);
console.log("BODY         =", hex(bodyStart), "..", hex(bodyEnd));
console.log("SIZE         =", bodyEnd - bodyStart);
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

let x = bodyStart;

const localCountInfo = readULEB(x);
x = localCountInfo.next;

console.log("LOCAL GROUP COUNT =", localCountInfo.value);

for (let i = 0; i < localCountInfo.value; i++) {
    const countInfo = readULEB(x);
    x = countInfo.next;

    const type = wasm[x++];

    console.log(
        `LOCAL GROUP ${i}: COUNT=${countInfo.value} TYPE=0x${type.toString(16)}`
    );
}

console.log("INSTRUCTIONS");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

/*
 * Lightweight symbolic stack.
 * Important: calls are represented conservatively so that
 * suspicious addresses are not falsely resolved.
 */

type Sym =
    | { kind: "const", value: number }
    | { kind: "global0" }
    | { kind: "local", index: number }
    | { kind: "add", a: Sym, b: Sym }
    | { kind: "sub", a: Sym, b: Sym }
    | { kind: "load", addr: Sym, offset: number }
    | { kind: "unknown", text: string };

function show(s: Sym): string {
    switch (s.kind) {
        case "const":
            return `CONST(${hex(s.value)})`;

        case "global0":
            return "GLOBAL0";

        case "local":
            return `LOCAL${s.index}`;

        case "add":
            return `(${show(s.a)} + ${show(s.b)})`;

        case "sub":
            return `(${show(s.a)} - ${show(s.b)})`;

        case "load":
            return `LOAD(${show(s.addr)}+${hex(s.offset)})`;

        case "unknown":
            return s.text;
    }
}

function knownValue(s: Sym): number | null {

    if (s.kind === "const")
        return s.value >>> 0;

    if (
        s.kind === "add" &&
        s.a.kind === "const" &&
        s.b.kind === "const"
    )
        return (s.a.value + s.b.value) >>> 0;

    if (
        s.kind === "sub" &&
        s.a.kind === "const" &&
        s.b.kind === "const"
    )
        return (s.a.value - s.b.value) >>> 0;

    return null;
}

const stack: Sym[] = [];
const locals = new Map<number, Sym>();

function pop(): Sym {
    return stack.length
        ? stack.pop()!
        : { kind: "unknown", text: "STACK_UNDERFLOW" };
}

function push(s: Sym) {
    stack.push(s);
}

function checkAddress(
    address: Sym,
    offset: number,
    at: number,
    type: string,
    value: Sym
) {
    const base = knownValue(address);

    const effective =
        base === null
            ? null
            : ((base + offset) >>> 0);

    const expr =
        effective !== null
            ? hex(effective)
            : `${show(address)} + ${hex(offset)}`;

    console.log("");
    console.log(`STORE @ ${hex(at)}`);
    console.log(`TYPE    = ${type}`);
    console.log(`ADDRESS = ${show(address)}`);
    console.log(`OFFSET  = ${hex(offset)}`);
    console.log(`VALUE   = ${show(value)}`);
    console.log(`EFFECTIVE = ${expr}`);

    if (
        effective !== null &&
        effective >= TARGET_START &&
        effective <= TARGET_END
    ) {
        console.log("*** TARGET RANGE HIT ***");
    }

    if (
        show(address).includes("GLOBAL0")
    ) {
        console.log("*** GLOBAL0-DERIVED ADDRESS ***");
    }
}

while (x < bodyEnd) {

    const at = x;
    const op = wasm[x++];

    if (op === 0x0b) {
        console.log(hex(at), "end");
        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x20) {
        const r = readULEB(x);
        x = r.next;

        const s =
            locals.get(r.value) ??
            { kind: "local", index: r.value } as Sym;

        push(s);

        console.log(hex(at), `local.get ${r.value} => ${show(s)}`);
        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {

        const r = readULEB(x);
        x = r.next;

        const value = pop();

        locals.set(r.value, value);

        console.log(
            hex(at),
            op === 0x21
                ? `local.set ${r.value} <= ${show(value)}`
                : `local.tee ${r.value} <= ${show(value)}`
        );

        if (op === 0x22)
            push(value);

        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x23) {

        const r = readULEB(x);
        x = r.next;

        if (r.value === 0) {
            const s: Sym = { kind: "global0" };
            push(s);

            console.log(
                hex(at),
                "global.get 0 => GLOBAL0"
            );
        } else {
            push({
                kind: "unknown",
                text: `GLOBAL${r.value}`
            });
        }

        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x24) {

        const r = readULEB(x);
        x = r.next;

        const value = pop();

        console.log(
            hex(at),
            `global.set ${r.value} <= ${show(value)}`
        );

        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x41) {

        const r = readULEB(x);
        x = r.next;

        const value = r.value >>> 0;

        const s: Sym = {
            kind: "const",
            value
        };

        push(s);

        console.log(
            hex(at),
            `i32.const ${value} (${hex(value)})`
        );

        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x6a || op === 0x6b) {

        const b = pop();
        const a = pop();

        const s: Sym =
            op === 0x6a
                ? { kind: "add", a, b }
                : { kind: "sub", a, b };

        push(s);

        console.log(
            hex(at),
            op === 0x6a
                ? `i32.add ${show(a)}, ${show(b)} => ${show(s)}`
                : `i32.sub ${show(a)}, ${show(b)} => ${show(s)}`
        );

        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x28 || op === 0x29) {

        const align = readULEB(x);
        x = align.next;

        const off = readULEB(x);
        x = off.next;

        const addr = pop();

        const s: Sym = {
            kind: "load",
            addr,
            offset: off.value
        };

        push(s);

        console.log(
            hex(at),
            op === 0x28
                ? `i32.load ADDR=${show(addr)} OFFSET=${hex(off.value)}`
                : `i64.load ADDR=${show(addr)} OFFSET=${hex(off.value)}`
        );

        continue;
    }

    if (
        op === 0x36 ||
        op === 0x37
    ) {

        const align = readULEB(x);
        x = align.next;

        const off = readULEB(x);
        x = off.next;

        const value = pop();
        const address = pop();

        checkAddress(
            address,
            off.value,
            at,
            op === 0x36
                ? "i32.store"
                : "i64.store",
            value
        );

        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x10) {

        const r = readULEB(x);
        x = r.next;

        console.log(
            hex(at),
            `call ${r.value}`
        );

        /*
         * We deliberately do NOT pretend the call result
         * has a known value. This avoids false address
         * resolution.
         */
        push({
            kind: "unknown",
            text: `CALL${r.value}_RESULT`
        });

        continue;
    }

    /*
     * Handle common simple instructions without trying
     * to infer their full semantics.
     */
    console.log(
        hex(at),
        `op_0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
    );
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("READ ONLY - NO EXECUTION - NO TRANSACTION");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceFunc3315Local8.mjs

import fs from "fs";

const wasmPath = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const FUNC = 3315;
const TARGET_LOCAL = 8;
const TARGET_CALL = 0x001078a9;

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// READ WASM
// ------------------------------------------------------------
const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// ULEB128
// ------------------------------------------------------------
function readULEB(buf, pos) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * (2 ** shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }

    throw new Error("ULEB128 overflow");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// LOCATE CODE SECTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------
let pos = 8;
let codeStart = -1;
let codeEnd = -1;

while (pos < wasm.length) {
    const sectionId = wasm[pos++];

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(wasm, pos);
    pos = sizeInfo.next;

    const start = pos;
    const end = start + sizeInfo.value;

    if (sectionId === 10) {
        codeStart = start;
        codeEnd = end;
        break;
    }

    pos = end;
}

if (codeStart < 0) {
    throw new Error("CODE section not found");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// LOCATE FUNC 3315
// ------------------------------------------------------------
let p = codeStart;

const countInfo = readULEB(wasm, p);
const functionCount = countInfo.value;
p = countInfo.next;

let bodyStart = -1;
let bodyEnd = -1;

for (let i = 0; i < functionCount; i++) {
    const sizeInfo = readULEB(wasm, p);
    const size = sizeInfo.value;
    p = sizeInfo.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = start + size;

    if (i === FUNC) {
        bodyStart = start;
        bodyEnd = end;
        break;
    }

    p = end;
}

if (bodyStart < 0) {
    throw new Error(`FUNC ${FUNC} not found`);
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// HELPERS
// ------------------------------------------------------------
function hex(v, width = 8) {
    return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function dumpRange(start, end, title) {
    console.log();
    console.log("=".repeat(72));
    console.log(title);
    console.log("=".repeat(72));

    for (let addr = start; addr < end; addr += 16) {
        const e = Math.min(addr + 16, end);
        const bytes = [];

        for (let i = addr; i < e; i++) {
            bytes.push(wasm[i].toString(16).padStart(2, "0"));
        }

        console.log(
            `${hex(addr)}  ${bytes.join(" ")}`
        );
    }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// FIND ALL LOCAL.SET 8
//
// local.set opcode = 0x21
// followed by local index ULEB
// ------------------------------------------------------------
const local8Sets = [];

for (let addr = bodyStart; addr < bodyEnd - 1; addr++) {

    if (wasm[addr] !== 0x21) {
        continue;
    }

    const info = readULEB(wasm, addr + 1);

    if (info.value === TARGET_LOCAL) {
        local8Sets.push({
            addr,
            next: info.next
        });
    }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// HEADER
// ------------------------------------------------------------
console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log(`FUNC ${FUNC} LOCAL 8 DATAFLOW`);
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("=".repeat(72));

console.log(`BODY   : ${hex(bodyStart)} .. ${hex(bodyEnd)}`);
console.log(`TARGET : ${hex(TARGET_CALL)}`);
console.log(`LOCAL  : ${TARGET_LOCAL}`);

console.log();
console.log(`LOCAL 8 SET COUNT : ${local8Sets.length}`);

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// EACH LOCAL 8 SET
// ------------------------------------------------------------
for (let n = 0; n < local8Sets.length; n++) {

    const item = local8Sets[n];
    const addr = item.addr;

    console.log();
    console.log("#".repeat(72));
    console.log(`LOCAL 8 SET #${n + 1}`);
    console.log("#".repeat(72));

    console.log(`SET ADDRESS : ${hex(addr)}`);

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // Show preceding 48 bytes
    // --------------------------------------------------------
    const start = Math.max(bodyStart, addr - 48);

    dumpRange(
        start,
        Math.min(bodyEnd, addr + 16),
        "RAW CONTEXT"
    );

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // Search backward for SELECT (0x1b)
    // --------------------------------------------------------
    let selectAddr = -1;

    for (let q = addr - 1; q >= start; q--) {
        if (wasm[q] === 0x1b) {
            selectAddr = q;
            break;
        }
    }

    if (selectAddr >= 0) {

        console.log();
        console.log("SELECT FOUND");
        console.log("-".repeat(72));
        console.log(`SELECT : ${hex(selectAddr)}`);

        dumpRange(
            Math.max(bodyStart, selectAddr - 24),
            Math.min(bodyEnd, selectAddr + 8),
            "SELECT CONTEXT"
        );

    } else {

        console.log();
        console.log("SELECT FOUND : NO");
    }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// SPECIFIC PATH TO CALL_INDIRECT
// ------------------------------------------------------------
console.log();
console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log("PATH IMMEDIATELY BEFORE CALL_INDIRECT");
console.log("=".repeat(72));

dumpRange(
    Math.max(bodyStart, TARGET_CALL - 80),
    Math.min(bodyEnd, TARGET_CALL + 16),
    "CALL PATH"
);

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// ALL local.get 8
//
// local.get opcode = 0x20
// ------------------------------------------------------------
console.log();
console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log("ALL LOCAL.GET 8 IN FUNC 3315");
console.log("=".repeat(72));

let get8Count = 0;

for (let addr = bodyStart; addr < bodyEnd - 1; addr++) {

    if (wasm[addr] !== 0x20) {
        continue;
    }

    const info = readULEB(wasm, addr + 1);

    if (info.value === TARGET_LOCAL) {
        console.log(`0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`);
        get8Count++;
    }
}

console.log(`COUNT : ${get8Count}`);

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// SEARCH FOR CONSTANTS NEAR SELECT
// ------------------------------------------------------------
console.log();
console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log("CONSTANT BYTES NEAR LOCAL 8 SELECT");
console.log("=".repeat(72));

for (const item of local8Sets) {

    const start = Math.max(bodyStart, item.addr - 20);
    const end = Math.min(bodyEnd, item.addr + 1);

    console.log();
    console.log(`LOCAL8 SET @ ${hex(item.addr)}`);

    for (let addr = start; addr < end; addr++) {

        if (wasm[addr] === 0x41) {

            try {
                const c = readULEB(wasm, addr + 1);

                console.log(
                    `${hex(addr)}  i32.const ${c.value} ` +
                    `(0x${c.value.toString(16)})`
                );

            } catch {
                // ignore
            }
        }
    }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// FINAL
// ------------------------------------------------------------
console.log();
console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log("DONE");
console.log("=".repeat(72));

FILE: scripts\traceFunc3315Raw.mjs

import fs from "fs";

const wasmPath = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const FUNC = 3315;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// READ WASM ULEB128
// ------------------------------------------------------------
function readULEB(buf, pos) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const b = buf[p++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return {
                value: value >>> 0,
                next: p
            };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }

    throw new Error("ULEB128 overflow");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// PARSE CODE SECTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------
let pos = 8;

if (
    wasm[0] !== 0x00 ||
    wasm[1] !== 0x61 ||
    wasm[2] !== 0x73 ||
    wasm[3] !== 0x6d
) {
    throw new Error("Not a valid WASM file");
}

let codeSectionStart = -1;
let codeSectionEnd = -1;

while (pos < wasm.length) {
    const sectionId = wasm[pos++];

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(wasm, pos);
    const sectionSize = sizeInfo.value;
    pos = sizeInfo.next;

    const sectionStart = pos;
    const sectionEnd = sectionStart + sectionSize;

    if (sectionId === 10) {
        codeSectionStart = sectionStart;
        codeSectionEnd = sectionEnd;
        break;
    }

    pos = sectionEnd;
}

if (codeSectionStart < 0) {
    throw new Error("CODE section not found");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// CODE VECTOR
// ------------------------------------------------------------
let p = codeSectionStart;

const funcCountInfo = readULEB(wasm, p);
const funcCount = funcCountInfo.value;
p = funcCountInfo.next;

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// LOCATE FUNCTION BODY
// ------------------------------------------------------------
let bodyStart = -1;
let bodyEnd = -1;
let targetBodySize = -1;

for (let i = 0; i < funcCount; i++) {
    const sizeInfo = readULEB(wasm, p);
    const bodySize = sizeInfo.value;
    p = sizeInfo.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + bodySize;

    if (i === FUNC) {
        bodyStart = start;
        bodyEnd = end;
        targetBodySize = bodySize;
        break;
    }

    p = end;
}

if (bodyStart < 0) {
    throw new Error(`FUNC ${FUNC} not found`);
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// OUTPUT
// ------------------------------------------------------------
console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log(`FUNC ${FUNC} RAW BODY`);
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("=".repeat(72));

console.log(`CODE SECTION : 0x${codeSectionStart.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} .. 0x${codeSectionEnd.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`);
console.log(`BODY         : 0x${bodyStart.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} .. 0x${bodyEnd.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`);
console.log(`SIZE         : ${targetBodySize}`);
console.log("-".repeat(72));

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// RAW HEX DUMP
// ------------------------------------------------------------
for (let addr = bodyStart; addr < bodyEnd; addr += 16) {
    const end = Math.min(addr + 16, bodyEnd);

    const bytes = [];

    for (let j = addr; j < end; j++) {
        bytes.push(
            wasm[j].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
        );
    }

    console.log(
        `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${bytes.join(" ")}`
    );
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// OPCODE-BY-OPCODE RAW SCAN
// NOTE:
// This is intentionally a raw byte scan.
// No execution and no interpretation of immediates.
// ------------------------------------------------------------
console.log();
console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log(`FUNC ${FUNC} RAW BYTE OFFSETS`);
console.log("=".repeat(72));

for (let addr = bodyStart; addr < bodyEnd; addr++) {
    console.log(
        `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ` +
        `0x${wasm[addr].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
    );
}

console.log();
console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log("DONE");
console.log("=".repeat(72));

FILE: scripts\traceFunc445Callers.mjs

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET = 445;
const IMPORTED = 83;

const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function uleb(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { value: v, next: p };
    s += 7;
  }
}

function readSections() {
  let p = 8;
  const out = {};

  while (p < b.length) {
    const id = b[p++];

    const z = uleb(p);
    const size = z.value;
    p = z.next;

    out[id] = {
      start: p,
      end: p + size
    };

    p += size;
  }

  return out;
}

function getFunctionBodies() {
  const sec = readSections();

  const funcSec = sec[3];
  const codeSec = sec[10];

  let fp = funcSec.start;
  const fc = uleb(fp);
  fp = fc.next;

  const types = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < fc.value; i++) {
    const x = uleb(fp);
    fp = x.next;
    types.push(x.value);
  }

  let cp = codeSec.start;
  const cc = uleb(cp);
  cp = cc.next;

  const bodies = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < cc.value; i++) {
    const z = uleb(cp);
    const size = z.value;
    cp = z.next;

    bodies.push({
      func: IMPORTED + i,
      type: types[i],
      start: cp,
      end: cp + size
    });

    cp += size;
  }

  return bodies;
}

const bodies = getFunctionBodies();

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 445 CALLER + ARGUMENT TRACE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

const target = bodies.find(x => x.func === TARGET);

if (!target) {
  console.log("FUNC445 NOT FOUND");
  process.exit(1);
}

console.log(
  `FUNC445 BODY : 0x${target.start.toString(16)} .. 0x${target.end.toString(16)}`
);
console.log(`SIZE         : ${target.end - target.start}`);

console.log("");
console.log("CALLERS OF FUNC445");
console.log("============================================================");

let callerCount = 0;

for (const f of bodies) {

  if (f.func === TARGET)
    continue;

  for (let p = f.start; p < f.end - 1; p++) {

    if (b[p] !== 0x10)
      continue;

    const x = uleb(p + 1);

    if (x.value !== TARGET)
      continue;

    callerCount++;

    console.log("");
    console.log(`############################################################`);
    console.log(`CALLER FUNC : ${f.func}`);
    console.log(
      `CALL SITE   : 0x${p.toString(16)}`
    );
    console.log(
      `BODY        : 0x${f.start.toString(16)} .. 0x${f.end.toString(16)}`
    );

    const from = Math.max(f.start, p - 80);
    const to = Math.min(f.end, p + 20);

    console.log("");
    console.log("RAW CONTEXT");
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    for (let q = from; q < to; q += 16) {

      const end = Math.min(q + 16, to);

      const hex = [];

      for (let r = q; r < end; r++)
        hex.push(b[r].toString(16).padStart(2, "0"));

      console.log(
        `0x${q.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${hex.join(" ")}`
      );
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("IMPORTANT");
    console.log(
      "Inspect stack values immediately before CALL445."
    );
    console.log(
      "We need to identify the origin of local0/local1/local2."
    );
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TOTAL CALLERS : ${callerCount}`);
console.log("============================================================");

if (callerCount === 0) {
  console.log("No direct CALL FUNC445 found.");
}

console.log("");
console.log("NEXT TARGET");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log(
  "Resolve the caller that feeds the object/reference into FUNC445."
);
console.log(
  "Then trace that object to the four selector fields:"
);
console.log("  +4   selector #4");
console.log("  +8   selector #2");
console.log("  +12  selector #1");
console.log("  +16  selector #3");

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

FILE: scripts\traceFunc457Stores.ts

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const START = 0x0001ddd6;
const END   = 0x0001dea0;

function uleb(buf: Buffer, p: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[p++];
        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function hex(n: number) {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function opname(op: number) {
    const m: Record<number, string> = {
        0x20: "local.get",
        0x21: "local.set",
        0x22: "local.tee",
        0x23: "global.get",
        0x24: "global.set",

        0x28: "i32.load",
        0x29: "i64.load",

        0x36: "i32.store",
        0x37: "i64.store",
        0x3a: "i32.store8",
        0x3b: "i32.store16",
        0x3e: "i64.store32",

        0x41: "i32.const",
        0x42: "i64.const",

        0x6a: "i32.add",
        0x6b: "i32.sub",
        0x6c: "i32.mul",
        0x71: "i32.and",
        0x72: "i32.or",
        0x73: "i32.xor",

        0x45: "i32.eqz",
        0x46: "i32.eq",
        0x47: "i32.ne",

        0x10: "call",
        0x11: "call_indirect",

        0x02: "block",
        0x03: "loop",
        0x04: "if",
        0x05: "else",
        0x0b: "end",
        0x0c: "br",
        0x0d: "br_if",
        0x0f: "return"
    };

    return m[op] ?? `op_${hex(op)}`;
}

console.log("TRACE FUNC 457 STORE ADDRESS EXPRESSIONS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("BODY =", hex(START), "..", hex(END));
console.log("");

let p = START;

/*
 * Skip local declarations.
 */
const localGroups = uleb(wasm, p);
p = localGroups.next;

console.log("LOCAL GROUPS =", localGroups.value);

for (let i = 0; i < localGroups.value; i++) {
    const count = uleb(wasm, p);
    p = count.next;
    p++; // valtype
}

console.log("");

/*
 * Symbolic stack.
 *
 * We deliberately preserve unknown values rather than
 * pretending that a CALL result is a simple stack item.
 */
const stack: string[] = [];
const locals = new Map<number, string>();

function pop(): string {
    return stack.length ? stack.pop()! : "UNKNOWN";
}

function push(v: string) {
    stack.push(v);
}

function localValue(n: number) {
    return locals.get(n) ?? `LOCAL${n}`;
}

let storeCount = 0;

while (p < END) {

    const at = p;
    const op = wasm[p++];

    if (op === 0x20) {

        const r = uleb(wasm, p);
        p = r.next;

        const v = localValue(r.value);

        push(v);

        console.log(
            `${hex(at)}  local.get ${r.value} => ${v}`
        );
    }

    else if (op === 0x21) {

        const r = uleb(wasm, p);
        p = r.next;

        const v = pop();

        locals.set(r.value, v);

        console.log(
            `${hex(at)}  local.set ${r.value} <= ${v}`
        );
    }

    else if (op === 0x22) {

        const r = uleb(wasm, p);
        p = r.next;

        const v = stack.length
            ? stack[stack.length - 1]
            : "UNKNOWN";

        locals.set(r.value, v);

        console.log(
            `${hex(at)}  local.tee ${r.value} <= ${v}`
        );
    }

    else if (op === 0x23) {

        const r = uleb(wasm, p);
        p = r.next;

        const v =
            r.value === 0
                ? "GLOBAL0"
                : `GLOBAL${r.value}`;

        push(v);

        console.log(
            `${hex(at)}  global.get ${r.value} => ${v}`
        );
    }

    else if (op === 0x24) {

        const r = uleb(wasm, p);
        p = r.next;

        const v = pop();

        console.log(
            `${hex(at)}  global.set ${r.value} <= ${v}`
        );
    }

    else if (op === 0x41) {

        const r = uleb(wasm, p);
        p = r.next;

        push(`CONST(${r.value})`);

        console.log(
            `${hex(at)}  i32.const ${r.value}`
        );
    }

    else if (op === 0x28 || op === 0x29) {

        const a = uleb(wasm, p);
        p = a.next;

        const o = uleb(wasm, p);
        p = o.next;

        const addr = pop();

        push(`LOAD(${addr}+${hex(o.value)})`);

        console.log(
            `${hex(at)}  ${opname(op)} ADDR=${addr} OFFSET=${hex(o.value)}`
        );
    }

    else if (
        op === 0x36 ||
        op === 0x37 ||
        op === 0x3a ||
        op === 0x3b ||
        op === 0x3e
    ) {

        const a = uleb(wasm, p);
        p = a.next;

        const o = uleb(wasm, p);
        p = o.next;

        /*
         * WASM store stack order:
         *
         * [... address, value]
         *
         * value is consumed first.
         */
        const value = pop();
        const address = pop();

        storeCount++;

        console.log("");
        console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
        console.log(`STORE #${storeCount} @ ${hex(at)}`);
        console.log("TYPE    =", opname(op));
        console.log("ADDRESS =", address);
        console.log("OFFSET  =", hex(o.value));
        console.log("VALUE   =", value);

        if (address.includes("GLOBAL0")) {
            console.log("*** DIRECT GLOBAL0-DERIVED ADDRESS ***");
        }

        if (
            address.includes("LOCAL2") ||
            address.includes("LOCAL3") ||
            address.includes("LOCAL4") ||
            address.includes("LOCAL5")
        ) {
            console.log("*** LOCAL-DERIVED ADDRESS ***");
        }

        console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
        console.log("");
    }

    else if (op === 0x6a || op === 0x6b) {

        const b = pop();
        const a = pop();

        let result = "UNKNOWN";

        if (
            a === "GLOBAL0" &&
            b.startsWith("CONST(")
        ) {
            result =
                `GLOBAL0 ${op === 0x6a ? "+" : "-"} ${b}`;
        }
        else if (
            a.startsWith("LOCAL") &&
            b.startsWith("CONST(")
        ) {
            result =
                `${a} ${op === 0x6a ? "+" : "-"} ${b}`;
        }
        else if (
            a.startsWith("LOAD(") &&
            b.startsWith("CONST(")
        ) {
            result =
                `${a} ${op === 0x6a ? "+" : "-"} ${b}`;
        }

        push(result);

        console.log(
            `${hex(at)}  ${opname(op)} ${a}, ${b} => ${result}`
        );
    }

    else if (op === 0x6c) {

        const b = pop();
        const a = pop();

        push(`MUL(${a},${b})`);

        console.log(
            `${hex(at)}  i32.mul ${a}, ${b}`
        );
    }

    else if (
        op === 0x71 ||
        op === 0x72 ||
        op === 0x73
    ) {

        const b = pop();
        const a = pop();

        push(`BINOP(${a},${b})`);

        console.log(
            `${hex(at)}  ${opname(op)} ${a}, ${b}`
        );
    }

    else if (op === 0x10) {

        const r = uleb(wasm, p);
        p = r.next;

        /*
         * Do NOT assume the call's result is directly
         * usable without knowing its signature.
         *
         * Mark it explicitly.
         */
        push(`CALL${r.value}_RESULT`);

        console.log(
            `${hex(at)}  call ${r.value}`
        );
    }

    else if (op === 0x11) {

        const t = uleb(wasm, p);
        p = t.next;

        const table = uleb(wasm, p);
        p = table.next;

        push("CALL_INDIRECT_RESULT");

        console.log(
            `${hex(at)}  call_indirect type=${t.value} table=${table.value}`
        );
    }

    else if (op === 0x0b) {

        console.log(
            `${hex(at)}  end`
        );
    }

    else {

        /*
         * For control/comparison instructions we don't try
         * to fake stack semantics here.
         */
        console.log(
            `${hex(at)}  ${opname(op)}`
        );
    }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SUMMARY");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("STORE COUNT =", storeCount);
console.log("GLOBAL0 INITIALIZER = 0x00266cc0");
console.log("");
console.log("READ ONLY - NO EXECUTION - NO TRANSACTION");
