import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const bytes = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const BODY_START = 0x236a8;
const BODY_END   = 0x2767b;

const TARGET_START = 0x19534c;
const TARGET_END   = 0x19535c;

const BULK_PCS = [
    0x2387e,
    0x238a8,
    0x23caa,
    0x23d21,
    0x2492d,

    0x243fc,
    0x24537,
    0x24675,
    0x248ce,
    0x24eb2,
    0x24fab,
    0x2516e,
    0x25256,
    0x253b5,
    0x259e3,
    0x26aa6,
    0x26c0c,
    0x26e2a,
    0x271c2,
];

function hex(n: number, w = 8) {
    return "0x" + (n >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(w, "0");
}

function readULEB(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = bytes[pos++];
        value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            break;

        shift += 7;
    }

    return { value, next: pos };
}

function readSLEB(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = bytes[pos++];
        value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            break;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
        value -= 2 ** shift;

    return { value, next: pos };
}

interface Ins {
    pc: number;
    text: string;
    imm?: number;
}

function decode(pc: number): Ins {
    const op = bytes[pc];

    if (op === 0x20) {
        const r = readULEB(pc + 1);
        return {
            pc,
            text: `local.get ${r.value}`,
            imm: r.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x21) {
        const r = readULEB(pc + 1);
        return {
            pc,
            text: `local.set ${r.value}`,
            imm: r.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x22) {
        const r = readULEB(pc + 1);
        return {
            pc,
            text: `local.tee ${r.value}`,
            imm: r.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x41) {
        const r = readSLEB(pc + 1);

        return {
            pc,
            text: `i32.const ${r.value} (${hex(r.value)})`,
            imm: r.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x6a)
        return { pc, text: "i32.add" };

    if (op === 0x6b)
        return { pc, text: "i32.sub" };

    if (op === 0x28) {
        const r = readULEB(pc + 2);
        return {
            pc,
            text: `i32.load offset=${hex(r.value)}`
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x36) {
        const r = readULEB(pc + 2);
        return {
            pc,
            text: `i32.store offset=${hex(r.value)}`
        };
    }

    if (op === 0xfc) {
        const r = readULEB(pc + 1);

        if (r.value === 10)
            return { pc, text: "memory.copy" };

        if (r.value === 11)
            return { pc, text: "memory.fill" };

        return {
            pc,
            text: `0xfc/${r.value}`
        };
    }

    return {
        pc,
        text: `opcode ${hex(op, 2)}`
    };
}

function nextPC(pc: number) {
    const op = bytes[pc];

    if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22)
        return readULEB(pc + 1).next;

    if (op === 0x41)
        return readSLEB(pc + 1).next;

    if (
        op === 0x28 ||
        op === 0x29 ||
        op === 0x2a ||
        op === 0x2b ||
        op === 0x36 ||
        op === 0x37 ||
        op === 0x38 ||
        op === 0x39
    )
        return readULEB(pc + 2).next;

    if (op === 0xfc)
        return readULEB(pc + 1).next;

    return pc + 1;
}

const instructions: Ins[] = [];

let p = BODY_START;

while (p < BODY_END) {
    const ins = decode(p);
    instructions.push(ins);

    const n = nextPC(p);

    if (n <= p)
        p++;
    else
        p = n;
}

function isTarget(v: number) {
    return v >= TARGET_START && v < TARGET_END;
}

function previousDefinition(local: number, beforeIndex: number) {
    for (let i = beforeIndex; i >= 0; i--) {
        const x = instructions[i];

        if (
            x.text === `local.set ${local}` ||
            x.text === `local.tee ${local}`
        ) {
            return i;
        }
    }

    return -1;
}

console.log("TRACE FUNC480 BULK DESTINATION");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`BODY = ${hex(BODY_START)} .. ${hex(BODY_END)}`);
console.log(`TARGET = ${hex(TARGET_START)} .. ${hex(TARGET_END - 1)}`);
console.log(`BULK OPS = ${BULK_PCS.length}`);

for (const pc of BULK_PCS) {

    const index = instructions.findIndex(x => x.pc === pc);

    console.log("");
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
    console.log(`BULK PC = ${hex(pc)}`);

    if (index < 0) {
        console.log("NOT FOUND IN BODY");
        continue;
    }

    const op = instructions[index];

    console.log(`OPERATION = ${op.text}`);

    const before = instructions.slice(
        Math.max(0, index - 10),
        index
    );

    for (const x of before)
        console.log(`${hex(x.pc)} ${x.text}`);

    /*
     * For these compiler patterns, inspect the last three
     * stack-producing operations before the bulk instruction.
     *
     * We do NOT claim static resolution unless all required
     * operands are constants.
     */

    console.log("");

    const last = before.slice(-3);

    if (op.text === "memory.fill") {
        console.log("STACK OPERANDS:");

        for (const x of last)
            console.log(`  ${x.text}`);

        if (
            last.length === 3 &&
            last[0].text.startsWith("i32.const") &&
            last[1].text.startsWith("i32.const") &&
            last[2].text.startsWith("i32.const")
        ) {
            const dst = last[0].imm!;
            const value = last[1].imm!;
            const len = last[2].imm!;

            const end = dst + len;

            console.log(`DEST = ${hex(dst)}`);
            console.log(`VALUE = ${hex(value)}`);
            console.log(`LENGTH = ${len}`);
            console.log(
                `OVERLAP = ${
                    dst < TARGET_END &&
                    end > TARGET_START
                        ? "YES"
                        : "NO"
                }`
            );
        }
        else {
            console.log("STATIC DEST = UNRESOLVED");

            /*
             * Look for a local.get in the destination position.
             */
            if (last[0]?.text.startsWith("local.get ")) {
                const local = last[0].imm!;

                console.log(
                    `DESTINATION LOCAL = ${local}`
                );

                const def = previousDefinition(
                    local,
                    index - 1
                );

                if (def >= 0) {
                    console.log(
                        `LOCAL DEFINITION @ ${hex(instructions[def].pc)}`
                    );

                    for (
                        let j = Math.max(0, def - 3);
                        j <= def;
                        j++
                    ) {
                        console.log(
                            `  ${hex(instructions[j].pc)} ${instructions[j].text}`
                        );
                    }

                    const producer = instructions[def - 1];

                    if (
                        producer &&
                        producer.text.startsWith("i32.const")
                    ) {
                        const v = producer.imm!;

                        console.log(
                            `PRODUCER CONSTANT = ${hex(v)}`
                        );

                        if (isTarget(v))
                            console.log(
                                ">>> TARGET CONSTANT REACHED"
                            );
                    }
                }
                else {
                    console.log(
                        "LOCAL DEFINITION = NOT FOUND"
                    );
                }
            }
        }
    }

    else if (op.text === "memory.copy") {
        console.log("STACK OPERANDS:");

        for (const x of last)
            console.log(`  ${x.text}`);

        console.log(
            "DESTINATION = first stack operand"
        );

        if (last[0]?.text.startsWith("local.get ")) {
            const local = last[0].imm!;

            console.log(
                `DESTINATION LOCAL = ${local}`
            );

            const def = previousDefinition(
                local,
                index - 1
            );

            if (def >= 0) {
                console.log(
                    `LOCAL DEFINITION @ ${hex(instructions[def].pc)}`
                );

                for (
                    let j = Math.max(0, def - 3);
                    j <= def;
                    j++
                ) {
                    console.log(
                        `  ${hex(instructions[j].pc)} ${instructions[j].text}`
                    );
                }

                const producer = instructions[def - 1];

                if (
                    producer &&
                    producer.text.startsWith("i32.const")
                ) {
                    const v = producer.imm!;

                    console.log(
                        `PRODUCER CONSTANT = ${hex(v)}`
                    );

                    if (isTarget(v))
                        console.log(
                            ">>> TARGET CONSTANT REACHED"
                        );
                }
            }
            else {
                console.log(
                    "LOCAL DEFINITION = NOT FOUND"
                );
            }
        }
        else if (
            last[0]?.text.startsWith("i32.const")
        ) {
            const dst = last[0].imm!;

            console.log(
                `STATIC DESTINATION = ${hex(dst)}`
            );

            if (isTarget(dst))
                console.log(
                    ">>> TARGET DESTINATION"
                );
        }
        else {
            console.log(
                "DESTINATION = NOT STATICALLY RESOLVED"
            );
        }
    }
}

console.log("");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceFunc480DestChain.ts

import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const bytes = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const BODY_START = 0x236a8;
const BODY_END   = 0x2767b;

const TARGETS = new Set([
    0x19534c,
    0x195350,
    0x195354,
    0x195358,
]);

const BULK_PCS = [
    0x2387e,
    0x238a8,
    0x23caa,
    0x23d21,
    0x2492d,
    0x243fc,
    0x24537,
    0x24675,
    0x248ce,
    0x24eb2,
    0x24fab,
    0x2516e,
    0x25256,
    0x253b5,
    0x259e3,
    0x26aa6,
    0x26c0c,
    0x26e2a,
    0x271c2,
];

function hex(n: number, w = 8) {
    return "0x" + (n >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(w, "0");
}

function uleb(p: number) {
    let v = 0;
    let s = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = bytes[p++];

        v += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;

        if (!(b & 0x80))
            break;

        s += 7;
    }

    return { value: v, next: p };
}

function sleb(p: number) {
    let v = 0;
    let s = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = bytes[p++];

        v += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
        s += 7;

        if (!(b & 0x80))
            break;
    }

    if (s < 32 && (b & 0x40))
        v -= 2 ** s;

    return { value: v, next: p };
}

interface Ins {
    pc: number;
    op: number;
    text: string;
    imm?: number;
}

function decode(p: number): Ins {
    const op = bytes[p];

    if (op === 0x20) {
        const r = uleb(p + 1);
        return {
            pc: p,
            op,
            text: `local.get ${r.value}`,
            imm: r.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x21) {
        const r = uleb(p + 1);
        return {
            pc: p,
            op,
            text: `local.set ${r.value}`,
            imm: r.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x22) {
        const r = uleb(p + 1);
        return {
            pc: p,
            op,
            text: `local.tee ${r.value}`,
            imm: r.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x41) {
        const r = sleb(p + 1);

        return {
            pc: p,
            op,
            text: `i32.const ${r.value} (${hex(r.value)})`,
            imm: r.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x23) {
        const r = uleb(p + 1);

        return {
            pc: p,
            op,
            text: `global.get ${r.value}`,
            imm: r.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x24) {
        const r = uleb(p + 1);

        return {
            pc: p,
            op,
            text: `global.set ${r.value}`,
            imm: r.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x28) {
        const r = uleb(p + 2);

        return {
            pc: p,
            op,
            text: `i32.load offset=${hex(r.value)}`,
            imm: r.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x6a)
        return { pc: p, op, text: "i32.add" };

    if (op === 0x6b)
        return { pc: p, op, text: "i32.sub" };

    if (op === 0x74)
        return { pc: p, op, text: "i32.shl" };

    if (op === 0x75)
        return { pc: p, op, text: "i32.shr_s" };

    if (op === 0x76)
        return { pc: p, op, text: "i32.shr_u" };

    if (op === 0x77)
        return { pc: p, op, text: "i32.rotl" };

    if (op === 0x78)
        return { pc: p, op, text: "i32.rotr" };

    if (op === 0xa7)
        return { pc: p, op, text: "i32.wrap_i64" };

    if (op === 0xfc) {
        const r = uleb(p + 1);

        if (r.value === 10)
            return { pc: p, op, text: "memory.copy" };

        if (r.value === 11)
            return { pc: p, op, text: "memory.fill" };

        return {
            pc: p,
            op,
            text: `0xfc/${r.value}`
        };
    }

    return {
        pc: p,
        op,
        text: `opcode ${hex(op, 2)}`
    };
}

function nextPC(p: number) {
    const op = bytes[p];

    if (
        op === 0x20 ||
        op === 0x21 ||
        op === 0x22 ||
        op === 0x23 ||
        op === 0x24
    )
        return uleb(p + 1).next;

    if (op === 0x41)
        return sleb(p + 1).next;

    if (
        op === 0x28 ||
        op === 0x29 ||
        op === 0x2a ||
        op === 0x2b
    )
        return uleb(p + 2).next;

    if (op === 0xfc)
        return uleb(p + 1).next;

    return p + 1;
}

const ins: Ins[] = [];

let p = BODY_START;

while (p < BODY_END) {
    const x = decode(p);

    ins.push(x);

    const n = nextPC(p);

    p = n > p ? n : p + 1;
}

function lastDefinition(local: number, before: number) {
    for (let i = before; i >= 0; i--) {
        if (
            (ins[i].op === 0x21 || ins[i].op === 0x22) &&
            ins[i].imm === local
        ) {
            return i;
        }
    }

    return -1;
}

function nearestProducer(defIndex: number) {
    const result: number[] = [];

    for (
        let i = defIndex - 1;
        i >= Math.max(0, defIndex - 12);
        i--
    ) {
        result.push(i);

        /*
         * Stop when another local assignment or
         * obvious control boundary is reached.
         */
        if (
            ins[i].op === 0x21 ||
            ins[i].op === 0x22
        )
            break;
    }

    return result.reverse();
}

function traceLocal(
    local: number,
    before: number,
    depth: number,
    seen: Set<string>
) {
    const indent = "  ".repeat(depth);

    if (depth > 8) {
        console.log(`${indent}DEPTH LIMIT`);
        return;
    }

    const key = `${local}@${before}`;

    if (seen.has(key)) {
        console.log(`${indent}RECURSION`);
        return;
    }

    seen.add(key);

    const def = lastDefinition(local, before);

    if (def < 0) {
        console.log(
            `${indent}LOCAL ${local}: DEFINITION NOT FOUND`
        );
        return;
    }

    console.log(
        `${indent}LOCAL ${local} <- ${hex(ins[def].pc)} ${ins[def].text}`
    );

    const producers = nearestProducer(def);

    for (const i of producers) {
        const x = ins[i];

        console.log(
            `${indent}  ${hex(x.pc)} ${x.text}`
        );

        if (
            x.op === 0x41 &&
            x.imm !== undefined
        ) {
            if (TARGETS.has(x.imm)) {
                console.log(
                    `${indent}  >>> TARGET CONSTANT REACHED: ${hex(x.imm)}`
                );
            }
        }

        if (
            x.op === 0x20 &&
            x.imm !== undefined
        ) {
            traceLocal(
                x.imm,
                i - 1,
                depth + 1,
                seen
            );
        }
    }
}

console.log("TRACE FUNC480 DESTINATION CHAINS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`BODY = ${hex(BODY_START)} .. ${hex(BODY_END)}`);
console.log(
    `TARGETS = ${[...TARGETS].map(x => hex(x)).join(", ")}`
);

for (const pc of BULK_PCS) {

    const idx = ins.findIndex(x => x.pc === pc);

    if (idx < 0)
        continue;

    const x = ins[idx];

    console.log("");
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
    console.log(`BULK = ${hex(pc)} ${x.text}`);

    /*
     * Inspect the three stack operands immediately before
     * the bulk instruction.
     */
    const operands = ins.slice(
        Math.max(0, idx - 3),
        idx
    );

    if (x.text === "memory.fill") {

        const dst = operands[0];

        console.log(
            `DEST OPERAND = ${dst.text}`
        );

        if (
            dst.op === 0x20 &&
            dst.imm !== undefined
        ) {
            traceLocal(
                dst.imm,
                idx - 1,
                0,
                new Set()
            );
        }
    }

    if (x.text === "memory.copy") {

        const dst = operands[0];

        console.log(
            `DEST OPERAND = ${dst.text}`
        );

        if (
            dst.op === 0x20 &&
            dst.imm !== undefined
        ) {
            traceLocal(
                dst.imm,
                idx - 1,
                0,
                new Set()
            );
        }
    }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FINAL");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("NO WASM EXECUTION");

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function leb(pos: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, p = pos;
  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
    s += 7;
  }
}

const types: any[] = [];

let p = 8;
while (p < wasm.length) {
  const id = wasm[p++];
  const r = leb(p);
  p = r.next;

  if (id === 1) {
    const end = p + r.value;
    const c = leb(p);
    p = c.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < c.value; i++) {
      p++; // form
      const pc = leb(p); p = pc.next;
      const params = [];
      for (let j = 0; j < pc.value; j++) params.push(wasm[p++]);

      const rc = leb(p); p = rc.next;
      const results = [];
      for (let j = 0; j < rc.value; j++) results.push(wasm[p++]);

      types.push({ params, results });
    }
    break;
  }

  p += r.value;
}

function tn(x: number) {
  return x === 0x7f ? "i32" :
         x === 0x7e ? "i64" :
         x === 0x7d ? "f32" :
         x === 0x7c ? "f64" :
         "0x" + x.toString(16);
}

let codeStart = 0;
p = 8;

while (p < wasm.length) {
  const id = wasm[p++];
  const r = leb(p);
  p = r.next;

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = p;
    break;
  }

  p += r.value;
}

let q = codeStart;
const count = leb(q);
q = count.next;

for (let func = 0; func <= 504; func++) {
  const br = leb(q);
  const bodySize = br.value;
  q = br.next;
  const bodyEnd = q + bodySize;

  if (func === 504) {
    console.log("FUNC 504");
    console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(`BODY SIZE : ${bodySize}`);

    // function section
    let fp = 8;
    let typeIndex = -1;

    while (fp < wasm.length) {
      const id = wasm[fp++];
      const r = leb(fp);
      fp = r.next;

      if (id === 3) {
        const fc = leb(fp);
        fp = fc.next;

        for (let i = 0; i <= 504; i++) {
          const tr = leb(fp);
          fp = tr.next;
          if (i === 504) {
            typeIndex = tr.value;
            break;
          }
        }
        break;
      }

      fp += r.value;
    }

    console.log(`TYPE INDEX : ${typeIndex}`);

    if (types[typeIndex]) {
      console.log(
        `PARAMS     : ${types[typeIndex].params.map(tn).join(", ")}`
      );
      console.log(
        `RESULTS    : ${types[typeIndex].results.map(tn).join(", ")}`
      );
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("CALLS:");
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    for (let x = q; x < bodyEnd; x++) {
      if (wasm[x] === 0x10) {
        const r = leb(x + 1);
        console.log(
          `CALL @ 0x${x.toString(16).padStart(6, "0")} -> FUNC ${r.value}`
        );
      }
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("DONE");
    break;
  }

  q = bodyEnd;
}

}

FILE: scripts\traceFunc656DispatchMemory.mjs

import fs from "fs";

const file = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGETS = [
  0x50098050,
  0x50098054
];

const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

let p = 8;

function u8() {
  return b[p++];
}

function readULEB() {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = u8();

    value += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(x & 0x80)) {
      return value >>> 0;
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readSLEB() {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = u8();

    value += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
    shift += 7;

    if (!(x & 0x80)) {
      if (shift < 32 && (x & 0x40)) {
        value -= 2 ** shift;
      }

      return value | 0;
    }
  }
}

function hex(v, width = 8) {
  return (
    "0x" +
    (v >>> 0)
      .toString(16)
      .padStart(width, "0")
  );
}

function readName() {
  const len = readULEB();
  const start = p;

  p += len;

  return b
    .subarray(start, start + len)
    .toString("utf8");
}

function skipLimits() {
  const flags = readULEB();

  readULEB();

  if (flags & 1) {
    readULEB();
  }
}

function skipGlobalType() {
  u8();
  u8();
}

// ============================================================
// MODULE PARSE
// ============================================================

let importedFunctions = 0;

const funcTypeIndices = [];
const codeBodies = [];
const dataSegments = [];

while (p < b.length) {

  const sectionId = u8();
  const sectionSize = readULEB();

  const sectionStart = p;
  const sectionEnd = sectionStart + sectionSize;

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // TYPE SECTION
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  if (sectionId === 1) {

    const count = readULEB();

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {

      const form = u8();

      if (form !== 0x60) {
        throw new Error(
          `Unexpected type form ${hex(form, 2)}`
        );
      }

      const paramCount = readULEB();

      for (let j = 0; j < paramCount; j++) {
        u8();
      }

      const resultCount = readULEB();

      for (let j = 0; j < resultCount; j++) {
        u8();
      }
    }
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // IMPORT SECTION
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  else if (sectionId === 2) {

    const count = readULEB();

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {

      readName();
      readName();

      const kind = u8();

      if (kind === 0) {

        readULEB();

        importedFunctions++;
      }

      else if (kind === 1) {

        u8();

        skipLimits();
      }

      else if (kind === 2) {

        skipLimits();
      }

      else if (kind === 3) {

        skipGlobalType();
      }
    }
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // FUNCTION SECTION
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  else if (sectionId === 3) {

    const count = readULEB();

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {

      funcTypeIndices.push(
        readULEB()
      );
    }
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // CODE SECTION
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  else if (sectionId === 10) {

    const count = readULEB();

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {

      const bodySize = readULEB();

      const start = p;
      const end = p + bodySize;

      codeBodies.push({
        start,
        end,
        size: bodySize
      });

      p = end;
    }
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // DATA SECTION
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  else if (sectionId === 11) {

    const count = readULEB();

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {

      const flags = readULEB();

      let memoryIndex = 0;
      let offsetValue = null;

      if (flags === 0) {

        const opcode = u8();

        if (opcode !== 0x41) {
          throw new Error(
            `Unexpected data offset opcode ${hex(opcode, 2)}`
          );
        }

        offsetValue = readSLEB();

        const endOpcode = u8();

        if (endOpcode !== 0x0b) {
          throw new Error(
            `Unexpected data offset END ${hex(endOpcode, 2)}`
          );
        }
      }

      else if (flags === 1) {

        offsetValue = null;
      }

      else if (flags === 2) {

        memoryIndex = readULEB();

        const opcode = u8();

        if (opcode !== 0x41) {
          throw new Error(
            `Unexpected data offset opcode ${hex(opcode, 2)}`
          );
        }

        offsetValue = readSLEB();

        const endOpcode = u8();

        if (endOpcode !== 0x0b) {
          throw new Error(
            `Unexpected data offset END ${hex(endOpcode, 2)}`
          );
        }
      }

      const size = readULEB();

      const dataStart = p;
      const dataEnd = p + size;

      dataSegments.push({
        memoryIndex,
        offset: offsetValue,
        start: dataStart,
        end: dataEnd,
        size
      });

      p = dataEnd;
    }
  }

  p = sectionEnd;
}

  );

    `DECIMAL       : ${target >>> 0}`

  for (const seg of dataSegments) {

    if (seg.offset === null) {
      continue;
    }

    if (
      target >= seg.offset &&

    ) {

      hits.push({
        seg,
        relative:
          target - seg.offset
      });
    }
  }

  if (hits.length === 0) {

    console.log(
      "DATA SEGMENT : NONE"
    );

  } else {

    console.log(
      `DATA SEGMENT HITS : ${hits.length}`
    );

    for (const h of hits) {

      console.log(

      );

      console.log(

      );

        h.seg.start + h.relative;

      const remaining =
        Math.min(
          16,
          h.seg.end - valueStart
        );

      const bytes =
        b.subarray(
          valueStart,
          valueStart + remaining
        );

      console.log(
        "  BYTES          : " +
        [...bytes]
          .map(x =>
            x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
          )
          .join(" ")
      );
    }
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // RAW BYTE SEARCH
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  const addr =
    target >>> 0;

  const le = [
    addr & 0xff,
    (addr >>> 8) & 0xff,
    (addr >>> 16) & 0xff,
    (addr >>> 24) & 0xff
  ];

  let rawHits = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i <= b.length - 4; i++) {

    if (
      b[i] === le[0] &&
      b[i + 1] === le[1] &&
      b[i + 2] === le[2] &&
      b[i + 3] === le[3]
    ) {

      rawHits++;

      if (rawHits <= 30) {

        console.log(
          `RAW 32BIT @ ${hex(i)}`
        );
      }
    }
  }

  console.log(
    `RAW 32BIT HIT COUNT : ${rawHits}`
  );
}

        });

      i < body.end;
      i++
    ) {

      if (b[i] !== 0x41) {
        continue;

      let value = 0;
      let shift = 0;

        const x = b[q++];

            `MEMOP @ ${hex(j)} ` +
            `opcode=${hex(op, 2)}`
          );

          break;
        }
      }
    }
  }

  console.log(
    `CANDIDATES : ${candidates}`
  );

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceFunc65ReturnSource.mjs

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const IMPORTED = 83;
const TARGET_FUNC = 65;

const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function uleb(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[pos++];
    value += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(x & 0x80)) {
      return { value, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function findCode() {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < b.length) {
    const id = b[p++];

    const s = uleb(p);
    p = s.next;

    if (id === 10) {
      return { start: p, end: p + s.value };
    }

    p += s.value;
  }

  throw new Error("CODE section not found");
}

function getFunction(index) {
  const code = findCode();

  let p = code.start;

  const count = uleb(p);
  p = count.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {

    const size = uleb(p);
    p = size.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + size.value;

    if (IMPORTED + i === index) {
      return { start, end, size: size.value };
    }

    p = end;
  }

  throw new Error(`FUNC ${index} not found`);
}

const fn = getFunction(TARGET_FUNC);

import fs from "fs";

const file =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

const START = 0x23a188;
const END   = 0x23a21b;

function readULEB(p: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };

    s += 7;
  }
}

function readSLEB(p: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;

    if (!(x & 0x80)) {
      if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40))
        v |= (~0 << (s + 7));

      return { value: v | 0, next: p };
    }

    s += 7;
  }
}

function hex(x: number) {
  return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

console.log("FUNC 6919 EXACT TRACE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`RANGE : ${hex(START)} .. ${hex(END)}`);
console.log("TARGET: MEM[0x25100C]");
console.log("============================================================");

let p = START;

while (p < END) {

  const a = p;
  const op = b[p++];

  let s = "";

  switch (op) {

    case 0x00:
      s = "unreachable";
      break;

    case 0x01:
      s = "nop";
      break;

    case 0x02:
      s = "block";
      p++;
      break;

    case 0x04:
      s = "if";
      p++;
      break;

    case 0x05:
      s = "else";
      break;

    case 0x0b:
      s = "end";
      break;

    case 0x0c: {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      p = r.next;
      s = `br ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x0d: {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      p = r.next;
      s = `br_if ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x0f:
      s = "return";
      break;

    case 0x10: {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      p = r.next;
      s = `call FUNC ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x20: {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      p = r.next;
      s = `local.get ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x21: {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      p = r.next;
      s = `local.set ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x22: {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      p = r.next;
      s = `local.tee ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x23: {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      p = r.next;
      s = `global.get ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x24: {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      p = r.next;
      s = `global.set ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x28: {
      const align = readULEB(p);
      p = align.next;

      const off = readULEB(p);
      p = off.next;

      s = `i32.load align=${align.value} offset=${off.value}`;

      if (off.value === 0)
        s += "  <-- LOAD";
      break;
    }

    case 0x36: {
      const align = readULEB(p);
      p = align.next;

      const off = readULEB(p);
      p = off.next;

      s = `i32.store align=${align.value} offset=${off.value}`;

      if (off.value === 0)
        s += "  <-- STORE";
      break;
    }

    case 0x41: {
      const r = readSLEB(p);
      p = r.next;

      s = `i32.const ${r.value}`;

      if ((r.value >>> 0) === 0x25100c)
        s += "  <-- TARGET ADDRESS";

      break;
    }

    case 0x45:
      s = "i32.eqz";
      break;

    case 0x46:
      s = "i32.eq";
      break;

    case 0x47:
      s = "i32.ne";
      break;

    case 0x48:
      s = "i32.lt_s";
      break;

    case 0x4a:
      s = "i32.gt_s";
      break;

    case 0x4b:
      s = "i32.gt_u";
      break;

    case 0x4c:
      s = "i32.le_s";
      break;

    case 0x4e:
      s = "i32.ge_s";
      break;

    case 0x6a:
      s = "i32.add";
      break;

    case 0x71:
      s = "i32.and";
      break;

    case 0x72:
      s = "i32.or";
      break;

    case 0x70:
      s = "i32.wrap? opcode=0x70";
      break;

    case 0x1a:
      s = "drop";
      break;

    default:
      s = `opcode 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
      break;
  }

  console.log(`${hex(a)}  ${s}`);
}

FILE: scripts\traceFunc6919Stack.ts

import fs from "fs";

const file =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

const START = 0x23a188;
const END   = 0x23a21b;

function readULEB(p: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];

    if (x === undefined)
      throw new Error(`ULEB OOB @ 0x${(p - 1).toString(16)}`);

    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readSLEB(p: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];

    if (x === undefined)
      throw new Error(`SLEB OOB @ 0x${(p - 1).toString(16)}`);

    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(x & 0x80)) {
      if (shift < 32 && (x & 0x40))
        v -= 2 ** (shift + 7);

      return { value: v | 0, next: p };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function hex(x: number) {
  return "0x" + (x >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

type V = string;

const stack: V[] = [];
const locals = new Map<number, V>();

function push(v: V) {
  stack.push(v);
}

function pop(): V {
  if (!stack.length)
    return "<STACK_EMPTY>";

  return stack.pop()!;
}

function dumpStack() {
  if (!stack.length)
    return "[]";

  return "[" + stack.map((x, i) => `${i}:${x}`).join(" | ") + "]";
}

function blockType(p: number) {
  const x = b[p];

  if (x === 0x40)
    return { next: p + 1 };

  if (
    x === 0x7f ||
    x === 0x7e ||
    x === 0x7d ||
    x === 0x7c
  )
    return { next: p + 1 };

  return readSLEB(p);
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 6919 TARGETED STACK / DATAFLOW TRACE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`BODY : ${hex(START)} .. ${hex(END)}`);
console.log("FOCUS:");
console.log("  FUNC821");
console.log("  FUNC822");
console.log("  MEM[0x25100C]");
console.log("============================================================");

let p = START;
let depth = 0;

while (p < END) {

  const at = p;
  const op = b[p++];

  if (op === undefined)
    break;

  let text = "";

  switch (op) {

    case 0x01:
      text = "nop";
      break;

    case 0x02:
    case 0x03:
    case 0x04: {
      const r = blockType(p);
      p = r.next;
      depth++;
      text =
        op === 0x02 ? "block" :
        op === 0x03 ? "loop" : "if";
      break;
    }

    case 0x05:
      text = "else";
      break;

    case 0x0b:
      text = "end";
      if (depth > 0)
        depth--;
      break;

    case 0x0c: {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      p = r.next;
      text = `br ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x0d: {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      p = r.next;
      const c = pop();
      text = `br_if ${r.value}  [condition=${c}]`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x0f:
      text = "return";
      break;

    case 0x10: {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      p = r.next;

      const fn = r.value;

      // Known signatures / argument counts for our target calls.
      const argc =
        fn === 270 ? 3 :
        fn === 821 ? 3 :
        fn === 822 ? 4 :
        fn === 215 ? 1 :
        0;

      const args: V[] = [];

      for (let i = 0; i < argc; i++)
        args.unshift(pop());

      if (fn === 821) {
        text =
          `CALL FUNC821(` +
          args.join(", ") +
          `)`;

        console.log("");
        console.log("############################################################");
        console.log(">>> FUNC821 CALL");
        console.log(`@ ${hex(at)}`);
        console.log(`ARGS : ${args.join(" , ")}`);
        console.log(`STACK BEFORE RETURN: ${dumpStack()}`);
        console.log("############################################################");
      }
      else if (fn === 822) {
        text =
          `CALL FUNC822(` +
          args.join(", ") +
          `)`;

        console.log("");
        console.log("############################################################");
        console.log(">>> FUNC822 CALL");
        console.log(`@ ${hex(at)}`);
        console.log(`ARGS : ${args.join(" , ")}`);
        console.log(`STACK BEFORE RETURN: ${dumpStack()}`);
        console.log("############################################################");
      }
      else {
        text = `CALL FUNC${fn}(${args.join(", ")})`;
      }

      // Known return-producing calls.
      if (
        fn === 270 ||
        fn === 821 ||
        fn === 822 ||
        fn === 215
      ) {
        push(`RET(FUNC${fn})`);
      }

      break;
    }

    case 0x1a: {
      const v = pop();
      text = `drop ${v}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x1b: {
      const cond = pop();
      const bval = pop();
      const aval = pop();

      const result =
        `SELECT(${cond} ? ${aval} : ${bval})`;

      push(result);

      text =
        `select => ${result}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x20: {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      p = r.next;

      const v = locals.get(r.value) ??
        `local${r.value}`;

      push(v);
      text = `local.get ${r.value} => ${v}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x21: {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      p = r.next;

      const v = pop();
      locals.set(r.value, v);

      text =
        `local.set ${r.value} = ${v}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x22: {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      p = r.next;

      const v = stack.length
        ? stack[stack.length - 1]
        : "<EMPTY>";

      locals.set(r.value, v);

      text =
        `local.tee ${r.value} = ${v}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x28: {
      const a = readULEB(p);
      p = a.next;

      const o = readULEB(p);
      p = o.next;

      const addr = pop();

      const v =
        o.value === 0
          ? `MEM[${addr}]`
          : `MEM[${addr}+${o.value}]`;

      push(v);

      text =
        `i32.load ${v}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x36: {
      const a = readULEB(p);
      p = a.next;

      const o = readULEB(p);
      p = o.next;

      const value = pop();
      const addr  = pop();

      const target =
        o.value === 0
          ? `${addr}`
          : `${addr}+${o.value}`;

      text =
        `i32.store MEM[${target}] = ${value}`;

      if (
        addr.includes("2428940") ||
        addr.includes("0x25100c") ||
        addr.includes("2428940")
      ) {
        console.log("");
        console.log(">>> TARGET STORE DETECTED");
        console.log(`@ ${hex(at)}`);
        console.log(`MEM[0x25100C] <= ${value}`);
        console.log(`STACK AFTER STORE: ${dumpStack()}`);
        console.log("");
      }

      break;
    }

    case 0x41: {
      const r = readSLEB(p);
      p = r.next;

      const v = r.value;

      push(
        v === 0x25100c
          ? "ADDR(0x25100C)"
          : `CONST(${v})`
      );

      text =
        v === 0x25100c
          ? "i32.const 0x25100C"
          : `i32.const ${v}`;

      break;
    }

    case 0x45: {
      const v = pop();
      push(`EQZ(${v})`);
      text = `i32.eqz(${v})`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x47: {
      const bval = pop();
      const aval = pop();

      push(`NE(${aval},${bval})`);

      text =
        `i32.ne(${aval},${bval})`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x71: {
      const bval = pop();
      const aval = pop();

      push(`AND(${aval},${bval})`);

      text =
        `i32.and(${aval},${bval})`;
      break;
    }

    default:
      text =
        `opcode 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
      break;
  }

  console.log(
    `${hex(at)}  ${text}`
  );

  if (
    at === 0x23a1b3 ||
    at === 0x23a1c2 ||
    at === 0x23a1c7 ||
    at === 0x23a201 ||
    at === 0x23a217
  ) {
    console.log(
      `           STACK = ${dumpStack()}`
    );
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`FINAL : ${hex(p)}`);
console.log(`EXPECT: ${hex(END)}`);
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

import fs from "fs";

const FILE = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const b = fs.readFileSync(FILE);

const IMPORTED = 83;
const TARGET_FUNC = 6991;

function readULEB(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[pos++];
    value |= (x & 0x7f) << shift;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function hx(v) {
  return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

  const start = p;
  const end = p + sz.value;

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = start;
    codeEnd = end;
  }

  p = end;
}

q = count.next;

  q = sz.next;

  const end = q + sz.value;

  console.log(`SIZE        : ${end - start}`);

import fs from "fs";

const wasmPath = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const TARGET_FUNC = 7151;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

function hex(v, width = 8) {
    return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function readULEB(buf, pos) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * (2 ** shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }

    throw new Error("ULEB128 overflow");
}

console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log(`FUNC ${TARGET_FUNC} RAW BODY`);
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("=".repeat(72));

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// WASM HEADER
// ------------------------------------------------------------
if (
    wasm[0] !== 0x00 ||
    wasm[1] !== 0x61 ||
    wasm[2] !== 0x73 ||
    wasm[3] !== 0x6d
) {
    throw new Error("Invalid WASM file");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// FIND IMPORTED FUNCTION COUNT
// ------------------------------------------------------------
let pos = 8;
let importedFunctions = 0;

while (pos < wasm.length) {

    const sectionId = wasm[pos++];

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(wasm, pos);
    const sectionSize = sizeInfo.value;
    pos = sizeInfo.next;

    const sectionStart = pos;
    const sectionEnd = pos + sectionSize;

    // IMPORT SECTION
    if (sectionId === 2) {

        let p = sectionStart;

        const countInfo = readULEB(wasm, p);
        const importCount = countInfo.value;
        p = countInfo.next;

        for (let i = 0; i < importCount; i++) {

            // module name
            let len = readULEB(wasm, p);
            p = len.next + len.value;

            // field/name
            len = readULEB(wasm, p);
            p = len.next + len.value;

            // external kind
            const kind = wasm[p++];

            if (kind === 0) {
                importedFunctions++;

                // type index
                const t = readULEB(wasm, p);
                p = t.next;
            }
            else if (kind === 1) {
                // table
                p = skipTableType(p);
            }
            else if (kind === 2) {
                // memory
                p = skipLimits(p);
            }
            else if (kind === 3) {
                // global
                p += 2;
            }
            else if (kind === 4) {
                // tag
                const attr = wasm[p++];
                const t = readULEB(wasm, p);
                p = t.next;
            }
        }
    }

    pos = sectionEnd;
}

function skipLimits(p) {

    const flags = wasm[p++];

    let r = readULEB(wasm, p);
    p = r.next;

    if (flags & 0x01) {
        r = readULEB(wasm, p);
        p = r.next;
    }

    return p;
}

function skipTableType(p) {

    // reference type
    p++;

    return skipLimits(p);
}

console.log(`IMPORTED FUNCTION COUNT : ${importedFunctions}`);
console.log();

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// FIND FUNCTION SECTION + CODE SECTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------
pos = 8;

let definedFunctionCount = 0;
let targetCodeOrdinal = null;

let functionTypes = [];

let codeSectionStart = null;
let codeSectionEnd = null;

while (pos < wasm.length) {

    const sectionId = wasm[pos++];

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(wasm, pos);
    const sectionSize = sizeInfo.value;
    pos = sizeInfo.next;

    const sectionStart = pos;
    const sectionEnd = pos + sectionSize;

    // FUNCTION SECTION
    if (sectionId === 3) {

        let p = sectionStart;

        const countInfo = readULEB(wasm, p);
        definedFunctionCount = countInfo.value;
        p = countInfo.next;

        for (let i = 0; i < definedFunctionCount; i++) {

            const t = readULEB(wasm, p);

            functionTypes.push(t.value);

            p = t.next;
        }
    }

    // CODE SECTION
    if (sectionId === 10) {

        codeSectionStart = sectionStart;
        codeSectionEnd = sectionEnd;

        let p = sectionStart;

        const countInfo = readULEB(wasm, p);
        const bodyCount = countInfo.value;
        p = countInfo.next;

        console.log(`DEFINED FUNCTION COUNT : ${bodyCount}`);
        console.log();

        for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < bodyCount; ordinal++) {

            const sizeInfo2 = readULEB(wasm, p);

            const bodySize = sizeInfo2.value;
            p = sizeInfo2.next;

            const bodyStart = p;
            const bodyEnd = p + bodySize;

            const functionIndex =
                importedFunctions + ordinal;

            if (functionIndex === TARGET_FUNC) {

                targetCodeOrdinal = ordinal;

                console.log("TARGET FOUND");
                console.log("-".repeat(72));
                console.log(`FUNCTION INDEX : ${functionIndex}`);
                console.log(`CODE ORDINAL   : ${ordinal}`);
                console.log(`BODY START     : ${hex(bodyStart)}`);
                console.log(`BODY END       : ${hex(bodyEnd)}`);
                console.log(`BODY SIZE      : ${bodySize}`);

                if (ordinal < functionTypes.length) {
                    console.log(
                        `TYPE INDEX     : ${functionTypes[ordinal]}`
                    );
                }

                console.log("-".repeat(72));
                console.log();

                // ------------------------------------------------
                // RAW BODY
                // ------------------------------------------------
                for (
                    let a = bodyStart;
                    a < bodyEnd;
                    a += 16
                ) {

                    const lineEnd =
                        Math.min(a + 16, bodyEnd);

                    let bytes = [];

                    for (let b = a; b < lineEnd; b++) {
                        bytes.push(
                            wasm[b]
                                .toString(16)
                                .padStart(2, "0")
                        );
                    }

                    console.log(
                        `${hex(a)}  ${bytes.join(" ")}`
                    );
                }

                console.log();
                console.log("=".repeat(72));
                console.log("DONE");
                console.log("=".repeat(72));

                process.exit(0);
            }

            p = bodyEnd;
        }
    }

    pos = sectionEnd;
}

console.log(
    `FUNC ${TARGET_FUNC} NOT FOUND`
);

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceFunc7484Local5.js

const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_FUNC = 7484;
const TARGET_INDEX = 2053;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

function readU32LE(pos) {
  return (
    wasm[pos] |
    (wasm[pos + 1] << 8) |
    (wasm[pos + 2] << 16) |
    (wasm[pos + 3] << 24)
  ) >>> 0;
}

function readULEB(pos) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
    shift += 7;
  }

  return { value: result >>> 0, next: p };
}

function readSLEB(pos) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let byte;

  do {
    byte = wasm[pos++];
    result |= (byte & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;
  } while (byte & 0x80);

  if (shift < 32 && (byte & 0x40)) {
    result |= (~0 << shift);
  }

  return { value: result | 0, next: pos };
}

function hex(n) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function readName(pos) {
  const len = readULEB(pos);
  const start = len.next;
  const end = start + len.value;
  return {
    text: wasm.subarray(start, end).toString("utf8"),
    next: end
  };
}

/*
 * Locate sections.
 */
let p = 8;
let codeSection = null;
let functionSection = null;

while (p < wasm.length) {
  const sectionId = wasm[p++];

  const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);
  const sectionSize = sizeInfo.value;
  const payloadStart = sizeInfo.next;
  const payloadEnd = payloadStart + sectionSize;

  if (sectionId === 3) {
    functionSection = {
      start: payloadStart,
      end: payloadEnd
    };
  }

  if (sectionId === 10) {
    codeSection = {
      start: payloadStart,
      end: payloadEnd
    };
  }

  p = payloadEnd;
}

if (!functionSection || !codeSection) {
  throw new Error("FUNCTION or CODE section not found");
}

/*
 * Parse function section.
 * Imported functions are assumed to occupy indices 0..importedCount-1.
 *
 * We derive imported count from import section.
 */
let importedCount = 0;

p = 8;

while (p < wasm.length) {
  const sectionId = wasm[p++];

  const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);
  const sectionSize = sizeInfo.value;
  const payloadStart = sizeInfo.next;
  const payloadEnd = payloadStart + sectionSize;

  if (sectionId === 2) {
    let q = payloadStart;

    const countInfo = readULEB(q);
    const importCount = countInfo.value;
    q = countInfo.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < importCount; i++) {
      const module = readName(q);
      q = module.next;

      const name = readName(q);
      q = name.next;

      const kind = wasm[q++];

      if (kind === 0) {
        readULEB(q);
        q = readULEB(q).next;
      } else if (kind === 1) {
        q++;
        const flags = wasm[q++];
        q = readULEB(q).next;
        if (flags & 1) q = readULEB(q).next;
      } else if (kind === 2) {
        const flags = readULEB(q);
        q = readULEB(q).next;
        if (flags.value & 1) q = readULEB(q).next;
      } else if (kind === 3) {
        q++;
        q++;
      }
    }

    importedCount = 0;

    /*
     * Re-parse more simply to count function imports.
     */
    q = payloadStart;
    const ci = readULEB(q);
    q = ci.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < ci.value; i++) {
      const m = readName(q);
      q = m.next;

      const n = readName(q);
      q = n.next;

      const k = wasm[q++];

      if (k === 0) {
        q = readULEB(q).next;
        importedCount++;
      } else if (k === 1) {
        q++;
        const flags = wasm[q++];
        q = readULEB(q).next;
        if (flags & 1) q = readULEB(q).next;
      } else if (k === 2) {
        const flags = readULEB(q);
        q = flags.next;

        const min = readULEB(q);
        q = min.next;

        if (flags.value & 1) q = readULEB(q).next;
      } else if (k === 3) {
        q++;
        q++;
      }
    }
  }

  p = payloadEnd;
}

/*
 * Function section gives type index for each defined function.
 */
let q = functionSection.start;

const functionCountInfo = readULEB(q);
q = functionCountInfo.next;

const functionTypes = [];

for (let i = 0; i < functionCountInfo.value; i++) {
  const t = readULEB(q);
  q = t.next;
  functionTypes.push(t.value);
}

/*
 * CODE section bodies.
 */
q = codeSection.start;

const bodyCountInfo = readULEB(q);
q = bodyCountInfo.next;

const bodies = [];

for (let i = 0; i < bodyCountInfo.value; i++) {
  const bodySizeInfo = readULEB(q);
  const bodySize = bodySizeInfo.value;
  const bodyStart = bodySizeInfo.next;
  const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize;

  const funcIndex = importedCount + i;

  bodies.push({
    funcIndex,
    bodyStart,
    bodyEnd
  });

  q = bodyEnd;
}

const body = bodies.find(x => x.funcIndex === TARGET_FUNC);

if (!body) {
  throw new Error(`FUNC ${TARGET_FUNC} not found`);
}

/*
 * Opcode names used by the local5 dataflow tracer.
 */
const names = {
  0x0b: "end",
  0x0c: "br",
  0x0d: "br_if",
  0x0e: "br_table",
  0x0f: "return",

  0x20: "local.get",
  0x21: "local.set",
  0x22: "local.tee",

  0x28: "i32.load",
  0x2d: "i32.load8_u",

  0x36: "i32.store",
  0x3a: "i32.store8",

  0x41: "i32.const",

  0x6a: "i32.add",
  0x6b: "i32.sub",
  0x6c: "i32.mul",

  0x45: "i32.eqz",
  0x46: "i32.eq",
  0x47: "i32.ne",
  0x48: "i32.lt_s",
  0x49: "i32.lt_u",
  0x4a: "i32.gt_s",
  0x4b: "i32.gt_u",
  0x4c: "i32.le_s",
  0x4d: "i32.le_u",
  0x4e: "i32.ge_s",
  0x4f: "i32.ge_u",

  0x10: "call",
  0x11: "call_indirect"
};

function decodeOne(pos) {
  const start = pos;
  const op = wasm[pos++];

  if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
    const x = readULEB(pos);
    return {
      start,
      end: x.next,
      op,
      text: `${names[op]} ${x.value}`,
      local: x.value
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x41) {
    const x = readSLEB(pos);
    return {
      start,
      end: x.next,
      op,
      text: `i32.const ${x.value}`,
      value: x.value
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x10) {
    const x = readULEB(pos);
    return {
      start,
      end: x.next,
      op,
      text: `call ${x.value}`,
      func: x.value
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x11) {
    const t = readULEB(pos);
    const table = readULEB(t.next);

    return {
      start,
      end: table.next,
      op,
      text: `call_indirect type=${t.value} table=${table.value}`,
      type: t.value
    };
  }

  if (
    op === 0x28 ||
    op === 0x2d ||
    op === 0x36 ||
    op === 0x3a
  ) {
    const align = readULEB(pos);
    const offset = readULEB(align.next);

    return {
      start,
      end: offset.next,
      op,
      text:
        `${names[op]} align=${align.value} offset=${offset.value}`,
      offset: offset.value
    };
  }

  return {
    start,
    end: pos,
    op,
    text: names[op] || `opcode=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
  };
}

/*
 * Decode function.
 */
let code = [];
p = body.bodyStart;

/*
 * Locals declaration.
 */
const localDecl = readULEB(p);
p = localDecl.next;

const localGroups = [];

for (let i = 0; i < localDecl.value; i++) {
  const count = readULEB(p);
  p = count.next;

  const type = wasm[p++];

  localGroups.push({
    count: count.value,
    type
  });
}

while (p < body.bodyEnd) {
  const ins = decodeOne(p);
  code.push(ins);
  p = ins.end;

  if (ins.op === 0x0b) break;
}

console.log("============================================================");

console.log("BODY END  :", hex(body.bodyEnd));
console.log("BODY BYTES:", body.bodyEnd - body.bodyStart);
console.log("IMPORTED FUNCTIONS:", importedCount);
console.log("LOCAL DECL GROUPS:", JSON.stringify(localGroups));
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

/*

console.log("LOCAL5 EVENTS");

let eventCount = 0;

for (const ins of code) {

 * by local.set / local.tee. We print a wider neighborhood around
 * those operations.

  const ins = code[i];

  if (
    (ins.op === 0x21 || ins.op === 0x22) &&
    ins.local === 5

    local5Assignments.push(i);

      console.log(
        marker,

        code[j].text

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function leb(pos: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, p = pos;
  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
    s += 7;
  }
}

function readTypeSection() {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const id = wasm[p++];
    const r = leb(p);
    p = r.next;

    if (id === 1) {
      const end = p + r.value;
      const count = leb(p);
      p = count.next;

      const types: any[] = [];

      for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
        const form = wasm[p++];

        const pc = leb(p);
        p = pc.next;

        const params = [];
        for (let j = 0; j < pc.value; j++) params.push(wasm[p++]);

        const rc = leb(p);
        p = rc.next;

        const results = [];
        for (let j = 0; j < rc.value; j++) results.push(wasm[p++]);

        types.push({ params, results });
      }

      return types;
    }

    p += r.value;
  }

  return [];
}

const types = readTypeSection();

let p = 8;
let codeStart = 0;

while (p < wasm.length) {
  const id = wasm[p++];
  const r = leb(p);
  p = r.next;

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = p;
    break;
  }

  p += r.value;
}

let q = codeStart;
const count = leb(q);
q = count.next;

for (let func = 0; func <= 76; func++) {
  const br = leb(q);
  const bodySize = br.value;
  q = br.next;

  const bodyEnd = q + bodySize;

  if (func === 76) {
    console.log("FUNC 76 TYPE + CALL TARGET");
    console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("============================================================");

    // Function section maps defined-function index -> type index.
    let fp = 8;
    let funcTypeIndex = -1;

    while (fp < wasm.length) {
      const id = wasm[fp++];
      const r = leb(fp);
      fp = r.next;

      if (id === 3) {
        const end = fp + r.value;
        const fc = leb(fp);
        fp = fc.next;

        for (let i = 0; i <= 76; i++) {
          const tr = leb(fp);
          fp = tr.next;
          if (i === 76) {
            funcTypeIndex = tr.value;
            break;
          }
        }
        break;
      }

      fp += r.value;
    }

    console.log(`TYPE INDEX : ${funcTypeIndex}`);

    if (funcTypeIndex >= 0 && types[funcTypeIndex]) {
      const t = types[funcTypeIndex];
      console.log(`PARAMS     : ${t.params.map((x: number) =>
        x === 0x7f ? "i32" :
        x === 0x7e ? "i64" :
        x === 0x7d ? "f32" :
        x === 0x7c ? "f64" : "0x" + x.toString(16)
      ).join(", ")}`);
      console.log(`RESULTS    : ${t.results.map((x: number) =>
        x === 0x7f ? "i32" :
        x === 0x7e ? "i64" :
        x === 0x7d ? "f32" :
        x === 0x7c ? "f64" : "0x" + x.toString(16)
      ).join(", ")}`);
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("CALLS:");
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    for (let x = q; x < bodyEnd; x++) {
      if (wasm[x] === 0x10) {
        const r = leb(x + 1);
        console.log(
          `CALL @ 0x${x.toString(16).padStart(6, "0")} -> FUNC ${r.value}`
        );
      }
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("DONE");
    break;
  }

  q = bodyEnd;
}

    v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };

    const b = wasm[p++];
    v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
    s += 7;
  }
}

let p = 8;
let codeStart = 0;

while (p < wasm.length) {
  const id = wasm[p++];
  const r = leb(p);
  p = r.next;

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = p;
    break;
  }
  p += r.value;
}

let q = codeStart;
const count = leb(q);
q = count.next;

for (let func = 0; func <= 76; func++) {
  const br = leb(q);
  const bodySize = br.value;
  q = br.next;
  const bodyEnd = q + bodySize;

  if (func === 76) {
    const localStart = q;
    const lc = leb(q);
    q = lc.next;

    console.log("FUNC 76 LOCALS");
    console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(`BODY SIZE       : ${bodySize}`);
    console.log(`LOCAL GROUPS    : ${lc.value}`);

    for (let i = 0; i < lc.value; i++) {
      const n = leb(q);
      q = n.next;
      const type = wasm[q++];

      const name =
        type === 0x7f ? "i32" :
        type === 0x7e ? "i64" :
        type === 0x7d ? "f32" :
        type === 0x7c ? "f64" :
        `0x${type.toString(16)}`;

      console.log(`GROUP ${i}: COUNT=${n.value} TYPE=${name}`);
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log(`CODE START : 0x${q.toString(16)}`);
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("DONE");
    break;
  }

  q = bodyEnd;
}

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function leb(pos: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, p = pos;
  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
    s += 7;
  }
}

let p = 8;

  const r = leb(p);
  p = r.next;

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = p;
    break;
  }
  p += r.value;
}

q = count.next;

  const bodySize = br.value;
  q = br.next;
  const bodyEnd = q + bodySize;

  if (func === 76) {
    console.log("FUNC 76 -> FUNC 504 INPUT");
    console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("============================================================");

    for (let x = q; x < bodyEnd; x++) {
      if (wasm[x] === 0x10) {
        const r = leb(x + 1);

        if (r.value === 504) {
          console.log(`CALL 504 @ 0x${x.toString(16).padStart(6, "0")}`);
          console.log("PRE-CALL BYTES:");

          const start = Math.max(q, x - 24);
          for (let y = start; y < x; y++) {
            console.log(
              `0x${y.toString(16).padStart(6, "0")}  0x${wasm[y].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
            );
          }
        }
      }
    }

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("DONE");
    break;
  }

  q = bodyEnd;
}

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const IMPORTED = 83;
const TARGET = 810;

function uleb(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { value: v, next: p };
    s += 7;
  }
}

function readSections() {
  let p = 8;
  const s = {};

  while (p < b.length) {
    const id = b[p++];

    const z = uleb(p);
    const size = z.value;
    p = z.next;

    s[id] = {
      start: p,
      end: p + size
    };

    p += size;
  }

  return s;
}

function getBodies() {

  const sec = readSections();

  const fs3 = sec[3];
  const cs = sec[10];

  let p = fs3.start;
  const n = uleb(p);
  p = n.next;

  const types = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
    const x = uleb(p);
    p = x.next;
    types.push(x.value);
  }

  p = cs.start;
  const c = uleb(p);
  p = c.next;

  const bodies = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < c.value; i++) {

    const z = uleb(p);
    const size = z.value;
    p = z.next;

    bodies.push({
      func: IMPORTED + i,
      type: types[i],
      start: p,
      end: p + size
    });

    p += size;
  }

  return bodies;
}

const bodies = getBodies();

FILE: scripts\traceFunc813_1079Stores.ts

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET_FUNCS = [813, 1079];

function u32(p: number): number {
    return (
        wasm[p] |
        (wasm[p + 1] << 8) |
        (wasm[p + 2] << 16) |
        (wasm[p + 3] << 24)
    ) >>> 0;
}

function uleb(p: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[p++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if (!(b & 0x80))
            return { value: value >>> 0, next: p };

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function sleb(p: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = wasm[p++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if (!(b & 0x80))
            break;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
        value |= (~0 << shift);

    return { value: value | 0, next: p };
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Find code section
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let p = 8;
let importedFunctions = 0;

const bodies = new Map<number, { start: number; end: number }>();

while (p < wasm.length) {
    const sectionId = wasm[p++];

    const s = uleb(p);
    const size = s.value;
    p = s.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + size;

    // Import section
    if (sectionId === 2) {
        let q = p;

        const c = uleb(q);
        q = c.next;

        for (let i = 0; i < c.value; i++) {
            const ml = uleb(q);
            q = ml.next + ml.value;

            const nl = uleb(q);
            q = nl.next + nl.value;

            const kind = wasm[q++];

            if (kind === 0) {
                importedFunctions++;

                const t = uleb(q);
                q = t.next;
            } else if (kind === 1) {
                q += 4;
            } else if (kind === 2) {
                const flags = wasm[q++];

                const min = uleb(q);
                q = min.next;

                if (flags & 1) {
                    const max = uleb(q);
                    q = max.next;
                }
            } else if (kind === 3) {
                q += 2;
            }
        }
    }

    // Code section
    if (sectionId === 10) {
        let q = p;

        const c = uleb(q);
        q = c.next;

        for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < c.value; ordinal++) {
            const bs = uleb(q);
            q = bs.next;

            const bodyStart = q;
            const bodyEnd = q + bs.value;

            const globalFunc = importedFunctions + ordinal;

            bodies.set(globalFunc, {
                start: bodyStart,
                end: bodyEnd,
            });

            q = bodyEnd;
        }
    }

    p = end;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Decode only relevant instructions
// ------------------------------------------------------------

type Ins = {
    off: number;
    op: number;
    imm?: number;
    text: string;
};

function decode(body: { start: number; end: number }): Ins[] {
    const out: Ins[] = [];

    let q = body.start;

    // locals
    const lc = uleb(q);
    q = lc.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < lc.value; i++) {
        const n = uleb(q);
        q = n.next;

        q += 1;
    }

    while (q < body.end - 1) {
        const off = q;
        const op = wasm[q++];

        let text = `opcode 0x${op.toString(16)}`;
        let imm: number | undefined;

        if (op === 0x41) {
            const x = sleb(q);
            q = x.next;

            imm = x.value >>> 0;
            text = `i32.const ${imm} (0x${imm.toString(16)})`;
        }
        else if (op === 0x20) {
            const x = uleb(q);
            q = x.next;

            imm = x.value;
            text = `local.get ${imm}`;
        }
        else if (op === 0x21) {
            const x = uleb(q);
            q = x.next;

            imm = x.value;
            text = `local.set ${imm}`;
        }
        else if (op === 0x22) {
            const x = uleb(q);
            q = x.next;

            imm = x.value;
            text = `local.tee ${imm}`;
        }
        else if (op === 0x28) {
            const a = uleb(q);
            q = a.next;

            const o = uleb(q);
            q = o.next;

            imm = o.value;
            text = `i32.load offset=${o.value}`;
        }
        else if (op === 0x36) {
            const a = uleb(q);
            q = a.next;

            const o = uleb(q);
            q = o.next;

            imm = o.value;
            text = `i32.store offset=${o.value}`;
        }
        else if (op === 0x10) {
            const x = uleb(q);
            q = x.next;

            imm = x.value;
            text = `call ${x.value}`;
        }
        else if (op === 0x6a) {
            text = "i32.add";
        }
        else if (op === 0x6b) {
            text = "i32.sub";
        }
        else if (op === 0x71) {
            text = "i32.and";
        }
        else if (op === 0x72) {
            text = "i32.or";
        }
        else if (op === 0x45) {
            text = "i32.eqz";
        }
        else if (op === 0x46) {
            text = "i32.eq";
        }
        else if (op === 0x47) {
            text = "i32.ne";
        }
        else if (op === 0x0b) {
            text = "end";
        }
        else if (op === 0x0f) {
            text = "return";
        }

        out.push({ off, op, imm, text });
    }

    return out;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Analysis
// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log("TRACE FUNC813 / FUNC1079 STORES");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const func of TARGET_FUNCS) {
    const body = bodies.get(func);

    if (!body) {
        console.log(`FUNC ${func}: NOT FOUND`);
        continue;
    }

    const ins = decode(body);

    console.log("");
    console.log(
        `FUNC ${func} @0x${body.start.toString(16)}`
    );

    console.log(
        `BODY = 0x${body.start
            .toString(16)
            .padStart(8, "0")} .. 0x${(body.end - 1)
            .toString(16)
            .padStart(8, "0")}`
    );

    console.log("");

    for (let i = 0; i < ins.length; i++) {
        const x = ins[i];

        if (
            x.text.startsWith("i32.store") ||
            x.text === "i32.add" ||
            x.text === "i32.sub"
        ) {
            const a = Math.max(0, i - 8);
            const b = Math.min(ins.length, i + 5);

            console.log(
                `--- ${x.text} @0x${x.off
                    .toString(16)
                    .padStart(8, "0")} ---`
            );

            for (let j = a; j < b; j++) {
                console.log(
                    `0x${ins[j].off
                        .toString(16)
                        .padStart(8, "0")}  ${ins[j].text}`
                );
            }

            console.log("");
        }
    }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("NO WASM EXECUTION");
