import fs from "fs";

const file =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

const funcs = [821, 822];

function readULEB(p: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };

    s += 7;
  }
}

function readSLEB(p: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;

    if (!(x & 0x80)) {
      if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40))
        v |= (~0 << (s + 7));

      return { value: v | 0, next: p };
    }

    s += 7;
  }
}

function hex(x: number) {
  return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

// Find CODE section
let p = 8;
let codeStart = -1;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];

  const sz = readULEB(p);
  p = sz.next;

  const start = p;
  const end = p + sz.value;

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = start;
    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

if (codeStart < 0)
  throw new Error("CODE section not found");

let q = codeStart;
const count = readULEB(q);
q = count.next;

const importedCount = 83;

const bodies = new Map<number, { start: number; end: number }>();

for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
  const sz = readULEB(q);
  q = sz.next;

  bodies.set(importedCount + i, {
    start: q,
    end: q + sz.value,
  });

  q += sz.value;
}

for (const fn of funcs) {

  const body = bodies.get(fn);

  if (!body) {
    console.log(`FUNC ${fn}: NOT FOUND`);
    continue;
  }

  console.log("\n############################################################");
  console.log(`FUNC ${fn}`);
  console.log("############################################################");
  console.log(`BODY : ${hex(body.start)} .. ${hex(body.end)}`);
  console.log(`SIZE : ${body.end - body.start}`);

  let x = body.start;

  while (x < body.end) {

    const a = x;
    const op = b[x++];

    let s = "";

    switch (op) {

      case 0x00:
        s = "unreachable";
        break;

      case 0x01:
        s = "nop";
        break;

      case 0x02:
        s = "block";
        x++;
        break;

      case 0x03:
        s = "loop";
        x++;
        break;

      case 0x04:
        s = "if";
        x++;
        break;

      case 0x05:
        s = "else";
        break;

      case 0x0b:
        s = "end";
        break;

      case 0x0c: {
        const r = readULEB(x);
        x = r.next;
        s = `br ${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x0d: {
        const r = readULEB(x);
        x = r.next;
        s = `br_if ${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x0f:
        s = "return";
        break;

      case 0x10: {
        const r = readULEB(x);
        x = r.next;
        s = `CALL FUNC ${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x11: {
        const t = readULEB(x);
        x = t.next;

        const table = readULEB(x);
        x = table.next;

        s = `CALL_INDIRECT type=${t.value} table=${table.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x1a:
        s = "drop";
        break;

      case 0x20: {
        const r = readULEB(x);
        x = r.next;
        s = `local.get ${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x21: {
        const r = readULEB(x);
        x = r.next;
        s = `local.set ${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x22: {
        const r = readULEB(x);
        x = r.next;
        s = `local.tee ${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x23: {
        const r = readULEB(x);
        x = r.next;
        s = `global.get ${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x24: {
        const r = readULEB(x);
        x = r.next;
        s = `global.set ${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x28: {
        const a1 = readULEB(x);
        x = a1.next;

        const off = readULEB(x);
        x = off.next;

        s = `i32.load align=${a1.value} offset=${off.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x36: {
        const a1 = readULEB(x);
        x = a1.next;

        const off = readULEB(x);
        x = off.next;

        s = `i32.store align=${a1.value} offset=${off.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x41: {
        const r = readSLEB(x);
        x = r.next;

        s = `i32.const ${r.value} (${hex(r.value >>> 0)})`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x45:
        s = "i32.eqz";
        break;

      case 0x46:
        s = "i32.eq";
        break;

      case 0x47:
        s = "i32.ne";
        break;

      case 0x48:
        s = "i32.lt_s";
        break;

      case 0x49:
        s = "i32.lt_u";
        break;

      case 0x4a:
        s = "i32.gt_s";
        break;

      case 0x4b:
        s = "i32.gt_u";
        break;

      case 0x4c:
        s = "i32.le_s";
        break;

      case 0x4d:
        s = "i32.le_u";
        break;

      case 0x4e:
        s = "i32.ge_s";
        break;

      case 0x4f:
        s = "i32.ge_u";
        break;

      case 0x6a:
        s = "i32.add";
        break;

      case 0x6b:
        s = "i32.sub";
        break;

      case 0x70:
        s = "i32.shl";
        break;

      case 0x71:
        s = "i32.and";
        break;

      case 0x72:
        s = "i32.or";
        break;

      case 0x73:
        s = "i32.xor";
        break;

      case 0x74:
        s = "i32.shl";
        break;

      case 0x75:
        s = "i32.shr_s";
        break;

      case 0x76:
        s = "i32.shr_u";
        break;

      case 0x7c:
        s = "i32.rotl";
        break;

      case 0x7d:
        s = "i32.rotr";
        break;

      default:
        s = `opcode 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
        break;
    }

    console.log(`${hex(a)}  ${s}`);
  }
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceFunc83Calls.mjs

import fs from "fs";

const file = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

const FUNC_INDEX = 83;
const IMPORT_COUNT = 83;

function uleb(p) {
  let v = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return { value: v, next: p };

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function sleb(p) {
  let v = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(x & 0x80)) {
      if (shift < 32 && (x & 0x40))
        v -= 2 ** (shift + 7);
      return { value: v, next: p };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Find Code Section
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let p = 8;
let codePayload = 0;
let codeEnd = 0;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];

  const s = uleb(p);
  const start = s.next;
  const end = start + s.value;

  if (id === 10) {
    codePayload = start;
    codeEnd = end;
    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

if (!codePayload)
  throw new Error("CODE section not found");

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Locate code ordinal = global function - imported functions
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const codeOrdinal = FUNC_INDEX - IMPORT_COUNT;

let q = codePayload;

const functionCount = uleb(q);
q = functionCount.next;

if (codeOrdinal < 0 || codeOrdinal >= functionCount.value)
  throw new Error(`Invalid code ordinal ${codeOrdinal}`);

let bodyStart = 0;
let bodySize = 0;

for (let i = 0; i < functionCount.value; i++) {
  const sz = uleb(q);
  const payloadStart = sz.next;
  const end = payloadStart + sz.value;

  if (i === codeOrdinal) {
    bodyStart = payloadStart;
    bodySize = sz.value;
  }

  q = end;
}

if (!bodyStart)
  throw new Error("FUNC83 body not found");

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Resolve function type from Function Section
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let typeSection = 0;
let typeSectionEnd = 0;

p = 8;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];

  const s = uleb(p);
  const start = s.next;
  const end = start + s.value;

  if (id === 1) {
    typeSection = start;
    typeSectionEnd = end;
    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

if (!typeSection)
  throw new Error("TYPE section not found");

// Function Section
let functionSection = 0;
p = 8;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];

  const s = uleb(p);
  const start = s.next;
  const end = start + s.value;

  if (id === 3) {
    functionSection = start;
    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

if (!functionSection)
  throw new Error("FUNCTION section not found");

let fp = functionSection;

const definedCount = uleb(fp);
fp = definedCount.next;

let funcTypeIndex = null;

for (let i = 0; i < definedCount.value; i++) {
  const x = uleb(fp);
  fp = x.next;

  if (i === codeOrdinal)
    funcTypeIndex = x.value;
}

if (funcTypeIndex === null)
  throw new Error("FUNC83 type index not found");

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Decode type
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let tp = typeSection;

const typeCount = uleb(tp);
tp = typeCount.next;

let params = [];
let results = [];

for (let i = 0; i < typeCount.value; i++) {
  const form = b[tp++];

  if (form !== 0x60)
    throw new Error(`Unexpected type form 0x${form.toString(16)}`);

  const pc = uleb(tp);
  tp = pc.next;

  const ps = [];

  for (let j = 0; j < pc.value; j++)
    ps.push(b[tp++]);

  const rc = uleb(tp);
  tp = rc.next;

  const rs = [];

  for (let j = 0; j < rc.value; j++)
    rs.push(b[tp++]);

  if (i === funcTypeIndex) {
    params = ps;
    results = rs;
  }
}

function vt(x) {
  if (x === 0x7f) return "i32";
  if (x === 0x7e) return "i64";
  if (x === 0x7d) return "f32";
  if (x === 0x7c) return "f64";
  return `0x${x.toString(16)}`;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Scan FUNC83 body
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize;

let r = bodyStart;

// locals declaration vector
const localGroups = uleb(r);
r = localGroups.next;

for (let i = 0; i < localGroups.value; i++) {
  const n = uleb(r);
  r = n.next;
  r++; // value type
}

const calls = [];
const indirects = [];

while (r < bodyEnd) {
  const at = r;
  const op = b[r++];

  // call
  if (op === 0x10) {
    const x = uleb(r);
    r = x.next;

    calls.push({
      at,
      func: x.value
    });

    continue;
  }

  // call_indirect
  if (op === 0x11) {
    const type = uleb(r);
    r = type.next;

    const table = uleb(r);
    r = table.next;

    indirects.push({
      at,
      type: type.value,
      table: table.value
    });

    continue;
  }

  // Skip immediates for common instructions.
  if (
    op === 0x41 || // i32.const
    op === 0x42    // i64.const
  ) {
    r = sleb(r).next;
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x43) {
    r += 4;
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x44) {
    r += 8;
    continue;
  }

  if (
    op === 0x20 || // local.get
    op === 0x21 || // local.set
    op === 0x22 || // local.tee
    op === 0x23 || // global.get
    op === 0x24    // global.set
  ) {
    r = uleb(r).next;
    continue;
  }

  if (
    (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x3e)
  ) {
    r = uleb(r).next;
    r = uleb(r).next;
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
    r = uleb(r).next;
    continue;
  }

  // block / loop / if
  if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
    r++;
    continue;
  }

  // br_table
  if (op === 0x0e) {
    const n = uleb(r);
    r = n.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < n.value + 1; i++)
      r = uleb(r).next;

    continue;
  }

  // memory.size / memory.grow
  if (op === 0x3f || op === 0x40) {
    r++;
    continue;
  }

  // 0xfc prefixed instructions
  if (op === 0xfc) {
    const sub = uleb(r);
    r = sub.next;

    // memory.copy / memory.fill etc. may have extra immediates.
    if (sub.value === 8 || sub.value === 9)
      r += 2;

    continue;
  }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 83 CALL TRACE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`BODY        : 0x${bodyStart.toString(16)} .. 0x${bodyEnd.toString(16)}`);
console.log(`BODY SIZE   : ${bodySize}`);
console.log(`TYPE INDEX  : ${funcTypeIndex}`);
console.log(
  `SIGNATURE   : (${params.map(vt).join(",")}) -> (${results.map(vt).join(",")})`
);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

console.log("DIRECT CALLS:");

if (calls.length === 0) {
  console.log("  NONE");
} else {
  for (const x of calls)
    console.log(
      `  0x${x.at.toString(16)} -> FUNC ${x.func}`
    );
}

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

console.log("CALL_INDIRECT:");

if (indirects.length === 0) {
  console.log("  NONE");
} else {
  for (const x of indirects)
    console.log(
      `  0x${x.at.toString(16)} -> TYPE[${x.type}] TABLE[${x.table}]`
    );
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceFunc83EffectiveAddress.mjs
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const FILE = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const START = 0x6e81;
const END   = 0x74a6;

const TARGET_START = 0x195348;
const TARGET_END   = 0x195360;

const b = fs.readFileSync(FILE);

function readULEB(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[pos++];

    value += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if ((x & 0x80) === 0)
      return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function hx(v, n = 8) {
  return "0x" + (v >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(n, "0");
}

function raw(a, z) {
  return [...b.subarray(a, z)]
    .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
    .join(" ");
}

/*
 * Lightweight symbolic stack.
 *
 * We only resolve addresses when the address expression is
 * unambiguous:
 *
 *   i32.const X
 *   local.get L
 *   i32.add
 *
 * or
 *
 *   i32.const X
 *   i32.load offset=Y
 *
 * Complex expressions remain UNKNOWN.
 */

const stack = [];
const locals = new Map();

function push(x) {
  stack.push(x);
}

function pop() {
  return stack.length ? stack.pop() : { type: "unknown" };
}

function unknown() {
  return { type: "unknown" };
}

function symbolicConst(v) {
  return {
    type: "const",
    value: v >>> 0
  };
}

function symbolicLocal(n) {
  if (locals.has(n))
    return locals.get(n);

  return {
    type: "local",
    index: n
  };
}

function add(a, c) {
  if (a?.type === "const" && c?.type === "const") {
    return symbolicConst((a.value + c.value) >>> 0);
  }

  if (a?.type === "const" && c?.type === "local") {
    return {
      type: "local_plus",
      index: c.index,
      base: a.value >>> 0
    };
  }

  if (a?.type === "local" && c?.type === "const") {
    return {
      type: "local_plus",
      index: a.index,
      base: c.value >>> 0
    };
  }

  return unknown();
}

function sub(a, c) {
  if (a?.type === "const" && c?.type === "const") {
    return symbolicConst((a.value - c.value) >>> 0);
  }

  return unknown();
}

function describe(x) {
  if (!x)
    return "UNKNOWN";

  if (x.type === "const")
    return hx(x.value);

  if (x.type === "local")
    return `LOCAL[${x.index}]`;

  if (x.type === "local_plus")
    return `LOCAL[${x.index}] + ${hx(x.base)}`;

  return "UNKNOWN";
}

function isTarget(v) {
  return v >= TARGET_START && v <= TARGET_END;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 83 EFFECTIVE ADDRESS TRACE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`BODY : ${hx(START)} .. ${hx(END)}`);
console.log(`TARGET: ${hx(TARGET_START)} .. ${hx(TARGET_END)}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let p = START;

/*
 * Function body begins with local declaration vector.
 */
const localGroups = readULEB(p);
p = localGroups.next;

for (let i = 0; i < localGroups.value; i++) {
  const count = readULEB(p);
  p = count.next;

  const type = b[p++];

  // Initialize locals as unknown.
  // Number is not needed for the symbolic pass.
}

let instructionCount = 0;

while (p < END) {
  const at = p;
  const op = b[p++];

  instructionCount++;

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // local.get
  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  if (op === 0x20) {
    const r = readULEB(p);
    p = r.next;

    push(symbolicLocal(r.value));
    continue;
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // local.set
  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  if (op === 0x21) {
    const r = readULEB(p);
    p = r.next;

    const v = pop();
    locals.set(r.value, v);

    continue;
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // local.tee
  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  if (op === 0x22) {
    const r = readULEB(p);
    p = r.next;

    const v = stack.length
      ? stack[stack.length - 1]
      : unknown();

    locals.set(r.value, v);

    continue;
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // global.get
  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  if (op === 0x23) {
    const r = readULEB(p);
    p = r.next;

    push({
      type: "global",
      index: r.value
    });

    continue;
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // global.set
  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  if (op === 0x24) {
    const r = readULEB(p);
    p = r.next;

    pop();
    continue;
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // i32.const
  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  if (op === 0x41) {
    const r = readULEB(p);
    p = r.next;

    push(symbolicConst(r.value));
    continue;
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // i32.add
  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  if (op === 0x6a) {
    const rhs = pop();
    const lhs = pop();

    push(add(lhs, rhs));
    continue;
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // i32.sub
  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  if (op === 0x6b) {
    const rhs = pop();
    const lhs = pop();

    push(sub(lhs, rhs));
    continue;
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // i32.or
  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  if (op === 0x72) {
    pop();
    pop();
    push(unknown());
    continue;
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // i32.xor
  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  if (op === 0x73) {
    pop();
    pop();
    push(unknown());
    continue;
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // i32.and
  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  if (op === 0x71) {
    pop();
    pop();
    push(unknown());
    continue;
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // comparisons
  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  if (op >= 0x45 && op <= 0x4f) {
    pop();
    pop();
    push(unknown());
    continue;
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // i32.load
  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  if (op === 0x28) {
    const align = readULEB(p);
    p = align.next;

    const off = readULEB(p);
    p = off.next;

    const base = pop();

    let effective = null;

    if (base?.type === "const") {
      effective = (base.value + off.value) >>> 0;
    }

    console.log(
      `LOAD  ${hx(at)}  ` +
      `raw=[${raw(at, p)}]  ` +
      `base=${describe(base)}  ` +
      `offset=${off.value}  ` +
      `EA=${effective === null ? "UNKNOWN" : hx(effective)}`
    );

    if (effective !== null && isTarget(effective)) {
      console.log(
        `       *** TARGET HIT: ${hx(effective)} ***`
      );
    }

    push(unknown());
    continue;
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // i32.store
  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  if (op === 0x36) {
    const align = readULEB(p);
    p = align.next;

    const off = readULEB(p);
    p = off.next;

    const value = pop();
    const base = pop();

    let effective = null;

    if (base?.type === "const") {
      effective = (base.value + off.value) >>> 0;
    }

    console.log(
      `STORE ${hx(at)}  ` +
      `raw=[${raw(at, p)}]  ` +
      `base=${describe(base)}  ` +
      `offset=${off.value}  ` +
      `EA=${effective === null ? "UNKNOWN" : hx(effective)}`
    );

    if (effective !== null && isTarget(effective)) {
      console.log(
        `       *** TARGET HIT: ${hx(effective)} ***`
      );
    }

    continue;
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // call
  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  if (op === 0x10) {
    const r = readULEB(p);
    p = r.next;

    push(unknown());
    continue;
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // call_indirect
  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  if (op === 0x11) {
    const type = readULEB(p);
    p = type.next;

    const table = readULEB(p);
    p = table.next;

    push(unknown());
    continue;
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // br / br_if
  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
    const r = readULEB(p);
    p = r.next;
    continue;
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // block / loop / if
  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
    p++;
    continue;
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // drop
  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  if (op === 0x1a) {
    pop();
    continue;
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // select
  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  if (op === 0x1b) {
    const c = pop();
    const b2 = pop();
    const a = pop();

    push(unknown());
    continue;
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // return
  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  if (op === 0x0f) {
    if (stack.length)
      pop();

    continue;
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // end
  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  if (op === 0x0b) {
    continue;
  }

  /*
   * For instructions we don't model, clear the symbolic
   * stack rather than making a false claim.
   */
  if (
    op !== 0x00 &&
    op !== 0x01
  ) {
    // Preserve locals, invalidate transient stack.
    stack.length = 0;
  }
}

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log(`INSTRUCTIONS SCANNED : ${instructionCount}`);
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceFunc83MemoryRaw.mjs

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const START = 0x6e81;
const END   = 0x74a6;

function uleb(p) {
  let v = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function hex(v, n = 8) {
  return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(n, "0");
}

function bytes(a, z) {
  return [...b.subarray(a, z)]
    .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
    .join(" ");
}

let p = START;

// Skip locals
const groups = uleb(p);
p = groups.next;

for (let i = 0; i < groups.value; i++) {
  const n = uleb(p);
  p = n.next;
  p++;
}

const hits = [];

while (p < END) {
  const at = p;
  const op = b[p++];

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // memory load
  // 0x28 = i32.load
  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  if (op === 0x28) {
    const a = uleb(p);
    p = a.next;

    const o = uleb(p);
    p = o.next;

    hits.push({
      at,
      kind: "LOAD",
      op,
      align: a.value,
      offset: o.value,
      rawEnd: p
    });

    continue;
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // i64.load etc.
  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  if (op >= 0x29 && op <= 0x35) {
    const a = uleb(p);
    p = a.next;

    const o = uleb(p);
    p = o.next;

    hits.push({
      at,
      kind: "LOAD",
      op,
      align: a.value,
      offset: o.value,
      rawEnd: p
    });

    continue;
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // stores
  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  if (op >= 0x36 && op <= 0x3e) {
    const a = uleb(p);
    p = a.next;

    const o = uleb(p);
    p = o.next;

    hits.push({
      at,
      kind: "STORE",
      op,
      align: a.value,
      offset: o.value,
      rawEnd: p
    });

    continue;
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // i32.const
  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  if (op === 0x41) {
    const x = uleb(p);
    p = x.next;
    continue;
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // i64.const
  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  if (op === 0x42) {
    const x = uleb(p);
    p = x.next;
    continue;
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // local/global
  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  if (
    op === 0x20 ||
    op === 0x21 ||
    op === 0x22 ||
    op === 0x23 ||
    op === 0x24
  ) {
    const x = uleb(p);
    p = x.next;
    continue;
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // br / br_if
  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
    const x = uleb(p);
    p = x.next;
    continue;
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // br_table
  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  if (op === 0x0e) {
    const n = uleb(p);
    p = n.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < n.value + 1; i++)
      p = uleb(p).next;

    continue;
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // block / loop / if
  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
    p++;
    continue;
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // call / call_indirect
  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  if (op === 0x10) {
    p = uleb(p).next;
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x11) {
    p = uleb(p).next;
    p = uleb(p).next;
    continue;
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // memory.size / memory.grow
  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  if (op === 0x3f || op === 0x40) {
    p++;
    continue;
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // select
  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  if (op === 0x1c) {
    const n = uleb(p);
    p = n.next;
    p += n.value;
    continue;
  }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 83 MEMORY RAW TRACE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`BODY : ${hex(START)} .. ${hex(END)}`);
console.log(`LOAD/STORE COUNT : ${hits.length}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (const h of hits) {
  const rawStart = Math.max(START, h.at - 8);
  const rawEnd = Math.min(END, h.rawEnd + 4);

  console.log(
    `${h.kind.padEnd(5)} ${hex(h.at)}` +
    ` opcode=0x${h.op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}` +
    ` align=${h.align}` +
    ` offset=${h.offset}`
  );

  console.log(
    `       RAW: ${bytes(rawStart, rawEnd)}`
  );

  if (
    h.offset >= 0x195000 ||
    h.offset <= 0x400
  ) {
    console.log(
      `       *** INTERESTING OFFSET ${hex(h.offset)} ***`
    );
  }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceFunc84.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const wasmPath = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const targetFunc = 84;

const b = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

function u32(p: number): number {
  return (
    b[p] |
    (b[p + 1] << 8) |
    (b[p + 2] << 16) |
    (b[p + 3] << 24)
  ) >>> 0;
}

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[pos++];
    value |= (x & 0x7f) << shift;
    if ((x & 0x80) === 0) break;
    shift += 7;
  }

  return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };
}

function readSLEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let byte = 0;

  while (true) {
    byte = b[pos++];
    value |= (byte & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;
    if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (byte & 0x40)) {
    value |= (~0 << shift);
  }

  return { value: value | 0, next: pos };
}

function sectionStart(id: number): { start: number; end: number } {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < b.length) {
    const sid = b[p++];
    const sz = readULEB(p);
    p = sz.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + sz.value;

    if (sid === id) return { start, end };
    p = end;
  }

  throw new Error(`section ${id} not found`);
}

function getCodeBody(funcIndex: number): {
  bodyStart: number;
  bodyEnd: number;
  typeIndex: number;
} {
  // Function section = 3
  const fs = sectionStart(3);
  let p = fs.start;

  const count = readULEB(p);
  p = count.next;

  const types: number[] = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const x = readULEB(p);
    p = x.next;
    types.push(x.value);
  }

  // Code section = 10
  const cs = sectionStart(10);
  p = cs.start;

  const cc = readULEB(p);
  p = cc.next;

  // func 84 is after imported functions.
  // Parse import section to determine defined-function ordinal.
  let importedFunctions = 0;

  try {
    const is = sectionStart(2);
    let q = is.start;

    const ic = readULEB(q);
    q = ic.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < ic.value; i++) {
      const ml = readULEB(q);
      q = ml.next + ml.value;

      const nl = readULEB(q);
      q = nl.next + nl.value;

      const kind = b[q++];

      if (kind === 0) {
        importedFunctions++;
        const t = readULEB(q);
        q = t.next;
      } else if (kind === 1) {
        q += 4;
        const flags = b[q - 1];
        if (flags & 1) {
          const x = readULEB(q);
          q = x.next;
        }
      } else if (kind === 2) {
        const flags = b[q + 1];
        q += 2;
        const x = readULEB(q);
        q = x.next;
        if (flags & 1) {
          const y = readULEB(q);
          q = y.next;
        }
      } else if (kind === 3) {
        q++;
        const x = readULEB(q);
        q = x.next;
      }
    }
  } catch {}

  const ordinal = funcIndex - importedFunctions;

  if (ordinal < 0 || ordinal >= cc.value) {
    throw new Error(
      `FUNC ${funcIndex} invalid: imported=${importedFunctions}, codeCount=${cc.value}`
    );
  }

  for (let i = 0; i < cc.value; i++) {
    const sz = readULEB(p);
    p = sz.next;

    const bodyStart = p;
    const bodyEnd = p + sz.value;

    if (i === ordinal) {
      return {
        bodyStart,
        bodyEnd,
        typeIndex: types[ordinal],
      };
    }

    p = bodyEnd;
  }

  throw new Error("body not found");
}

const { bodyStart, bodyEnd, typeIndex } = getCodeBody(targetFunc);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 84 EXACT TRACE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`BODY : 0x${bodyStart.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} .. 0x${bodyEnd.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`);
console.log(`SIZE : ${bodyEnd - bodyStart}`);
console.log(`TYPE : ${typeIndex}`);
console.log("============================================================");

let p = bodyStart;

// locals
const localDecl = readULEB(p);
p = localDecl.next;

const localTypes: number[] = [];

for (let i = 0; i < localDecl.value; i++) {
  const n = readULEB(p);
  p = n.next;

  const vt = b[p++];
  for (let j = 0; j < n.value; j++) {
    localTypes.push(vt);
  }
}

function hex(n: number): string {
  return "0x" + (n >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function readMemArg(pos: number): {
  next: number;
  align: number;
  offset: number;
} {
  const a = readULEB(pos);
  const o = readULEB(a.next);

  return {
    next: o.next,
    align: a.value,
    offset: o.value,
  };
}

const names: Record<number, string> = {
  0x00: "unreachable",
  0x01: "nop",
  0x02: "block",
  0x03: "loop",
  0x04: "if",
  0x05: "else",
  0x0b: "end",
  0x0c: "br",
  0x0d: "br_if",
  0x0e: "br_table",
  0x0f: "return",

  0x10: "CALL",
  0x11: "CALL_INDIRECT",

  0x1a: "drop",
  0x1b: "select",

  0x20: "local.get",
  0x21: "local.set",
  0x22: "local.tee",

  0x23: "global.get",

  0x28: "i32.load",

  0x2d: "i32.load8_u",
  0x36: "i32.store",

  0x76: "i32.shr_u",

function blockTypeName(x: number): string {
  const m: Record<number, string> = {
    0x40: "void",
    0x7f: "i32",
    0x7e: "i64",
    0x7d: "f32",
    0x7c: "f64",
  };
  return m[x] ?? hex(x);
}

while (p < bodyEnd) {
  const addr = p;
  const op = b[p++];

  if (op === 0x10) {
    const x = readULEB(p);
    p = x.next;
    console.log(`0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  CALL FUNC ${x.value}`);
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x11) {
    const x = readULEB(p);
    p = x.next;

    const table = b[p++];

    console.log(
      `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  CALL_INDIRECT type=${x.value} table=${table}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  if (
    op === 0x28 ||
    op === 0x29 ||
    op === 0x2a ||
    op === 0x2b ||
    op === 0x2c ||
    op === 0x2d ||
    op === 0x2e ||
    op === 0x2f ||
    op === 0x36 ||
    op === 0x37 ||
    op === 0x38 ||
    op === 0x39 ||
    op === 0x3a ||
    op === 0x3b
  ) {
    const m = readMemArg(p);
    p = m.next;

    console.log(
      `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${names[op] ?? "MEM"} align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
    const bt = b[p++];
    console.log(
      `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${names[op]} type=${blockTypeName(bt)}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
    const x = readULEB(p);
    p = x.next;

    console.log(
      `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${names[op]} ${x.value}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x0e) {
    const n = readULEB(p);
    p = n.next;

    const labels: number[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
      const x = readULEB(p);
      p = x.next;
      labels.push(x.value);
    }

    const def = readULEB(p);
    p = def.next;

    console.log(
      `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  br_table [${labels.join(",")}] default=${def.value}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
    const x = readULEB(p);
    p = x.next;

    console.log(
      `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${names[op]} ${x.value}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x23 || op === 0x24) {
    const x = readULEB(p);
    p = x.next;

    console.log(
      `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${names[op]} ${x.value}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x41) {
    const x = readSLEB(p);
    p = x.next;

    console.log(
      `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  i32.const ${x.value} (${hex(x.value)})`
    );
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x42) {
    const x = readSLEB(p);
    p = x.next;

    console.log(
      `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  i64.const ${x.value}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  console.log(
    `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${names[op] ?? `opcode 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`}`
  );
}

console.log("DONE");

import fs from "fs";

const file =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

const TARGET = 85;

function readULEB(p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];

    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };

    s += 7;
  }
}

function readSLEB(p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];

    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;

    if (!(x & 0x80)) {
      if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40))
        v |= (~0 << (s + 7));

      return { value: v | 0, next: p };
    }

    s += 7;
  }
}

function hex(x: number) {
  return "0x" + (x >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

let p = 8;
let codeStart = -1;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];

  const sz = readULEB(p);
  p = sz.next;

  const end = p + sz.value;

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = p;
    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

if (codeStart < 0)
  throw new Error("CODE section not found");

let q = codeStart;

const count = readULEB(q);
q = count.next;

const importedCount = 83;

let bodyStart = -1;
let bodyEnd = -1;

for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {

  const sz = readULEB(q);
  q = sz.next;

  const fn = importedCount + i;

  if (fn === TARGET) {
    bodyStart = q;
    bodyEnd = q + sz.value;
    break;
  }

  q += sz.value;
}

if (bodyStart < 0)
  throw new Error("FUNC85 not found");

console.log(`SIZE : ${bodyEnd - bodyStart}`);
console.log("============================================================");

let x = bodyStart;

while (x < bodyEnd) {

  const addr = x;
  const op = b[x++];

  let s = "";

  switch (op) {

    case 0x00:
      s = "unreachable";
      break;

    case 0x01:
      s = "nop";
      break;

    case 0x02:
      s = "block";
      x++;
      break;

    case 0x03:
      s = "loop";
      x++;
      break;

    case 0x04:
      s = "if";
      x++;
      break;

    case 0x05:
      s = "else";
      break;

    case 0x0b:
      s = "end";
      break;

    case 0x0c: {
      const r = readULEB(x);
      x = r.next;
      s = `br ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x0d: {
      const r = readULEB(x);
      x = r.next;
      s = `br_if ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x0f:
      s = "return";
      break;

    case 0x10: {
      const r = readULEB(x);
      x = r.next;
      s = `CALL FUNC ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x20: {
      const r = readULEB(x);
      x = r.next;
      s = `local.get ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x21: {
      const r = readULEB(x);
      x = r.next;
      s = `local.set ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x22: {
      const r = readULEB(x);
      x = r.next;
      s = `local.tee ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x23: {
      const r = readULEB(x);
      x = r.next;
      s = `global.get ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x24: {
      const r = readULEB(x);
      x = r.next;
      s = `global.set ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x28: {
      const a = readULEB(x);
      x = a.next;

      const off = readULEB(x);
      x = off.next;

      s = `i32.load align=${a.value} offset=${off.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x36: {
      const a = readULEB(x);
      x = a.next;

      const off = readULEB(x);
      x = off.next;

      s = `i32.store align=${a.value} offset=${off.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x41: {
      const r = readSLEB(x);
      x = r.next;
      s = `i32.const ${r.value} (${hex(r.value)})`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x42: {
      const r = readSLEB(x);
      x = r.next;
      s = `i64.const ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x45:
      s = "i32.eqz";
      break;

    case 0x46:
      s = "i32.eq";
      break;

    case 0x47:
      s = "i32.ne";
      break;

    case 0x48:
      s = "i32.lt_s";
      break;

    case 0x49:
      s = "i32.lt_u";
      break;

    case 0x4a:
      s = "i32.gt_s";
      break;

    case 0x4b:
      s = "i32.gt_u";
      break;

    case 0x4c:
      s = "i32.le_s";
      break;

    case 0x4d:
      s = "i32.le_u";
      break;

    case 0x4e:
      s = "i32.ge_s";
      break;

    case 0x4f:
      s = "i32.ge_u";
      break;

    case 0x6a:
      s = "i32.add";
      break;

    case 0x6b:
      s = "i32.sub";
      break;

    case 0x71:
      s = "i32.and";
      break;

    case 0x72:
      s = "i32.or";
      break;

    case 0x73:
      s = "i32.xor";
      break;

    default:
      s = `opcode 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
      break;
  }

  console.log(`${hex(addr)}  ${s}`);
}

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm"
);

const TARGET = 86;

function uleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x;
  do {
    x = wasm[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;
  } while (x & 0x80);
  return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
}

function sleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x = 0;
  do {
    x = wasm[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;
  } while (x & 0x80);

  if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40)) v |= (~0 << s);

  return { value: v | 0, next: p };
}

function section(id: number) {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const sid = wasm[p++];
    const z = uleb(p);
    p = z.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + z.value;

    if (sid === id) return { start, end };

    p = end;
  }

  throw new Error(`Section ${id} not found`);
}

function importedFunctionCount() {
  const s = section(2);
  let p = s.start;

  const c = uleb(p);
  p = c.next;

  let n = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < c.value; i++) {
    const ml = uleb(p);
    p = ml.next + ml.value;

    const nl = uleb(p);
    p = nl.next + nl.value;

    const kind = wasm[p++];

    if (kind === 0) {
      n++;
      const t = uleb(p);
      p = t.next;
    } else if (kind === 1) {
      p += 4;
      if (wasm[p - 1] & 1) {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;
      }
    } else if (kind === 2) {
      const flags = wasm[p + 1];
      p += 2;

      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      if (flags & 1) {
        const y = uleb(p);
        p = y.next;
      }
    } else if (kind === 3) {
      p++;
      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;
    }
  }

  return n;
}

function getBody(funcIndex: number) {
  const imported = importedFunctionCount();
  const ordinal = funcIndex - imported;

  const fs = section(3);
  let p = fs.start;

  const fc = uleb(p);
  p = fc.next;

  const types: number[] = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < fc.value; i++) {
    const x = uleb(p);
    p = x.next;
    types.push(x.value);
  }

  const cs = section(10);
  p = cs.start;

  const cc = uleb(p);
  p = cc.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < cc.value; i++) {
    const z = uleb(p);
    p = z.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + z.value;

    if (i === ordinal) {
      return {
        start,
        end,
        type: types[ordinal]
      };
    }

    p = end;
  }

  throw new Error("FUNC body not found");
}

const body = getBody(TARGET);

    .padStart(8, "0")}`
);

console.log(`TYPE : ${body.type}`);

  p = n.next;
  p++;
}

function memarg(pos: number) {
  const a = uleb(pos);
  const o = uleb(a.next);
  return {
    next: o.next,
    align: a.value,
    offset: o.value
  };
}

  if (op === 0x41) {
    const x = sleb(p);
    p = x.next;

    console.log(
      `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  i32.const ${x.value} (0x${(
        x.value >>> 0
      )
        .toString(16)
        .padStart(8, "0")})`
    );
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x42) {
    const x = sleb(p);
    p = x.next;

    console.log(
      `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  i64.const ${x.value}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  console.log(
    `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${
      names[op] ?? `opcode 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
    }`
  );
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function leb(pos: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, p = pos;
  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
    s += 7;
  }
}

let p = 8;
let codeStart = 0;

while (p < wasm.length) {
  const id = wasm[p++];
  const r = leb(p);
  p = r.next;

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = p;
    break;
  }

  p += r.value;
}

let q = codeStart;
const count = leb(q);
q = count.next;

for (let func = 0; func <= 87; func++) {
  const br = leb(q);
  const bodySize = br.value;
  q = br.next;

  const bodyEnd = q + bodySize;

  if (func !== 87) {
    q = bodyEnd;
    continue;
  }

  console.log("FUNC 87 EXACT BODY");
  console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(`BODY SIZE : ${bodySize}`);
  console.log("");

  for (let x = q; x < bodyEnd; x++) {
    console.log(
      `0x${x.toString(16).padStart(6, "0")}  0x${wasm[x].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
    );
  }

  console.log("");
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log("DONE");
  break;
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceFunc87Inputs.ts

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const START = 0x000000;
const TARGET = 87;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 87 - TYPE / CALLER INPUT TRACE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

// Find all direct calls to function 87 in the whole WASM.
for (let p = 0; p < wasm.length; p++) {
  if (wasm[p] !== 0x10) continue;

  let q = p + 1;
  let func = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (q < wasm.length) {
    const b = wasm[q++];
    func |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
    shift += 7;
    if (shift > 35) break;
  }

  if ((func >>> 0) !== TARGET) continue;

  const from = Math.max(0, p - 40);
  const to = Math.min(wasm.length, q + 4);

  const raw = [...wasm.subarray(from, to)]
    .map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
    .join(" ");

  console.log("");
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
  console.log(`CALL SITE : 0x${p.toString(16)}`);
  console.log(`RAW       : ${raw}`);
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function leb(pos: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, p = pos;
  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
    s += 7;
  }
}

let p = 8;
let codeStart = 0;

while (p < wasm.length) {
  const id = wasm[p++];
  const r = leb(p);
  p = r.next;

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = p;
    break;
  }

  p += r.value;
}

let q = codeStart;
const count = leb(q);
q = count.next;

for (let func = 0; func <= 87; func++) {
  const br = leb(q);
  const bodySize = br.value;
  q = br.next;
  const bodyEnd = q + bodySize;

  if (func === 87) {
    console.log("FUNC 87 -> FUNC 76");
    console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("============================================================");

    for (let x = q; x < bodyEnd; x++) {
      if (wasm[x] === 0x10) {
        const r = leb(x + 1);

        if (r.value === 76) {
          console.log(`CALL 76 @ 0x${x.toString(16).padStart(6, "0")}`);
          console.log("");
          console.log("BYTES BEFORE CALL:");

          const start = Math.max(q, x - 40);

          for (let y = start; y < x; y++) {
            console.log(
              `0x${y.toString(16).padStart(6, "0")}  0x${wasm[y].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
            );
          }
        }
      }
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("DONE");
    break;
  }

  q = bodyEnd;
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceFunc9106Fields.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const START = 0x317300;
const END   = 0x317700;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 9106 STRUCTURE FIELD FLOW");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`SCAN = 0x${START.toString(16)} .. 0x${END.toString(16)}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("TARGET STRUCTURE:");
console.log("  base = local3");
console.log("  important fields:");
console.log("    +0x1c");
console.log("    +0x30");
console.log("    +0x34");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

function u32leb(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (p < buf.length) {
    const b = buf[p++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
    shift += 7;
  }

  return { value: value >>> 0, next: p };
}

function s32leb(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (p < buf.length) {
    b = buf[p++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
    shift += 7;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    value |= (~0 << shift);
  }

  return { value: value | 0, next: p };
}

function hx(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function out(pos: number, bytes: string, text: string) {
  console.log(`${hx(pos)}  ${bytes.padEnd(18)} ${text}`);
}

function fieldName(offset: number) {
  switch (offset) {
    case 0x1c: return "FIELD +0x1C";
    case 0x30: return "FIELD +0x30";
    case 0x34: return "FIELD +0x34";
    default: return `FIELD +${hx(offset)}`;
  }
}

let p = START;

while (p < END) {
  const pos = p;
  const op = buf[p++];

  // local.get
  if (op === 0x20) {
    const r = u32leb(p);
    p = r.next;

    out(
      pos,
      `20 ${r.value.toString(16)}`,
      `LOCAL.GET ${r.value}${r.value === 3 ? "   <-- STRUCT BASE" : ""}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  // local.set
  if (op === 0x21) {
    const r = u32leb(p);
    p = r.next;

    out(pos, `21 ${r.value.toString(16)}`, `LOCAL.SET ${r.value}`);
    continue;
  }

  // local.tee
  if (op === 0x22) {
    const r = u32leb(p);
    p = r.next;

    out(pos, `22 ${r.value.toString(16)}`, `LOCAL.TEE ${r.value}`);
    continue;
  }

  // global.get
  if (op === 0x23) {
    const r = u32leb(p);
    p = r.next;

    out(pos, `23 ${r.value.toString(16)}`, `GLOBAL.GET ${r.value}`);
    continue;
  }

  // global.set
  if (op === 0x24) {
    const r = u32leb(p);
    p = r.next;

    out(pos, `24 ${r.value.toString(16)}`, `GLOBAL.SET ${r.value}`);
    continue;
  }

  // i32.const
  if (op === 0x41) {
    const r = s32leb(p);
    p = r.next;

    out(
      pos,
      `41 ${(r.value >>> 0).toString(16)}`,
      `I32.CONST ${r.value < 0 ? r.value : hx(r.value >>> 0)}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  // i32.load
  if (op === 0x28) {
    const a = u32leb(p);
    p = a.next;

    const o = u32leb(p);
    p = o.next;

    const note =
      o.value === 0x1c ||
      o.value === 0x30 ||
      o.value === 0x34
        ? `   <-- ${fieldName(o.value)} READ`
        : "";

    out(
      pos,
      `28 ${a.value.toString(16)} ${o.value.toString(16)}`,
      `I32.LOAD align=${a.value} offset=${hx(o.value)}${note}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  // i32.store
  if (op === 0x36) {
    const a = u32leb(p);
    p = a.next;

    const o = u32leb(p);
    p = o.next;

    const note =
      o.value === 0x1c ||
      o.value === 0x30 ||
      o.value === 0x34
        ? `   <-- ${fieldName(o.value)} WRITE`
        : "";

    out(
      pos,
      `36 ${a.value.toString(16)} ${o.value.toString(16)}`,
      `I32.STORE align=${a.value} offset=${hx(o.value)}${note}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  // i32.load8_u
  if (op === 0x2d) {
    const a = u32leb(p);
    p = a.next;

    const o = u32leb(p);
    p = o.next;

    out(
      pos,
      `2d ${a.value.toString(16)} ${o.value.toString(16)}`,
      `I32.LOAD8_U align=${a.value} offset=${hx(o.value)}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  // i32.load16_u
  if (op === 0x2f) {
    const a = u32leb(p);
    p = a.next;

    const o = u32leb(p);
    p = o.next;

    out(
      pos,
      `2f ${a.value.toString(16)} ${o.value.toString(16)}`,
      `I32.LOAD16_U align=${a.value} offset=${hx(o.value)}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  // i32.store8
  if (op === 0x3a) {
    const a = u32leb(p);
    p = a.next;

    const o = u32leb(p);
    p = o.next;

    out(
      pos,
      `3a ${a.value.toString(16)} ${o.value.toString(16)}`,
      `I32.STORE8 align=${a.value} offset=${hx(o.value)}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  // i32.add
  if (op === 0x6a) {
    out(pos, "6a", "I32.ADD");
    continue;
  }

  // i32.sub
  if (op === 0x6b) {
    out(pos, "6b", "I32.SUB");
    continue;
  }

  // i32.and
  if (op === 0x71) {
    out(pos, "71", "I32.AND");
    continue;
  }

  // i32.or
  if (op === 0x72) {
    out(pos, "72", "I32.OR");
    continue;
  }

  // i32.xor
  if (op === 0x73) {
    out(pos, "73", "I32.XOR");
    continue;
  }

  // i32.shl
  if (op === 0x74) {
    out(pos, "74", "I32.SHL");
    continue;
  }

  // i32.shr_u
  if (op === 0x76) {
    out(pos, "76", "I32.SHR_U");
    continue;
  }

  // i32.eqz
  if (op === 0x45) {
    out(pos, "45", "I32.EQZ");
    continue;
  }

  // i32.eq
  if (op === 0x46) {
    out(pos, "46", "I32.EQ");
    continue;
  }

  // i32.ne
  if (op === 0x47) {
    out(pos, "47", "I32.NE");
    continue;
  }

  // i32.lt_u
  if (op === 0x49) {
    out(pos, "49", "I32.LT_U");
    continue;
  }

  // i32.gt_u
  if (op === 0x4b) {
    out(pos, "4b", "I32.GT_U");
    continue;
  }

  // block
  if (op === 0x02) {
    const blockType = buf[p++];

    out(
      pos,
      `02 ${blockType.toString(16)}`,
      `BLOCK type=0x${blockType.toString(16)}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  // loop
  if (op === 0x03) {
    const blockType = buf[p++];

    out(
      pos,
      `03 ${blockType.toString(16)}`,
      `LOOP type=0x${blockType.toString(16)}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  // if
  if (op === 0x04) {
    const blockType = buf[p++];

    out(
      pos,
      `04 ${blockType.toString(16)}`,
      `IF type=0x${blockType.toString(16)}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  // else
  if (op === 0x05) {
    out(pos, "05", "ELSE");
    continue;
  }

  // end
  if (op === 0x0b) {
    out(pos, "0b", "END");
    continue;
  }

  // br
  if (op === 0x0c) {
    const r = u32leb(p);
    p = r.next;

    out(pos, `0c ${r.value.toString(16)}`, `BR ${r.value}`);
    continue;
  }

  // br_if
  if (op === 0x0d) {
    const r = u32leb(p);
    p = r.next;

    out(pos, `0d ${r.value.toString(16)}`, `BR_IF ${r.value}`);
    continue;
  }

  // call
  if (op === 0x10) {
    const r = u32leb(p);
    p = r.next;

    out(pos, `10 ${r.value.toString(16)}`, `CALL FUNC${r.value}`);
    continue;
  }

  // call_indirect
  if (op === 0x11) {
    const t = u32leb(p);
    p = t.next;

    const table = u32leb(p);
    p = table.next;

    out(
      pos,
      `11 ${t.value.toString(16)} ${table.value.toString(16)}`,
      `CALL_INDIRECT TYPE${t.value} TABLE${table.value}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  // drop
  if (op === 0x1a) {
    out(pos, "1a", "DROP");
    continue;
  }

  // select
  if (op === 0x1b) {
    out(pos, "1b", "SELECT");
    continue;
  }

  // return
  if (op === 0x0f) {
    out(pos, "0f", "RETURN");
    continue;
  }

  // memory.size / memory.grow
  if (op === 0x3f) {
    const x = buf[p++];
    out(pos, `3f ${x.toString(16)}`, "MEMORY.SIZE");
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x40) {
    const x = buf[p++];
    out(pos, `40 ${x.toString(16)}`, "MEMORY.GROW");
    continue;
  }

  out(
    pos,
    op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"),
    `OPCODE 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
  );
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END");

console.log("  This is a static/offline field-flow scan.");
console.log("  No WASM execution.");
console.log("  No transaction.");
console.log("  No semantic field names are assumed.");

import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const FUNC_START = 0x31740f;
const FUNC_END   = 0x317520;

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 9106 FRAME / local5 DATAFLOW");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`RANGE = 0x${FUNC_START.toString(16)} .. 0x${FUNC_END.toString(16)}`);
console.log("TARGET:");
console.log("  local5 = global.get0 - 0x90");
console.log("  local3 = FUNC265(local5 + 4)");
console.log("  local1 = MEM[local3]");
console.log("  FUNC113(local1, 0x10)");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

function u32leb(pos: number) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (p < buf.length) {
    const b = buf[p++];
    result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
    shift += 7;
  }

  return { value: result >>> 0, next: p };
}

function s32leb(pos: number) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (p < buf.length) {
    b = buf[p++];
    result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    result |= (~0 << shift);
  }

  return { value: result | 0, next: p };
}

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function print(pos: number, bytes: string, text: string) {
  console.log(`${hex(pos)}  ${bytes.padEnd(16)} ${text}`);
}

let p = FUNC_START;

while (p < FUNC_END) {
  const start = p;
  const op = buf[p++];

  // local.get
  if (op === 0x20) {
    const r = u32leb(p);
    p = r.next;

    const local = r.value;

    if (local === 5) {
      print(start, "20 05", "LOCAL.GET 5   <-- FRAME POINTER");
    } else if (local === 3) {
      print(start, "20 03", "LOCAL.GET 3");
    } else if (local === 1) {
      print(start, "20 01", "LOCAL.GET 1");
    } else if (local === 0) {
      print(start, "20 00", "LOCAL.GET 0");
    } else {
      print(start, `20 ${local.toString(16)}`, `LOCAL.GET ${local}`);
    }

    continue;
  }

  // local.set
  if (op === 0x21) {
    const r = u32leb(p);
    p = r.next;

    const local = r.value;

    print(
      start,
      `21 ${local.toString(16)}`,
      `LOCAL.SET ${local}${local === 5 ? "   <-- FRAME POINTER" : ""}`
    );

    continue;
  }

  // local.tee
  if (op === 0x22) {
    const r = u32leb(p);
    p = r.next;

    const local = r.value;

    print(
      start,
      `22 ${local.toString(16)}`,
      `LOCAL.TEE ${local}${local === 5 ? "   <-- FRAME POINTER" : ""}`
    );

    continue;
  }

  // global.get
  if (op === 0x23) {
    const r = u32leb(p);
    p = r.next;

    print(start, `23 ${r.value.toString(16)}`, `GLOBAL.GET ${r.value}`);
    continue;
  }

  // global.set
  if (op === 0x24) {
    const r = u32leb(p);
    p = r.next;

    print(start, `24 ${r.value.toString(16)}`, `GLOBAL.SET ${r.value}`);
    continue;
  }

  // i32.const
  if (op === 0x41) {
    const r = s32leb(p);
    p = r.next;

    print(
      start,
      `41 ${r.value.toString(16)}`,
      `I32.CONST ${r.value < 0 ? r.value : hex(r.value >>> 0)}`
    );

    continue;
  }

  // i32.add
  if (op === 0x6a) {
    print(start, "6a", "I32.ADD");
    continue;
  }

  // i32.sub
  if (op === 0x6b) {
    print(start, "6b", "I32.SUB");
    continue;
  }

  // i32.load
  if (op === 0x28) {
    const align = u32leb(p);
    p = align.next;

    const offset = u32leb(p);
    p = offset.next;

    print(
      start,
      `28 ${align.value.toString(16)} ${offset.value.toString(16)}`,
      `I32.LOAD align=${align.value} offset=${hex(offset.value)}`
    );

    continue;
  }

  // i32.store
  if (op === 0x36) {
    const align = u32leb(p);
    p = align.next;

    const offset = u32leb(p);
    p = offset.next;

    print(
      start,
      `36 ${align.value.toString(16)} ${offset.value.toString(16)}`,
      `I32.STORE align=${align.value} offset=${hex(offset.value)}`
    );

    continue;
  }

  // call
  if (op === 0x10) {
    const r = u32leb(p);
    p = r.next;

    print(start, `10 ${r.value.toString(16)}`, `CALL FUNC${r.value}`);

    continue;
  }

  // call_indirect
  if (op === 0x11) {
    const type = u32leb(p);
    p = type.next;

    const table = u32leb(p);
    p = table.next;

    print(
      start,
      `11 ${type.value.toString(16)} ${table.value.toString(16)}`,
      `CALL_INDIRECT TYPE${type.value} TABLE${table.value}`
    );

    continue;
  }

  // i32.load8_u
  if (op === 0x2d) {
    const align = u32leb(p);
    p = align.next;

    const offset = u32leb(p);
    p = offset.next;

    print(
      start,
      `2d ${align.value.toString(16)} ${offset.value.toString(16)}`,
      `I32.LOAD8_U align=${align.value} offset=${hex(offset.value)}`
    );

    continue;
  }

  // i32.load16_u
  if (op === 0x2f) {
    const align = u32leb(p);
    p = align.next;

    const offset = u32leb(p);
    p = offset.next;

    print(
      start,
      `2f ${align.value.toString(16)} ${offset.value.toString(16)}`,
      `I32.LOAD16_U align=${align.value} offset=${hex(offset.value)}`
    );

    continue;
  }

  // drop
  if (op === 0x1a) {
    print(start, "1a", "DROP");
    continue;
  }

  // return
  if (op === 0x0f) {
    print(start, "0f", "RETURN");
    continue;
  }

  // if
  if (op === 0x04) {
    print(start, "04", "IF");
    continue;
  }

  // else
  if (op === 0x05) {
    print(start, "05", "ELSE");
    continue;
  }

  // end
  if (op === 0x0b) {
    print(start, "0b", "END");
    continue;
  }

  // br
  if (op === 0x0c) {
    const r = u32leb(p);
    p = r.next;

    print(start, `0c ${r.value.toString(16)}`, `BR ${r.value}`);
    continue;
  }

  // br_if
  if (op === 0x0d) {
    const r = u32leb(p);
    p = r.next;

    print(start, `0d ${r.value.toString(16)}`, `BR_IF ${r.value}`);
    continue;
  }

  // select
  if (op === 0x1b) {
    print(start, "1b", "SELECT");
    continue;
  }

  // Unknown opcode
  print(
    start,
    op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"),
    `OPCODE 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
  );
}

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("KEY CHECKS");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("1. Search for LOCAL.GET 5 around pointer arithmetic.");
console.log("2. Search for I32.STORE following local5-derived addresses.");
console.log("3. Search for I32.LOAD offset=0 after local3.");
console.log("4. Confirm whether [local5+4] is written inside FUNC9106.");
console.log("5. Do NOT assign semantic names to fields yet.");
console.log("============================================================");

import fs from "fs";

const wasmPath = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const TARGET_FUNC = 91;
const TARGET_TABLE_INDEX = 4790;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

function readULEB(data: Buffer, pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = data[pos++];

        if (b === undefined) {
            throw new Error("Unexpected EOF while reading ULEB");
        }

        value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error("ULEB too large");
        }
    }
}

function readSLEB(data: Buffer, pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let byte = 0;

    while (true) {
        byte = data[pos++];

        if (byte === undefined) {
            throw new Error("Unexpected EOF while reading SLEB");
        }

        value |= (byte & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) {
            if (shift < 32 && (byte & 0x40)) {
                value |= -(1 << shift);
            }
            return { value, next: pos };
        }

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error("SLEB too large");
        }
    }
}

function hexByte(b: number): string {
    return b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0");
}

function hexAt(pos: number, count: number): string {
    const out: string[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < count && pos + i < wasm.length; i++) {
        out.push(hexByte(wasm[pos + i]));
    }

    return out.join(" ");
}

function sectionName(id: number): string {
    switch (id) {
        case 0: return "CUSTOM";
        case 1: return "TYPE";
        case 2: return "IMPORT";
        case 3: return "FUNCTION";
        case 4: return "TABLE";
        case 5: return "MEMORY";
        case 6: return "GLOBAL";
        case 7: return "EXPORT";
        case 8: return "START";
        case 9: return "ELEMENT";
        case 10: return "CODE";
        case 11: return "DATA";
        default: return `SECTION_${id}`;
    }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TRACE FUNC 91 CALLERS / TABLE DISPATCH");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TARGET FUNC       = ${TARGET_FUNC}`);
console.log(`TARGET TABLE IDX  = ${TARGET_TABLE_INDEX}`);
console.log();

let p = 8;

let importedFuncCount = 0;
let typeSectionStart = -1;
let elementSectionStart = -1;
let elementSectionEnd = -1;
let codeSectionStart = -1;
let codeSectionEnd = -1;

while (p < wasm.length) {
    const sectionId = wasm[p++];

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(wasm, p);
    const size = sizeInfo.value;
    p = sizeInfo.next;

    const payloadStart = p;
    const payloadEnd = p + size;

    console.log(
        `SECTION ${sectionId} ${sectionName(sectionId)} ` +
        `0x${payloadStart.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} .. ` +
        `0x${payloadEnd.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} SIZE=${size}`
    );

    if (sectionId === 1) {
        typeSectionStart = payloadStart;
    }

    if (sectionId === 2) {
        let q = payloadStart;

        const countInfo = readULEB(wasm, q);
        const importCount = countInfo.value;
        q = countInfo.next;

        for (let i = 0; i < importCount; i++) {
            const moduleLen = readULEB(wasm, q);
            q = moduleLen.next + moduleLen.value;

            const nameLen = readULEB(wasm, q);
            q = nameLen.next + nameLen.value;

            const kind = wasm[q++];

            if (kind === 0) {
                const typeInfo = readULEB(wasm, q);
                q = typeInfo.next;
                importedFuncCount++;
            } else if (kind === 1) {
                q += 1;

                const flags = wasm[q++];

                const minInfo = readULEB(wasm, q);
                q = minInfo.next;

                if (flags & 1) {
                    const maxInfo = readULEB(wasm, q);
                    q = maxInfo.next;
                }
            } else if (kind === 2) {
                const flags = wasm[q++];

                const minInfo = readULEB(wasm, q);
                q = minInfo.next;

                if (flags & 1) {
                    const maxInfo = readULEB(wasm, q);
                    q = maxInfo.next;
                }
            } else if (kind === 3) {
                q += 2;
            }
        }
    }

    if (sectionId === 9) {
        elementSectionStart = payloadStart;
        elementSectionEnd = payloadEnd;
    }

    if (sectionId === 10) {
        codeSectionStart = payloadStart;
        codeSectionEnd = payloadEnd;
    }

    p = payloadEnd;
}

console.log();
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("IMPORT SUMMARY");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log(`IMPORTED FUNCTIONS = ${importedFuncCount}`);
console.log();

//
// ELEMENT SECTION
//
if (elementSectionStart < 0) {
    throw new Error("ELEMENT section not found");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("ELEMENT TABLE SCAN");
console.log("============================================================");

let e = elementSectionStart;

const elemCountInfo = readULEB(wasm, e);
const elemCount = elemCountInfo.value;
e = elemCountInfo.next;

console.log(`ELEMENT SEGMENT COUNT = ${elemCount}`);
console.log();

let targetFound = false;

for (let seg = 0; seg < elemCount; seg++) {
    const flagsInfo = readULEB(wasm, e);
    const flags = flagsInfo.value;
    e = flagsInfo.next;

    console.log(`ELEMENT SEGMENT #${seg} FLAGS=0x${flags.toString(16)}`);

    //
    // Handle common active element segment forms.
    //
    if (flags === 0) {
        // table index 0, offset expression, function indices
        const opcode = wasm[e++];

        if (opcode !== 0x41) {
            throw new Error(`Unexpected element offset opcode 0x${hexByte(opcode)}`);
        }

        const offsetInfo = readSLEB(wasm, e);
        const offset = offsetInfo.value;
        e = offsetInfo.next;

        if (wasm[e++] !== 0x0b) {
            throw new Error("Missing end in element offset expression");
        }

        const countInfo = readULEB(wasm, e);
        const count = countInfo.value;
        e = countInfo.next;

        console.log(`  OFFSET = ${offset}`);
        console.log(`  COUNT  = ${count}`);

        for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            const fInfo = readULEB(wasm, e);
            const f = fInfo.value;
            e = fInfo.next;

            const tableIndex = offset + i;

            if (f === TARGET_FUNC || tableIndex === TARGET_TABLE_INDEX) {
                console.log(
                    `  MATCH table[${tableIndex}] = FUNC ${f} ` +
                    `(0x${f.toString(16)})`
                );

                if (f === TARGET_FUNC && tableIndex === TARGET_TABLE_INDEX) {
                    targetFound = true;
                }
            }
        }
    } else {
        console.log(
            `  UNSUPPORTED/COMPLEX ELEMENT FLAGS = 0x${flags.toString(16)}`
        );

        // Stop rather than guessing the binary layout.
        throw new Error(
            `Element segment ${seg} uses flags 0x${flags.toString(16)}. ` +
            `Need dedicated decoder before continuing.`
        );
    }

    console.log();
}

console.log("TARGET TABLE/FUNC MATCH FOUND =", targetFound);
console.log();

//
// CODE SECTION
//
if (codeSectionStart < 0) {
    throw new Error("CODE section not found");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("CALL_INDIRECT SCAN");
console.log("============================================================");

let c = codeSectionStart;

const bodyCountInfo = readULEB(wasm, c);
const bodyCount = bodyCountInfo.value;
c = bodyCountInfo.next;

console.log(`DEFINED FUNCTION BODIES = ${bodyCount}`);
console.log(`IMPORTED FUNCTIONS     = ${importedFuncCount}`);
console.log();

let indirectCount = 0;
let nearbyCount = 0;

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < bodyCount; ordinal++) {
    const sizeInfo = readULEB(wasm, c);
    const bodySize = sizeInfo.value;
    const bodyStart = sizeInfo.next;
    const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize;

    const globalFunc = importedFuncCount + ordinal;

    let i = bodyStart;

    while (i < bodyEnd) {
        const op = wasm[i];

        if (op === 0x11) {
            indirectCount++;

            console.log(
                `call_indirect @0x${i.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} ` +
                `IN FUNC ${globalFunc} (ordinal ${ordinal})`
            );

            console.log(`  RAW = ${hexAt(i, Math.min(8, bodyEnd - i))}`);

            //
            // MVP call_indirect encoding:
            // 0x11 typeidx 0x00
            //
            const typeInfo = readULEB(wasm, i + 1);
            const typeIndex = typeInfo.value;

            console.log(`  TYPE INDEX = ${typeIndex}`);

            let afterType = typeInfo.next;

            if (afterType < bodyEnd) {
                console.log(
                    `  TABLE IMM = 0x${hexByte(wasm[afterType])}`
                );
            }

            console.log();

            nearbyCount++;
        }

        //
        // We intentionally do not attempt a full WASM decoder here.
        // Advance conservatively for known immediate-bearing opcodes
        // that occur commonly in this module.
        //
        if (
            op === 0x20 || // local.get
            op === 0x21 || // local.set
            op === 0x22 || // local.tee
            op === 0x23 || // global.get
            op === 0x24 || // global.set
            op === 0x0c || // br
            op === 0x0d || // br_if
            op === 0x10    // call
        ) {
            const x = readULEB(wasm, i + 1);
            i = x.next;
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x11) {
            const x = readULEB(wasm, i + 1);
            i = x.next + 1;
            continue;
        }

        if (
            op === 0x28 || // i32.load
            op === 0x29 || // i64.load
            op === 0x2a ||
            op === 0x2b ||
            op === 0x2c ||
            op === 0x2d ||
            op === 0x2e ||
            op === 0x2f ||
            op === 0x30 ||
            op === 0x31 ||
            op === 0x32 ||
            op === 0x33 ||
            op === 0x34 ||
            op === 0x35 ||
            op === 0x36 || // store
            op === 0x37 ||
            op === 0x38 ||
            op === 0x39 ||
            op === 0x3a ||
            op === 0x3b ||
            op === 0x3c ||
            op === 0x3d ||
            op === 0x3e
        ) {
            const a = readULEB(wasm, i + 1);
            const b = readULEB(wasm, a.next);
            i = b.next;
            continue;
        }

        i++;
    }

    c = bodyEnd;
}

console.log("END");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceFunction10.js
