const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET = 10;

const bytes = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

function uleb(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = bytes[pos++];

    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;

    shift += 7;

    if (shift > 35) {
      throw new Error("ULEB too long");
    }
  }

  return {
    value: value >>> 0,
    next: pos
  };
}

function sleb(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b;

  while (true) {
    b = bytes[pos++];

    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    shift += 7;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;

    if (shift > 35) {
      throw new Error("SLEB too long");
    }
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    value |= (~0 << shift);
  }

  return {
    value: value | 0,
    next: pos
  };
}

function hex(n) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function readSections() {
  let p = 8;

  const sections = [];

  while (p < bytes.length) {
    const id = bytes[p++];

    const len = uleb(p);
    p = len.next;

    sections.push({
      id,
      start: p,
      end: p + len.value
    });

    p += len.value;
  }

  return sections;
}

function parseImports(section) {
  let p = section.start;

  const count = uleb(p);
  p = count.next;

  let functions = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {

    const mod = uleb(p);
    p = mod.next + mod.value;

    const name = uleb(p);
    p = name.next + name.value;

    const kind = bytes[p++];

    if (kind === 0) {
      const type = uleb(p);
      p = type.next;
      functions++;
    }

    else if (kind === 1) {
      p++;

      const flags = bytes[p++];

      const min = uleb(p);
      p = min.next;

      if (flags & 1) {
        const max = uleb(p);
        p = max.next;
      }
    }

    else if (kind === 2) {
      const flags = bytes[p++];

      const min = uleb(p);
      p = min.next;

      if (flags & 1) {
        const max = uleb(p);
        p = max.next;
      }
    }

    else if (kind === 3) {
      p += 2;
    }
  }

  return functions;
}

function parseFunctionTypes(section) {
  let p = section.start;

  const count = uleb(p);
  p = count.next;

  const ids = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const x = uleb(p);
    p = x.next;

    ids.push(x.value);
  }

  return ids;
}

function parseCode(section, functionTypes, importedCount) {
  let p = section.start;

  const count = uleb(p);
  p = count.next;

  const funcs = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {

    const size = uleb(p);
    p = size.next;

    const bodyStart = p;
    const bodyEnd = p + size.value;

    funcs.push({
      funcIndex: importedCount + i,
      typeIndex: functionTypes[i],
      start: bodyStart,
      end: bodyEnd
    });

    p = bodyEnd;
  }

  return funcs;
}

function decode(func) {

  let p = func.start;

  const localCount = uleb(p);
  p = localCount.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < localCount.value; i++) {

    const n = uleb(p);
    p = n.next;

    p++;
  }

  console.log(`BODY : ${hex(func.start)} .. ${hex(func.end)}`);
  console.log(`TYPE : ${func.typeIndex}`);

  console.log(
    "------------------------------------------------------------"
  );

  while (p < func.end) {

    const at = p;
    const op = bytes[p++];

    let text = null;

    switch (op) {

      case 0x00:
        text = "unreachable";
        break;

      case 0x01:
        text = "nop";
        break;

      case 0x0b:
        text = "end";
        break;

      case 0x0f:
        text = "return";
        break;

      case 0x10: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        text = `call ${x.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x11: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        const y = uleb(p);
        p = y.next;

        text =
          `call_indirect type=${x.value} table=${y.value}`;

        break;
      }

      case 0x1a:
        text = "drop";
        break;

      case 0x1b:
        text = "select";
        break;

      case 0x20: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        text = `local.get ${x.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x21: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        text = `local.set ${x.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x22: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        text = `local.tee ${x.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x23: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        text = `global.get ${x.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x24: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        text = `global.set ${x.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x41: {
        const x = sleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        text =
          `i32.const ${x.value} (${hex(x.value >>> 0)})`;

        break;
      }

      case 0x42: {

        let value = 0n;
        let shift = 0n;

        while (true) {

          const b = BigInt(bytes[p++]);

          value |=
            (b & 0x7fn) << shift;

          if ((b & 0x80n) === 0n) {
            break;
          }

          shift += 7n;
        }

        text = `i64.const ${value}`;

        break;
      }

      case 0x28:
      case 0x29:
      case 0x2c:
      case 0x2d:
      case 0x2e:
      case 0x2f:
      case 0x30:
      case 0x31:
      case 0x32:
      case 0x33:
      case 0x34:
      case 0x35: {

        const align = uleb(p);
        p = align.next;

        const offset = uleb(p);
        p = offset.next;

        const names = {
          0x28: "i32.load",
          0x29: "i64.load",
          0x2c: "i32.load8_s",
          0x2d: "i32.load8_u",
          0x2e: "i32.load16_s",
          0x2f: "i32.load16_u",
          0x30: "i64.load8_s",
          0x31: "i64.load8_u",
          0x32: "i64.load16_s",
          0x33: "i64.load16_u",
          0x34: "i64.load32_s",
          0x35: "i64.load32_u"
        };

        text =
          `${names[op]} ` +
          `align=${align.value} ` +
          `offset=${offset.value}`;

        break;
      }

      case 0x36:
      case 0x37:
      case 0x3a:
      case 0x3b:
      case 0x3c:
      case 0x3d:
      case 0x3e: {

        const align = uleb(p);
        p = align.next;

        const offset = uleb(p);
        p = offset.next;

        const names = {
          0x36: "i32.store",
          0x37: "i64.store",
          0x3a: "i32.store8",
          0x3b: "i32.store16",
          0x3c: "i64.store8",
          0x3d: "i64.store16",
          0x3e: "i64.store32"
        };

        text =
          `${names[op]} ` +
          `align=${align.value} ` +
          `offset=${offset.value}`;

        break;
      }

      case 0x45:
        text = "i32.eqz";
        break;

      case 0x46:
        text = "i32.eq";
        break;

      case 0x47:
        text = "i32.ne";
        break;

      case 0x48:
        text = "i32.lt_s";
        break;

      case 0x49:
        text = "i32.lt_u";
        break;

      case 0x4a:
        text = "i32.gt_s";
        break;

      case 0x4b:
        text = "i32.gt_u";
        break;

      case 0x4c:
        text = "i32.le_s";
        break;

      case 0x4d:
        text = "i32.le_u";
        break;

      case 0x4e:
        text = "i32.ge_s";
        break;

      case 0x4f:
        text = "i32.ge_u";
        break;

      case 0x6a:
        text = "i32.add";
        break;

      case 0x6b:
        text = "i32.sub";
        break;

      case 0x6c:
        text = "i32.mul";
        break;

      case 0x71:
        text = "i32.and";
        break;

      case 0x72:
        text = "i32.or";
        break;

      case 0x73:
        text = "i32.xor";
        break;

      case 0x74:
        text = "i32.shl";
        break;

      case 0x75:
        text = "i32.shr_s";
        break;

      case 0x76:
        text = "i32.shr_u";
        break;

      case 0x77:
        text = "i32.rotl";
        break;

      case 0x78:
        text = "i32.rotr";
        break;

      case 0x02:
      case 0x03:
      case 0x04: {

        const blockType = bytes[p++];

        const name =
          op === 0x02
            ? "block"
            : op === 0x03
              ? "loop"
              : "if";

        text =
          `${name} type=${hex(blockType)}`;

        break;
      }

      case 0x05:
        text = "else";
        break;

      case 0x0c: {

        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        text = `br ${x.value}`;

        break;
      }

      case 0x0d: {

        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        text = `br_if ${x.value}`;

        break;
      }

      case 0x0e: {

        const n = uleb(p);
        p = n.next;

        const labels = [];

        for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {

          const x = uleb(p);
          p = x.next;

          labels.push(x.value);
        }

        const d = uleb(p);
        p = d.next;

        labels.push(`default=${d.value}`);

        text =
          `br_table ${labels.join(",")}`;

        break;
      }

      default:

        console.log(
          `${hex(at)}  UNKNOWN opcode ${hex(op)}`
        );

        console.log(
          "RAW REMAINING : " +
          Buffer.from(
            bytes.subarray(at, func.end)
          ).toString("hex")
        );

        return;
    }

    console.log(
      `${hex(at)}  ${text}`
    );
  }
}

console.log(
  "============================================================"
);

console.log("FUNCTION 10 TRACE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");

console.log(
  "============================================================"
);

console.log(
  `WASM SIZE : ${bytes.length.toLocaleString()}`
);

console.log("");

import fs from "fs";

const WASM_PATH =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const FUNC_START = 0x0001226d;
const FUNC_END   = 0x0001295c;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM_PATH);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 122 DATAFLOW TRACE v3");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log(" SYMBOLIC STACK / LOCAL TRACKING");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");
console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`Function  : 122`);
console.log(
  `Range     : 0x${FUNC_START.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} .. 0x${(
    FUNC_END - 1
  ).toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
);
console.log("");

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: bigint; next: number } {
  let result = 0n;
  let shift = 0n;

  while (true) {
    if (pos >= wasm.length) throw new Error("ULEB beyond WASM");

    const b = wasm[pos++];

    result |= BigInt(b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value: result, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7n;

    if (shift > 70n) throw new Error("ULEB too long");
  }
}

function readSLEB(pos: number): { value: bigint; next: number } {
  let result = 0n;
  let shift = 0n;
  let byte = 0;

  while (true) {
    if (pos >= wasm.length) throw new Error("SLEB beyond WASM");

    byte = wasm[pos++];

    result |= BigInt(byte & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7n;

    if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) break;

    if (shift > 70n) throw new Error("SLEB too long");
  }

  if (shift < 64n && (byte & 0x40)) {
    result |= (-1n) << shift;
  }

  return { value: result, next: pos };
}

function readMemArg(pos: number) {
  const a = readULEB(pos);
  const o = readULEB(a.next);

  return {
    align: Number(a.value),
    offset: Number(o.value),
    next: o.next,
  };
}

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function clean(s: string): string {
  if (s.length <= 180) return s;
  return s.slice(0, 177) + "...";
}

function frameSlot(expr: string): string | null {
  const m = expr.match(/^\(frame \+ (-?\d+)\)$/);

  if (!m) return null;

  const offset = Number(m[1]);

  if (offset >= 0 && offset < 512 && offset % 16 === 0) {
    return `S${offset / 16}`;
  }

  return null;
}

type Value = string;

const stack: Value[] = [];
const locals = new Map<number, Value>();

let frameExpr = "frame";

function pop(): Value {
  if (stack.length === 0) {
    throw new Error("STACK UNDERFLOW");
  }

  return stack.pop()!;
}

function push(v: Value) {
  stack.push(v);
}

function bin(op: string) {
  const b = pop();
  const a = pop();
  push(`(${a} ${op} ${b})`);
}

function unary(op: string) {
  const a = pop();
  push(`${op}(${a})`);
}

function loadExpr(type: string, offset: number) {
  const addr = pop();

  if (type === "i64") {
    push(`MEM64[${addr}${offset ? `+${offset}` : ""}]`);
  } else {
    push(`MEM32[${addr}${offset ? `+${offset}` : ""}]`);
  }
}

function storeExpr(type: string, offset: number) {
  const value = pop();
  const addr = pop();

  const mem =
    type === "i64"
      ? `MEM64[${addr}${offset ? `+${offset}` : ""}]`
      : `MEM32[${addr}${offset ? `+${offset}` : ""}]`;

  console.log(`      STORE ${mem} = ${clean(value)}`);
}

let p = FUNC_START;
let instructionCount = 0;
let call87Count = 0;

const producedSlots: {
  call: number;
  offset: number;
  destination: string;
  slot: string;
  args: string[];
}[] = [];

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" SYMBOLIC TRACE OF CALL 87");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");

while (p < FUNC_END) {
  const offset = p;
  const op = wasm[p++];

  instructionCount++;

  try {
    switch (op) {
      // ------------------------------------------------------
      // CONTROL
      // ------------------------------------------------------

      case 0x02: {
        // block
        p++;
        break;
      }

      case 0x03: {
        // loop
        p++;
        break;
      }

      case 0x04: {
        // if
        pop();
        p++;
        break;
      }

      case 0x05:
        // else
        break;

      case 0x0b:
        // end
        break;

      case 0x0c: {
        // br
        readULEB(p);
        p = readULEB(p).next;
        break;
      }

      case 0x0d: {
        // br_if
        pop();
        const x = readULEB(p);
        p = x.next;
        break;
      }

      case 0x0f:
        // return
        break;

      // ------------------------------------------------------
      // CALL
      // ------------------------------------------------------

      case 0x10: {
        const x = readULEB(p);
        p = x.next;

        const fn = Number(x.value);

        if (fn === 87) {
          call87Count++;

          const arg4 = pop();
          const arg3 = pop();
          const arg2 = pop();
          const arg1 = pop();
          const arg0 = pop();

          const args = [arg0, arg1, arg2, arg3, arg4];

          const slot = frameSlot(arg0);

          const slotName = slot ?? "UNKNOWN";

          producedSlots.push({
            call: call87Count,
            offset,
            destination: arg0,
            slot: slotName,
            args,
          });

          console.log(
            `CALL #${String(call87Count).padStart(2, " ")} @ ${hex(offset)}`
          );

          console.log(`  destination = ${clean(arg0)}`);
          console.log(`  slot        = ${slotName}`);
          console.log(`  arg1        = ${clean(arg1)}`);
          console.log(`  arg2        = ${clean(arg2)}`);
          console.log(`  arg3        = ${clean(arg3)}`);
          console.log(`  arg4        = ${clean(arg4)}`);
          console.log("");
        }

        break;
      }

      // ------------------------------------------------------
      // TABLE
      // ------------------------------------------------------

      case 0x25: {
        pop();
        push("TABLE_VALUE");
        readULEB(p);
        p = readULEB(p).next;
        break;
      }

      // ------------------------------------------------------
      // LOCALS / GLOBALS
      // ------------------------------------------------------

      case 0x20: {
        const x = readULEB(p);
        p = x.next;

        const n = Number(x.value);

        push(locals.get(n) ?? `local${n}`);
        break;
      }

      case 0x21: {
        const x = readULEB(p);
        p = x.next;

        const n = Number(x.value);

        locals.set(n, pop());
        break;
      }

      case 0x22: {
        const x = readULEB(p);
        p = x.next;

        const n = Number(x.value);
        const v = pop();

        locals.set(n, v);
        push(v);
        break;
      }

      case 0x23: {
        const x = readULEB(p);
        p = x.next;

        const n = Number(x.value);

        if (n === 0) {
          push("STACK_PTR");
        } else {
          push(`GLOBAL${n}`);
        }

        break;
      }

      case 0x24: {
        const x = readULEB(p);
        p = x.next;

        const n = Number(x.value);
        const v = pop();

        if (n === 0) {
          push(`SET_GLOBAL0(${v})`);
        }

        break;
      }

      // ------------------------------------------------------
      // MEMORY
      // ------------------------------------------------------

      case 0x28: {
        const m = readMemArg(p);
        p = m.next;

        loadExpr("i32", m.offset);
        break;
      }

      case 0x29: {
        const m = readMemArg(p);
        p = m.next;

        loadExpr("i64", m.offset);
        break;
      }

      case 0x36: {
        const m = readMemArg(p);
        p = m.next;

        storeExpr("i32", m.offset);
        break;
      }

      case 0x37: {
        const m = readMemArg(p);
        p = m.next;

        storeExpr("i64", m.offset);
        break;
      }

      // ------------------------------------------------------
      // CONSTANTS
      // ------------------------------------------------------

      case 0x41: {
        const x = readSLEB(p);
        p = x.next;

        push(`i32(${x.value})`);
        break;
      }

      case 0x42: {
        const x = readSLEB(p);
        p = x.next;

        push(`i64(${x.value})`);
        break;
      }

      // ------------------------------------------------------
      // i32
      // ------------------------------------------------------

      case 0x45:
        unary("i32.eqz");
        break;

      case 0x46:
        bin("i32.eq");
        break;

      case 0x47:
        bin("i32.ne");
        break;

      case 0x48:
        bin("i32.lt_s");
        break;

      case 0x49:
        bin("i32.lt_u");
        break;

      case 0x4a:
        bin("i32.gt_s");
        break;

      case 0x4b:
        bin("i32.gt_u");
        break;

      case 0x4c:
        bin("i32.le_s");
        break;

      case 0x4d:
        bin("i32.le_u");
        break;

      case 0x4e:
        bin("i32.ge_s");
        break;

      case 0x4f:
        bin("i32.ge_u");
        break;

      case 0x6a:
        bin("i32.add");
        break;

      case 0x6b:
        bin("i32.sub");
        break;

      case 0x6c:
        bin("i32.mul");
        break;

      case 0x74:
        bin("i32.shr_s");
        break;

      case 0x75:
        bin("i32.shr_u");
        break;

      // ------------------------------------------------------
      // i64 comparisons
      // ------------------------------------------------------

      case 0x50:
        unary("i64.eqz");
        break;

      case 0x51:
        bin("i64.eq");
        break;

      case 0x52:
        bin("i64.ne");
        break;

      case 0x53:
        bin("i64.lt_s");
        break;

      case 0x54:
        bin("i64.lt_u");
        break;

      case 0x55:
        bin("i64.gt_s");
        break;

      case 0x56:
        bin("i64.gt_u");
        break;

      case 0x57:
        bin("i64.le_s");
        break;

      case 0x58:
        bin("i64.le_u");
        break;

      case 0x59:
        bin("i64.ge_s");
        break;

      case 0x5a:
        bin("i64.ge_u");
        break;

      // ------------------------------------------------------
      // i64 arithmetic
      // ------------------------------------------------------

      case 0x78:
        unary("i64.clz");
        break;

      case 0x79:
        unary("i64.ctz");
        break;

      case 0x7a:
        unary("i64.popcnt");
        break;

      case 0x7b:
        bin("i64.add");
        break;

      case 0x7c:
        bin("i64.sub");
        break;

      case 0x7d:
        bin("i64.mul");
        break;

      case 0x7e:
        bin("i64.div_s");
        break;

      case 0x7f:
        bin("i64.div_u");
        break;

      case 0x80:
        bin("i64.rem_s");
        break;

      case 0x81:
        bin("i64.rem_u");
        break;

      case 0x82:
        bin("i64.and");
        break;

      case 0x83:
        bin("i64.or");
        break;

      case 0x84:
        bin("i64.xor");
        break;

      case 0x85:

      case 0x86:

      case 0x87:

        break;

      case 0x88:
        bin("i64.rotl");
        break;

      case 0x89:
        bin("i64.rotr");
        break;

      // ------------------------------------------------------
      // conversions
      // ------------------------------------------------------

      case 0xa7:
        unary("i32.wrap_i64");
        break;

      case 0xac:
        unary("i64.extend_i32_s");
        break;

      case 0xad:
        unary("i64.extend_i32_u");
        break;

      // ------------------------------------------------------
      // SELECT
      // ------------------------------------------------------

      case 0x1b: {
        const condition = pop();
        const b = pop();
        const a = pop();

        push(`select(${a},${b},${condition})`);
        break;
      }

      // ------------------------------------------------------
      // DROP
      // ------------------------------------------------------

      case 0x1a:
        pop();
        break;

      default:
        throw new Error(
          `UNKNOWN OPCODE 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")} at ${hex(
            offset
          )}`
        );
    }
  } catch (e) {
    console.log("");

    console.log(" TRACE ERROR");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(`Offset : ${hex(offset)}`);
    console.log(
      `Opcode : 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
    );
    console.log(`Stack  : ${stack.length}`);
    console.log(`Error  : ${e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)}`);
    console.log("");

    throw e;
  }

import fs from "fs";

const WASM_PATH =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const START = 0x0001226d;
const END   = 0x0001295c;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM_PATH);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 122 DATAFLOW TRACE v3.1");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log(" SYMBOLIC STACK / LOCAL TRACKING");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");
console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`Range     : 0x${START.toString(16)} .. 0x${(END - 1).toString(16)}`);
console.log("");

function uleb(pos: number) {
  let v = 0n;
  let s = 0n;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];
    v |= BigInt(b & 0x7f) << s;

    if (!(b & 0x80))
      return { value: v, next: pos };

    s += 7n;
  }
}

function sleb(pos: number) {
  let v = 0n;
  let s = 0n;
  let b = 0;

  while (true) {
    b = wasm[pos++];
    v |= BigInt(b & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7n;

    if (!(b & 0x80))
      break;
  }

  if (s < 64n && (b & 0x40))
    v |= (-1n) << s;

  return { value: v, next: pos };
}

function memarg(pos: number) {
  const a = uleb(pos);
  const o = uleb(a.next);

  return {
    align: Number(a.value),
    offset: Number(o.value),
    next: o.next
  };
}

function H(x: number) {
  return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function short(x: string) {
  return x.length > 220 ? x.slice(0, 217) + "..." : x;
}

type V = string;

const stack: V[] = [];
const locals = new Map<number, V>();

function push(x: V) {
  stack.push(x);
}

function pop(): V {
  if (!stack.length)
    return "STACK_UNKNOWN";

  return stack.pop()!;
}

function binary(op: string) {
  const b = pop();
  const a = pop();
  push(`(${a} ${op} ${b})`);
}

function unary(op: string) {
  const a = pop();
  push(`${op}(${a})`);
}

function frameSlot(x: string): string {
  const m = x.match(/^\(frame \+ (-?\d+)\)$/);

  if (!m)
    return "UNKNOWN";

  const n = Number(m[1]);

  if (n >= 0 && n <= 464 && n % 16 === 0)
    return `S${n / 16}`;

  return "UNKNOWN";
}

let p = START;
let count = 0;
let calls = 0;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" CALL 87 DATAFLOW");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");

while (p < END) {
  const at = p;
  const op = wasm[p++];

  count++;

  switch (op) {

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // control
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    case 0x02: {
      // blocktype is signed LEB128 / valtype
      const x = sleb(p);
      p = x.next;
      break;
    }

    case 0x03: {
      const x = sleb(p);
      p = x.next;
      break;
    }

    case 0x04: {
      // condition
      pop();

      const x = sleb(p);
      p = x.next;
      break;
    }

    case 0x05:
      break;

    case 0x0b:
      break;

    case 0x0c: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;
      break;
    }

    case 0x0d: {
      pop();

      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;
      break;
    }

    case 0x0f:
      break;

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // call
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    case 0x10: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      const fn = Number(x.value);

      if (fn === 87) {
        calls++;

        const a4 = pop();
        const a3 = pop();
        const a2 = pop();
        const a1 = pop();
        const a0 = pop();

        console.log(
          `CALL #${String(calls).padStart(2, " ")} @ ${H(at)}`
        );

        console.log(`  destination = ${short(a0)}`);
        console.log(`  slot        = ${frameSlot(a0)}`);
        console.log(`  arg1        = ${short(a1)}`);
        console.log(`  arg2        = ${short(a2)}`);
        console.log(`  arg3        = ${short(a3)}`);
        console.log(`  arg4        = ${short(a4)}`);
        console.log("");
      }

      break;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // locals
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    case 0x20: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      const n = Number(x.value);

      push(locals.get(n) ?? `local${n}`);
      break;
    }

    case 0x21: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      const n = Number(x.value);
      locals.set(n, pop());
      break;
    }

    case 0x22: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      const n = Number(x.value);
      const v = pop();

      locals.set(n, v);
      push(v);
      break;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // globals
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    case 0x23: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      const n = Number(x.value);

      push(n === 0 ? "STACK_PTR" : `GLOBAL${n}`);
      break;
    }

    case 0x24: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      const n = Number(x.value);
      const v = pop();

      push(`SET_GLOBAL${n}(${v})`);
      break;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // table.get
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    case 0x25: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      const index = pop();

      push(`TABLE[${index}]`);
      break;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // memory
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    case 0x28: {
      const m = memarg(p);
      p = m.next;

      const addr = pop();

      push(`MEM32[${addr}${m.offset ? "+" + m.offset : ""}]`);
      break;

      p = m.next;

      push(`MEM64[${addr}${m.offset ? "+" + m.offset : ""}]`);

      p = m.next;

      const value = pop();
      const addr = pop();

      if (addr !== "STACK_UNKNOWN")

          `STORE32 ${short(addr)}${m.offset ? "+" + m.offset : ""} = ${short(value)}`

      p = x.next;

      push(`i32(${x.value})`);
      break;
    }

    case 0x42: {
      const x = sleb(p);

  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

function uleb(pos: number) {
  let v = 0n;
  let s = 0n;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];

    v |= BigInt(b & 0x7f) << s;

    if (!(b & 0x80))
      return { value: v, next: pos };

    s += 7n;
  }
}

function sleb(pos: number) {
  let v = 0n;
  let s = 0n;
  let b = 0;

  while (true) {
    b = wasm[pos++];

    v |= BigInt(b & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7n;

    if (!(b & 0x80))
      break;
  }

  if (s < 64n && (b & 0x40))
    v |= (-1n) << s;

  return { value: v, next: pos };
}

function memarg(pos: number) {
  const a = uleb(pos);
  const o = uleb(a.next);

  return {
    align: Number(a.value),
    offset: Number(o.value),
    next: o.next
  };
}

function H(x: number) {
  return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function short(x: string) {
  return x.length > 220 ? x.slice(0, 217) + "..." : x;
}

type V = string;

const stack: V[] = [];
const locals = new Map<number, V>();

function push(x: V) {
  stack.push(x);
}

function pop(): V {
  if (!stack.length)
    return "STACK_UNKNOWN";

  return stack.pop()!;
}

function binary(op: string) {
  const b = pop();
  const a = pop();

  push(`(${a} ${op} ${b})`);
}

function unary(op: string) {
  const a = pop();

  push(`${op}(${a})`);
}

/*
 * Normalize:
 *
 *   ((STACK_PTR i32.sub i32(512)) i32.add i32(432))
 *
 * into:
 *
 *   FRAME+432
 *
 * where:
 *
 *   FRAME = STACK_PTR-512
 *
 * This is exactly the frame convention used by the
 * existing Function 122 trace.
 */
function resolveFrameSlot(x: string): string {

  const normalized = x.replace(/\s+/g, "");

  const m = normalized.match(
    /^\(\(STACK_PTRi32\.subi32\(512\)\)i32\.addi32\((-?\d+)\)\)$/
  );

  if (m) {
    const offset = Number(m[1]);

    if (offset >= 0 && offset % 16 === 0) {
      return `S${offset / 16}`;
    }

    return `FRAME+${offset}`;
  }

  /*
   * Also recognize the algebraically equivalent form:
   *
   * ((STACK_PTR i32.sub i32(512)) i32.sub i32(64))
   *
   */

  const m2 = normalized.match(
    /^\(\(STACK_PTRi32\.subi32\(512\)\)i32\.subi32\((-?\d+)\)\)$/
  );

  if (m2) {
    const sub = Number(m2[1]);
    const offset = -sub;

    if (offset >= 0 && offset % 16 === 0) {
      return `S${offset / 16}`;
    }

    return `FRAME${offset >= 0 ? "+" : ""}${offset}`;
  }

  /*
   * Simple FRAME form.
   */

  const m3 = normalized.match(
    /^FRAME(?:\+(-?\d+))?$/
  );

  if (m3) {
    const offset = Number(m3[1] ?? "0");

    if (offset >= 0 && offset % 16 === 0)
      return `S${offset / 16}`;

    return `FRAME+${offset}`;
  }

  return "UNKNOWN";
}

let calls = 0;

import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const START = 0x1226d;
const END   = 0x1295b;

function hex(n: number): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function readULEB(buf: Buffer, p: number): {
    value: number;
    next: number;
} {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readSLEB(buf: Buffer, p: number): {
    value: bigint;
    next: number;
} {
    let result = 0n;
    let shift = 0n;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = buf[p++];

        result |= BigInt(b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            break;
        }

        shift += 7n;
    }

    if (shift < 64n && (b & 0x40)) {
        result |= (-1n) << (shift + 7n);
    }

    return {
        value: result,
        next: p
    };
}

type Instr = {
    pc: number;
    op: number;
    name: string;
    a?: number;
    b?: number;
};

function decode(buf: Buffer, p: number): {
    instr: Instr;
    next: number;
} {
    const pc = p;
    const op = buf[p++];

    switch (op) {

        case 0x00:
            return {
                instr: { pc, op, name: "unreachable" },
                next: p
            };

        case 0x01:
            return {
                instr: { pc, op, name: "nop" },
                next: p
            };

        case 0x02: {
            const x = readSLEB(buf, p);
            return {
                instr: { pc, op, name: "block" },
                next: x.next
            };
        }

        case 0x03: {
            const x = readSLEB(buf, p);
            return {
                instr: { pc, op, name: "loop" },
                next: x.next
            };
        }

        case 0x04: {
            const x = readSLEB(buf, p);
            return {
                instr: { pc, op, name: "if" },
                next: x.next
            };
        }

        case 0x05:
            return {
                instr: { pc, op, name: "else" },
                next: p
            };

        case 0x0b:
            return {
                instr: { pc, op, name: "end" },
                next: p
            };

        case 0x0c: {
            const x = readULEB(buf, p);

            return {
                instr: {
                    pc,
                    op,
                    name: "br",
                    a: x.value
                },
                next: x.next
            };
        }

        case 0x0d: {
            const x = readULEB(buf, p);

            return {
                instr: {
                    pc,
                    op,
                    name: "br_if",
                    a: x.value
                },
                next: x.next
            };
        }

        case 0x0f:
            return {
                instr: { pc, op, name: "return" },
                next: p
            };

        case 0x10: {
            const x = readULEB(buf, p);

            return {
                instr: {
                    pc,
                    op,
                    name: "call",
                    a: x.value
                },
                next: x.next
            };
        }

        case 0x20: {
            const x = readULEB(buf, p);

            return {
                instr: {
                    pc,
                    op,
                    name: "local.get",
                    a: x.value
                },
                next: x.next
            };
        }

        case 0x21: {
            const x = readULEB(buf, p);

            return {
                instr: {
                    pc,
                    op,
                    name: "local.set",
                    a: x.value
                },
                next: x.next
            };
        }

        case 0x22: {
            const x = readULEB(buf, p);

            return {
                instr: {
                    pc,
                    op,
                    name: "local.tee",
                    a: x.value
                },
                next: x.next
            };
        }

        case 0x41: {
            const x = readSLEB(buf, p);

            return {
                instr: {
                    pc,
                    op,
                    name: "i32.const"
                },
                next: x.next
            };
        }

        case 0x42: {
            const x = readSLEB(buf, p);

            return {
                instr: {
                    pc,
                    op,
                    name: "i64.const",
                    a: Number(x.value)
                },
                next: x.next
            };
        }

        case 0x28: {
            const a = readULEB(buf, p);
            const b = readULEB(buf, a.next);

            return {
                instr: {
                    pc,
                    op,
                    name: "i32.load",
                    a: a.value,
                    b: b.value
                },
                next: b.next
            };
        }

        case 0x29: {
            const a = readULEB(buf, p);
            const b = readULEB(buf, a.next);

            return {
                instr: {
                    pc,
                    op,
                    name: "i64.load",
                    a: a.value,
                    b: b.value
                },
                next: b.next
            };
        }

        case 0x36: {
            const a = readULEB(buf, p);
            const b = readULEB(buf, a.next);

            return {
                instr: {
                    pc,
                    op,
                    name: "i32.store",
                    a: a.value,
                    b: b.value
                },
                next: b.next
            };
        }

        case 0x37: {
            const a = readULEB(buf, p);
            const b = readULEB(buf, a.next);

            return {
                instr: {
                    pc,
                    op,
                    name: "i64.store",
                    a: a.value,
                    b: b.value
                },
                next: b.next
            };
        }

        case 0x7c: return { instr: { pc, op, name: "i64.add" }, next: p };
        case 0x7d: return { instr: { pc, op, name: "i64.sub" }, next: p };
        case 0x7e: return { instr: { pc, op, name: "i64.mul" }, next: p };
        case 0x7f: return { instr: { pc, op, name: "i64.div_s" }, next: p };
        case 0x80: return { instr: { pc, op, name: "i64.div_u" }, next: p };
        case 0x81: return { instr: { pc, op, name: "i64.rem_s" }, next: p };
        case 0x82: return { instr: { pc, op, name: "i64.rem_u" }, next: p };
        case 0x83: return { instr: { pc, op, name: "i64.and" }, next: p };
        case 0x84: return { instr: { pc, op, name: "i64.or" }, next: p };
        case 0x85: return { instr: { pc, op, name: "i64.xor" }, next: p };
        case 0x86: return { instr: { pc, op, name: "i64.shl" }, next: p };
        case 0x87: return { instr: { pc, op, name: "i64.shr_s" }, next: p };
        case 0x88: return { instr: { pc, op, name: "i64.shr_u" }, next: p };
        case 0x89: return { instr: { pc, op, name: "i64.rotl" }, next: p };
        case 0x8a: return { instr: { pc, op, name: "i64.rotr" }, next: p };

        default:
            return {
                instr: {
                    pc,
                    op,
                    name: `UNKNOWN(0x${op.toString(16)})`
                },
                next: p
            };
    }
}

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 122 MEMORY FLOW TRACE v3");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log(" RAW LOAD / STORE / CALL ANALYSIS");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");

console.log("WASM size :", wasm.length.toLocaleString());
console.log("Range     :", `${hex(START)} .. ${hex(END)}`);
console.log("");

const code: Instr[] = [];

let p = START;

while (p < END) {
    const d = decode(wasm, p);

    code.push(d.instr);

    if (d.next <= p) {
        throw new Error(`Decoder stalled at ${hex(p)}`);
    }

    p = d.next;
}

console.log("Instructions decoded :", code.length);
console.log("Final position       :", hex(p));
console.log("Expected end         :", hex(END));

if (p !== END) {
    throw new Error("EXACT END MISMATCH");
}

const calls87 = code.filter(
    x => x.name === "call" && x.a === 87
);

const loads = code.filter(
    x => x.name === "i64.load"
);

const stores = code.filter(
    x => x.name === "i64.store"
);

console.log("");
console.log("CALL 87 count :", calls87.length);
console.log("i64.load count:", loads.length);
console.log("i64.store count:", stores.length);
console.log("");

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" ALL i64.STORE");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const x of stores) {
    console.log(
        `${hex(x.pc)}  i64.store  align=${x.a} offset=${x.b}`
    );
}

console.log("");

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" ALL i64.LOAD");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const x of loads) {
    console.log(
        `${hex(x.pc)}  i64.load   align=${x.a} offset=${x.b}`
    );
}

console.log("");

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" CALL 87 + LOCAL MEMORY OPERATIONS");
console.log("============================================================");

for (let i = 0; i < calls87.length; i++) {

    const call = calls87[i];

    console.log("");
    console.log(
        `CALL #${String(i + 1).padStart(2, "0")} @ ${hex(call.pc)}`
    );

    /*
     * Show a compact instruction neighborhood.
     *
     * This is intentionally raw and does not claim
     * semantic dataflow.
     */
    const index = code.findIndex(
        x => x.pc === call.pc
    );

    const from = Math.max(0, index - 8);
    const to   = Math.min(code.length - 1, index + 12);

    for (let j = from; j <= to; j++) {

        const x = code[j];

        let extra = "";

        if (x.name === "call") {
            extra = ` func=${x.a}`;
        }
        else if (
            x.name === "i64.load" ||
            x.name === "i64.store"
        ) {
            extra = ` align=${x.a} offset=${x.b}`;
        }
        else if (
            x.name === "local.get" ||
            x.name === "local.set" ||
            x.name === "local.tee"
        ) {
            extra = ` local=${x.a}`;
        }
        else if (x.name === "br" || x.name === "br_if") {
            extra = ` depth=${x.a}`;
        }

        console.log(
            `  ${hex(x.pc)}  ${x.name}${extra}`
        );
    }
}

console.log("");

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" STORE DISTANCE FROM CALL 87");
console.log("============================================================");

for (let i = 0; i < calls87.length; i++) {

    const call = calls87[i];

    const nextStores = stores
        .filter(x => x.pc > call.pc)
        .slice(0, 6);

    console.log("");
    console.log(
        `CALL #${String(i + 1).padStart(2, "0")} @ ${hex(call.pc)}`
    );

    if (nextStores.length === 0) {
        console.log("  no subsequent i64.store");
        continue;
    }

    for (const s of nextStores) {
        console.log(
            `  STORE @ ${hex(s.pc)}  delta=${s.pc - call.pc}  offset=${s.b}`
        );
    }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" STATUS");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");
console.log("RAW MEMORY OPERATIONS EXTRACTED.");
console.log("NO SEMANTIC DESTINATION WAS ASSUMED.");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceFunction122MemoryProvenanceV3.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const FUNC_START = 0x0001226d;
const FUNC_END   = 0x0001295b;

const CALL_TARGET = 87;

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function u8(p: number): number {
    return buf[p];
}

function hex(n: number): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = u8(pos++);
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            return { value, next: pos };

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readSLEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = u8(pos++);
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            break;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
        value |= (~0 << shift);

    return { value, next: pos };
}

type Expr =
    | { kind: "local"; index: number }
    | { kind: "const"; value: number }
    | { kind: "unknown"; opcode: number }
    | { kind: "add"; a: Expr; b: Expr }
    | { kind: "sub"; a: Expr; b: Expr }
    | { kind: "mul"; a: Expr; b: Expr }
    | { kind: "load64"; addr: Expr; offset: number }
    | { kind: "load32"; addr: Expr; offset: number }
    | { kind: "shl"; a: Expr; b: Expr }
    | { kind: "shr_s"; a: Expr; b: Expr }
    | { kind: "shr_u"; a: Expr; b: Expr }
    | { kind: "and"; a: Expr; b: Expr }
    | { kind: "or"; a: Expr; b: Expr }
    | { kind: "xor"; a: Expr; b: Expr };

function expr(e: Expr): string {
    switch (e.kind) {
        case "local":
            return `L${e.index}`;

        case "const":
            return `I32CONST(${e.value})`;

        case "unknown":
            return `UNKNOWN(0x${e.opcode.toString(16)})`;

        case "add":
            return `(${expr(e.a)}+${expr(e.b)})`;

        case "sub":
            return `(${expr(e.a)}-${expr(e.b)})`;

        case "mul":
            return `(${expr(e.a)}*${expr(e.b)})`;

        case "load64":
            return `MEM64[${expr(e.addr)}+${e.offset}]`;

        case "load32":
            return `MEM32[${expr(e.addr)}+${e.offset}]`;

        case "shl":
            return `(${expr(e.a)}<<${expr(e.b)})`;

        case "shr_s":
            return `(${expr(e.a)}>>s${expr(e.b)})`;

        case "shr_u":
            return `(${expr(e.a)}>>u${expr(e.b)})`;

        case "and":
            return `(${expr(e.a)}&${expr(e.b)})`;

        case "or":
            return `(${expr(e.a)}|${expr(e.b)})`;

        case "xor":
            return `(${expr(e.a)}^${expr(e.b)})`;
    }
}

type Instr = {
    pos: number;
    op: number;
    text: string;
};

function decodeInstructions(start: number, end: number): Instr[] {
    const out: Instr[] = [];
    let p = start;

    while (p < end) {
        const pos = p;
        const op = u8(p++);

        let text = `UNKNOWN(0x${op.toString(16)})`;

        switch (op) {
            case 0x20: {
                const r = readULEB(p);
                p = r.next;
                text = `local.get ${r.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x21: {
                const r = readULEB(p);
                p = r.next;
                text = `local.set ${r.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x22: {
                const r = readULEB(p);
                p = r.next;
                text = `local.tee ${r.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x41: {
                const r = readSLEB(p);
                p = r.next;
                text = `i32.const ${r.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x42: {
                const r = readSLEB(p);
                p = r.next;
                text = `i64.const ${r.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x28: {
                const align = u8(p++);
                const r = readULEB(p);
                p = r.next;
                text = `i32.load align=${align} offset=${r.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x29: {
                const align = u8(p++);
                const r = readULEB(p);
                p = r.next;
                text = `i64.load align=${align} offset=${r.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x37: {
                const align = u8(p++);
                const r = readULEB(p);
                p = r.next;
                text = `i64.store align=${align} offset=${r.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x6a:
                text = "i32.add";
                break;

            case 0x6b:
                text = "i32.sub";
                break;

            case 0x7c:
                text = "i64.add";
                break;

            case 0x7d:
                text = "i64.sub";
                break;

            case 0x7e:
                text = "i64.mul";
                break;

            case 0x83:
                text = "i64.and";
                break;

            case 0x84:
                text = "i64.or";
                break;

            case 0x85:
                text = "i64.xor";
                break;

            case 0x86:
                text = "i64.shl";
                break;

            case 0x87:
                text = "i64.shr_s";
                break;

            case 0x88:
                text = "i64.shr_u";
                break;

            case 0x10: {
                const r = readULEB(p);
                p = r.next;
                text = `call ${r.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x0b:
                text = "end";
                break;

            case 0x02:
                text = "block";
                break;

            case 0x03:
                text = "loop";
                break;

            case 0x04:
                text = "if";
                break;

            case 0x0c: {
                const r = readULEB(p);
                p = r.next;
                text = `br ${r.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x0d: {
                const r = readULEB(p);
                p = r.next;
                text = `br_if ${r.value}`;
                break;
            }
        }

        out.push({ pos, op, text });
    }

    return out;
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * Conservative symbolic stack
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 *
 * IMPORTANT:
 * CALL 87 is modeled as:
 *
 *     destination, A, 0, B, 0
 *
 * and RETURNS NOTHING.
 *
 * Function 87 writes:
 *
 *     [destination + 0] = LOW
 *     [destination + 8] = HIGH
 *
 * Therefore we record memory writes rather than pushing
 * synthetic return values.
 */

type Val = Expr | { kind: "i64zero" };

function zero64(): Expr {
    return { kind: "const", value: 0 };
}

function pop(stack: Val[], where: number): Val {
    if (stack.length === 0) {
        throw new Error(`STACK_UNDERFLOW @ ${hex(where)}`);
    }

    return stack.pop()!;
}

function asExpr(v: Val): Expr {
    return v.kind === "i64zero" ? zero64() : v;
}

function sameExpr(a: Expr, b: Expr): boolean {
    return expr(a) === expr(b);
}

type StoreRecord = {
    callNo: number;
    callPos: number;
    destination: Expr;
    lowOffset: number;
    highOffset: number;
};

type LoadRecord = {
    pos: number;
    addr: Expr;
    offset: number;
};

const ins = decodeInstructions(FUNC_START, FUNC_END);

const stack: Val[] = [];
const locals = new Map<number, Val>();

const stores: StoreRecord[] = [];
const loads: LoadRecord[] = [];

let callNo = 0;

console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log(" FUNCTION 122 MEMORY PROVENANCE v3");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log(" CALL 87 RETURNS NOTHING");
console.log(" TRACK CALL87 MEMORY WRITES + LATER LOADS");
console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log();

for (const i of ins) {
    try {
        switch (i.op) {

            case 0x20: {
                const n = parseInt(i.text.split(" ")[2]);
                stack.push(locals.get(n) ?? {
                    kind: "local",
                    index: n
                });
                break;
            }

            case 0x21: {
                const n = parseInt(i.text.split(" ")[2]);
                locals.set(n, pop(stack, i.pos));
                break;
            }

            case 0x22: {
                const n = parseInt(i.text.split(" ")[2]);
                const v = pop(stack, i.pos);
                locals.set(n, v);
                stack.push(v);
                break;
            }

            case 0x41: {
                const n = Number(i.text.substring("i32.const ".length));
                stack.push({
                    kind: "const",
                    value: n
                });
                break;
            }

            case 0x42: {
                const n = Number(i.text.substring("i64.const ".length));
                stack.push({
                    kind: "const",
                    value: n
                });
                break;
            }

            case 0x29: {
                const offset = Number(
                    i.text.match(/offset=(\d+)/)?.[1] ?? 0
                );

                const addr = asExpr(pop(stack, i.pos));

                const load: Expr = {
                    kind: "load64",
                    addr,
                    offset
                };

                stack.push(load);

                loads.push({
                    pos: i.pos,
                    addr,
                    offset
                });

                break;
            }

            case 0x28: {
                const offset = Number(
                    i.text.match(/offset=(\d+)/)?.[1] ?? 0
                );

                const addr = asExpr(pop(stack, i.pos));

                stack.push({
                    kind: "load32",
                    addr,
                    offset
                });

                break;
            }

            case 0x6a: {
                const b = asExpr(pop(stack, i.pos));
                const a = asExpr(pop(stack, i.pos));

                stack.push({
                    kind: "add",
                    a,
                    b
                });

                break;
            }

            case 0x6b: {
                const b = asExpr(pop(stack, i.pos));
                const a = asExpr(pop(stack, i.pos));

                stack.push({
                    kind: "sub",
                    a,
                    b
                });

                break;
            }

            case 0x7c: {
                const b = asExpr(pop(stack, i.pos));
                const a = asExpr(pop(stack, i.pos));

                stack.push({
                    kind: "add",
                    a,
                    b
                });

                break;
            }

            case 0x7d: {
                const b = asExpr(pop(stack, i.pos));
                const a = asExpr(pop(stack, i.pos));

                stack.push({
                    kind: "sub",
                    a,
                    b
                });

                break;
            }

            case 0x7e: {
                const b = asExpr(pop(stack, i.pos));
                const a = asExpr(pop(stack, i.pos));

                stack.push({
                    kind: "mul",
                    a,
                    b
                });

                break;
            }

            case 0x83: {
                const b = asExpr(pop(stack, i.pos));
                const a = asExpr(pop(stack, i.pos));

                stack.push({
                    kind: "and",
                    a,
                    b
                });

                break;
            }

            case 0x84: {
                const b = asExpr(pop(stack, i.pos));
                const a = asExpr(pop(stack, i.pos));

                stack.push({
                    kind: "or",
                    a,
                    b
                });

                break;
            }

            case 0x85: {
                const b = asExpr(pop(stack, i.pos));
                const a = asExpr(pop(stack, i.pos));

                stack.push({
                    kind: "xor",
                    a,
                    b
                });

                break;
            }

            case 0x86: {
                const b = asExpr(pop(stack, i.pos));
                const a = asExpr(pop(stack, i.pos));

                stack.push({
                    kind: "shl",
                    a,
                    b
                });

                break;
            }

            case 0x87: {
                const b = asExpr(pop(stack, i.pos));
                const a = asExpr(pop(stack, i.pos));

                stack.push({
                    kind: "shr_s",
                    a,
                    b
                });

                break;
            }

            case 0x88: {
                const b = asExpr(pop(stack, i.pos));
                const a = asExpr(pop(stack, i.pos));

                stack.push({
                    kind: "shr_u",
                    a,
                    b
                });

                break;
            }

            case 0x10: {
                const target = Number(
                    i.text.substring("call ".length)
                );

                if (target !== CALL_TARGET) {
                    throw new Error(
                        `UNSUPPORTED CALL ${target} @ ${hex(i.pos)}`
                    );
                }

                callNo++;

                /*
                 * Exact Function 87 ABI:
                 *
                 * destination
                 * A
                 * 0
                 * B
                 * 0
                 *
                 * No return value.
                 */

                const arg4 = pop(stack, i.pos);
                const B    = asExpr(pop(stack, i.pos));
                const arg2 = pop(stack, i.pos);
                const A    = asExpr(pop(stack, i.pos));
                const dest = asExpr(pop(stack, i.pos));

                console.log(
                    `CALL #${callNo} @ ${hex(i.pos)}`
                );
                console.log(
                    `  DEST = ${expr(dest)}`
                );
                console.log(
                    `  A    = ${expr(A)}`
                );
                console.log(
                    `  B    = ${expr(B)}`
                );

                /*
                 * The two stores are not computed here.
                 * We attach symbolic Function-87 output labels
                 * to the destination memory.
                 */

                stores.push({
                    callNo,
                    callPos: i.pos,
                    destination: dest,
                    lowOffset: 0,
                    highOffset: 8
                });

                console.log(
                    `  WRITE LOW  -> [${expr(dest)} + 0]`
                );
                console.log(
                    `  WRITE HIGH -> [${expr(dest)} + 8]`
                );
                console.log();

                break;
            }

            case 0x37: {
                /*
                 * We don't need ordinary stores for this phase.
                 * Consume them conservatively.
                 */

                const offset = Number(
                    i.text.match(/offset=(\d+)/)?.[1] ?? 0
                );

                const value = pop(stack, i.pos);
                const addr = pop(stack, i.pos);

                console.log(
                    `ORDINARY STORE @ ${hex(i.pos)}`
                );
                console.log(
                    `  ADDR=${expr(asExpr(addr))} + ${offset}`
                );
                console.log(
                    `  VALUE=${expr(asExpr(value))}`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0x02:
            case 0x03:
            case 0x04:
            case 0x0b:
            case 0x0c:
            case 0x0d:
                /*
                 * Control-flow instructions are intentionally
                 * not given fake stack effects here.
                 */
                break;

            default:
                throw new Error(
                    `UNKNOWN_OPCODE 0x${i.op.toString(16)} @ ${hex(i.pos)}`
                );
        }

    } catch (e) {
        console.log();
        console.log("!".repeat(72));
        console.log(`TRACE STOP @ ${hex(i.pos)}`);
        console.log(String(e));
        console.log("!".repeat(72));
        console.log();
        break;
    }
}

console.log();
console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log(" CALL87 DESTINATION SUMMARY");
console.log("=".repeat(72));

for (const s of stores) {
    console.log(
        `CALL #${s.callNo} @ ${hex(s.callPos)}`
    );
    console.log(
        `  DEST = ${expr(s.destination)}`
    );
    console.log(
        `  LOW  = [${expr(s.destination)} + 0]`
    );
    console.log(
        `  HIGH = [${expr(s.destination)} + 8]`
    );
}

console.log();
console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log(" LOADS FROM CALL87 DESTINATIONS");
console.log("=".repeat(72));

for (const l of loads) {
    const matches: string[] = [];

    for (const s of stores) {
        const base = expr(s.destination);
        const addr = expr(l.addr);

        /*
         * Exact textual match first.
         *
         * This intentionally does NOT attempt algebraic
         * equivalence through unknown instructions.
         */
        if (addr === base) {
            if (l.offset === 0)
                matches.push(`CALL #${s.callNo} LOW`);

            if (l.offset === 8)
                matches.push(`CALL #${s.callNo} HIGH`);
        }
    }

    if (matches.length > 0) {
        console.log(
            `${hex(l.pos)} LOAD offset=${l.offset} -> ${matches.join(", ")}`
        );
    }
}

console.log();
console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log(" END");
console.log("=".repeat(72));

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceFunction122MemoryProvenanceV4.ts

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const START = 0x0001226d;
const END   = 0x0001295b;

const TARGET_CALL = 87;

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function byte(p: number): number {
    return buf[p];
}

function hex(n: number): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function uleb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = byte(pos++);
        value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            return { value, next: pos };

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function sleb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = byte(pos++);
        value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            break;
    }

    if ((b & 0x40) !== 0)
        value -= 2 ** shift;

    return { value, next: pos };
}

type E =
    | { k: "local"; n: number }
    | { k: "i32"; n: number }
    | { k: "i64"; n: number }
    | { k: "load64"; a: E; o: number }
    | { k: "load32"; a: E; o: number }
    | { k: "un"; op: string; a: E }
    | { k: "bin"; op: string; a: E; b: E }
    | { k: "unknown"; op: number };

function show(e: E): string {
    switch (e.k) {
        case "local":
            return `L${e.n}`;

        case "i32":
            return `I32CONST(${e.n})`;

        case "i64":
            return `I64CONST(${e.n})`;

        case "load64":
            return `MEM64[${show(e.a)}+${e.o}]`;

        case "load32":
            return `MEM32[${show(e.a)}+${e.o}]`;

        case "un":
            return `${e.op}(${show(e.a)})`;

        case "bin":
            return `(${show(e.a)}${e.op}${show(e.b)})`;

        case "unknown":
            return `UNKNOWN(0x${e.op.toString(16)})`;
    }
}

type V = E;

function local(n: number): E {
    return { k: "local", n };
}

function i64zero(): E {
    return { k: "i64", n: 0 };
}

function pop(stack: V[], pos: number): V {
    if (!stack.length)
        throw new Error(`STACK_UNDERFLOW @ ${hex(pos)}`);

    return stack.pop()!;
}

function bin(
    stack: V[],
    op: string,
    pos: number
) {
    const b = pop(stack, pos);
    const a = pop(stack, pos);

    stack.push({
        k: "bin",
        op,
        a,
        b
    });
}

function un(
    stack: V[],
    op: string,
    pos: number
) {
    const a = pop(stack, pos);

    stack.push({
        k: "un",
        op,
        a
    });
}

interface CallWrite {
    callNo: number;
    pos: number;
    dest: E;
    A: E;
    B: E;
}

interface Barrier {
    pos: number;
    opcode: number;
}

const stack: V[] = [];
const locals = new Map<number, V>();

const calls: CallWrite[] = [];
const barriers: Barrier[] = [];

let callNo = 0;

console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log(" FUNCTION 122 MEMORY PROVENANCE v4");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log(" CALL 87 RETURNS NOTHING");
console.log(" UNKNOWN OPCODES = PROVENANCE BARRIERS");
console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log();

let p = START;

while (p < END) {

    const pos = p;
    const op = byte(p++);

    try {

        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // locals
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        if (op === 0x20) {
            const r = uleb(p);
            p = r.next;

            stack.push(
                locals.get(r.value) ?? local(r.value)
            );

            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x21) {
            const r = uleb(p);
            p = r.next;

            locals.set(r.value, pop(stack, pos));

            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x22) {
            const r = uleb(p);
            p = r.next;

            const v = pop(stack, pos);

            locals.set(r.value, v);
            stack.push(v);

            continue;
        }

        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // constants
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        if (op === 0x41) {
            const r = sleb(p);
            p = r.next;

            stack.push({
                k: "i32",
                n: r.value
            });

            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x42) {
            const r = sleb(p);
            p = r.next;

            stack.push({
                k: "i64",
                n: r.value
            });

            continue;
        }

        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // memory loads
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        if (op === 0x28 || op === 0x29) {

            p++; // alignment

            const r = uleb(p);
            p = r.next;

            const addr = pop(stack, pos);

            if (op === 0x29) {
                stack.push({
                    k: "load64",
                    a: addr,
                    o: r.value
                });
            } else {
                stack.push({
                    k: "load32",
                    a: addr,
                    o: r.value
                });
            }

            continue;
        }

        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // i32 arithmetic
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        if (op === 0x6a) {
            bin(stack, "+", pos);
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x6b) {
            bin(stack, "-", pos);
            continue;
        }

        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // i64 arithmetic
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        if (op === 0x7c) {
            bin(stack, "+", pos);
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x7d) {
            bin(stack, "-", pos);
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x7e) {
            bin(stack, "*", pos);
            continue;
        }

        // IMPORTANT:
        // 0x7f = i64.div_s
        if (op === 0x7f) {
            bin(stack, "/s", pos);
            continue;
        }

        // 0x80 = i64.div_u
        if (op === 0x80) {
            bin(stack, "/u", pos);
            continue;
        }

        // 0x81 = i64.rem_s
        if (op === 0x81) {
            bin(stack, "%s", pos);
            continue;
        }

        // 0x82 = i64.rem_u
        if (op === 0x82) {
            bin(stack, "%u", pos);
            continue;
        }

        // 0x83 = i64.and
        if (op === 0x83) {
            bin(stack, "&", pos);
            continue;
        }

        // 0x84 = i64.or
        if (op === 0x84) {
            bin(stack, "|", pos);
            continue;
        }

        // 0x85 = i64.xor
        if (op === 0x85) {
            bin(stack, "^", pos);
            continue;
        }

        // 0x86 = i64.shl
        if (op === 0x86) {
            bin(stack, "<<", pos);
            continue;
        }

        // 0x87 = i64.shr_s
        if (op === 0x87) {
            bin(stack, ">>s", pos);
            continue;
        }

        // 0x88 = i64.shr_u
        if (op === 0x88) {
            bin(stack, ">>u", pos);
            continue;
        }

        // 0x89 = i64.rotl
        if (op === 0x89) {
            bin(stack, "rotl", pos);
            continue;
        }

        // 0x8a = i64.rotr
        if (op === 0x8a) {
            bin(stack, "rotr", pos);
            continue;
        }

        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // CALL
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        if (op === 0x10) {

            const r = uleb(p);
            p = r.next;

            const target = r.value;

            if (target !== TARGET_CALL) {

                /*
                 * Unknown call ABI.
                 * Do not guess its stack effect.
                 */

                console.log(
                    `CALL ${target} @ ${hex(pos)} -> ABI UNKNOWN`
                );

                barriers.push({
                    pos,
                    opcode: op
                });

                continue;
            }

            callNo++;

            /*
             * Exact known ABI of Function 87:
             *
             * destination
             * A
             * 0
             * B
             * 0
             *
             * Function 87 returns NOTHING.
             */

            const arg4 = pop(stack, pos);
            const B = pop(stack, pos);
            const arg2 = pop(stack, pos);
            const A = pop(stack, pos);
            const dest = pop(stack, pos);

            console.log(
                `CALL #${callNo} @ ${hex(pos)}`
            );

            console.log(
                `  DEST = ${show(dest)}`
            );

            console.log(
                `  A    = ${show(A)}`
            );

            console.log(
                `  B    = ${show(B)}`
            );

            console.log(
                `  WRITE LOW  = MEM64[DEST+0]`
            );

            console.log(
                `  WRITE HIGH = MEM64[DEST+8]`
            );

            console.log();

            calls.push({
                callNo,
                pos,
                dest,
                A,
                B
            });

            continue;
        }

        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // stores
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        if (op === 0x37) {

            p++; // alignment

            const r = uleb(p);
            p = r.next;

            const value = pop(stack, pos);
            const addr = pop(stack, pos);

            console.log(
                `STORE @ ${hex(pos)}`
            );

            console.log(
                `  ADDR  = ${show(addr)} + ${r.value}`
            );

            console.log(
                `  VALUE = ${show(value)}`
            );

            console.log();

            continue;
        }

        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // control flow
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        if (
            op === 0x02 ||
            op === 0x03 ||
            op === 0x04
        ) {
            /*
             * We deliberately don't invent block stack effects.
             * The important memory provenance before the barrier
             * remains valid.
             */

            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x0b) {
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
            const r = uleb(p);
            p = r.next;
            continue;
        }

        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // UNKNOWN
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        console.log(
            `UNKNOWN OPCODE @ ${hex(pos)} = 0x${op.toString(16)}`
        );

        barriers.push({
            pos,
            opcode: op
        });

        /*
         * Do NOT guess stack effect.
         *
         * Clear symbolic stack because subsequent expressions
         * cannot safely inherit an unknown stack state.
         */

        stack.length = 0;

    } catch (err) {

        console.log();
        console.log(
            `TRACE ERROR @ ${hex(pos)}`
        );

        console.log(String(err));

        console.log(
            "SYMBOLIC STACK CLEARED"
        );

        stack.length = 0;

        barriers.push({
            pos,
            opcode: op
        });
    }
}

console.log();
console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log("CALL 87 MEMORY WRITE SUMMARY");
console.log("=".repeat(72));

for (const c of calls) {

    console.log(
        `CALL #${c.callNo} @ ${hex(c.pos)}`
    );

    console.log(
        `  DEST = ${show(c.dest)}`
    );

    console.log(
        `  LOW  = [${show(c.dest)} + 0]`
    );

    console.log(
        `  HIGH = [${show(c.dest)} + 8]`
    );
}

console.log();
console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log("UNKNOWN OPCODE BARRIERS");
console.log("=".repeat(72));

for (const b of barriers) {
    console.log(
        `${hex(b.pos)} -> 0x${b.opcode.toString(16)}`
    );
}

console.log();
console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log(`CALL87 COUNT = ${calls.length}`);
console.log(`BARRIERS     = ${barriers.length}`);
console.log("=".repeat(72));
