FILE: scripts\traceFunction122PreludeV2.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const START = 0x12690;
const END = 0x126fa;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function readU32LEB(buf: Buffer, p: { v: number }): number {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[p.v++];
        result += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;

        shift += 7;
    }

    return result;
}

function readS32LEB(buf: Buffer, p: { v: number }): number {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = buf[p.v++];

        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;

        shift += 7;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
        result |= (~0 << shift);
    }

    return result | 0;
}

function readS64LEB(buf: Buffer, p: { v: number }): bigint {
    let result = 0n;
    let shift = 0n;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = buf[p.v++];

        result |= BigInt(b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;

        shift += 7n;
    }

    if (shift < 64n && (b & 0x40)) {
        result |= (-1n) << shift;
    }

    return result;
}

function hex(n: number): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

/* ============================================================
   IMPORTANT KNOWN INFORMATION
   ============================================================ */

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 122 PRELUDE V2");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log(" CONTROL FLOW + STACK");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`Window    : ${hex(START)} .. ${hex(END)}`);

console.log("");
console.log("Known import #66:");
console.log("  a.ma : (i32, i32, i32, i32) -> ()");

console.log("");
console.log("Known function #87:");
console.log("  destination + A + B");
console.log("  returns no stack value");
console.log("  writes 16 bytes to destination");

/* ============================================================
   STACK
   ============================================================ */

const stack: string[] = [];

let unreachable = false;

function pop(): string {
    if (unreachable) {
        return "POLYMORPHIC";
    }

    if (stack.length === 0) {
        return "STACK_UNDERFLOW";
    }

    return stack.pop()!;
}

function push(v: string) {
    if (!unreachable) {
        stack.push(v);
    }
}

function showStack(): string {
    if (unreachable) return "[POLYMORPHIC / UNREACHABLE]";

    if (stack.length === 0) return "[]";

    return "[" + stack.join(" | ") + "]";
}

/* ============================================================
   LOCALS
   ============================================================ */

const locals = new Map<number, string>();

function getLocal(i: number): string {
    return locals.get(i) ?? `L${i}`;
}

/* ============================================================
   CONTROL FLOW
   ============================================================ */

interface Frame {
    kind: string;
    startPc: number;
}

const control: Frame[] = [];

/* ============================================================
   DECODE
   ============================================================ */

const p = { v: START };

while (p.v < END) {
    const pc = p.v;
    const op = wasm[p.v++];

    let desc = "";

    switch (op) {

        /* ----------------------------------------------------
           CONTROL
        ---------------------------------------------------- */

        case 0x00:
            desc = "unreachable";

            unreachable = true;
            break;

        case 0x02: {
            const blockType = wasm[p.v++];

            control.push({
                kind: "block",
                startPc: pc
            });

            desc =
                `block type=0x${blockType.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;

            break;
        }

        case 0x03: {
            const blockType = wasm[p.v++];

            control.push({
                kind: "loop",
                startPc: pc
            });

            desc =
                `loop type=0x${blockType.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;

            break;
        }

        case 0x04: {
            const blockType = wasm[p.v++];

            control.push({
                kind: "if",
                startPc: pc
            });

            const cond = pop();

            desc =
                `if type=0x${blockType.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")} cond=${cond}`;

            break;
        }

        case 0x05:
            desc = "else";

            /*
             * For our limited trace, ELSE switches execution
             * back from the unreachable branch to the reachable
             * branch if this is the matching IF.
             */
            unreachable = false;

            break;

        case 0x0b: {
            const frame = control.pop();

            desc =
                `end${frame ? ` (${frame.kind})` : ""}`;

            /*
             * Do not blindly clear stack here.
             * We only clear polymorphic state.
             */
            if (unreachable) {
                unreachable = false;
            }

            break;
        }

        case 0x0c: {
            const depth = readU32LEB(wasm, p);

            desc = `br ${depth}`;

            /*
             * A branch terminates the current path.
             */
            unreachable = true;

            break;
        }

        case 0x0d: {
            const depth = readU32LEB(wasm, p);
            const cond = pop();

            desc =
                `br_if ${depth} cond=${cond}`;

            break;
        }

        case 0x0f:
            desc = "return";
            unreachable = true;
            break;

        /* ----------------------------------------------------
           CALL
        ---------------------------------------------------- */

        case 0x10: {
            const idx = readU32LEB(wasm, p);

            if (idx === 66) {
                const a4 = pop();
                const a3 = pop();
                const a2 = pop();
                const a1 = pop();

                desc =
                    `call 66 a.ma ` +
                    `(i32 ${a1}, i32 ${a2}, i32 ${a3}, i32 ${a4}) -> ()`;
            }
            else if (idx === 87) {

                /*
                 * Function 87 has three i64 parameters:
                 *
                 * destination, A, B
                 *
                 * and NO results.
                 */
                const b = pop();
                const a = pop();
                const dst = pop();

                desc =
                    `call 87 ` +
                    `DEST=${dst} A=${a} B=${b} -> ()`;
            }
            else {
                desc = `call ${idx} (not expanded)`;
            }

            break;
        }

        /* ----------------------------------------------------
           LOCALS
        ---------------------------------------------------- */

        case 0x20: {
            const idx = readU32LEB(wasm, p);

            const v = getLocal(idx);

            push(v);

            desc =
                `local.get ${idx} -> ${v}`;

            break;
        }

        case 0x21: {
            const idx = readU32LEB(wasm, p);

            const v = pop();

            locals.set(idx, v);

            desc =
                `local.set ${idx} = ${v}`;

            break;
        }

        case 0x22: {
            const idx = readU32LEB(wasm, p);

            const v = pop();

            locals.set(idx, v);

            push(v);

            desc =
                `local.tee ${idx} = ${v}`;

            break;
        }

        /* ----------------------------------------------------
           CONSTANTS
        ---------------------------------------------------- */

        case 0x41: {
            const v = readS32LEB(wasm, p);

            push(`i32.const(${v})`);

            desc = `i32.const ${v}`;

            break;
        }

        case 0x42: {
            const v = readS64LEB(wasm, p);

            push(`i64.const(${v})`);

            desc = `i64.const ${v}`;

            break;
        }

        /* ----------------------------------------------------
           INTEGER OPS
        ---------------------------------------------------- */

        case 0x6a: {
            const b = pop();
            const a = pop();

            push(`(${a} + ${b})`);

            desc = `i32.add`;

            break;
        }

        case 0x6b: {
            const b = pop();
            const a = pop();

            push(`(${a} - ${b})`);

            desc = `i32.sub`;

            break;
        }

        case 0x7c: {
            const b = pop();
            const a = pop();

            push(`(${a} + ${b})`);

            desc = `i64.add`;

            break;
        }

        case 0x7d: {
            const b = pop();
            const a = pop();

            push(`(${a} - ${b})`);

            desc = `i64.sub`;

            break;
        }

        case 0x7e: {
            const b = pop();
            const a = pop();

            push(`(${a} * ${b})`);

            desc = `i64.mul`;

            break;
        }

        case 0x83: {
            const b = pop();
            const a = pop();

            push(`(${a} & ${b})`);

            desc = `i64.and`;

            break;
        }

        case 0x84: {
            const b = pop();
            const a = pop();

            push(`(${a} | ${b})`);

            desc = `i64.or`;

            break;
        }

        case 0x86: {
            const b = pop();
            const a = pop();

            push(`(${a} << ${b})`);

            desc = `i64.shl`;

            break;
        }

        case 0x88: {
            const b = pop();
            const a = pop();

            push(`(${a} >>u ${b})`);

            desc = `i64.shr_u`;

            break;
        }

        case 0xad: {
            const a = pop();

            push(`extend32s(${a})`);

            desc = `i64.extend32_s`;

            break;
        }

        /* ----------------------------------------------------
           COMPARISONS
        ---------------------------------------------------- */

        case 0x54: {
            const b = pop();
            const a = pop();

            push(`(${a} <u ${b})`);

            desc = `i64.lt_u`;

            break;
        }

        case 0x56: {
            const b = pop();
            const a = pop();

            push(`(${a} >u ${b})`);

            desc = `i64.gt_u`;

            break;
        }

        /* ----------------------------------------------------
           MEMORY
        ---------------------------------------------------- */

        case 0x29: {
            const align = readU32LEB(wasm, p);
            const offset = readU32LEB(wasm, p);

            const addr = pop();

            const value =
                `MEM64[${addr} + ${offset}]`;

            push(value);

            desc =
                `i64.load align=${align} offset=${offset}`;

            break;
        }

        case 0x37: {
            const align = readU32LEB(wasm, p);
            const offset = readU32LEB(wasm, p);

            const value = pop();
            const addr = pop();

            desc =
                `i64.store align=${align} offset=${offset} ` +
                `addr=${addr} value=${value}`;

            break;
        }

        /* ----------------------------------------------------
           GLOBAL SET
        ---------------------------------------------------- */

        case 0x24: {
            const idx = readU32LEB(wasm, p);

            const value = pop();

            desc =
                `global.set ${idx} = ${value}`;

            break;
        }

        /* ----------------------------------------------------
           DEFAULT
        ---------------------------------------------------- */

        default:
            desc =
                `UNKNOWN opcode=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
            break;
    }

    console.log(
        `${hex(pc)}  ${desc.padEnd(65)} STACK=${showStack()}`
    );
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" CONTROL STACK");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const c of control) {
    console.log(
        `${c.kind} @ ${hex(c.startPc)}`
    );
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FINAL");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(`PC    : ${hex(p.v)}`);
console.log(`Stack : ${showStack()}`);
console.log(`Depth : ${stack.length}`);
console.log(`Unreachable : ${unreachable}`);

console.log("");
console.log("DONE - READ ONLY / OFFLINE");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceFunction122ResultDataflowV1.ts

import fs from "fs";

const WASM_PATH =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const START = 0x0001226d;
const END = 0x0001295b;

function hex8(n: number): string {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function hex64(n: bigint): string {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(16, "0");
}

function readULEB(buf: Buffer, pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = buf[pos++];

    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readSLEB(buf: Buffer, pos: number): { value: bigint; next: number } {
  let result = 0n;
  let shift = 0n;

  while (true) {
    const b = buf[pos++];

    result |= BigInt(b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      if ((b & 0x40) !== 0) {
        result |= (-1n) << (shift + 7n);
      }

      return {
        value: result,
        next: pos,
      };
    }

    shift += 7n;
  }
}

type Instr = {
  pos: number;
  op: number;
  name: string;
  arg?: bigint | number;
};

function decodeInstructions(buf: Buffer): Instr[] {
  const out: Instr[] = [];

  let p = START;

  while (p <= END) {
    const pos = p;
    const op = buf[p++];

    let name = `UNKNOWN(0x${op.toString(16)})`;
    let arg: bigint | number | undefined;

    switch (op) {
      case 0x00:
        name = "unreachable";
        break;

      case 0x01:
        name = "nop";
        break;

      case 0x02:
        name = "block";
        break;

      case 0x03:
        name = "loop";
        break;

      case 0x04:
        name = "if";
        break;

      case 0x05:
        name = "else";
        break;

      case 0x0b:
        name = "end";
        break;

      case 0x0c: {
        const r = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = r.next;
        arg = r.value;
        name = "br";
        break;
      }

      case 0x0d: {
        const r = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = r.next;
        arg = r.value;
        name = "br_if";
        break;
      }

      case 0x0e: {
        const count = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = count.next;

        for (let i = 0; i < count.value + 1; i++) {
          const r = readULEB(buf, p);
          p = r.next;
        }

        name = "br_table";
        break;
      }

      case 0x0f:
        name = "return";
        break;

      case 0x10: {
        const r = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = r.next;
        arg = r.value;
        name = "call";
        break;
      }

      case 0x11: {
        const r1 = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = r1.next;

        const r2 = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = r2.next;

        name = "call_indirect";
        arg = r1.value;
        break;
      }

      case 0x20: {
        const r = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = r.next;
        arg = r.value;
        name = "local.get";
        break;
      }

      case 0x21: {
        const r = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = r.next;
        arg = r.value;
        name = "local.set";
        break;
      }

      case 0x22: {
        const r = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = r.next;
        arg = r.value;
        name = "local.tee";
        break;
      }

      case 0x23: {
        const r = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = r.next;
        arg = r.value;
        name = "global.get";
        break;
      }

      case 0x24: {
        const r = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = r.next;
        arg = r.value;
        name = "global.set";
        break;
      }

      case 0x28:
        name = "i32.load";
        p += readULEB(buf, p).next - p;
        break;

      case 0x29:
        name = "i64.load";
        p += readULEB(buf, p).next - p;
        break;

      case 0x36:
        name = "i32.store";
        p += readULEB(buf, p).next - p;
        break;

      case 0x37:
        name = "i64.store";
        p += readULEB(buf, p).next - p;
        break;

      case 0x41: {
        const r = readSLEB(buf, p);
        p = r.next;
        arg = r.value;
        name = "i32.const";
        break;
      }

      case 0x42: {
        const r = readSLEB(buf, p);
        p = r.next;
        arg = r.value;
        name = "i64.const";
        break;
      }

      case 0x45:
        name = "i32.eqz";
        break;

      case 0x46:
        name = "i32.eq";
        break;

      case 0x47:
        name = "i32.ne";
        break;

      case 0x48:
        name = "i32.lt_s";
        break;

      case 0x49:
        name = "i32.lt_u";
        break;

      case 0x4a:
        name = "i32.gt_s";
        break;

      case 0x4b:
        name = "i32.gt_u";
        break;

      case 0x4c:
        name = "i32.le_s";
        break;

      case 0x4d:
        name = "i32.le_u";
        break;

      case 0x4e:
        name = "i32.ge_s";
        break;

      case 0x4f:
        name = "i32.ge_u";
        break;

      case 0x50:
        name = "i64.eqz";
        break;

      case 0x51:
        name = "i64.eq";
        break;

      case 0x52:
        name = "i64.ne";
        break;

      case 0x53:
        name = "i64.lt_s";
        break;

      case 0x54:
        name = "i64.lt_u";
        break;

      case 0x55:
        name = "i64.gt_s";
        break;

      case 0x56:
        name = "i64.gt_u";
        break;

      case 0x57:
        name = "i64.le_s";
        break;

      case 0x58:
        name = "i64.le_u";
        break;

      case 0x59:
        name = "i64.ge_s";
        break;

      case 0x5a:
        name = "i64.ge_u";
        break;

      case 0x5b:
        name = "i32.clz";
        break;

      case 0x5c:
        name = "i32.ctz";
        break;

      case 0x5d:
        name = "i32.popcnt";
        break;

      case 0x7c:
        name = "i64.add";
        break;

      case 0x7d:
        name = "i64.sub";
        break;

      case 0x7e:
        name = "i64.mul";
        break;

      case 0x7f:
        name = "i64.div_s";
        break;

      case 0x80:
        name = "i64.div_u";
        break;

      case 0x81:
        name = "i64.rem_s";
        break;

      case 0x82:
        name = "i64.rem_u";
        break;

      case 0x83:
        name = "i64.and";
        break;

      case 0x84:
        name = "i64.or";
        break;

      case 0x85:
        name = "i64.xor";
        break;

      case 0x86:
        name = "i64.shl";
        break;

      case 0x87:
        name = "i64.shr_s";
        break;

      case 0x88:
        name = "i64.shr_u";
        break;

      case 0x89:
        name = "i64.rotl";
        break;

      case 0x8a:
        name = "i64.rotr";
        break;
    }

    out.push({
      pos,
      op,
      name,
      arg,
    });

    if (p > END + 1) {
      break;
    }
  }

  return out;
}

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM_PATH);
const ins = decodeInstructions(wasm);

const calls = ins.filter(
  (x) => x.name === "call" && Number(x.arg) === 87
);

const stores = ins.filter((x) => x.name === "i64.store");

/*
 * Function 87 returns two i64 values according to the exact
 * previously established ABI/dataflow:
 *
 *   result-low
 *   result-high
 *
 * In Function 122, the caller immediately supplies a destination
 * address before CALL 87. We therefore inspect the instruction
 * sequence immediately following each CALL and track:
 *
 *   CALL
 *     ↓
 *   local.get
 *     ↓
 *   i32.const
 *     ↓
 *   i32.add / i32.sub
 *
 * No semantic meaning is assigned.
 */

for (let i = 0; i < calls.length; i++) {
  const c = calls[i];
  const idx = ins.indexOf(c);

  console.log();
  console.log(
    `CALL #${String(i + 1).padStart(2, "0")} @ ${hex8(c.pos)}`
  );

  const before = ins.slice(Math.max(0, idx - 10), idx);
  const after = ins.slice(idx + 1, Math.min(ins.length, idx + 16));

  console.log("  BEFORE CALL:");

  for (const x of before) {
    console.log(
      `    ${hex8(x.pos)}  ${x.name}${
        x.arg !== undefined ? ` ${x.arg.toString()}` : ""
      }`
    );
  }

  console.log("  AFTER CALL:");

  for (const x of after) {
    console.log(
      `    ${hex8(x.pos)}  ${x.name}${
        x.arg !== undefined ? ` ${x.arg.toString()}` : ""
      }`
    );
  }

  /*
   * Search forward until the next CALL/store boundary for local
   * operations which may carry the returned values.
   */
  const localOps = [];

  for (
    let j = idx + 1;
    j < ins.length && j < idx + 80;
    j++
  ) {
    const x = ins[j];

    if (
      x.name === "call" ||
      x.name === "i64.store" ||
      x.name === "end"
    ) {
      break;
    }

    if (
      x.name === "local.get" ||
      x.name === "local.set" ||
      x.name === "local.tee" ||
      x.name === "i64.add" ||
      x.name === "i64.sub" ||
      x.name === "i64.mul" ||
      x.name === "i64.and" ||
      x.name === "i64.or" ||
      x.name === "i64.xor" ||
      x.name === "i64.shl" ||
      x.name === "i64.shr_s" ||
      x.name === "i64.shr_u"
    ) {
      localOps.push(x);
    }
  }

  console.log("  DATAFLOW CANDIDATES:");

  if (localOps.length === 0) {
    console.log("    NONE");
  } else {
    for (const x of localOps) {
      console.log(
        `    ${hex8(x.pos)}  ${x.name}${
          x.arg !== undefined ? ` ${x.arg.toString()}` : ""
        }`
      );
    }
  }
}

import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const FUNC_START = 0x1226d;
const FUNC_END   = 0x1295b;

function hex(n: number): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function readULEB(buf: Buffer, p: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error("ULEB too large");
        }
    }
}

function readSLEB(buf: Buffer, p: number): {
    value: bigint;
    next: number;
} {
    let result = 0n;
    let shift = 0n;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = buf[p++];

        result |= BigInt(b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            break;
        }

        shift += 7n;

        if (shift > 70n) {
            throw new Error("SLEB too large");
        }
    }

    if ((shift < 64n) && (b & 0x40)) {
        result |= (-1n) << (shift + 7n);
    }

    return {
        value: result,
        next: p,
    };
}

type Value = {
    id: number;
    text: string;
    origin?: string;
};

let nextId = 1;

function value(text: string, origin?: string): Value {
    return {
        id: nextId++,
        text,
        origin,
    };
}

type Instr = {
    pc: number;
    opcode: number;
    name: string;
    imm?: string;
};

function decodeInstruction(buf: Buffer, p: number): {
    instr: Instr;
    next: number;
} {
    const pc = p;
    const op = buf[p++];

    switch (op) {
        case 0x0b:
            return {
                instr: { pc, opcode: op, name: "end" },
                next: p,
            };

        case 0x20: {
            const x = readULEB(buf, p);
            return {
                instr: {
                    pc,
                    opcode: op,
                    name: "local.get",
                    imm: String(x.value),
                },
                next: x.next,
            };
        }

        case 0x21: {
            const x = readULEB(buf, p);
            return {
                instr: {
                    pc,
                    opcode: op,
                    name: "local.set",
                    imm: String(x.value),
                },
                next: x.next,
            };
        }

        case 0x22: {
            const x = readULEB(buf, p);
            return {
                instr: {
                    pc,
                    opcode: op,
                    name: "local.tee",
                    imm: String(x.value),
                },
                next: x.next,
            };
        }

        case 0x41: {
            const x = readSLEB(buf, p);
            return {
                instr: {
                    pc,
                    opcode: op,
                    name: "i32.const",
                    imm: x.value.toString(),
                },
                next: x.next,
            };
        }

        case 0x42: {
            const x = readSLEB(buf, p);
            return {
                instr: {
                    pc,
                    opcode: op,
                    name: "i64.const",
                    imm: x.value.toString(),
                },
                next: x.next,
            };
        }

        case 0x10: {
            const x = readULEB(buf, p);
            return {
                instr: {
                    pc,
                    opcode: op,
                    name: "call",
                    imm: String(x.value),
                },
                next: x.next,
            };
        }

        case 0x28: {
            const align = readULEB(buf, p);
            const offset = readULEB(buf, align.next);

            return {
                instr: {
                    pc,
                    opcode: op,
                    name: "i32.load",
                    imm: `align=${align.value} offset=${offset.value}`,
                },
                next: offset.next,
            };
        }

        case 0x29: {
            const align = readULEB(buf, p);
            const offset = readULEB(buf, align.next);

            return {
                instr: {
                    pc,
                    opcode: op,
                    name: "i64.load",
                    imm: `align=${align.value} offset=${offset.value}`,
                },
                next: offset.next,
            };
        }

        case 0x36: {
            const align = readULEB(buf, p);
            const offset = readULEB(buf, align.next);

            return {
                instr: {
                    pc,
                    opcode: op,
                    name: "i32.store",
                    imm: `align=${align.value} offset=${offset.value}`,
                },
                next: offset.next,
            };
        }

        case 0x37: {
            const align = readULEB(buf, p);
            const offset = readULEB(buf, align.next);

            return {
                instr: {
                    pc,
                    opcode: op,
                    name: "i64.store",
                    imm: `align=${align.value} offset=${offset.value}`,
                },
                next: offset.next,
            };
        }

        case 0x7c: return { instr: { pc, opcode: op, name: "i64.add" }, next: p };
        case 0x7d: return { instr: { pc, opcode: op, name: "i64.sub" }, next: p };
        case 0x7e: return { instr: { pc, opcode: op, name: "i64.mul" }, next: p };
        case 0x7f: return { instr: { pc, opcode: op, name: "i64.div_s" }, next: p };
        case 0x80: return { instr: { pc, opcode: op, name: "i64.div_u" }, next: p };
        case 0x81: return { instr: { pc, opcode: op, name: "i64.rem_s" }, next: p };
        case 0x82: return { instr: { pc, opcode: op, name: "i64.rem_u" }, next: p };
        case 0x83: return { instr: { pc, opcode: op, name: "i64.and" }, next: p };
        case 0x84: return { instr: { pc, opcode: op, name: "i64.or" }, next: p };
        case 0x85: return { instr: { pc, opcode: op, name: "i64.xor" }, next: p };
        case 0x86: return { instr: { pc, opcode: op, name: "i64.shl" }, next: p };
        case 0x87: return { instr: { pc, opcode: op, name: "i64.shr_s" }, next: p };
        case 0x88: return { instr: { pc, opcode: op, name: "i64.shr_u" }, next: p };
        case 0x89: return { instr: { pc, opcode: op, name: "i64.rotl" }, next: p };
        case 0x8a: return { instr: { pc, opcode: op, name: "i64.rotr" }, next: p };

        default:
            return {
                instr: {
                    pc,
                    opcode: op,
                    name: `UNKNOWN(0x${op.toString(16)})`,
                },
                next: p,
            };
    }
}

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 122 SLOT CONSUMER TRACE v1");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log(" RAW WASM DATAFLOW");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");
console.log("WASM size :", wasm.length.toLocaleString());
console.log("Range     :", `${hex(FUNC_START)} .. ${hex(FUNC_END)}`);
console.log("");

/*
 * Decode the exact Function-122 byte range.
 */
const instructions: Instr[] = [];

let p = FUNC_START;

while (p < FUNC_END) {
    const d = decodeInstruction(wasm, p);
    instructions.push(d.instr);

    if (d.next <= p) {
        throw new Error(`Decoder did not advance at ${hex(p)}`);
    }

    p = d.next;
}

console.log("Instructions decoded :", instructions.length);
console.log("Final position       :", hex(p));
console.log("Expected end         :", hex(FUNC_END));

if (p !== FUNC_END) {
    throw new Error("EXACT END MISMATCH");
}

/*
 * Function 87 is function index 87.
 *
 * We first identify all CALL 87 instructions.
 */
const calls87 = instructions.filter(
    x => x.name === "call" && x.imm === "87"
);

console.log("CALL 87 count        :", calls87.length);
console.log("");

/*
 * The important part:
 *
 * A CALL 87 receives:
 *
 *   arg0 = destination pointer
 *   arg1 = A
 *   arg2 = 0
 *   arg3 = B
 *   arg4 = 0
 *   arg5 = temporary/local value
 *
 * We do not infer the destination from the slot number.
 *
 * Instead we perform a lightweight symbolic stack simulation around
 * every CALL 87 to recover the actual argument expressions.
 */

type Sym = {
    text: string;
};

function S(text: string): Sym {
    return { text };
}

function bin(op: string, a: Sym, b: Sym): Sym {
    return S(`(${a.text} ${op} ${b.text})`);
}

function unary(op: string, a: Sym): Sym {
    return S(`(${a.text} ${op})`);
}

function constI64(n: string): Sym {
    return S(`i64(${n})`);
}

function parseMemoryImmediate(imm?: string): {
    align: number;
    offset: number;
} {
    if (!imm) {
        throw new Error("Missing memory immediate");
    }

    const m = /^align=(\d+) offset=(\d+)$/.exec(imm);

    if (!m) {
        throw new Error(`Bad memory immediate: ${imm}`);
    }

    return {
        align: Number(m[1]),
        offset: Number(m[2]),
    };
}

/*
 * Build a symbolic trace independently for each CALL 87.
 *
 * This is deliberately conservative:
 * unsupported control-flow is reported instead of guessed.
 */
type CallTrace = {
    call: Instr;
    args: Sym[];
    before: number;
    status: string;
};

function traceCall(index: number): CallTrace {
    const locals = new Map<number, Sym>();

    for (let i = 0; i < 16; i++) {
        locals.set(i, S(`local${i}`));
    }

    const stack: Sym[] = [];

    /*
     * We need the instruction stream preceding the CALL.
     * Because Function 122 is straight-line for this region,
     * replay from the function beginning.
     */
    for (let i = 0; i <= index; i++) {
        const ins = instructions[i];

        switch (ins.name) {
            case "local.get": {
                const n = Number(ins.imm);
                stack.push(locals.get(n) ?? S(`local${n}`));
                break;
            }

            case "local.set": {
                const n = Number(ins.imm);
                const v = stack.pop();

                if (!v) {
                    return {
                        call: instructions[index],
                        args: [],
                        before: stack.length,
                        status: `STACK UNDERFLOW at ${hex(ins.pc)}`,
                    };
                }

                locals.set(n, v);
                break;
            }

            case "local.tee": {
                const n = Number(ins.imm);
                const v = stack[stack.length - 1];

                if (!v) {
                    return {
                        call: instructions[index],
                        args: [],
                        before: stack.length,
                        status: `STACK UNDERFLOW at ${hex(ins.pc)}`,
                    };
                }

                locals.set(n, v);
                break;
            }

            case "i32.const":
            case "i64.const":
                stack.push(S(`${ins.name === "i32.const" ? "i32" : "i64"}(${ins.imm})`));
                break;

            case "i64.load": {
                const mem = parseMemoryImmediate(ins.imm);
                const addr = stack.pop();

                if (!addr) {
                    return {
                        call: instructions[index],
                        args: [],
                        before: stack.length,
                        status: `LOAD ADDRESS UNDERFLOW at ${hex(ins.pc)}`,
                    };
                }

                stack.push(
                    S(`MEM64[${addr.text}+${mem.offset}]`)
                );
                break;
            }

            case "i64.store": {
                /*
                 * address + value
                 */
                const value = stack.pop();
                const addr = stack.pop();

                if (!value || !addr) {
                    return {
                        call: instructions[index],
                        args: [],
                        before: stack.length,
                        status: `STORE STACK UNDERFLOW at ${hex(ins.pc)}`,
                    };
                }

                break;
            }

            case "i64.add": {
                const b = stack.pop();
                const a = stack.pop();

                if (!a || !b) {
                    return {
                        call: instructions[index],
                        args: [],
                        before: stack.length,
                        status: `ADD STACK UNDERFLOW at ${hex(ins.pc)}`,
                    };
                }

                stack.push(bin("i64.add", a, b));
                break;
            }

            case "i64.sub": {
                const b = stack.pop();
                const a = stack.pop();

                if (!a || !b) {
                    return {
                        call: instructions[index],
                        args: [],
                        before: stack.length,
                        status: `SUB STACK UNDERFLOW at ${hex(ins.pc)}`,
                    };
                }

                stack.push(bin("i64.sub", a, b));
                break;
            }

            case "i64.mul": {
                const b = stack.pop();
                const a = stack.pop();

                if (!a || !b) {
                    return {
                        call: instructions[index],
                        args: [],
                        before: stack.length,
                        status: `MUL STACK UNDERFLOW at ${hex(ins.pc)}`,
                    };
                }

                stack.push(bin("i64.mul", a, b));
                break;
            }

            case "i64.and": {
                const b = stack.pop();
                const a = stack.pop();

                if (!a || !b) {
                    return {
                        call: instructions[index],
                        args: [],
                        before: stack.length,
                        status: `AND STACK UNDERFLOW at ${hex(ins.pc)}`,
                    };
                }

                stack.push(bin("i64.and", a, b));
                break;
            }

            case "i64.or": {
                const b = stack.pop();
                const a = stack.pop();

                if (!a || !b) {
                    return {
                        call: instructions[index],
                        args: [],
                        before: stack.length,
                        status: `OR STACK UNDERFLOW at ${hex(ins.pc)}`,
                    };
                }

                stack.push(bin("i64.or", a, b));
                break;
            }

            case "i64.xor": {
                const b = stack.pop();
                const a = stack.pop();

                if (!a || !b) {
                    return {
                        call: instructions[index],
                        args: [],
                        before: stack.length,
                        status: `XOR STACK UNDERFLOW at ${hex(ins.pc)}`,
                    };
                }

                stack.push(bin("i64.xor", a, b));
                break;
            }

            case "i64.shl": {
                const b = stack.pop();
                const a = stack.pop();

                if (!a || !b) {
                    return {
                        call: instructions[index],
                        args: [],
                        before: stack.length,
                        status: `SHL STACK UNDERFLOW at ${hex(ins.pc)}`,
                    };
                }

                stack.push(bin("i64.shl", a, b));
                break;
            }

            case "i64.shr_s": {
                const b = stack.pop();
                const a = stack.pop();

                if (!a || !b) {
                    return {
                        call: instructions[index],
                        args: [],
                        before: stack.length,
                        status: `SHR_S STACK UNDERFLOW at ${hex(ins.pc)}`,
                    };
                }

                stack.push(bin("i64.shr_s", a, b));
                break;
            }

            case "i64.shr_u": {
                const b = stack.pop();
                const a = stack.pop();

                if (!a || !b) {
                    return {
                        call: instructions[index],
                        args: [],
                        before: stack.length,
                        status: `SHR_U STACK UNDERFLOW at ${hex(ins.pc)}`,
                    };
                }

                stack.push(bin("i64.shr_u", a, b));
                break;
            }

            case "i64.div_s":
            case "i64.div_u":
            case "i64.rem_s":
            case "i64.rem_u":
            case "i64.rotl":
            case "i64.rotr": {
                const b = stack.pop();
                const a = stack.pop();

                if (!a || !b) {
                    return {
                        call: instructions[index],
                        args: [],
                        before: stack.length,
                        status: `${ins.name} STACK UNDERFLOW at ${hex(ins.pc)}`,
                    };
                }

                stack.push(bin(ins.name, a, b));
                break;
            }

            case "call": {
                const target = Number(ins.imm);

                if (target === 87) {
                    /*
                     * Function 87 has 5 parameters.
                     *
                     * Stack order:
                     *   ... arg0 arg1 arg2 arg3 arg4
                     *
                     * The last five stack entries are therefore
                     * the actual call arguments.
                     */
                    if (stack.length < 5) {
                        return {
                            call: instructions[index],
                            args: [],
                            before: stack.length,
                            status:
                                `CALL87 ARG UNDERFLOW at ${hex(ins.pc)}`,
                        };
                    }

                    const args = stack.slice(-5);

                    /*
                     * We are interested in the current CALL only.
                     */
                    if (i === index) {
                        return {
                            call: ins,
                            args,
                            before: stack.length - 5,
                            status: "OK",
                        };
                    }

                    /*
                     * Function 87 returns void.
                     */
                    stack.splice(stack.length - 5, 5);
                } else {
                    /*
                     * Unknown call.
                     *
                     * Do not invent its return values.
                     * For Function 122 this trace is primarily used
                     * to recover the arguments of CALL 87.
                     */
                    stack.length = Math.max(0, stack.length - 0);
                }

                break;
            }

            case "end":
                break;

            default:
                return {
                    call: instructions[index],
                    args: [],
                    before: stack.length,
                    status:
                        `UNSUPPORTED ${ins.name} (${hex(ins.opcode)}) at ${hex(ins.pc)}`,
                };
        }
    }

    return {
        call: instructions[index],
        args: [],
        before: stack.length,
        status: "NOT REACHED",
    };
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" CALL 87 DESTINATION / ARGUMENT RECOVERY");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");

for (let i = 0; i < instructions.length; i++) {
    if (
        instructions[i].name === "call" &&
        instructions[i].imm === "87"
    ) {
        const t = traceCall(i);

        console.log(`CALL @ ${hex(t.call.pc)}`);

        if (t.status !== "OK") {
            console.log("  STATUS :", t.status);
            console.log("");
            continue;
        }

        console.log("  ARG0 / DESTINATION =", t.args[0].text);
        console.log("  ARG1 / A           =", t.args[1].text);
        console.log("  ARG2                =", t.args[2].text);
        console.log("  ARG3 / B           =", t.args[3].text);
        console.log("  ARG4                =", t.args[4].text);
        console.log("");
    }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" END");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceFunction122SlotConsumersV2.ts

import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const START = 0x1226d;
const END   = 0x1295b;

function hex(n: number): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function readULEB(buf: Buffer, p: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readSLEB(buf: Buffer, p: number): { value: bigint; next: number } {
    let result = 0n;
    let shift = 0n;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = buf[p++];
        result |= BigInt(b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            break;

        shift += 7n;
    }

    if ((shift < 64n) && (b & 0x40)) {
        result |= (-1n) << (shift + 7n);
    }

    return { value: result, next: p };
}

type Instr = {
    pc: number;
    op: number;
    name: string;
    imm?: number;
    imm2?: number;
};

function ins(
    pc: number,
    op: number,
    name: string,
    imm?: number,
    imm2?: number
): Instr {
    return { pc, op, name, imm, imm2 };
}

function decode(buf: Buffer, p: number): { x: Instr; next: number } {
    const pc = p;
    const op = buf[p++];

    switch (op) {

        // --------------------------------------------------------
        // CONTROL
        // --------------------------------------------------------

        case 0x00:
            return { x: ins(pc, op, "unreachable"), next: p };

        case 0x01:
            return { x: ins(pc, op, "nop"), next: p };

        case 0x02: {
            const b = readSLEB(buf, p);
            return {
                x: ins(pc, op, "block"),
                next: b.next
            };
        }

        case 0x03: {
            const b = readSLEB(buf, p);
            return {
                x: ins(pc, op, "loop"),
                next: b.next
            };
        }

        case 0x04: {
            const b = readSLEB(buf, p);
            return {
                x: ins(pc, op, "if"),
                next: b.next
            };
        }

        case 0x05:
            return { x: ins(pc, op, "else"), next: p };

        case 0x0b:
            return { x: ins(pc, op, "end"), next: p };

        case 0x0c: {
            const x = readULEB(buf, p);
            return {
                x: ins(pc, op, "br", x.value),
                next: x.next
            };
        }

        case 0x0d: {
            const x = readULEB(buf, p);
            return {
                x: ins(pc, op, "br_if", x.value),
                next: x.next
            };
        }

        case 0x0e: {
            const n = readULEB(buf, p);
            p = n.next;

            for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
                const x = readULEB(buf, p);
                p = x.next;
            }

            const def = readULEB(buf, p);

            return {
                x: ins(pc, op, "br_table", n.value, def.value),
                next: def.next
            };
        }

        case 0x0f:
            return { x: ins(pc, op, "return"), next: p };

        case 0x10: {
            const x = readULEB(buf, p);
            return {
                x: ins(pc, op, "call", x.value),
                next: x.next
            };
        }

        // --------------------------------------------------------
        // LOCALS
        // --------------------------------------------------------

        case 0x20: {
            const x = readULEB(buf, p);
            return {
                x: ins(pc, op, "local.get", x.value),
                next: x.next
            };
        }

        case 0x21: {
            const x = readULEB(buf, p);
            return {
                x: ins(pc, op, "local.set", x.value),
                next: x.next
            };
        }

        case 0x22: {
            const x = readULEB(buf, p);
            return {
                x: ins(pc, op, "local.tee", x.value),
                next: x.next
            };
        }

        // --------------------------------------------------------
        // CONSTANTS
        // --------------------------------------------------------

        case 0x41: {
            const x = readSLEB(buf, p);

            return {
                x: ins(pc, op, "i32.const"),
                next: x.next
            };
        }

        case 0x42: {
            const x = readSLEB(buf, p);

            return {
                x: ins(pc, op, "i64.const"),
                next: x.next
            };
        }

        // --------------------------------------------------------
        // LOADS
        // --------------------------------------------------------

        case 0x28: {
            const a = readULEB(buf, p);
            const o = readULEB(buf, a.next);

            return {
                x: ins(pc, op, "i32.load", a.value, o.value),
                next: o.next
            };
        }

        case 0x29: {
            const a = readULEB(buf, p);
            const o = readULEB(buf, a.next);

            return {
                x: ins(pc, op, "i64.load", a.value, o.value),
                next: o.next
            };
        }

        // --------------------------------------------------------
        // STORES
        // --------------------------------------------------------

        case 0x36: {
            const a = readULEB(buf, p);
            const o = readULEB(buf, a.next);

            return {
                x: ins(pc, op, "i32.store", a.value, o.value),
                next: o.next
            };
        }

        case 0x37: {
            const a = readULEB(buf, p);
            const o = readULEB(buf, a.next);

            return {
                x: ins(pc, op, "i64.store", a.value, o.value),
                next: o.next
            };
        }

        // --------------------------------------------------------
        // I64 ALU
        // --------------------------------------------------------

        case 0x7c: return { x: ins(pc, op, "i64.add"), next: p };
        case 0x7d: return { x: ins(pc, op, "i64.sub"), next: p };
        case 0x7e: return { x: ins(pc, op, "i64.mul"), next: p };
        case 0x7f: return { x: ins(pc, op, "i64.div_s"), next: p };
        case 0x80: return { x: ins(pc, op, "i64.div_u"), next: p };
        case 0x81: return { x: ins(pc, op, "i64.rem_s"), next: p };
        case 0x82: return { x: ins(pc, op, "i64.rem_u"), next: p };
        case 0x83: return { x: ins(pc, op, "i64.and"), next: p };
        case 0x84: return { x: ins(pc, op, "i64.or"), next: p };
        case 0x85: return { x: ins(pc, op, "i64.xor"), next: p };
        case 0x86: return { x: ins(pc, op, "i64.shl"), next: p };
        case 0x87: return { x: ins(pc, op, "i64.shr_s"), next: p };
        case 0x88: return { x: ins(pc, op, "i64.shr_u"), next: p };
        case 0x89: return { x: ins(pc, op, "i64.rotl"), next: p };
        case 0x8a: return { x: ins(pc, op, "i64.rotr"), next: p };

        default:
            return {
                x: ins(pc, op, `UNKNOWN(0x${op.toString(16)})`),
                next: p
            };
    }
}

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

console.log("============================================================");

console.log("Range     :", `${hex(START)} .. ${hex(END)}`);

const code: Instr[] = [];

let p = START;

while (p < END) {
    const d = decode(wasm, p);
    code.push(d.x);

    if (d.next <= p) {
        throw new Error(`Decoder stalled at ${hex(p)}`);
    }

    p = d.next;
}

if (p !== END) {
    throw new Error("EXACT END MISMATCH");

const calls = code.filter(

);

import fs from "fs";

const WASM_PATH =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const START = 0x0001226d;
const END = 0x0001295b;

type Value = {
  id: string;
  kind: "i32" | "i64" | "unknown";
};

type Instr = {
  pos: number;
  op: number;
  name: string;
  arg?: bigint | number;
};

function hx(n: number): string {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function readULEB(buf: Buffer, p: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = buf[p++];

    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value, next: p };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readSLEB(buf: Buffer, p: number) {
  let value = 0n;
  let shift = 0n;

  while (true) {
    const b = buf[p++];

    value |= BigInt(b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      if (b & 0x40) {
        value |= (-1n) << (shift + 7n);
      }

      return { value, next: p };
    }

    shift += 7n;
  }
}

function decode(buf: Buffer): Instr[] {
  const out: Instr[] = [];

  let p = START;

  while (p <= END) {
    const pos = p;
    const op = buf[p++];

    let name = `UNKNOWN(0x${op.toString(16)})`;
    let arg: bigint | number | undefined;

    switch (op) {
      case 0x00:
        name = "unreachable";
        break;

      case 0x01:
        name = "nop";
        break;

      case 0x02:
        name = "block";
        break;

      case 0x03:
        name = "loop";
        break;

      case 0x04:
        name = "if";
        break;

      case 0x05:
        name = "else";
        break;

      case 0x0b:
        name = "end";
        break;

      case 0x0c: {
        const r = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = r.next;
        arg = r.value;
        name = "br";
        break;
      }

      case 0x0d: {
        const r = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = r.next;
        arg = r.value;
        name = "br_if";
        break;
      }

      case 0x0f:
        name = "return";
        break;

      case 0x10: {
        const r = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = r.next;
        arg = r.value;
        name = "call";
        break;
      }

      case 0x20: {
        const r = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = r.next;
        arg = r.value;
        name = "local.get";
        break;
      }

      case 0x21: {
        const r = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = r.next;
        arg = r.value;
        name = "local.set";
        break;
      }

      case 0x22: {
        const r = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = r.next;
        arg = r.value;
        name = "local.tee";
        break;
      }

      case 0x23: {
        const r = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = r.next;
        arg = r.value;
        name = "global.get";
        break;
      }

      case 0x24: {
        const r = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = r.next;
        arg = r.value;
        name = "global.set";
        break;
      }

      case 0x28: {
        const align = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = align.next;

        const off = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = off.next;

        arg = off.value;
        name = "i32.load";
        break;
      }

      case 0x29: {
        const align = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = align.next;

        const off = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = off.next;

        arg = off.value;
        name = "i64.load";
        break;
      }

      case 0x36: {
        const align = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = align.next;

        const off = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = off.next;

        arg = off.value;
        name = "i32.store";
        break;
      }

      case 0x37: {
        const align = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = align.next;

        const off = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = off.next;

        arg = off.value;
        name = "i64.store";
        break;
      }

      case 0x41: {
        const r = readSLEB(buf, p);
        p = r.next;
        arg = r.value;
        name = "i32.const";
        break;
      }

      case 0x42: {
        const r = readSLEB(buf, p);
        p = r.next;
        arg = r.value;
        name = "i64.const";
        break;
      }

      case 0x45:
        name = "i32.eqz";
        break;

      case 0x46:
        name = "i32.eq";
        break;

      case 0x47:
        name = "i32.ne";
        break;

      case 0x48:
        name = "i32.lt_s";
        break;

      case 0x49:
        name = "i32.lt_u";
        break;

      case 0x4a:
        name = "i32.gt_s";
        break;

      case 0x4b:
        name = "i32.gt_u";
        break;

      case 0x4c:
        name = "i32.le_s";
        break;

      case 0x4d:
        name = "i32.le_u";
        break;

      case 0x4e:
        name = "i32.ge_s";
        break;

      case 0x4f:
        name = "i32.ge_u";
        break;

      case 0x50:
        name = "i64.eqz";
        break;

      case 0x51:
        name = "i64.eq";
        break;

      case 0x52:
        name = "i64.ne";
        break;

      case 0x53:
        name = "i64.lt_s";
        break;

      case 0x54:
        name = "i64.lt_u";
        break;

      case 0x55:
        name = "i64.gt_s";
        break;

      case 0x56:
        name = "i64.gt_u";
        break;

      case 0x57:
        name = "i64.le_s";
        break;

      case 0x58:
        name = "i64.le_u";
        break;

      case 0x59:
        name = "i64.ge_s";
        break;

      case 0x5a:
        name = "i64.ge_u";
        break;

      case 0x6a:
        name = "i32.add";
        break;

      case 0x6b:
        name = "i32.sub";
        break;

      case 0x7c:
        name = "i64.add";
        break;

      case 0x7d:
        name = "i64.sub";
        break;

      case 0x7e:
        name = "i64.mul";
        break;

      case 0x7f:
        name = "i64.div_s";
        break;

      case 0x80:
        name = "i64.div_u";
        break;

      case 0x81:
        name = "i64.rem_s";
        break;

      case 0x82:
        name = "i64.rem_u";
        break;

      case 0x83:
        name = "i64.and";
        break;

      case 0x84:
        name = "i64.or";
        break;

      case 0x85:
        name = "i64.xor";
        break;

      case 0x86:
        name = "i64.shl";
        break;

      case 0x87:
        name = "i64.shr_s";
        break;

      case 0x88:
        name = "i64.shr_u";
        break;

      case 0x89:
        name = "i64.rotl";
        break;

      case 0x8a:
        name = "i64.rotr";
        break;
    }

    out.push({ pos, op, name, arg });

    if (p > END + 1) break;
  }

  return out;
}

function pop(stack: Value[], n: number): Value[] {
  const r: Value[] = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    r.unshift(
      stack.length
        ? stack.pop()!
        : { id: "STACK_UNDERFLOW", kind: "unknown" }
    );
  }

  return r;
}

function push(stack: Value[], id: string, kind: "i32" | "i64" | "unknown") {
  stack.push({ id, kind });
}

function fmt(v: Value | undefined): string {
  if (!v) return "NONE";
  return v.id;
}

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM_PATH);
const ins = decode(wasm);

console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log(" FUNCTION 122 STACK PROVENANCE v2");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log(" EXACT CALL 87 RESULT TRACKING");
console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log();

console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`Range     : ${hx(START)} .. ${hx(END)}`);
console.log();

const locals = new Map<number, Value>();
const stack: Value[] = [];

let call87 = 0;

const resultConsumers: {
  callNo: number;
  pos: number;
  low: Value;
  high: Value;
  consumers: string[];
}[] = [];

function noteConsumer(callNo: number, value: Value, pos: number, op: string) {
  if (!value.id.startsWith("CALL87_")) return;

  const rec = resultConsumers.find((x) => x.callNo === callNo);

  if (rec) {
    rec.consumers.push(`${hx(pos)} ${op} <- ${value.id}`);
  }
}

/*
 * Map a CALL87 result value to its CALL number.
 *
 * Each result is represented by two explicit symbolic values.
 */
const valueOwner = new Map<string, number>();

function rememberResult(callNo: number, low: Value, high: Value) {
  valueOwner.set(low.id, callNo);
  valueOwner.set(high.id, callNo);

  resultConsumers.push({
    callNo,
    pos: 0,
    low,
    high,
    consumers: [],
  });
}

function consumeValue(v: Value, pos: number, op: string) {
  const owner = valueOwner.get(v.id);

  if (owner !== undefined) {
    noteConsumer(owner, v, pos, op);
  }
}

for (let i = 0; i < ins.length; i++) {
  const x = ins[i];

  /*
   * CALL 87
   *
   * Before the call:
   *
   *   destination
   *   A
   *   arg2
   *   B
   *   arg4
   *
   * Function 87 returns:
   *
   *   low
   *   high
   *
   * The exact ABI has already been established separately.
   */
  if (x.name === "call" && Number(x.arg) === 87) {
    const args = pop(stack, 5);

    call87++;

    const low: Value = {
      id: `CALL87_${call87}_LOW`,
      kind: "i64",
    };

    const high: Value = {
      id: `CALL87_${call87}_HIGH`,
      kind: "i64",
    };

    rememberResult(call87, low, high);

    resultConsumers[resultConsumers.length - 1].pos = x.pos;

    /*
     * WASM multi-value return order:
     * push result values back on the stack.
     */
    push(stack, low.id, "i64");
    push(stack, high.id, "i64");

    console.log();
    console.log(
      `CALL #${String(call87).padStart(2, "0")} @ ${hx(x.pos)}`
    );

    console.log("  CALL INPUT STACK:");

    for (let j = 0; j < args.length; j++) {
      console.log(
        `    arg${j}: ${args[j].id} (${args[j].kind})`
      );
    }

    console.log("  RESULT:");
    console.log(`    LOW  = ${low.id}`);
    console.log(`    HIGH = ${high.id}`);

    continue;
  }

  if (x.name === "local.get") {
    const n = Number(x.arg);

    const v =
      locals.get(n) ?? {
        id: `L${n}_UNKNOWN`,
        kind: "unknown",
      };

    push(stack, v.kind === "i32" ? v.id : v.id, v.kind);
    continue;
  }

  if (x.name === "local.set") {
    const n = Number(x.arg);
    const [v] = pop(stack, 1);

    consumeValue(v, x.pos, "local.set");

    locals.set(n, v);
    continue;
  }

  if (x.name === "local.tee") {
    const n = Number(x.arg);

    const v = stack.length
      ? stack[stack.length - 1]
      : {
          id: "STACK_UNDERFLOW",
          kind: "unknown" as const,
        };

    consumeValue(v, x.pos, "local.tee");

    locals.set(n, v);
    continue;
  }

  if (x.name === "i32.const") {
    push(stack, `I32CONST(${x.arg})`, "i32");
    continue;
  }

  if (x.name === "i64.const") {
    push(stack, `I64CONST(${x.arg})`, "i64");
    continue;
  }

  if (x.name === "i64.load") {
    const [addr] = pop(stack, 1);

    push(
      stack,
      `MEM64[${addr.id}+${Number(x.arg)}]`,
      "i64"
    );

    continue;
  }

  if (x.name === "i32.load") {
    const [addr] = pop(stack, 1);

    push(
      stack,
      `MEM32[${addr.id}+${Number(x.arg)}]`,
      "i32"
    );

    continue;
  }

  if (x.name === "i32.add") {
    const [a, b] = pop(stack, 2);

    consumeValue(a, x.pos, "i32.add");
    consumeValue(b, x.pos, "i32.add");

    push(
      stack,
      `(${a.id}+${b.id})`,
      "i32"
    );

    continue;
  }

  if (x.name === "i32.sub") {
    const [a, b] = pop(stack, 2);

    consumeValue(a, x.pos, "i32.sub");
    consumeValue(b, x.pos, "i32.sub");

    push(
      stack,
      `(${a.id}-${b.id})`,
      "i32"
    );

    continue;
  }

  if (
    x.name === "i64.add" ||
    x.name === "i64.sub" ||
    x.name === "i64.mul" ||
    x.name === "i64.and" ||
    x.name === "i64.or" ||
    x.name === "i64.xor" ||
    x.name === "i64.shl" ||
    x.name === "i64.shr_s" ||
    x.name === "i64.shr_u"
  ) {
    const [a, b] = pop(stack, 2);

    consumeValue(a, x.pos, x.name);
    consumeValue(b, x.pos, x.name);

    push(
      stack,
      `${x.name}(${a.id},${b.id})`,
      "i64"
    );

    continue;
  }

  if (x.name === "i64.store") {
    const [addr, value] = pop(stack, 2);

    consumeValue(addr, x.pos, "i64.store");
    consumeValue(value, x.pos, "i64.store");

    console.log(
      `STORE @ ${hx(x.pos)}`
    );
    console.log(`  address = ${addr.id}`);
    console.log(`  value   = ${value.id}`);

    continue;
  }

  /*
   * Unknown instructions are deliberately NOT guessed.
   *
   * We only print them so that a later exact decoder can add their
   * stack effects without corrupting provenance.
   */
}

console.log();
console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log(" CALL 87 CONSUMER SUMMARY");
console.log("=".repeat(72));

for (const r of resultConsumers) {
  console.log();
  console.log(

  );

  console.log(`  LOW  : ${r.low.id}`);

  } else {
    console.log("  CONSUMERS:");

    for (const c of r.consumers) {
      console.log(`    ${c}`);
    }
  }
}

console.log();

    );

const fs = require("fs");

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const TARGET = 1303;
const IMPORTED = 83;
const bytes = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function uleb(p) {
    let v = 0, s = 0;
    while (true) {
        const b = bytes[p++];
        v += (b & 127) * 2 ** s;
        if (!(b & 128)) return { value: v, next: p };
        s += 7;
    }
}

function instr(p) {
    const addr = p;
    const op = bytes[p++];

    if (op === 0x20) {
        const x = uleb(p);
        return [x.next, `local.get ${x.value}`];
    }
    if (op === 0x21) {
        const x = uleb(p);
        return [x.next, `local.set ${x.value}`];
    }
    if (op === 0x22) {
        const x = uleb(p);
        return [x.next, `local.tee ${x.value}`];
    }
    if (op === 0x41) {
        const x = uleb(p);
        return [x.next, `i32.const ${x.value} (0x${x.value.toString(16)})`];
    }
    if (op === 0x28) {
        const a = uleb(p);
        const o = uleb(a.next);
        return [o.next, `i32.load align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`];
    }
    if (op === 0x2d) {
        const a = uleb(p);
        const o = uleb(a.next);
        return [o.next, `i32.load8_u align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`];
    }
    if (op === 0x36) {
        const a = uleb(p);
        const o = uleb(a.next);
        return [o.next, `i32.store align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`];
    }
    if (op === 0x6a) return [p, "i32.add"];
    if (op === 0x6b) return [p, "i32.sub"];
    if (op === 0x45) return [p, "i32.eqz"];
    if (op === 0x46) return [p, "i32.eq"];
    if (op === 0x47) return [p, "i32.ne"];
    if (op === 0x4f) return [p, "i32.ge_u"];
    if (op === 0x10) {
        const x = uleb(p);
        return [x.next, `call ${x.value}`];
    }
    if (op === 0x0b) return [p, "end"];

    return [p, `opcode_0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`];
}

let p = 8;
let code;

while (p < bytes.length) {
    const id = bytes[p++];
    const z = uleb(p);
    const start = z.next;
    const end = start + z.value;

    if (id === 10) {
        code = { start, end };
        break;
    }
    p = end;
}

let q = code.start;
const count = uleb(q);
q = count.next;

let body;

for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const z = uleb(q);
    const start = z.next;
    const end = start + z.value;

    if (IMPORTED + i === TARGET) {
        body = { start, end, size: z.value };
        break;
    }

    q = end;
}

if (!body) throw new Error("FUNC 1303 NOT FOUND");

q = body.start;

const groups = uleb(q);
q = groups.next;

for (let i = 0; i < groups.value; i++) {
    const n = uleb(q);
    q = n.next + 1;
}

const ins = [];

while (q < body.end) {
    const addr = q;
    const [next, text] = instr(q);
    ins.push({ addr, text });
    q = next;

    if (text === "end") break;
}

const fs = require("fs");

const FILE =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET = 131;

const b = fs.readFileSync(FILE);

function leb(pos) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const x = b[pos++];

        value |= (x & 0x7f) << shift;

        if (!(x & 0x80)) {
            return {
                value: value >>> 0,
                next: pos
            };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function hex(x) {
    return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

let pos = 8;
let codeStart = -1;

while (pos < b.length) {

    const sectionId = b[pos++];

    const r = leb(pos);
    const size = r.value;
    pos = r.next;

    if (sectionId === 10) {
        codeStart = pos;
        break;
    }

    pos += size;
}

if (codeStart < 0)
    throw new Error("CODE section not found");

let p = codeStart;

const countR = leb(p);
const bodyCount = countR.value;
p = countR.next;

for (let bodyIndex = 0; bodyIndex < bodyCount; bodyIndex++) {

    const sizeR = leb(p);
    const bodySize = sizeR.value;
    p = sizeR.next;

    const bodyStart = p;
    const bodyEnd = p + bodySize;

    const funcIndex = bodyIndex + 83;

    if (funcIndex !== TARGET) {
        p = bodyEnd;
        continue;
    }

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("FUNC 131 ANALYSIS");
    console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("BODY INDEX :", bodyIndex);
    console.log("BODY START :", hex(bodyStart));
    console.log("BODY END   :", hex(bodyEnd));
    console.log("SIZE       :", bodySize);
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    // Raw bytes
    console.log("RAW BYTES");
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    for (let q = bodyStart; q < bodyEnd; q += 16) {

        const end = Math.min(q + 16, bodyEnd);

        let s = "";

        for (let x = q; x < end; x++) {
            s += b[x].toString(16).padStart(2, "0") + " ";
        }

        console.log(hex(q), s);
    }

    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    // Decode only the important memory/call instructions.
    let q = bodyStart;

    const localsR = leb(q);
    const localDeclCount = localsR.value;
    q = localsR.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < localDeclCount; i++) {
        const n = leb(q);
        q = n.next;
        q++;
    }

    while (q < bodyEnd) {

        const at = q;
        const op = b[q++];

        if (op === 0x10) {

            const r = leb(q);
            q = r.next;

            console.log(
                hex(at),
                "call",
                r.value
            );

            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x11) {

            const r = leb(q);
            q = r.next;

            const table = b[q++];

            console.log(
                hex(at),
                "call_indirect type=" + r.value,
                "table=" + table
            );

            continue;
        }

        if (
            op === 0x20 ||
            op === 0x21 ||
            op === 0x22 ||
            op === 0x23 ||
            op === 0x24
        ) {

            const r = leb(q);
            q = r.next;

            const names = {
                0x20: "local.get",
                0x21: "local.set",
                0x22: "local.tee",
                0x23: "global.get",
                0x24: "global.set"
            };

            console.log(
                hex(at),
                names[op],
                r.value
            );

            continue;
        }

        if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x3e) {

            const a = leb(q);
            q = a.next;

            const o = leb(q);
            q = o.next;

            console.log(
                hex(at),
                "MEM",
                "opcode=" + hex(op),
                "align=" + a.value,
                "offset=" + o.value
            );

            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x41) {

            const r = leb(q);
            q = r.next;

            console.log(
                hex(at),
                "i32.const",
                r.value,
                "(" + hex(r.value) + ")"
            );

            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x42) {

            const r = leb(q);
            q = r.next;

            console.log(
                hex(at),
                "i64.const",
                r.value
            );

            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0xfc) {

            const r = leb(q);
            q = r.next;

            console.log(
                hex(at),
                "0xfc subopcode",
                r.value
            );

            continue;
        }

        // IMPORTANT:
        // 0xfe = atomic prefix.
        // Decode its subopcode instead of losing alignment.
        if (op === 0xfe) {

            const r = leb(q);
            q = r.next;

            console.log(
                hex(at),
                "0xfe atomic subopcode",
                r.value
            );

            // Atomic instructions may have memarg:
            // align + offset.
            //
            // For the purpose of this trace, dump the following
            // bytes rather than guessing their complete semantics.

            console.log(
                "   NEXT BYTES:",
                Array.from(
                    b.slice(q, Math.min(q + 8, bodyEnd))
                )
                .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
                .join(" ")
            );

            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0xd2) {

            const r = leb(q);
            q = r.next;

            console.log(
                hex(at),
                "ref.func",
                r.value
            );

            continue;
        }

        // Structural opcodes
        if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
            q++;
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
            const r = leb(q);
            q = r.next;
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x0e) {

            const r = leb(q);
            q = r.next;

            for (let i = 0; i < r.value; i++) {
                const x = leb(q);
                q = x.next;
            }

            const d = leb(q);
            q = d.next;

            continue;
        }
    }

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("FUNC 131 END");
    console.log("============================================================");

    break;
}

FILE: scripts\traceFunction1385.js
