const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET = 1385;

const bytes = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

function uleb(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = bytes[pos++];

    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;

    shift += 7;

    if (shift > 35) {
      throw new Error("ULEB too long");
    }
  }

  return {
    value: value >>> 0,
    next: pos
  };
}

function sleb(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b;

  while (true) {
    b = bytes[pos++];

    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;

    if (shift > 35) {
      throw new Error("SLEB too long");
    }
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    value |= (~0 << shift);
  }

  return {
    value: value | 0,
    next: pos
  };
}

function hex(n) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function readSections() {
  let p = 8;
  const sections = [];

  while (p < bytes.length) {
    const id = bytes[p++];

    const len = uleb(p);
    p = len.next;

    sections.push({
      id,
      start: p,
      end: p + len.value
    });

    p += len.value;
  }

  return sections;
}

function parseImports(section) {
  let p = section.start;

  const count = uleb(p);
  p = count.next;

  let functions = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {

    const mod = uleb(p);
    p = mod.next + mod.value;

    const name = uleb(p);
    p = name.next + name.value;

    const kind = bytes[p++];

    if (kind === 0) {
      const type = uleb(p);
      p = type.next;
      functions++;
    }

    else if (kind === 1) {
      p++;

      const flags = bytes[p++];

      const min = uleb(p);
      p = min.next;

      if (flags & 1) {
        const max = uleb(p);
        p = max.next;
      }
    }

    else if (kind === 2) {
      const flags = bytes[p++];

      const min = uleb(p);
      p = min.next;

      if (flags & 1) {
        const max = uleb(p);
        p = max.next;
      }
    }

    else if (kind === 3) {
      p += 2;
    }
  }

  return functions;
}

function parseFunctionTypes(section) {
  let p = section.start;

  const count = uleb(p);
  p = count.next;

  const ids = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const x = uleb(p);
    p = x.next;

    ids.push(x.value);
  }

  return ids;
}

function parseCode(section, functionTypes, importedCount) {
  let p = section.start;

  const count = uleb(p);
  p = count.next;

  const funcs = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {

    const size = uleb(p);
    p = size.next;

    const bodyStart = p;
    const bodyEnd = p + size.value;

    funcs.push({
      funcIndex: importedCount + i,
      typeIndex: functionTypes[i],
      start: bodyStart,
      end: bodyEnd
    });

    p = bodyEnd;
  }

  return funcs;
}

function decode(func) {

  let p = func.start;

  const localCount = uleb(p);
  p = localCount.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < localCount.value; i++) {
    const n = uleb(p);
    p = n.next;
    p++;
  }

  console.log(`BODY : ${hex(func.start)} .. ${hex(func.end)}`);
  console.log(`TYPE : ${func.typeIndex}`);

  console.log(
    "------------------------------------------------------------"
  );

  while (p < func.end) {

    const at = p;
    const op = bytes[p++];

    let text = null;

    switch (op) {

      case 0x00:
        text = "unreachable";
        break;

      case 0x01:
        text = "nop";
        break;

      case 0x0b:
        text = "end";
        break;

      case 0x0f:
        text = "return";
        break;

      case 0x10: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        text = `call ${x.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x11: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        const y = uleb(p);
        p = y.next;

        text =
          `call_indirect type=${x.value} table=${y.value}`;

        break;
      }

      case 0x1a:
        text = "drop";
        break;

      case 0x1b:
        text = "select";
        break;

      case 0x20: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        text = `local.get ${x.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x21: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        text = `local.set ${x.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x22: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        text = `local.tee ${x.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x23: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        text = `global.get ${x.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x24: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        text = `global.set ${x.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x41: {
        const x = sleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        text =
          `i32.const ${x.value} (${hex(x.value >>> 0)})`;

        break;
      }

      case 0x42: {

        let value = 0n;
        let shift = 0n;

        while (true) {

          const b = BigInt(bytes[p++]);

          value |=
            (b & 0x7fn) << shift;

          if ((b & 0x80n) === 0n) break;

          shift += 7n;
        }

        text = `i64.const ${value}`;

        break;
      }

      case 0x28:
      case 0x29:
      case 0x2c:
      case 0x2d:
      case 0x2e:
      case 0x2f:
      case 0x30:
      case 0x31:
      case 0x32:
      case 0x33:
      case 0x34:
      case 0x35: {

        const align = uleb(p);
        p = align.next;

        const offset = uleb(p);
        p = offset.next;

        const names = {
          0x28: "i32.load",
          0x29: "i64.load",
          0x2c: "i32.load8_s",
          0x2d: "i32.load8_u",
          0x2e: "i32.load16_s",
          0x2f: "i32.load16_u",
          0x30: "i64.load8_s",
          0x31: "i64.load8_u",
          0x32: "i64.load16_s",
          0x33: "i64.load16_u",
          0x34: "i64.load32_s",
          0x35: "i64.load32_u"
        };

        text =
          `${names[op]} align=${align.value} offset=${offset.value}`;

        break;
      }

      case 0x36:
      case 0x37:
      case 0x3a:
      case 0x3b:
      case 0x3c:
      case 0x3d:
      case 0x3e: {

        const align = uleb(p);
        p = align.next;

        const offset = uleb(p);
        p = offset.next;

        const names = {
          0x36: "i32.store",
          0x37: "i64.store",
          0x3a: "i32.store8",
          0x3b: "i32.store16",
          0x3c: "i64.store8",
          0x3d: "i64.store16",
          0x3e: "i64.store32"
        };

        text =
          `${names[op]} align=${align.value} offset=${offset.value}`;

        break;
      }

      case 0x45:
        text = "i32.eqz";
        break;

      case 0x46:
        text = "i32.eq";
        break;

      case 0x47:
        text = "i32.ne";
        break;

      case 0x48:
        text = "i32.lt_s";
        break;

      case 0x49:
        text = "i32.lt_u";
        break;

      case 0x4a:
        text = "i32.gt_s";
        break;

      case 0x4b:
        text = "i32.gt_u";
        break;

      case 0x4c:
        text = "i32.le_s";
        break;

      case 0x4d:
        text = "i32.le_u";
        break;

      case 0x4e:
        text = "i32.ge_s";
        break;

      case 0x4f:
        text = "i32.ge_u";
        break;

      case 0x6a:
        text = "i32.add";
        break;

      case 0x6b:
        text = "i32.sub";
        break;

      case 0x6c:
        text = "i32.mul";
        break;

      case 0x71:
        text = "i32.and";
        break;

      case 0x72:
        text = "i32.or";
        break;

      case 0x73:
        text = "i32.xor";
        break;

      case 0x74:
        text = "i32.shl";
        break;

      case 0x75:
        text = "i32.shr_s";
        break;

      case 0x76:
        text = "i32.shr_u";
        break;

      case 0x77:
        text = "i32.rotl";
        break;

      case 0x78:
        text = "i32.rotr";
        break;

      case 0x02:
      case 0x03:
      case 0x04: {

        const blockType = bytes[p++];

        const name =
          op === 0x02
            ? "block"
            : op === 0x03
              ? "loop"
              : "if";

        text =
          `${name} type=${hex(blockType)}`;

        break;
      }

      case 0x05:
        text = "else";
        break;

      case 0x0c: {

        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        text = `br ${x.value}`;

        break;
      }

      case 0x0d: {

        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        text = `br_if ${x.value}`;

        break;
      }

      case 0x0e: {

        const n = uleb(p);
        p = n.next;

        const labels = [];

        for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {

          const x = uleb(p);
          p = x.next;

          labels.push(x.value);
        }

        const d = uleb(p);
        p = d.next;

        labels.push(`default=${d.value}`);

        text =
          `br_table ${labels.join(",")}`;

        break;
      }

      default:

        console.log(
          `${hex(at)}  UNKNOWN opcode ${hex(op)}`
        );

        console.log(
          "RAW REMAINING : " +
          Buffer.from(
            bytes.subarray(at, func.end)
          ).toString("hex")
        );

        return;
    }

    console.log(`${hex(at)}  ${text}`);
  }
}

  "============================================================"

const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET = 1386;

const bytes = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

function uleb(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = bytes[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;

    shift += 7;
    if (shift > 35) throw new Error("ULEB too long");
  }

  return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };
}

function sleb(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b;

  while (true) {
    b = bytes[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
    if (shift > 35) throw new Error("SLEB too long");
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    value |= (~0 << shift);
  }

  return { value: value | 0, next: pos };
}

function hex(n) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function readSections() {
  let p = 8;
  const sections = [];

  while (p < bytes.length) {
    const id = bytes[p++];

    const len = uleb(p);
    p = len.next;

    sections.push({
      id,
      start: p,
      end: p + len.value
    });

    p += len.value;
  }

  return sections;
}

function parseTypes(section) {
  let p = section.start;

  const count = uleb(p);
  p = count.next;

  const types = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const form = bytes[p++];

    if (form !== 0x60) {
      throw new Error(`Unsupported type form at ${hex(p - 1)}`);
    }

    const pc = uleb(p);
    p = pc.next;

    const params = [];

    for (let j = 0; j < pc.value; j++) {
      params.push(bytes[p++]);
    }

    const rc = uleb(p);
    p = rc.next;

    const results = [];

    for (let j = 0; j < rc.value; j++) {
      results.push(bytes[p++]);
    }

    types.push({ params, results });
  }

  return types;
}

function parseImports(section) {
  let p = section.start;

  const count = uleb(p);
  p = count.next;

  let functions = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const mod = uleb(p);
    p = mod.next + mod.value;

    const name = uleb(p);
    p = name.next + name.value;

    const kind = bytes[p++];

    if (kind === 0) {
      const t = uleb(p);
      p = t.next;
      functions++;
    } else if (kind === 1) {
      p++;
      const lim = bytes[p++];

      const a = uleb(p);
      p = a.next;

      if (lim & 1) {
        const b = uleb(p);
        p = b.next;
      }
    } else if (kind === 2) {
      const lim = bytes[p++];

      const a = uleb(p);
      p = a.next;

      if (lim & 1) {
        const b = uleb(p);
        p = b.next;
      }
    } else if (kind === 3) {
      p += 2;
    }
  }

  return functions;
}

function parseFunctionTypes(section) {
  let p = section.start;

  const count = uleb(p);
  p = count.next;

  const ids = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const x = uleb(p);
    p = x.next;
    ids.push(x.value);
  }

  return ids;
}

function parseCode(section, functionTypes, importedCount) {
  let p = section.start;

  const count = uleb(p);
  p = count.next;

  const funcs = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const size = uleb(p);
    p = size.next;

    const bodyStart = p;
    const bodyEnd = p + size.value;

    funcs.push({
      funcIndex: importedCount + i,
      typeIndex: functionTypes[i],
      start: bodyStart,
      end: bodyEnd
    });

    p = bodyEnd;
  }

  return funcs;
}

        const name =

        text = `${name} type=${hex(blockType)}`;

        break;

        break;

      case 0x0d: {

        p = x.next;
        text = `br_if ${x.value}`;

const fs = require("fs");

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const bytes = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const TARGET = 165;
const IMPORTED = 83;

function uleb(p) {
    let v = 0, s = 0;
    while (true) {
        const b = bytes[p++];
        v += (b & 127) * 2 ** s;
        if (!(b & 128)) return { value: v, next: p };
        s += 7;
    }
}

function instr(p) {
    const a = p;
    const op = bytes[p++];

    if (op === 0x20) {
        const x = uleb(p);
        return [x.next, `local.get ${x.value}`];
    }

    if (op === 0x21) {
        const x = uleb(p);
        return [x.next, `local.set ${x.value}`];
    }

    if (op === 0x22) {
        const x = uleb(p);
        return [x.next, `local.tee ${x.value}`];
    }

    if (op === 0x41) {
        const x = uleb(p);
        return [x.next, `i32.const ${x.value} (0x${x.value.toString(16)})`];
    }

    if (op === 0x28) {
        const x = uleb(p);
        const y = uleb(x.next);
        return [y.next, `i32.load align=${x.value} offset=${y.value}`];
    }

    if (op === 0x2d) {
        const x = uleb(p);
        const y = uleb(x.next);
        return [y.next, `i32.load8_u align=${x.value} offset=${y.value}`];
    }

    if (op === 0x36) {
        const x = uleb(p);
        const y = uleb(x.next);
        return [y.next, `i32.store align=${x.value} offset=${y.value}`];
    }

    if (op === 0x6a) return [p, "i32.add"];
    if (op === 0x6b) return [p, "i32.sub"];
    if (op === 0x45) return [p, "i32.eqz"];
    if (op === 0x46) return [p, "i32.eq"];
    if (op === 0x47) return [p, "i32.ne"];
    if (op === 0x4f) return [p, "i32.ge_u"];

    if (op === 0x10) {
        const x = uleb(p);
        return [x.next, `call ${x.value}`];
    }

    if (op === 0x0b) return [p, "end"];

    return [p, `opcode_0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`];
}

let p = 8;
let code = null;

while (p < bytes.length) {
    const id = bytes[p++];
    const z = uleb(p);
    const start = z.next;
    const end = start + z.value;

    if (id === 10) {
        code = { start, end };
        break;
    }

    p = end;
}

let q = code.start;
const count = uleb(q);
q = count.next;

let body = null;

for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const z = uleb(q);
    const start = z.next;
    const end = start + z.value;

    if (IMPORTED + i === TARGET) {
        body = { start, end, size: z.value };
        break;
    }

    q = end;
}

if (!body) throw new Error("FUNC 165 NOT FOUND");

let x = body.start;

const groups = uleb(x);
x = groups.next;

for (let i = 0; i < groups.value; i++) {
    const n = uleb(x);
    x = n.next + 1;
}

const ins = [];

while (x < body.end) {
    const addr = x;
    const [next, text] = instr(x);

    ins.push({ addr, text });
    x = next;

    if (text === "end") break;
}

FILE: scripts\traceFunction1795.js

const fs = require("fs");

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm"
);

const TARGET = 1795;

function uleb(pos) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, p = pos;
  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
    s += 7;
  }
  return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
}

function sleb(pos) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, p = pos, b;
  while (true) {
    b = wasm[p++];
    v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
  }
  if (s < 32 && (b & 0x40)) v |= (~0 << s);
  return { value: v | 0, next: p };
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Locate CODE section
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let p = 8;
let codeStart = -1;
let codeEnd = -1;

while (p < wasm.length) {
  const id = wasm[p++];
  const s = uleb(p);
  const size = s.value;
  p = s.next;

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = p;
    codeEnd = p + size;
    break;
  }

  p += size;
}

if (codeStart < 0)
  throw new Error("CODE section not found");

const count = uleb(codeStart);
const bodyCount = count.value;

p = count.next;

let start = -1;
let end = -1;

for (let i = 0; i < bodyCount; i++) {
  const s = uleb(p);
  p = s.next;

  const bs = p;
  const be = p + s.value;

  if (i === TARGET) {
    start = bs;
    end = be;
    break;
  }

  p = be;
}

if (start < 0)
  throw new Error("FUNC 1795 not found");

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Decode
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let q = start;

const locals = uleb(q);
const localDeclCount = locals.value;
q = locals.next;

let localTypes = [];

for (let i = 0; i < localDeclCount; i++) {
  const n = uleb(q);
  q = n.next;

  const type = wasm[q++];

  for (let j = 0; j < n.value; j++)
    localTypes.push(type);
}

const ins = [];

while (q < end) {
  const at = q;
  const op = wasm[q++];

  if (op === 0x0b) {
    ins.push({ at, op, text: "end" });
    break;
  }

  if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
    const x = uleb(q);
    q = x.next;

    const name =
      op === 0x20 ? "local.get" :
      op === 0x21 ? "local.set" :
      "local.tee";

    ins.push({
      at,
      op,
      local: x.value,
      text: `${name} local${x.value}`
    });

    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x41) {
    const x = sleb(q);
    q = x.next;

    ins.push({
      at,
      op,
      value: x.value,
      text: `i32.const ${x.value}`
    });

    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x10) {
    const x = uleb(q);
    q = x.next;

    ins.push({
      at,
      op,
      target: x.value,
      text: `call ${x.value}`
    });

    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x11) {
    const t = uleb(q);
    q = t.next;

    const tb = uleb(q);
    q = tb.next;

    ins.push({
      at,
      op,
      type: t.value,
      table: tb.value,
      text: `call_indirect type=${t.value} table=${tb.value}`
    });

    continue;
  }

  if (
    op === 0x28 ||
    op === 0x29 ||
    op === 0x2d ||
    op === 0x36 ||
    op === 0x37 ||
    op === 0x3a ||
    op === 0x3b ||
    op === 0x3e
  ) {
    const a = uleb(q);
    q = a.next;

    const o = uleb(q);
    q = o.next;

    const names = {
      0x28: "i32.load",
      0x29: "i64.load",
      0x2d: "i32.load8_u",
      0x36: "i32.store",
      0x37: "i64.store",
      0x3a: "i32.store8",
      0x3b: "i32.store16",
      0x3e: "i64.store32"
    };

    ins.push({
      at,
      op,
      offset: o.value,
      text: `${names[op]} align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`
    });

    continue;
  }

  const names = {
    0x02: "block",
    0x03: "loop",
    0x04: "if",
    0x05: "else",
    0x0c: "br",
    0x0d: "br_if",
    0x0f: "return",

    0x45: "i32.eqz",
    0x46: "i32.eq",
    0x47: "i32.ne",
    0x48: "i32.lt_s",
    0x49: "i32.lt_u",
    0x4a: "i32.gt_s",
    0x4b: "i32.gt_u",
    0x4c: "i32.le_s",
    0x4d: "i32.le_u",
    0x4e: "i32.ge_s",
    0x4f: "i32.ge_u",

    0x6a: "i32.add",
    0x6b: "i32.sub",
    0x6c: "i32.mul",
    0x71: "i32.and",
    0x72: "i32.or",
    0x73: "i32.xor",
    0x74: "i32.shl",
    0x75: "i32.shr_s",
    0x76: "i32.shr_u"
  };

  if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
    const x = uleb(q);
    q = x.next;

    ins.push({
      at,
      op,
      text: `${names[op]} ${x.value}`
    });

    continue;
  }

  ins.push({
    at,
    op,
    text: names[op] || `opcode 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
  });
}

import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const START = 0x126b4;
const END   = 0x1295b;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 205 CALL #26 POST-USE TRACE v3");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();
console.log(`WASM size : ${buf.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`Function 205 range : ${hex(0x1226d)} .. ${hex(0x1295b)}`);
console.log(`CALL #26 : ${hex(START)}`);
console.log(`Scan     : ${hex(START + 1)} .. ${hex(END)}`);
console.log();

function hex(n: number, w = 8): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(w, "0");
}

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return {
                value: value >>> 0,
                next: p,
            };
        }

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error(`Invalid ULEB at ${hex(pos)}`);
        }
    }

    throw new Error(`EOF while reading ULEB at ${hex(pos)}`);
}

function readSLEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;
    let byte = 0;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        byte = buf[p++];

        value += (byte & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);
        shift += 7;

        if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) {
            if (shift < 32 && (byte & 0x40)) {
                value -= Math.pow(2, shift);
            }

            return {
                value,
                next: p,
            };
        }

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error(`Invalid SLEB at ${hex(pos)}`);
        }
    }

    throw new Error(`EOF while reading SLEB at ${hex(pos)}`);
}

function decodeOne(p: number): {
    size: number;
    text: string;
    kind: string;
    local?: number;
    offset?: number;
    call?: number;
} {
    const op = buf[p];

    if (op === 0x20) {
        const u = readULEB(p + 1);
        return {
            size: u.next - p,
            text: `local.get ${u.value}`,
            kind: "local.get",
            local: u.value,
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x21) {
        const u = readULEB(p + 1);
        return {
            size: u.next - p,
            text: `local.set ${u.value}`,
            kind: "local.set",
            local: u.value,
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x22) {
        const u = readULEB(p + 1);
        return {
            size: u.next - p,
            text: `local.tee ${u.value}`,
            kind: "local.tee",
            local: u.value,
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x41) {
        const u = readSLEB(p + 1);
        return {
            size: u.next - p,
            text: `i32.const ${u.value}`,
            kind: "i32.const",
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x42) {
        const u = readSLEB(p + 1);
        return {
            size: u.next - p,
            text: `i64.const ${u.value}`,
            kind: "i64.const",
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x10) {
        const u = readULEB(p + 1);
        return {
            size: u.next - p,
            text: `call ${u.value}`,
            kind: "call",
            call: u.value,
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x29) {
        let q = p + 1;

        const align = readULEB(q);
        q = align.next;

        const off = readULEB(q);

        return {
            size: off.next - p,
            text:
                `i64.load align=${align.value} ` +
                `offset=${off.value}`,
            kind: "i64.load",
            offset: off.value,
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x28) {
        let q = p + 1;

        const align = readULEB(q);
        q = align.next;

        const off = readULEB(q);

        return {
            size: off.next - p,
            text:
                `i32.load align=${align.value} ` +
                `offset=${off.value}`,
            kind: "i32.load",
            offset: off.value,
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x37) {
        let q = p + 1;

        const align = readULEB(q);
        q = align.next;

        const off = readULEB(q);

        return {
            size: off.next - p,
            text:
                `i64.store align=${align.value} ` +
                `offset=${off.value}`,
            kind: "i64.store",
            offset: off.value,
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x36) {
        let q = p + 1;

        const align = readULEB(q);
        q = align.next;

        const off = readULEB(q);

        return {
            size: off.next - p,
            text:
                `i32.store align=${align.value} ` +
                `offset=${off.value}`,
            kind: "i32.store",
            offset: off.value,
        };
    }

    const names: Record<number, string> = {
        0x1a: "drop",
        0x22: "local.tee",
        0x24: "global.set",
        0x23: "global.get",
        0x6a: "i32.add",
        0x6b: "i32.sub",
        0x71: "i32.and",
        0x72: "i32.or",
        0x73: "i32.xor",
        0x74: "i32.shl",
        0x75: "i32.shr_s",
        0x76: "i32.shr_u",
        0x7c: "i64.add",
        0x7d: "i64.sub",
        0x7e: "i64.mul",
        0x83: "i64.and",
        0x84: "i64.or",
        0x85: "i64.xor",
        0x86: "i64.shl",
        0x87: "i64.shr_s",
        0x88: "i64.shr_u",
    };

    return {
        size: 1,
        text: names[op] ?? `op=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`,
        kind: "other",
    };
}

function isLocal2(p: number): boolean {
    return buf[p] === 0x20 && buf[p + 1] === 0x02;
}

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("POST-CALL INSTRUCTION TRACE");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let p = START + 1;
let count = 0;

while (p < END) {
    const d = decodeOne(p);

    console.log(`${hex(p)}  ${d.text}`);

    p += d.size;
    count++;

    if (count > 1000) {
        console.log("SAFETY STOP");
        break;
    }
}

console.log();
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("DIRECT FRAME-BASE PATTERNS");

let direct = 0;

for (let q = START + 1; q < END - 5; q++) {
    if (!isLocal2(q)) continue;

    let r = q + 2;

    if (buf[r] !== 0x41) continue;

    const c = readSLEB(r + 1);

    if (
        c.value === 432 ||
        c.value === 440 ||
        c.value === 432 + 8 ||
        c.value === 432 - 8
    ) {
        direct++;

        console.log();
        console.log(
            `FRAME REFERENCE #${direct} @ ${hex(q)}`
        );

        console.log(
            `  local.get 2 + i32.const ${c.value}`
        );

        let x = q;
        const stop = Math.min(END, q + 24);

        while (x < stop) {
            const z = decodeOne(x);
            console.log(`  ${hex(x)}  ${z.text}`);
            x += z.size;
        }
    }
}

console.log();

let loads = 0;

p = START + 1;

while (p < END) {

    if (
        d.kind === "i64.load" ||
        d.kind === "i32.load"
    ) {
        loads++;

        console.log(

import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const START = 0x126b4;
const END   = 0x1295b;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 205 CALL #26 REAL TARGET TRACE v6");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();

console.log(`WASM size : ${buf.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`CALL #26  : 0x${START.toString(16)}`);

console.log();

function hex(n: number): string {
    return "0x" + (n >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function readSLEB(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
                value -= 2 ** shift;
            }

            return { value, next: p };
        }

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error("SLEB too long");
        }
    }

    throw new Error("EOF");
}

function isLocalGet2(p: number): boolean {
    return buf[p] === 0x20 && buf[p + 1] === 0x02;
}

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("A) REAL CALL #26 DESTINATION");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

console.log(`
CALL #26 @ ${hex(START)}

arg0 = local2 - 0x40
arg1 = local17
arg2 = 0
arg3 = local34
arg4 = 0

Function 87 writes:

  LOW  -> L2 - 0x40
  HIGH -> L2 - 0x38
`);

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let lowHits = 0;

for (let p = START + 1; p < END - 4; p++) {

    if (!isLocalGet2(p)) continue;

    if (buf[p + 2] !== 0x41) continue;

    let c;

    try {
        c = readSLEB(p + 3);
    } catch {
        continue;
    }

    if (c.value !== -64) continue;

    lowHits++;

    console.log();
    console.log(
        `LOW HIT #${lowHits} @ ${hex(p)}`
    );

    console.log(
        `  local.get 2`
    );

    console.log(
        `  i32.const ${c.value}`
    );

    const a = Math.max(START, p - 12);
    const z = Math.min(END, c.next + 24);

    let bytes = "";

    for (let q = a; q < z; q++) {
        bytes += buf[q].toString(16).padStart(2, "0") + " ";
    }

    console.log(`  bytes: ${bytes.trim()}`);
}

if (lowHits === 0) {
    console.log("NONE");
}

console.log();
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("C) DIRECT REFERENCES TO L2 - 0x38");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let highHits = 0;

for (let p = START + 1; p < END - 4; p++) {

    if (!isLocalGet2(p)) continue;

    if (buf[p + 2] !== 0x41) continue;

    let c;

    try {
        c = readSLEB(p + 3);
    } catch {
        continue;
    }

    if (c.value !== -56) continue;

    highHits++;

    console.log();
    console.log(
        `HIGH HIT #${highHits} @ ${hex(p)}`
    );

    console.log(
        `  local.get 2`
    );

    console.log(
        `  i32.const ${c.value}`
    );

    const a = Math.max(START, p - 12);
    const z = Math.min(END, c.next + 24);

    let bytes = "";

    for (let q = a; q < z; q++) {
        bytes += buf[q].toString(16).padStart(2, "0") + " ";
    }

    console.log(`  bytes: ${bytes.trim()}`);
}

if (highHits === 0) {
    console.log("NONE");
}

console.log();
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("D) ALL NEGATIVE L2 REFERENCES");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let negative = 0;

for (let p = START + 1; p < END - 4; p++) {

    if (!isLocalGet2(p)) continue;

    if (buf[p + 2] !== 0x41) continue;

    let c;

    try {
        c = readSLEB(p + 3);
    } catch {
        continue;
    }

    if (c.value >= 0) continue;

    negative++;

    console.log(
        `${hex(p)}  L2 ${c.value}`
    );
}

if (negative === 0) {
    console.log("NONE");
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SUMMARY");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(`L2 - 0x40 references : ${lowHits}`);
console.log(`L2 - 0x38 references : ${highHits}`);
console.log(`Negative L2 refs     : ${negative}`);

console.log();
console.log("CORRECT CALL #26 OUTPUT:");
console.log("  LOW  = L2 - 0x40");
console.log("  HIGH = L2 - 0x38");
console.log();
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceFunction205Local11V1.ts
====================================================================================================
import * as fs from "fs";

const wasmPath = process.argv[2];

if (!wasmPath) {
    console.error("WASM path is required.");
    process.exit(1);
}

const buf = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const START = 0x1227c;
const END = 0x12451;

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[pos++];
        value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readSLEB64(pos: number): { value: bigint; next: number } {
    let value = 0n;
    let shift = 0n;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[pos++];

        value |= BigInt(b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            if (b & 0x40) {
                value -= 1n << (shift + 7n);
            }

            return { value, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7n;
    }
}

function readSLEB32(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = buf[pos++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            break;
        }
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
        value -= 2 ** shift;
    }

    return { value, next: pos };
}

function opname(op: number): string {
    const names: Record<number, string> = {
        0x03: "block",
        0x04: "if",
        0x05: "else",
        0x0b: "end",
        0x0c: "br",
        0x0d: "br_if",
        0x0f: "return",
        0x10: "call",
        0x1a: "drop",

        0x20: "local.get",
        0x21: "local.set",
        0x22: "local.tee",

        0x23: "global.get",
        0x24: "global.set",

        0x28: "i32.load",
        0x29: "i64.load",

        0x36: "i32.store",
        0x37: "i64.store",

        0x41: "i32.const",
        0x42: "i64.const",

        0x45: "i32.eqz",
        0x46: "i32.eq",
        0x47: "i32.ne",

        0x4c: "i32.le_s",
        0x4d: "i32.le_u",
        0x4e: "i32.ge_s",
        0x4f: "i32.ge_u",

        0x6a: "i32.add",
        0x6b: "i32.sub",

        0x7c: "i64.add",
        0x7d: "i64.sub",
        0x7e: "i64.mul",
        0x83: "i64.and",
        0x84: "i64.or",
        0x85: "i64.xor",
        0x86: "i64.shl",
        0x87: "i64.shr_s",
        0x88: "i64.shr_u"
    };

    return names[op] ?? `OP_0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 205 LOCAL11 PRODUCER TRACE v1");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();

console.log(`WASM size : ${buf.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`Range     : 0x${START.toString(16)} .. 0x${END.toString(16)}`);
console.log();

let p = START;
let depth = 0;
let lastWrite: number | undefined;

while (p < END) {
    const addr = p;
    const op = buf[p++];

    let extra = "";

    switch (op) {
        case 0x03:
        case 0x04: {
            const t = buf[p++];
            depth++;

            extra =
                ` type=0x${t.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}` +
                ` depth=${depth}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x05:
            extra = ` depth=${depth}`;
            break;

        case 0x0b:
            extra = ` depth=${depth}`;

            if (depth > 0) {
                depth--;
            }
            break;

        case 0x0c:
        case 0x0d: {
            const x = readULEB(p);
            p = x.next;
            extra = ` ${x.value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x20:
        case 0x21:
        case 0x22: {
            const x = readULEB(p);
            p = x.next;

            extra = ` ${x.value}`;

            if (x.value === 11) {
                if (op === 0x20) {
                    console.log(
                        `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ` +
                        `*** READ local11 ***`
                    );
                } else {
                    const kind =
                        op === 0x21
                            ? "local.set"
                            : "local.tee";

                    console.log(
                        `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ` +
                        `*** WRITE local11 via ${kind} ***`
                    );

                    lastWrite = addr;
                }
            }

            break;
        }

        case 0x23:
        case 0x24: {
            const x = readULEB(p);
            p = x.next;
            extra = ` ${x.value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x28:
        case 0x29: {
            const a = readULEB(p);
            p = a.next;

            const o = readULEB(p);
            p = o.next;

            extra =
                ` align=${a.value}` +
                ` offset=${o.value}` +
                ` (0x${o.value.toString(16)})`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x36:
        case 0x37: {
            const a = readULEB(p);
            p = a.next;

            const o = readULEB(p);
            p = o.next;

            extra =
                ` align=${a.value}` +
                ` offset=${o.value}` +
                ` (0x${o.value.toString(16)})`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x41: {
            const x = readSLEB32(p);
            p = x.next;

            extra =
                ` ${x.value}` +
                ` (0x${(x.value >>> 0).toString(16)})`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x42: {
            const x = readSLEB64(p);
            p = x.next;

            extra = ` ${x.value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x10: {
            const x = readULEB(p);
            p = x.next;

            extra = ` ${x.value}`;

            if (x.value === 87) {
                extra += " [FUNCTION 87]";
            }

            break;
        }

        default:
            break;
    }

    /*
     * Print all instructions only around local11 activity.
     * Also show a wider context once a local11 write is encountered.
     */
    if (
        (addr >= 0x12380 && addr <= 0x12400) ||
        (addr >= 0x12420 && addr <= 0x12451) ||
        op === 0x21 ||
        op === 0x22
    ) {
        console.log(
            `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ` +
            `${opname(op).padEnd(16)}${extra}`
        );
    }
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" RESULT");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();

if (lastWrite === undefined) {
    console.log("local11: NO WRITE FOUND");
} else {
    console.log(
        `local11 last write @ 0x${lastWrite.toString(16)}`
    );
}

console.log();
console.log("Target use:");
console.log("  local34 = local11 << 1");
console.log("  CALL 87 @ 0x126b4 uses local34 as arg3");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceFunction205Local2V1.ts

import * as fs from "fs";

const wasmPath = process.argv[2];

if (!wasmPath) {
    console.error("Usage:");
    console.error(
        '  npx.cmd tsx "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\scripts\\traceFunction205Local2V1.ts" "WASM_PATH"'
    );
    process.exit(1);
}

const buf = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const FUNC_INDEX = 205;
const CODE_START = 0x1226d;
const CODE_END = 0x1295c;

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[pos++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error("Invalid ULEB128");
        }
    }
}

function readSLEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = buf[pos++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            break;
        }

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error("Invalid SLEB128");
        }
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
        value -= 2 ** shift;
    }

    return { value, next: pos };
}

function hex(v: number): string {
    return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function opcodeName(op: number): string {
    switch (op) {
        case 0x20: return "local.get";
        case 0x21: return "local.set";
        case 0x22: return "local.tee";

        case 0x23: return "global.get";
        case 0x24: return "global.set";

        case 0x28: return "i32.load";
        case 0x29: return "i64.load";
        case 0x2d: return "i32.load8_u";
        case 0x2f: return "i32.load16_u";

        case 0x36: return "i32.store";
        case 0x37: return "i64.store";

        case 0x41: return "i32.const";
        case 0x42: return "i64.const";

        case 0x45: return "i32.eqz";
        case 0x46: return "i32.eq";
        case 0x47: return "i32.ne";

        case 0x6a: return "i32.add";
        case 0x6b: return "i32.sub";

        case 0x7c: return "i64.add";
        case 0x7d: return "i64.sub";

        case 0x10: return "call";

        case 0x0b: return "end";

        default:
            return `opcode 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
    }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 205 LOCAL #2 TRACE v1");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();

console.log(`WASM size : ${buf.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`Function  : ${FUNC_INDEX}`);
console.log(`Range     : ${hex(CODE_START)} .. ${hex(CODE_END)}`);
console.log();

console.log("TARGET LOCAL");
console.log("  local index : 2");
console.log();

let p = CODE_START;

// Function body begins with local declaration vector.
// We already know CODE_START points immediately after body-size LEB,
// so parse local declarations here.

const localDeclCountInfo = readULEB(p);
const localDeclCount = localDeclCountInfo.value;
p = localDeclCountInfo.next;

console.log(`local declaration groups : ${localDeclCount}`);
console.log();

let localIndex = 0;

for (let g = 0; g < localDeclCount; g++) {
    const countInfo = readULEB(p);
    p = countInfo.next;

    const type = buf[p++];

    console.log(
        `group ${g}: count=${countInfo.value} type=0x${type
            .toString(16)
            .padStart(2, "0")} locals=${localIndex}..${
            localIndex + countInfo.value - 1
        }`
    );

    localIndex += countInfo.value;
}

console.log();
console.log(`total locals including params : ${localIndex}`);
console.log();

if (localIndex <= 2) {
    console.log("WARNING: local #2 does not exist.");
    process.exit(2);
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" SCANNING LOCAL #2 REFERENCES");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();

p = localDeclCountInfo.next;

// Skip declarations again.
for (let g = 0; g < localDeclCount; g++) {
    const countInfo = readULEB(p);
    p = countInfo.next;
    p += 1;
}

let instructionCount = 0;

while (p < CODE_END) {
    const addr = p;
    const op = buf[p++];

    instructionCount++;

    if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
        const idxInfo = readULEB(p);
        p = idxInfo.next;

        const idx = idxInfo.value;

        if (idx === 2) {
            console.log(
                `${hex(addr)}  ${opcodeName(op).padEnd(12)} local=2`
            );
        }

        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x41) {
        const x = readSLEB(p);
        p = x.next;
        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x42) {
        // signed 64-bit LEB
        let shift = 0;
        let value = 0n;
        let b = 0;

        while (true) {
            b = buf[p++];

            value |= BigInt(b & 0x7f) << BigInt(shift);
            shift += 7;

            if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
                break;
            }

            if (shift > 70) {
                throw new Error("Invalid i64.const");
            }
        }

        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x10) {
        const f = readULEB(p);
        p = f.next;
        continue;
    }

    // memory load/store immediates
    if (
        (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x35) ||
        (op >= 0x36 && op <= 0x3e)
    ) {
        const align = readULEB(p);
        p = align.next;

        const offset = readULEB(p);
        p = offset.next;

        continue;
    }

    // global get/set
    if (op === 0x23 || op === 0x24) {
        const g = readULEB(p);
        p = g.next;
        continue;
    }

    // Most remaining instructions have no immediates for our scan.
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" END");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`instructions scanned : ${instructionCount}`);

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceFunction205Locals17_34V1.ts

import * as fs from "fs";

const wasmPath = process.argv[2];

if (!wasmPath) {
    console.error("WASM path is required.");
    process.exit(1);
}

const buf = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const START = 0x1227c;
const END = 0x126b4;

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[pos++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readSLEB32(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = buf[pos++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            break;
        }
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
        value -= 2 ** shift;
    }

    return { value, next: pos };
}

function readSLEB64(pos: number): { value: bigint; next: number } {
    let value = 0n;
    let shift = 0n;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[pos++];

        value |= BigInt(b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            if ((b & 0x40) !== 0) {
                value -= 1n << (shift + 7n);
            }

            return { value, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7n;
    }
}

function opname(op: number): string {
    const names: Record<number, string> = {
        0x03: "block",
        0x04: "if",
        0x05: "else",
        0x0b: "end",
        0x0c: "br",
        0x0d: "br_if",
        0x0e: "br_table",
        0x0f: "return",

        0x10: "call",

        0x1a: "drop",

        0x20: "local.get",
        0x21: "local.set",
        0x22: "local.tee",

        0x23: "global.get",
        0x24: "global.set",

        0x28: "i32.load",
        0x29: "i64.load",
        0x2a: "f32.load",
        0x2b: "f64.load",

        0x36: "i32.store",
        0x37: "i64.store",

        0x41: "i32.const",
        0x42: "i64.const",

        0x45: "i32.eqz",
        0x46: "i32.eq",
        0x47: "i32.ne",
        0x48: "i32.lt_s",
        0x49: "i32.lt_u",
        0x4a: "i32.gt_s",
        0x4b: "i32.gt_u",
        0x4c: "i32.le_s",
        0x4d: "i32.le_u",
        0x4e: "i32.ge_s",
        0x4f: "i32.ge_u",

        0x50: "i64.eqz",

        0x6a: "i32.add",
        0x6b: "i32.sub",

        0x7c: "i64.add",
        0x7d: "i64.sub",
        0x7e: "i64.mul",
        0x7f: "i64.div_s",
        0x80: "i64.div_u",
        0x81: "i64.rem_s",
        0x82: "i64.rem_u",
        0x83: "i64.and",
        0x84: "i64.or",
        0x85: "i64.xor",
        0x86: "i64.shl",
        0x87: "i64.shr_s",
        0x88: "i64.shr_u",

        0x92: "f64.add",
        0x93: "f64.sub",
        0x94: "f64.mul",
        0x95: "f64.div",
    };

    return names[op] ?? `opcode 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

const interesting = new Set([17, 34]);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 205 LOCAL17 / LOCAL34 PRODUCER TRACE v1");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();

console.log(`WASM size : ${buf.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`Range     : 0x${START.toString(16)} .. 0x${END.toString(16)}`);
console.log(`Targets   : local17, local34`);
console.log();

let p = START;
let depth = 0;

const lastWrite = new Map<number, number>();

while (p < END) {
    const addr = p;
    const op = buf[p++];

    switch (op) {

        case 0x03:
        case 0x04: {
            const blockType = buf[p++];
            depth++;

            console.log(
                `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ` +
                `${opname(op)} type=0x${blockType.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")} ` +
                `depth=${depth}`
            );
            break;
        }

        case 0x05:
            console.log(
                `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  else depth=${depth}`
            );
            break;

        case 0x0b:
            console.log(
                `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  end depth=${depth}`
            );

            if (depth > 0) depth--;

            break;

        case 0x0c: {
            const x = readULEB(p);
            p = x.next;

            console.log(
                `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  br ${x.value}`
            );
            break;
        }

        case 0x0d: {
            const x = readULEB(p);
            p = x.next;

            console.log(
                `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  br_if ${x.value}`
            );
            break;
        }

        case 0x0f:
            console.log(
                `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  return`
            );
            break;

        case 0x20: {
            const x = readULEB(p);
            p = x.next;

            if (interesting.has(x.value)) {
                console.log(
                    `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ` +
                    `*** READ local${x.value} ***`
                );

                const w = lastWrite.get(x.value);

                if (w !== undefined) {
                    console.log(
                        `                         last write = ` +
                        `0x${w.toString(16)}`
                    );
                } else {
                    console.log(
                        `                         last write = NONE`
                    );
                }
            }

            break;
        }

        case 0x21:
        case 0x22: {
            const x = readULEB(p);
            p = x.next;

            if (interesting.has(x.value)) {
                const kind = op === 0x21
                    ? "local.set"
                    : "local.tee";

                console.log(
                    `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ` +
                    `*** WRITE local${x.value} via ${kind} ***`
                );

                lastWrite.set(x.value, addr);
            }

            break;
        }

        case 0x23:
        case 0x24: {
            readULEB(p);
            p = readULEB(p - 1).next;
            break;
        }

        case 0x41:
            p = readSLEB32(p).next;
            break;

        case 0x42:
            p = readSLEB64(p).next;
            break;

        case 0x28:
        case 0x29:
        case 0x2a:
        case 0x2b: {
            const a = readULEB(p);
            p = a.next;

            const o = readULEB(p);
            p = o.next;
            break;
        }

        case 0x36:
        case 0x37: {
            const a = readULEB(p);
            p = a.next;

            const o = readULEB(p);
            p = o.next;
            break;
        }

        case 0x10: {
            const x = readULEB(p);
            p = x.next;

            if (x.value === 87) {
                console.log(
                    `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ` +
                    `*** CALL FUNCTION 87 ***`
                );
            }

            break;
        }

        default:
            break;
    }
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FINAL PRODUCER MAP");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const n of [17, 34]) {
    const w = lastWrite.get(n);

    console.log(
        `local${n}: ` +
        (w === undefined
            ? "NO WRITE FOUND"
            : `last write @ 0x${w.toString(16)}`)
    );
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceFunction205PointerOps.ts
====================================================================================================
import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_FUNC = 205;

const bytes = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function hx(n: number): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function uleb(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = bytes[pos++];
        value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if (!(b & 0x80))
            return { value, next: pos };

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function sleb(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = bytes[pos++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if (!(b & 0x80)) {
            if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
                value |= (-1 << (shift + 7));

            return {
                value: value | 0,
                next: pos
            };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}


/* ============================================================ */
/* SECTION LOCATOR                                               */
/* ============================================================ */

type Section = {
    id: number;
    payloadStart: number;
    payloadEnd: number;
};

function sections(): Section[] {

    const out: Section[] = [];

    let p = 8;

    while (p < bytes.length) {

        const id = bytes[p++];

        const size = uleb(p);
        p = size.next;

        const start = p;
        const end = p + size.value;

        out.push({
            id,
            payloadStart: start,
            payloadEnd: end
        });

        p = end;
    }

    return out;
}

const secs = sections();

const typeSec = secs.find(x => x.id === 1);
const funcSec = secs.find(x => x.id === 3);
const codeSec = secs.find(x => x.id === 10);

if (!typeSec || !funcSec || !codeSec)
    throw new Error("Required WASM sections not found");


/* ============================================================ */
/* TYPE SECTION                                                  */
/* ============================================================ */

type FuncType = {
    params: number[];
    results: number[];
};

function parseTypes(): FuncType[] {

    const result: FuncType[] = [];

    let p = typeSec!.payloadStart;

    const count = uleb(p);
    p = count.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {

        const form = bytes[p++];

        if (form !== 0x60)
            throw new Error(`Unexpected type form at ${hx(p - 1)}`);

        const pc = uleb(p);
        p = pc.next;

        const params: number[] = [];

        for (let j = 0; j < pc.value; j++)
            params.push(bytes[p++]);

        const rc = uleb(p);
        p = rc.next;

        const results: number[] = [];

        for (let j = 0; j < rc.value; j++)
            results.push(bytes[p++]);

        result.push({
            params,
            results
        });
    }

    return result;
}


/* ============================================================ */
/* FUNCTION SECTION                                              */
/* ============================================================ */

function functionTypeIndices(): number[] {

    let p = funcSec!.payloadStart;

    const count = uleb(p);
    p = count.next;

    const result: number[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {

        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        result.push(x.value);
    }

    return result;
}


/* ============================================================ */
/* CODE BODY LOCATOR                                              */
/* ============================================================ */

type Body = {
    functionIndex: number;
    bodyIndex: number;
    sizePos: number;
    codeStart: number;
    codeEnd: number;
};

function codeBodies(): Body[] {

    let p = codeSec!.payloadStart;

    const count = uleb(p);
    p = count.next;

    const out: Body[] = [];

    /*
     * Imported functions have no code body.
     * The first code body corresponds to the first
     * non-imported function.
     *
     * From the verified WASM layout used in this project,
     * the function body numbering is already aligned with
     * real function indices after the import range.
     *
     * We recover the real function index from the known
     * import count below.
     */

    const importSec = secs.find(x => x.id === 2);

    let importFuncCount = 0;

    if (importSec) {

        let q = importSec.payloadStart;

        const n = uleb(q);
        q = n.next;

        for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {

            const moduleLen = uleb(q);
            q = moduleLen.next + moduleLen.value;

            const nameLen = uleb(q);
            q = nameLen.next + nameLen.value;

            const kind = bytes[q++];

            if (kind === 0) {
                importFuncCount++;
                q = uleb(q).next;
            } else if (kind === 1) {
                q += 4;
            } else if (kind === 2) {
                q += 5;
            } else if (kind === 3) {
                q += 2;
            }
        }
    }

    for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {

        const sizePos = p;

        const sz = uleb(p);
        p = sz.next;

        const codeStart = p;
        const codeEnd = p + sz.value;

        const functionIndex =
            importFuncCount + i;

        out.push({
            functionIndex,
            bodyIndex: i,
            sizePos,
            codeStart,
            codeEnd
        });

        p = codeEnd;
    }

    return out;
}


const types = parseTypes();
const typeIndices = functionTypeIndices();
const bodies = codeBodies();

const body = bodies.find(
    x => x.functionIndex === TARGET_FUNC
);

if (!body)
    throw new Error(
        `Function ${TARGET_FUNC} body not found`
    );

const typeIndex = typeIndices[
    TARGET_FUNC - (bodies[0]?.functionIndex ?? 0)
];


/*
 * The index calculation above is intentionally verified below
 * against the known body numbering.  If the module has imported
 * functions, function section index = real index - imports.
 */

const importedFunctions =
    bodies.length > 0
        ? bodies[0].functionIndex
        : 0;

const actualTypeIndex =
    typeIndices[TARGET_FUNC - importedFunctions];

const ft = types[actualTypeIndex];

function valType(x: number): string {

    switch (x) {
        case 0x7f: return "i32";
        case 0x7e: return "i64";
        case 0x7d: return "f32";
        case 0x7c: return "f64";
        case 0x70: return "funcref";
        case 0x6f: return "externref";
        default:
            return `0x${x.toString(16)}`;
    }
}


/* ============================================================ */
/* FUNCTION 205 LOCAL DECLARATIONS                              */
/* ============================================================ */

function parseLocals(): {
    params: number[];
    locals: number[];
} {

    let p = body!.codeStart;

    const localGroups = uleb(p);
    p = localGroups.next;

    const locals: number[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < localGroups.value; i++) {

        const n = uleb(p);
        p = n.next;

        const type = bytes[p++];

        for (let j = 0; j < n.value; j++)
            locals.push(type);
    }

    return {
        params: ft.params,
        locals
    };
}


/* ============================================================ */
/* INSTRUCTION DECODER                                           */
/* ============================================================ */

type Ins = {
    pc: number;
    op: string;
    text: string;
    imm?: number;
};

function decodeAt(p: number): Ins {

    const pc = p;
    const b = bytes[p++];

    if (b === 0x20) {
        const x = uleb(p);
        return {
            pc,
            op: "local.get",
            imm: x.value,
            text: `local.get ${x.value}`
        };
    }

    if (b === 0x21) {
        const x = uleb(p);
        return {
            pc,
            op: "local.set",
            imm: x.value,
            text: `local.set ${x.value}`
        };
    }

    if (b === 0x22) {
        const x = uleb(p);
        return {
            pc,
            op: "local.tee",
            imm: x.value,
            text: `local.tee ${x.value}`
        };
    }

    if (b === 0x41) {
        const x = sleb(p);
        return {
            pc,
            op: "i32.const",
            imm: x.value,
            text: `i32.const ${x.value}`
        };
    }

    if (b === 0x42) {

        let q = p;

        while (true) {
            const z = bytes[q++];
            if (!(z & 0x80))
                break;
        }

        return {
            pc,
            op: "i64.const",
            text: "i64.const"
        };
    }

    if (b === 0x28) {

        const a = uleb(p);
        const o = uleb(a.next);

        return {
            pc,
            op: "i32.load",
            imm: o.value,
            text: `i32.load offset=${o.value}`
        };
    }

    if (b === 0x29) {

        const a = uleb(p);
        const o = uleb(a.next);

        return {
            pc,
            op: "i64.load",
            imm: o.value,
            text: `i64.load offset=${o.value}`
        };
    }

    if (b === 0x2d) {

        const a = uleb(p);
        const o = uleb(a.next);

        return {
            pc,
            op: "i32.load8_u",
            imm: o.value,
            text: `i32.load8_u offset=${o.value}`
        };
    }

    if (b === 0x31) {

        const a = uleb(p);
        const o = uleb(a.next);

        return {
            pc,
            op: "i64.load8_u",
            imm: o.value,
            text: `i64.load8_u offset=${o.value}`
        };
    }

    if (b === 0x36) {

        const a = uleb(p);
        const o = uleb(a.next);

        return {
            pc,
            op: "i32.store",
            imm: o.value,
            text: `i32.store offset=${o.value}`
        };
    }

    if (b === 0x37) {

        const a = uleb(p);
        const o = uleb(a.next);

        return {
            pc,
            op: "i64.store",
            imm: o.value,
            text: `i64.store offset=${o.value}`
        };
    }

    if (b === 0x10) {

        const x = uleb(p);

        return {
            pc,
            op: "call",
            imm: x.value,
            text: `call ${x.value}`
        };
    }

    if (b === 0x23) {
        const x = uleb(p);

        return {
            pc,
            op: "global.get",
            imm: x.value,
            text: `global.get ${x.value}`
        };
    }

    if (b === 0x24) {
        const x = uleb(p);

        return {
            pc,
            op: "global.set",
            imm: x.value,
            text: `global.set ${x.value}`
        };
    }

    if (b === 0x40) {
        const blockType = bytes[p++];

        return {
            pc,
            op: "loop",
            text:
                blockType === 0x40
                    ? "loop void"
                    : `loop blocktype=0x${blockType.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
        };
    }

    if (b === 0x54) {
        return {
            pc,
            op: "i64.lt_u",
            text: "i64.lt_u"
        };
    }

    if (b === 0x56) {
        return {
            pc,
            op: "i64.gt_u",
            text: "i64.gt_u"
        };
    }

    if (b === 0xad) {
        return {
            pc,
            op: "i64.extend_i32_u",
            text: "i64.extend_i32_u"
        };
    }

    const simple: Record<number, string> = {

        0x1a: "drop",
        0x1b: "select",

        0x45: "i32.eqz",
        0x46: "i32.eq",
        0x47: "i32.ne",

        0x48: "i32.lt_s",
        0x49: "i32.lt_u",
        0x4a: "i32.gt_s",
        0x4b: "i32.gt_u",

        0x6a: "i32.add",
        0x6b: "i32.sub",
        0x6c: "i32.mul",
        0x74: "i32.shl",
        0x75: "i32.shr_s",
        0x76: "i32.shr_u",

        0x7c: "i64.add",
        0x7d: "i64.sub",
        0x7e: "i64.mul",

        0x83: "i64.and",
        0x84: "i64.or",
        0x85: "i64.xor",

        0x86: "i64.shl",
        0x88: "i64.shr_u",

        0x0b: "end",
        0x05: "else",

        0x0c: "br",
        0x0d: "br_if",

        0x02: "block",
        0x03: "loop",
        0x04: "if"
    };

    if (simple[b]) {

        return {
            pc,
            op: simple[b],
            text: simple[b]
        };
    }

    return {
        pc,
        op: "unknown",
        text:
            `UNKNOWN 0x${b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
    };
}


/* ============================================================ */
/* EXACT LENGTH                                                  */
/* ============================================================ */

function nextPc(p: number): number {

    const b = bytes[p];
    let q = p + 1;

    if (
        b === 0x20 ||
        b === 0x21 ||
        b === 0x22 ||
        b === 0x10 ||
        b === 0x0c ||
        b === 0x0d
    )
        return uleb(q).next;
    if (b === 0x23 || b === 0x24)
        return uleb(q).next;

    if (b === 0x40)
        return q + 1;

    if (b === 0x41)
        return sleb(q).next;

    if (b === 0x42) {

        while (true) {

            const z = bytes[q++];

            if (!(z & 0x80))
                return q;
        }
    }

    if (
        b === 0x28 ||
        b === 0x29 ||
        b === 0x2d ||
        b === 0x31 ||
        b === 0x36 ||
        b === 0x37
    ) {

        q = uleb(q).next;
        q = uleb(q).next;

        return q;
    }

    return q;
}


/* ============================================================ */
/* DECODE FUNCTION 205                                           */
/* ============================================================ */

const instructions: Ins[] = [];

let p = body.codeStart;

/*
 * Skip local declaration vector.
 */

const lg = uleb(p);
p = lg.next;

for (let i = 0; i < lg.value; i++) {

    const n = uleb(p);
    p = n.next;

    p++;
}

const instructionStart = p;

while (p < body.codeEnd) {

    const ins = decodeAt(p);

    instructions.push(ins);

    const q = nextPc(p);

    if (q <= p)
        throw new Error(
            `Decoder did not advance at ${hx(p)}`
        );

    p = q;
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceFunction205PointerOpsV1.ts

import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_FUNC = 205;

const bytes = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function hx(n: number): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function uleb(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = bytes[pos++];
        value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if (!(b & 0x80))
            return { value, next: pos };

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function sleb(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = bytes[pos++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if (!(b & 0x80)) {
            if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
                value |= (-1 << (shift + 7));

            return {
                value: value | 0,
                next: pos
            };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}


/* ============================================================ */
/* SECTION LOCATOR                                               */
/* ============================================================ */

type Section = {
    id: number;
    payloadStart: number;
    payloadEnd: number;
};

function sections(): Section[] {

    const out: Section[] = [];

    let p = 8;

    while (p < bytes.length) {

        const id = bytes[p++];

        const size = uleb(p);
        p = size.next;

        const start = p;
        const end = p + size.value;

        out.push({
            id,
            payloadStart: start,
            payloadEnd: end
        });

        p = end;
    }

    return out;
}

const secs = sections();

const typeSec = secs.find(x => x.id === 1);
const funcSec = secs.find(x => x.id === 3);
const codeSec = secs.find(x => x.id === 10);

if (!typeSec || !funcSec || !codeSec)
    throw new Error("Required WASM sections not found");


/* ============================================================ */
/* TYPE SECTION                                                  */
/* ============================================================ */

type FuncType = {
    params: number[];
    results: number[];
};

function parseTypes(): FuncType[] {

    const result: FuncType[] = [];

    let p = typeSec!.payloadStart;

    const count = uleb(p);
    p = count.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {

        const form = bytes[p++];

        if (form !== 0x60)
            throw new Error(`Unexpected type form at ${hx(p - 1)}`);

        const pc = uleb(p);
        p = pc.next;

        const params: number[] = [];

        for (let j = 0; j < pc.value; j++)
            params.push(bytes[p++]);

        const rc = uleb(p);
        p = rc.next;

        const results: number[] = [];

        for (let j = 0; j < rc.value; j++)
            results.push(bytes[p++]);

        result.push({
            params,
            results
        });
    }

    return result;
}
