/* ============================================================ */

function functionTypeIndices(): number[] {

    let p = funcSec!.payloadStart;

    const count = uleb(p);
    p = count.next;

    const result: number[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {

        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        result.push(x.value);
    }

    return result;
}


/* ============================================================ */
/* CODE BODY LOCATOR                                              */
/* ============================================================ */

type Body = {
    functionIndex: number;
    bodyIndex: number;
    sizePos: number;
    codeStart: number;
    codeEnd: number;
};

function codeBodies(): Body[] {

    let p = codeSec!.payloadStart;

    const count = uleb(p);
    p = count.next;

    const out: Body[] = [];

    /*
     * Imported functions have no code body.
     * The first code body corresponds to the first
     * non-imported function.
     *
     * From the verified WASM layout used in this project,
     * the function body numbering is already aligned with
     * real function indices after the import range.
     *
     * We recover the real function index from the known
     * import count below.
     */

    const importSec = secs.find(x => x.id === 2);

    let importFuncCount = 0;

    if (importSec) {

        let q = importSec.payloadStart;

        const n = uleb(q);
        q = n.next;

        for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {

            const moduleLen = uleb(q);
            q = moduleLen.next + moduleLen.value;

            const nameLen = uleb(q);
            q = nameLen.next + nameLen.value;

            const kind = bytes[q++];

            if (kind === 0) {
                importFuncCount++;
                q = uleb(q).next;
            } else if (kind === 1) {
                q += 4;
            } else if (kind === 2) {
                q += 5;
            } else if (kind === 3) {
                q += 2;
            }
        }
    }

    for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {

        const sizePos = p;

        const sz = uleb(p);
        p = sz.next;

        const codeStart = p;
        const codeEnd = p + sz.value;

        const functionIndex =
            importFuncCount + i;

        out.push({
            functionIndex,
            bodyIndex: i,
            sizePos,
            codeStart,
            codeEnd
        });

        p = codeEnd;
    }

    return out;
}


const types = parseTypes();
const typeIndices = functionTypeIndices();
const bodies = codeBodies();

const body = bodies.find(
    x => x.functionIndex === TARGET_FUNC
);

if (!body)
    throw new Error(
        `Function ${TARGET_FUNC} body not found`
    );

const typeIndex = typeIndices[
    TARGET_FUNC - (bodies[0]?.functionIndex ?? 0)
];


/*
 * The index calculation above is intentionally verified below
 * against the known body numbering.  If the module has imported
 * functions, function section index = real index - imports.
 */

const importedFunctions =
    bodies.length > 0
        ? bodies[0].functionIndex
        : 0;

const actualTypeIndex =
    typeIndices[TARGET_FUNC - importedFunctions];

const ft = types[actualTypeIndex];

function valType(x: number): string {

    switch (x) {
        case 0x7f: return "i32";
        case 0x7e: return "i64";
        case 0x7d: return "f32";
        case 0x7c: return "f64";
        case 0x70: return "funcref";
        case 0x6f: return "externref";
        default:
            return `0x${x.toString(16)}`;
    }
}


/* ============================================================ */
/* FUNCTION 205 LOCAL DECLARATIONS                              */
/* ============================================================ */

function parseLocals(): {
    params: number[];
    locals: number[];
} {

    let p = body!.codeStart;

    const localGroups = uleb(p);
    p = localGroups.next;

    const locals: number[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < localGroups.value; i++) {

        const n = uleb(p);
        p = n.next;

        const type = bytes[p++];

        for (let j = 0; j < n.value; j++)
            locals.push(type);
    }

    return {
        params: ft.params,
        locals
    };
}


/* ============================================================ */
/* INSTRUCTION DECODER                                           */
/* ============================================================ */

type Ins = {
    pc: number;
    op: string;
    text: string;
    imm?: number;
};

function decodeAt(p: number): Ins {

    const pc = p;
    const b = bytes[p++];

    if (b === 0x20) {
        const x = uleb(p);
        return {
            pc,
            op: "local.get",
            imm: x.value,
            text: `local.get ${x.value}`
        };
    }

    if (b === 0x21) {
        const x = uleb(p);
        return {
            pc,
            op: "local.set",
            imm: x.value,
            text: `local.set ${x.value}`
        };
    }

    if (b === 0x22) {
        const x = uleb(p);
        return {
            pc,
            op: "local.tee",
            imm: x.value,
            text: `local.tee ${x.value}`
        };
    }

    if (b === 0x41) {
        const x = sleb(p);
        return {
            pc,
            op: "i32.const",
            imm: x.value,
            text: `i32.const ${x.value}`
        };
    }

    if (b === 0x42) {

        let q = p;

        while (true) {
            const z = bytes[q++];
            if (!(z & 0x80))
                break;
        }

        return {
            pc,
            op: "i64.const",
            text: "i64.const"
        };
    }

    if (b === 0x28) {

        const a = uleb(p);
        const o = uleb(a.next);

        return {
            pc,
            op: "i32.load",
            imm: o.value,
            text: `i32.load offset=${o.value}`
        };
    }

    if (b === 0x29) {

        const a = uleb(p);
        const o = uleb(a.next);

        return {
            pc,
            op: "i64.load",
            imm: o.value,
            text: `i64.load offset=${o.value}`
        };
    }

    if (b === 0x2d) {

        const a = uleb(p);
        const o = uleb(a.next);

        return {
            pc,
            op: "i32.load8_u",
            imm: o.value,
            text: `i32.load8_u offset=${o.value}`
        };
    }

    if (b === 0x31) {

        const a = uleb(p);
        const o = uleb(a.next);

        return {
            pc,
            op: "i64.load8_u",
            imm: o.value,
            text: `i64.load8_u offset=${o.value}`
        };
    }

    if (b === 0x36) {

        const a = uleb(p);
        const o = uleb(a.next);

        return {
            pc,
            op: "i32.store",
            imm: o.value,
            text: `i32.store offset=${o.value}`
        };
    }

    if (b === 0x37) {

        const a = uleb(p);
        const o = uleb(a.next);

        return {
            pc,
            op: "i64.store",
            imm: o.value,
            text: `i64.store offset=${o.value}`
        };
    }

    if (b === 0x10) {

        const x = uleb(p);

        return {
            pc,
            op: "call",
            imm: x.value,
            text: `call ${x.value}`
        };
    }

    const simple: Record<number, string> = {

        0x1a: "drop",
        0x1b: "select",

        0x45: "i32.eqz",
        0x46: "i32.eq",
        0x47: "i32.ne",

        0x48: "i32.lt_s",
        0x49: "i32.lt_u",
        0x4a: "i32.gt_s",
        0x4b: "i32.gt_u",

        0x6a: "i32.add",
        0x6b: "i32.sub",
        0x6c: "i32.mul",
        0x74: "i32.shl",
        0x75: "i32.shr_s",
        0x76: "i32.shr_u",

        0x7c: "i64.add",
        0x7d: "i64.sub",
        0x7e: "i64.mul",

        0x83: "i64.and",
        0x84: "i64.or",
        0x85: "i64.xor",

        0x86: "i64.shl",
        0x88: "i64.shr_u",

        0x0b: "end",
        0x05: "else",

        0x0c: "br",
        0x0d: "br_if",

        0x02: "block",
        0x03: "loop",
        0x04: "if"
    };

    if (simple[b]) {

        return {
            pc,
            op: simple[b],
            text: simple[b]
        };
    }

    return {
        pc,
        op: "unknown",
        text:
            `UNKNOWN 0x${b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
    };
}


/* ============================================================ */
/* EXACT LENGTH                                                  */
/* ============================================================ */

function nextPc(p: number): number {

    const b = bytes[p];
    let q = p + 1;

    if (
        b === 0x20 ||
        b === 0x21 ||
        b === 0x22 ||
        b === 0x10 ||
        b === 0x0c ||
        b === 0x0d
    )
        return uleb(q).next;

    if (b === 0x41)
        return sleb(q).next;

    if (b === 0x42) {

        while (true) {

            const z = bytes[q++];

            if (!(z & 0x80))
                return q;
        }
    }

    if (
        b === 0x28 ||
        b === 0x29 ||
        b === 0x2d ||
        b === 0x31 ||
        b === 0x36 ||
        b === 0x37
    ) {

        q = uleb(q).next;
        q = uleb(q).next;

        return q;
    }

    return q;
}


/* ============================================================ */
/* DECODE FUNCTION 205                                           */
/* ============================================================ */

const instructions: Ins[] = [];

let p = body.codeStart;

/*
 * Skip local declaration vector.
 */

const lg = uleb(p);
p = lg.next;

for (let i = 0; i < lg.value; i++) {

    const n = uleb(p);
    p = n.next;

    p++;
}

const instructionStart = p;

while (p < body.codeEnd) {

    const ins = decodeAt(p);

    instructions.push(ins);

    const q = nextPc(p);

    if (q <= p)
        throw new Error(
            `Decoder did not advance at ${hx(p)}`
        );

    p = q;
}

import fs from "fs";

const wasmPath =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const START = 0x1226d;
const END   = 0x1295c;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 205 STORE CONTEXT v14");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();
console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`Range     : 0x${START.toString(16)} .. 0x${END.toString(16)}`);
console.log();

function readU32Leb(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (pos < wasm.length) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];
        value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            return { value, next: pos };

        shift += 7;
    }

    throw new Error("ULEB EOF");
}

function opcodeName(op: number): string {
    switch (op) {
        case 0x36: return "i32.store";
        case 0x37: return "i64.store";
        case 0x38: return "i32.store8";   // note: wasm spec prefix mapping handled below
        case 0x39: return "i32.store16";
        case 0x3a: return "i64.store8";
        case 0x3b: return "i64.store16";
        case 0x3c: return "i64.store32";
        default: return `opcode 0x${op.toString(16)}`;
    }
}

/*
 * We intentionally only identify the raw store opcode here.
 * Context bytes are the authoritative data.
 */
const storeOps = new Set([
    0x36, 0x37, 0x38, 0x39, 0x3a, 0x3b, 0x3c
]);

function hex(n: number) {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

let stores: {
    pos: number;
    op: number;
    align: number;
    offset: number;
}[] = [];

for (let p = START; p < END; p++) {
    const op = wasm[p];

    if (!storeOps.has(op))
        continue;

    try {
        const a = readU32Leb(p + 1);
        const o = readU32Leb(a.next);

        stores.push({
            pos: p,
            op,
            align: a.value,
            offset: o.value
        });
    } catch {
        // ignore undecodable candidates
    }
}

console.log(`STORE CANDIDATES : ${stores.length}`);
console.log();

for (let i = 0; i < stores.length; i++) {
    const s = stores[i];

    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
    console.log(
        `STORE #${i + 1} @ ${hex(s.pos)}  ` +
        `op=0x${s.op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}  ` +
        `${opcodeName(s.op)}  ` +
        `align=${s.align} offset=${s.offset} (${hex(s.offset)})`
    );

    const from = Math.max(START, s.pos - 18);
    const to   = Math.min(END, s.pos + 4);

    for (let p = from; p < to; p++) {
        const mark = p === s.pos ? ">>>" : "   ";
        console.log(
            `${mark} ${hex(p)}  ` +
            `0x${wasm[p].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
        );
    }
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" IMPORTANT TARGET");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("Looking for stores whose address may resolve to:");
console.log("    L2 + 0x1F0");
console.log();
console.log("Next step will symbolically decode the address-building");
console.log("instructions immediately before the relevant stores.");
console.log("============================================================");

import fs from "fs";

const wasmPath =

console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`Range     : 0x${START.toString(16)} .. 0x${END.toString(16)}`);
console.log();

function readULEB(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (pos < wasm.length) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            return { value, next: pos };

        shift += 7;
    }

    throw new Error("ULEB EOF");
}

function hex(n: number) {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function storeName(op: number): string {
    switch (op) {
        case 0x36: return "i32.store";
        case 0x37: return "i64.store";
        case 0x38: return "f32.store";
        case 0x39: return "f64.store";
        case 0x3a: return "i32.store8";
        case 0x3b: return "i32.store16";
        case 0x3c: return "i64.store8";
        case 0x3d: return "i64.store16";
        case 0x3e: return "i64.store32";
        default: return "UNKNOWN";
    }
}

const storeOps = new Set([
    0x36, 0x37, 0x38, 0x39,
    0x3a, 0x3b, 0x3c, 0x3d, 0x3e
]);

let count = 0;

for (let p = START; p < END; p++) {

    const op = wasm[p];

    if (!storeOps.has(op))
        continue;

    let align;
    let offset;

    try {
        const a = readULEB(p + 1);
        const o = readULEB(a.next);

        align = a.value;
        offset = o.value;
    } catch {
        continue;
    }

    count++;

    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
    console.log(
        `STORE #${count} @ ${hex(p)}  ` +
        `${storeName(op)}  ` +
        `align=${align}  offset=${offset} (${hex(offset)})`
    );

    console.log("RAW CONTEXT:");

    const from = Math.max(START, p - 24);
    const to   = Math.min(END, p + 3);

    for (let q = from; q < to; q++) {

        const mark = q === p ? ">>>" : "   ";

        console.log(
            `${mark} ${hex(q)} : ` +
            `0x${wasm[q].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
        );
    }

    console.log();
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TOTAL REAL STORES : ${count}`);
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceFunction2094Call205ArgsV1.ts

import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_FUNC = 2094;

const CALLS = [
    0x000969de,
    0x00096aa1,
    0x00096aad,
    0x00096ab4,
];

const CODE_START = 0x00006e7d;
const CODE_END   = 0x00324ef5;

function hex(n: number, w = 8) {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(w, "0");
}

function readULEB(b: Buffer, p: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let q = p;

    while (true) {
        const x = b[q++];
        value |= (x & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((x & 0x80) === 0) break;

        shift += 7;
        if (shift > 35) throw new Error("ULEB overflow");
    }

    return { value: value >>> 0, next: q };
}

function readSLEB(b: Buffer, p: number) {
    let value = 0n;
    let shift = 0n;
    let q = p;
    let byte = 0;

    while (true) {
        byte = b[q++];
        value |= BigInt(byte & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) break;

        shift += 7n;
        if (shift > 70n) throw new Error("SLEB overflow");
    }

    if ((shift < 64n) && (byte & 0x40)) {
        value |= (-1n) << (shift + 7n);
    }

    return { value, next: q };
}

function readI32(b: Buffer, p: number) {
    const r = readSLEB(b, p);
    return { value: Number(r.value), next: r.next };
}

function readI64(b: Buffer, p: number) {
    return readSLEB(b, p);
}

function parseFunctionBodies(b: Buffer) {
    let p = CODE_START;

    const count = readULEB(b, p);
    p = count.next;

    const map: {
        func: number;
        body: number;
        sizeField: number;
        codeStart: number;
        bodyEnd: number;
    }[] = [];

    for (let body = 0; body < count.value; body++) {
        const sizeField = p;
        const s = readULEB(b, p);

        const codeStart = s.next;
        const bodyEnd = codeStart + s.value;

        map.push({
            func: 83 + body,
            body,
            sizeField,
            codeStart,
            bodyEnd,
        });

        p = bodyEnd;
    }

    if (p !== CODE_END) {
        throw new Error(
            `Function map mismatch: ${hex(p)} != ${hex(CODE_END)}`
        );
    }

    return map;
}

function opcodeName(op: number): string {
    const m: Record<number, string> = {
        0x1a: "drop",
        0x1b: "select",

        0x20: "local.get",
        0x21: "local.set",
        0x22: "local.tee",

        0x23: "global.get",
        0x24: "global.set",

        0x28: "i32.load",
        0x29: "i64.load",
        0x2d: "i32.load8_u",
        0x30: "i64.load8_u",
        0x31: "i64.load8_s",
        0x34: "i64.load32_s",

        0x36: "i32.store",
        0x37: "i64.store",
        0x3a: "i32.store8",
        0x3e: "i64.store32",

        0x41: "i32.const",
        0x42: "i64.const",

        0x45: "i32.eqz",
        0x46: "i32.eq",
        0x4a: "i32.gt_u",
        0x4b: "i32.lt_u",
        0x4f: "i32.ge_u",

        0x51: "i64.eqz",
        0x54: "i64.lt_s",
        0x56: "i64.gt_u",

        0x6a: "i32.add",
        0x6b: "i32.sub",
        0x71: "i32.and",
        0x72: "i32.or",
        0x74: "i32.shl",

        0x7c: "i64.add",
        0x7d: "i64.sub",
        0x83: "i64.and",
        0x84: "i64.or",
        0x85: "i64.xor",
        0x86: "i64.shl",
        0x88: "i64.shr_u",

        0xa7: "i32.wrap_i64",
        0xac: "i64.extend_i32_s",
        0xad: "i64.extend_i32_u",

        0x10: "call",
    };

    return m[op] ?? `op=${hex(op, 2)}`;
}

type Sym =
    | { kind: "i32"; text: string }
    | { kind: "i64"; text: string }
    | { kind: "unknown"; text: string };

function i32(text: string): Sym {
    return { kind: "i32", text };
}

function i64(text: string): Sym {
    return { kind: "i64", text };
}

function unknown(text: string): Sym {
    return { kind: "unknown", text };
}

function pop(stack: Sym[], n = 1): Sym[] {
    const out: Sym[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        out.unshift(stack.length
            ? stack.pop()!
            : unknown("<STACK-UNDERFLOW>"));
    }

    return out;
}

function main() {
    const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" FUNCTION 2094 CALL 205 ARGUMENT TRACE V1");
    console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log();
    console.log(`WASM size : ${b.length.toLocaleString()}`);
    console.log(`Target    : Function ${TARGET_FUNC}`);
    console.log();

    const map = parseFunctionBodies(b);
    const target = map.find(x => x.func === TARGET_FUNC);

    if (!target) {
        throw new Error("Function 2094 not found");
    }

    console.log(`Body #    : ${target.body}`);
    console.log(`Code      : ${hex(target.codeStart)} .. ${hex(target.bodyEnd)}`);
    console.log();

    /*
     * We use a symbolic stack.
     *
     * Important:
     * this is NOT an execution engine.
     * It only tracks stack expressions sufficiently to expose
     * the arguments immediately before CALL 205.
     */

    let p = target.codeStart;

    // Skip local declarations.
    const localGroups = readULEB(b, p);
    p = localGroups.next;

    for (let g = 0; g < localGroups.value; g++) {
        const count = readULEB(b, p);
        p = count.next;
        p++; // value type
    }

    const stack: Sym[] = [];
    const locals = new Map<number, Sym>();

    // Initialize locals symbolically.
    for (let i = 0; i < 40; i++) {
        locals.set(i, unknown(`L${i}`));
    }

    console.log(`Instruction start : ${hex(p)}`);
    console.log();

    const targetSet = new Set(CALLS);

    let instructionCount = 0;

    while (p < target.bodyEnd) {
        const pc = p;
        const op = b[p++];

        instructionCount++;

        /*
         * Control instructions:
         * We don't attempt structured control-flow execution here.
         * Their stack effect is generally empty for the locations
         * we are targeting.
         */
        if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
            if (p < target.bodyEnd) {
                const bt = b[p++];

                if (bt >= 0x40) {
                    // empty block type
                } else if (bt >= 0x7c) {
                    // value block type
                }
            }

            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x05 || op === 0x0b) {
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
            readULEB(b, p);
            p = readULEB(b, p).next;
            continue;
        }

        /*
         * local.get
         */
        if (op === 0x20) {
            const r = readULEB(b, p);
            p = r.next;

            const v = locals.get(r.value) ?? unknown(`L${r.value}`);

            stack.push(v);
            continue;
        }

        /*
         * local.set
         */
        if (op === 0x21) {
            const r = readULEB(b, p);
            p = r.next;

            const v = pop(stack)[0];
            locals.set(r.value, v);
            continue;
        }

        /*
         * local.tee
         */
        if (op === 0x22) {
            const r = readULEB(b, p);
            p = r.next;

            const v = stack.length
                ? stack[stack.length - 1]
                : unknown("<STACK-UNDERFLOW>");

            locals.set(r.value, v);
            continue;
        }

        /*
         * global.get
         */
        if (op === 0x23) {
            const r = readULEB(b, p);
            p = r.next;

            stack.push(i32(`G${r.value}`));
            continue;
        }

        /*
         * global.set
         */
        if (op === 0x24) {
            readULEB(b, p);
            p = readULEB(b, p).next;
            pop(stack);
            continue;
        }

        /*
         * i32.const
         */
        if (op === 0x41) {
            const r = readI32(b, p);
            p = r.next;

            stack.push(i32(r.value.toString()));
            continue;
        }

        /*
         * i64.const
         */
        if (op === 0x42) {
            const r = readI64(b, p);
            p = r.next;

            stack.push(i64(r.value.toString()));
            continue;
        }

        /*
         * i32 arithmetic
         */
        if (op === 0x6a || op === 0x6b || op === 0x71 ||
            op === 0x72 || op === 0x74) {

            const [a, c] = pop(stack, 2);

            let text = "";

            if (op === 0x6a) text = `(${a.text}+${c.text})`;
            if (op === 0x6b) text = `(${a.text}-${c.text})`;
            if (op === 0x71) text = `(${a.text}&${c.text})`;
            if (op === 0x72) text = `(${a.text}|${c.text})`;
            if (op === 0x74) text = `(${a.text}<<${c.text})`;

            stack.push(i32(text));
            continue;
        }

        /*
         * i64 arithmetic
         */
        if (op === 0x7c || op === 0x7d ||
            op === 0x83 || op === 0x84 ||
            op === 0x85 || op === 0x86 ||
            op === 0x88) {

            const [a, c] = pop(stack, 2);

            let text = "";

            if (op === 0x7c) text = `(${a.text}+${c.text})`;
            if (op === 0x7d) text = `(${a.text}-${c.text})`;
            if (op === 0x83) text = `(${a.text}&${c.text})`;
            if (op === 0x84) text = `(${a.text}|${c.text})`;
            if (op === 0x85) text = `(${a.text}^${c.text})`;
            if (op === 0x86) text = `(${a.text}<<${c.text})`;
            if (op === 0x88) text = `(${a.text}>>u${c.text})`;

            stack.push(i64(text));
            continue;
        }

        /*
         * Comparisons.
         */
        if (op === 0x45 || op === 0x46 ||
            op === 0x4a || op === 0x4b ||
            op === 0x4f || op === 0x51 ||
            op === 0x54 || op === 0x56) {

            if (op === 0x45 || op === 0x51) {
                const [a] = pop(stack);
                stack.push(i32(`eqz(${a.text})`));
            } else {
                const [a, c] = pop(stack, 2);
                stack.push(i32(`${opcodeName(op)}(${a.text},${c.text})`));
            }

            continue;
        }

        /*
         * Loads.
         *
         * We preserve the address expression.
         */
        if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x35) {
            const align = readULEB(b, p);
            p = align.next;

            const off = readULEB(b, p);
            p = off.next;

            const [addr] = pop(stack);

            let type: "i32" | "i64" = "i32";

            if (
                op === 0x29 ||
                op === 0x30 ||
                op === 0x31 ||
                op === 0x34 ||
                op === 0x35
            ) {
                type = "i64";
            }

            const expr = `MEM[${addr.text}+${off.value}]`;

            stack.push(type === "i64" ? i64(expr) : i32(expr));
            continue;
        }

        /*
         * Stores.
         */
        if (op >= 0x36 && op <= 0x3e) {
            const align = readULEB(b, p);
            p = align.next;

            const off = readULEB(b, p);
            p = off.next;

            pop(stack, 2);
            continue;
        }

        /*
         * Numeric conversions.
         */
        if (op === 0xa7 || op === 0xac || op === 0xad) {
            const [a] = pop(stack);

            if (op === 0xa7) {
                stack.push(i32(`wrap(${a.text})`));
            } else if (op === 0xac) {
                stack.push(i64(`extend_s(${a.text})`));
            } else {
                stack.push(i64(`extend_u(${a.text})`));
            }

            continue;
        }

        /*
         * CALL
         */
        if (op === 0x10) {
            const r = readULEB(b, p);
            p = r.next;

            const fn = r.value;

            /*
             * We know Function 205 is the target of interest.
             *
             * IMPORTANT:
             * The argument count is NOT guessed here.
             *
             * We simply print the complete symbolic stack immediately
             * before the call, so we can identify the actual five
             * arguments from the compiled stack state.
             */
            if (fn === 205 && targetSet.has(pc)) {
                console.log("============================================================");
                console.log(`CALL 205 @ ${hex(pc)}`);
                console.log("============================================================");

                console.log(`Stack depth before CALL : ${stack.length}`);

                const start = Math.max(0, stack.length - 8);

                for (let i = start; i < stack.length; i++) {
                    console.log(
                        `stack[${i}] = ${stack[i].text}`
                    );
                }

                console.log();
                console.log("Top 5 stack values (candidate CALL arguments):");

                const args = stack.slice(Math.max(0, stack.length - 5));

                args.forEach((x, i) => {
                    console.log(
                        `  arg${i} = ${x.text}`
                    );
                });

                console.log();

                /*
                 * Function 205 has the known five-argument call pattern
                 * established earlier:
                 *
                 *   (i32, i64, i64, i64, i64)
                 *
                 * Do NOT remove values from the symbolic stack here until
                 * we know whether the compiler-generated call state needs
                 * additional context.
                 */
            }

            /*
             * For unknown calls we cannot safely infer the exact signature.
             * Preserve symbolic stack conservatively.
             *
             * For the targeted CALL 205 we leave the stack untouched,
             * because this tool is only an argument-state tracer.
             */
            continue;
        }

        /*
         * drop
         */
        if (op === 0x1a) {
            pop(stack);
            continue;
        }

        /*
         * select
         */
        if (op === 0x1b) {
            const [cond, b2, a2] = pop(stack, 3);
            stack.push(unknown(
                `select(${cond.text},${b2.text},${a2.text})`
            ));
            continue;
        }

        /*
         * Extended opcode.
         *
         * We only need to consume it safely here.
         */
        if (op === 0xfc) {
            const sub = readULEB(b, p);
            p = sub.next;

            switch (sub.value) {
                case 0x08:
                    p = readULEB(b, p).next;
                    p += 1;
                    break;

                case 0x09:
                    p = readULEB(b, p).next;
                    break;

                case 0x0a:
                    p += 2;
                    break;

                case 0x0b:
                    p += 1;
                    break;

                case 0x0c:
                    p = readULEB(b, p).next;
                    p += 1;
                    break;

                case 0x0d:
                    p = readULEB(b, p).next;
                    break;

                case 0x0e:
                    p += 2;
                    break;

                case 0x0f:
                case 0x10:
                case 0x11:
                    p = readULEB(b, p).next;
                    break;

                default:
                    console.log(
                        `WARNING: unknown 0xfc subopcode ${hex(sub.value, 2)} @ ${hex(pc)}`
                    );
                    break;
            }

            continue;
        }

        /*
         * Any other opcode:
         *
         * We deliberately do not guess its stack effect.
         * The purpose of V1 is to identify the stack state at the
         * four known CALL sites without corrupting the analysis.
         */
    }

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("TRACE COMPLETE");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(`Instructions scanned : ${instructionCount}`);
    console.log(`Final PC              : ${hex(p)}`);
    console.log(`Body end              : ${hex(target.bodyEnd)}`);
}

main();

FILE: scripts\traceFunction2094Call205Sites.ts

import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const START = 0x00095e62;
const END   = 0x00096da8;

const CALLS = [
    0x000969de,
    0x00096aa1,
    0x00096aad,
    0x00096ab4,
];

const WINDOW_BEFORE = 0x90;
const WINDOW_AFTER  = 0x20;

const bytes = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function hx(n: number): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function uleb(pos: number) {

    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {

        const b = bytes[pos++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if (!(b & 0x80))
            return {
                value,
                next: pos
            };

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function sleb32(pos: number) {

    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {

        const b = bytes[pos++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if (!(b & 0x80)) {

            if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
                value |= (-1 << (shift + 7));

            return {
                value: value | 0,
                next: pos
            };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}


/* ============================================================ */
/* INSTRUCTION DECODER                                          */
/* ============================================================ */

type Ins = {
    pc: number;
    op: string;
    imm?: number;
    text: string;
};

function decodeAt(pos: number): Ins | null {

    if (pos >= END)
        return null;

    const pc = pos;
    const b = bytes[pos++];

    try {

        /* local.get */

        if (b === 0x20) {

            const x = uleb(pos);

            return {
                pc,
                op: "local.get",
                imm: x.value,
                text: `local.get ${x.value}`
            };
        }


        /* local.set */

        if (b === 0x21) {

            const x = uleb(pos);

            return {
                pc,
                op: "local.set",
                imm: x.value,
                text: `local.set ${x.value}`
            };
        }


        /* local.tee */

        if (b === 0x22) {

            const x = uleb(pos);

            return {
                pc,
                op: "local.tee",
                imm: x.value,
                text: `local.tee ${x.value}`
            };
        }


        /* global.get */

        if (b === 0x23) {

            const x = uleb(pos);

            return {
                pc,
                op: "global.get",
                imm: x.value,
                text: `global.get ${x.value}`
            };
        }


        /* global.set */

        if (b === 0x24) {

            const x = uleb(pos);

            return {
                pc,
                op: "global.set",
                imm: x.value,
                text: `global.set ${x.value}`
            };
        }


        /* i32.const */

        if (b === 0x41) {

            const x = sleb32(pos);

            return {
                pc,
                op: "i32.const",
                imm: x.value,
                text: `i32.const ${x.value}`
            };
        }


        /* i64.const */

        if (b === 0x42) {

            let q = pos;

            while (q < END) {

                const z = bytes[q++];

                if (!(z & 0x80))
                    break;
            }

            return {
                pc,
                op: "i64.const",
                text: "i64.const"
            };
        }


        /* i32.load */

        if (b === 0x28) {

            const a = uleb(pos);
            const o = uleb(a.next);

            return {
                pc,
                op: "i32.load",
                imm: o.value,
                text: `i32.load offset=${o.value}`
            };
        }


        /* i64.load */

        if (b === 0x29) {

            const a = uleb(pos);
            const o = uleb(a.next);

            return {
                pc,
                op: "i64.load",
                imm: o.value,
                text: `i64.load offset=${o.value}`
            };
        }


        /* i64.load8_u */

        if (b === 0x31) {

            const a = uleb(pos);
            const o = uleb(a.next);

            return {
                pc,
                op: "i64.load8_u",
                imm: o.value,
                text: `i64.load8_u offset=${o.value}`
            };
        }


        /* i32.store */

        if (b === 0x36) {

            const a = uleb(pos);
            const o = uleb(a.next);

            return {
                pc,
                op: "i32.store",
                imm: o.value,
                text: `i32.store offset=${o.value}`
            };
        }


        /* i64.store */

        if (b === 0x37) {

            const a = uleb(pos);
            const o = uleb(a.next);

            return {
                pc,
                op: "i64.store",
                imm: o.value,
                text: `i64.store offset=${o.value}`
            };
        }


        /* call */

        if (b === 0x10) {

            const x = uleb(pos);

            return {
                pc,
                op: "call",
                imm: x.value,
                text: `call ${x.value}`
            };
        }


        /* br */

        if (b === 0x0c) {

            const x = uleb(pos);

            return {
                pc,
                op: "br",
                imm: x.value,
                text: `br ${x.value}`
            };
        }


        /* br_if */

        if (b === 0x0d) {

            const x = uleb(pos);

            return {
                pc,
                op: "br_if",
                imm: x.value,
                text: `br_if ${x.value}`
            };
        }


        /* block */

        if (b === 0x02) {

            const x = sleb32(pos);

            return {
                pc,
                op: "block",
                text: "block"
            };
        }


        /* loop */

        if (b === 0x03) {

            const x = sleb32(pos);

            return {
                pc,
                op: "loop",
                text: "loop"
            };
        }


        /* if */

        if (b === 0x04) {

            const x = sleb32(pos);

            return {
                pc,
                op: "if",
                text: "if"
            };
        }


        /* else */

        if (b === 0x05) {

            return {
                pc,
                op: "else",
                text: "else"
            };
        }


        /* end */

        if (b === 0x0b) {

            return {
                pc,
                op: "end",
                text: "end"
            };
        }


        const simple: Record<number, string> = {

            0x1a: "drop",
            0x1b: "select",

            0x45: "i32.eqz",
            0x46: "i32.eq",
            0x47: "i32.ne",
            0x48: "i32.lt_s",
            0x49: "i32.lt_u",
            0x4a: "i32.gt_s",
            0x4b: "i32.gt_u",

            0x6a: "i32.add",
            0x6b: "i32.sub",
            0x74: "i32.shl",
            0x75: "i32.shr_s",
            0x76: "i32.shr_u",

            0x7c: "i64.add",
            0x7d: "i64.sub",
            0x7e: "i64.mul",
            0x83: "i64.and",
            0x84: "i64.or",
            0x85: "i64.xor",
            0x86: "i64.shl",
            0x88: "i64.shr_u",
        };

        if (simple[b]) {

            return {
                pc,
                op: simple[b],
                text: simple[b]
            };
        }


        return {
            pc,
            op: "unknown",
            text:
                `UNKNOWN 0x${b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
        };

    } catch {

        return {
            pc,
            op: "decode_error",
            text: "DECODE_ERROR"
        };
    }
}


/* ============================================================ */
/* DECODE COMPLETE FUNCTION                                     */
/* ============================================================ */

function decodeFunction(): Ins[] {

    const result: Ins[] = [];

    let p = START;

    while (p < END) {

        const x = decodeAt(p);

        if (!x)
            break;

        result.push(x);

        /*
         * Determine instruction length by decoding the next
         * instruction boundary from the raw byte stream.
         *
         * For exact boundary recovery we use a small scanner
         * that mirrors decodeAt.
         */

        let q = p + 1;

        try {

            const b = bytes[p];

            if (
                b === 0x20 ||
                b === 0x21 ||
                b === 0x22 ||
                b === 0x23 ||
                b === 0x24 ||
                b === 0x10 ||
                b === 0x0c ||
                b === 0x0d
            ) {

                q = uleb(q).next;

            } else if (b === 0x41) {

                q = sleb32(q).next;

            } else if (b === 0x42) {

                while (q < END) {

                    const z = bytes[q++];

                    if (!(z & 0x80))
                        break;
                }

            } else if (
                b === 0x28 ||
                b === 0x29 ||
                b === 0x31 ||
                b === 0x36 ||
                b === 0x37
            ) {

                q = uleb(q).next;
                q = uleb(q).next;

            } else if (
                b === 0x02 ||
                b === 0x03 ||
                b === 0x04
            ) {

                q = sleb32(q).next;
            }

        } catch {

            q = p + 1;
        }

        if (q <= p)
            q = p + 1;

        p = q;
    }

    return result;
}


/* ============================================================ */
/* RAW HEX WINDOW                                                */
/* ============================================================ */

function dumpHex(center: number) {

    const from = Math.max(
        START,
        center - WINDOW_BEFORE
    );

    const to = Math.min(
        END,
        center + WINDOW_AFTER
    );

    console.log("");
    console.log(
        `RAW BYTES ${hx(from)} .. ${hx(to)}`
    );
    console.log("");

    for (
        let p = from;
        p < to;
        p += 16
    ) {

        const lineEnd = Math.min(
            p + 16,
            to
        );

        const hex: string[] = [];

        for (
            let q = p;
            q < lineEnd;
            q++
        ) {

            hex.push(
                bytes[q]
                    .toString(16)
                    .padStart(2, "0")
            );
        }

        console.log(
            `${hx(p)}  ${hex.join(" ")}`
        );
    }
}


/* ============================================================ */
/* FIND INSTRUCTION AT EXACT PC                                  */
/* ============================================================ */

const instructions = decodeFunction();

function findInstruction(pc: number) {

    return instructions.find(
        x => x.pc === pc
    );
}

/* ============================================================ */
/* EACH CALL SITE                                                */
/* ============================================================ */

for (const callPc of CALLS) {

    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );

    console.log(
        `CALL SITE ${hx(callPc)}`
    );

    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );

    console.log("");

    const call = findInstruction(callPc);

    if (!call) {

        console.log(
            "CALL INSTRUCTION NOT FOUND"
        );

        continue;
    }

    console.log(
        `Confirmed instruction: ${call.text}`
    );

    console.log("");

    const nearby = instructions.filter(
        x =>
            x.pc >= callPc - WINDOW_BEFORE &&
            x.pc <= callPc
    );

    console.log(
        "INSTRUCTIONS BEFORE CALL"
    );

    console.log("");

    for (const x of nearby) {

        console.log(
            `${hx(x.pc)}  ${x.text}`
        );
    }

    console.log("");

    dumpHex(callPc);

    console.log("");
}

import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const CODE_START = 0x6e7d;
const TARGET_FUNC = 205;

const CALLS = [
  { name: "#1", pc: 0x969de, arg0: "G-208", arg1: "G-336" },
  { name: "#2", pc: 0x96aa1, arg0: "G-272", arg1: "G-208" },
  { name: "#3", pc: 0x96aad, arg0: "G-208", arg1: "G-528" },
  { name: "#4", pc: 0x96ab4, arg0: "G-528", arg1: "G-464" },
];

function readULEB(buf: Buffer, off: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = off;

  while (p < buf.length) {
    const b = buf[p++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value: value >>> 0, next: p };
    }

    shift += 7;
    if (shift > 35) throw new Error(`ULEB too long @ 0x${off.toString(16)}`);
  }

  throw new Error("Unexpected EOF in ULEB");
}

function findCodeSection(buf: Buffer) {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < buf.length) {
    const id = buf[p++];
    const s = readULEB(buf, p);
    const payloadStart = s.next;
    const payloadEnd = payloadStart + s.value;

    if (id === 10) {
      return {
        header: p - 1,
        payloadStart,
        payloadEnd,
      };
    }

    p = payloadEnd;
  }

  throw new Error("Code section not found");
}

function buildFunctionMap(buf: Buffer) {
  const code = findCodeSection(buf);

  let p = code.payloadStart;

  const countInfo = readULEB(buf, p);
  const count = countInfo.value;
  p = countInfo.next;

  const functions: {
    bodyIndex: number;
    realIndex: number;
    sizeField: number;
    codeStart: number;
    bodyEnd: number;
    bodySize: number;
  }[] = [];

  /*
   * The first real function index is 83 in this WASM.
   * This was already verified against the complete code-section traversal.
   */
  const FIRST_REAL_FUNC = 83;

  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    const sizeField = p;
    const sizeInfo = readULEB(buf, p);
    const codeStart = sizeInfo.next;
    const bodyEnd = codeStart + sizeInfo.value;

    functions.push({
      bodyIndex: i,
      realIndex: FIRST_REAL_FUNC + i,
      sizeField,
      codeStart,
      bodyEnd,
      bodySize: sizeInfo.value,
    });

    p = bodyEnd;
  }

  if (p !== code.payloadEnd) {
    throw new Error(
      `Code section mismatch: parser=0x${p.toString(16)} end=0x${code.payloadEnd.toString(16)}`
    );
  }

  return { code, functions };
}

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function readI32S(buf: Buffer, off: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = off;

  while (p < buf.length) {
    const b = buf[p++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      if (shift < 31 && (b & 0x40)) {
        value |= (~0 << (shift + 7));
      }

      return { value: value | 0, next: p };
    }

    shift += 7;
    if (shift > 35) {
      throw new Error(`SLEB too long @ ${hex(off)}`);
    }
  }

  throw new Error("Unexpected EOF in SLEB");
}

/*
 * Scan only the verified Function 205 body.
 *
 * We deliberately do NOT attempt full symbolic execution here.
 * Instead we identify the exact memory accesses which are relevant
 * to the four caller-provided L1 addresses.
 */
function scanFunction205(
  buf: Buffer,
  fn: {
    codeStart: number;
    bodyEnd: number;
    realIndex: number;
  }
) {
  const start = fn.codeStart;
  const end = fn.bodyEnd;

  console.log();
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log("FUNCTION 205 — FOUR INVOCATION MEMORY TRACE");
  console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(`Function       : ${fn.realIndex}`);
  console.log(`Code start     : ${hex(start)}`);
  console.log(`Body end       : ${hex(end)}`);
  console.log(`Body bytes     : ${end - start}`);

  console.log();
  console.log("Relevant Function 205 accesses:");
  console.log();

  let p = start;

  let localCount = -1;
  let localIndex = 0;

  /*
   * Decode local declarations first.
   *
   * Function 205 has:
   *   local0..2  i32
   *   local3..39 i64
   */
  if (buf[p] !== 0x02) {
    console.log(
      `WARNING: unexpected local declaration prefix ${hex(buf[p])} @ ${hex(p)}`
    );
  }

  if (buf[p] === 0x02) {
    const localDeclCount = buf[p + 1];

    console.log(`Local declaration groups : ${localDeclCount}`);

    p += 2;

    for (let i = 0; i < localDeclCount; i++) {
      const c = readULEB(buf, p);
      p = c.next;

      const type = buf[p++];

      console.log(
        `  group ${i}: count=${c.value} type=0x${type.toString(16)}`
      );

      localCount += c.value;
    }
  }

  console.log();
  console.log("Expected:");
  console.log("  L6  = MEM64[L1 + 0]");
  console.log("  L12 = MEM64[L1 + 8]");
  console.log("  L13 = MEM64[L2 + 496]");
  console.log("  L10 = low64(MEM128[L2 + 368])");
  console.log("  L29 = L10 << 1");
  console.log();

  /*
   * Lightweight opcode scanner.
   *
   * We are interested specifically in:
   *   0x20 local.get
   *   0x21 local.set
   *   0x22 local.tee
   *   0x29 i64.load
   *   0x37 i64.store
   *   0x10 call
   *
   * Memory immediates are decoded carefully as ULEB.
   *
   * This scanner is intentionally conservative: unknown instructions
   * are skipped only when their immediate size is unambiguous.
   */
  p = fn.codeStart;

  const hits: {
    pc: number;
    kind: string;
    local?: number;
    offset?: number;
    call?: number;
  }[] = [];

  while (p < end) {
    const pc = p;
    const op = buf[p++];

    if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
      const x = readULEB(buf, p);
      p = x.next;

      hits.push({
        pc,
        kind:
          op === 0x20
            ? "local.get"
            : op === 0x21
              ? "local.set"
              : "local.tee",
        local: x.value,
      });

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x10) {
      const x = readULEB(buf, p);
      p = x.next;

      hits.push({
        pc,
        kind: "call",
        call: x.value,
      });

      continue;
    }

    /*
     * i64.load:
     *   opcode 0x29
     *   alignment ULEB
     *   offset ULEB
     */
    if (op === 0x29) {
      const align = readULEB(buf, p);
      p = align.next;

      const off = readULEB(buf, p);
      p = off.next;

      hits.push({
        pc,
        kind: "i64.load",
        offset: off.value,
      });

      continue;
    }

    /*
     * i64.store:
     *   opcode 0x37
     *   alignment ULEB
     *   offset ULEB
     */
    if (op === 0x37) {
      const align = readULEB(buf, p);
      p = align.next;

      const off = readULEB(buf, p);
      p = off.next;

      hits.push({
        pc,
        kind: "i64.store",
        offset: off.value,
      });

      continue;
    }

    /*
     * Constants.
     */
    if (op === 0x41) {
      const x = readI32S(buf, p);
      p = x.next;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x42) {
      /*
       * Signed i64 LEB.
       */
      let q = p;
      let shift = 0;

      while (q < end) {
        const b = buf[q++];
        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 70) {
          throw new Error(`Bad i64.const @ ${hex(pc)}`);
        }
      }

      p = q;
      continue;
    }

    /*
     * Simple fixed-size instructions.
     */
    if (
      op === 0x00 || // unreachable
      op === 0x01 || // nop
      op === 0x0b || // end
      op === 0x0f || // return
      op === 0x1a || // drop
      op === 0x1b || // select
      op === 0x45 || // i32.eqz
      op === 0x46 || // i32.eq
      op === 0x47 || // i32.ne
      op === 0x48 || // i32.lt_s
      op === 0x49 || // i32.lt_u
      op === 0x4a || // i32.gt_s
      op === 0x4b || // i32.gt_u
      op === 0x4c || // i32.le_s
      op === 0x4d || // i32.le_u
      op === 0x4e || // i32.ge_s
      op === 0x4f || // i32.ge_u
      op === 0x50 || // i64.eqz
      op === 0x51 || // i64.eq
      op === 0x52 || // i64.ne
      op === 0x53 || // i64.lt_s
      op === 0x54 || // i64.lt_u
      op === 0x55 || // i64.gt_s
      op === 0x56 || // i64.gt_u
      op === 0x57 || // i64.le_s
      op === 0x58 || // i64.le_u
      op === 0x59 || // i64.ge_s
      op === 0x5a || // i64.ge_u
      op === 0x5b || // i64.extend8_s
      op === 0x5c || // i64.extend16_s
      op === 0x5d || // i64.extend32_s
      op === 0x67 || // i32.clz
      op === 0x68 || // i32.ctz
      op === 0x69 || // i32.popcnt
      op === 0x6a || // i32.add
      op === 0x6b || // i32.sub
      op === 0x6c || // i32.mul
      op === 0x6d || // i32.div_s
      op === 0x6e || // i32.div_u
      op === 0x6f || // i32.rem_s
      op === 0x70 || // i32.rem_u
      op === 0x71 || // i32.and
      op === 0x72 || // i32.or
      op === 0x73 || // i32.xor
      op === 0x74 || // i32.shl
      op === 0x75 || // i32.shr_s
      op === 0x76 || // i32.shr_u
      op === 0x77 || // i32.rotl
      op === 0x78 || // i32.rotr
      op === 0x79 || // i64.clz
      op === 0x7a || // i64.ctz
      op === 0x7b || // i64.popcnt
      op === 0x7c || // i64.add
      op === 0x7d || // i64.sub
      op === 0x7e || // i64.mul
      op === 0x7f || // i64.div_s
      op === 0x80 || // i64.div_u
      op === 0x81 || // i64.rem_s
      op === 0x82 || // i64.rem_u
      op === 0x83 || // i64.and
      op === 0x84 || // i64.or
      op === 0x85 || // i64.xor
      op === 0x86 || // i64.shl
      op === 0x87 || // i64.shr_s
      op === 0x88 || // i64.shr_u
      op === 0x89 || // i64.rotl
      op === 0x8a || // i64.rotr
      op === 0xa7 || // i32.wrap_i64
      op === 0xac || // i64.extend_i32_s
      op === 0xad    // i64.extend_i32_u
    ) {
      continue;
    }

    /*
     * Structured control.
     *
     * block/loop/if have a block type byte.
     * br/br_if/br_table/call_indirect need immediates.
     */
    if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
      p++;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
      const x = readULEB(buf, p);
      p = x.next;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x0e) {
      const n = readULEB(buf, p);
      p = n.next;

      for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
        const x = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = x.next;
      }

      const def = readULEB(buf, p);
      p = def.next;
      continue;
    }

    /*
     * Anything else is retained as an unknown byte, but we do not
     * pretend to decode it.
     */
    hits.push({
      pc,
      kind: `UNKNOWN opcode=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`,
    });
  }

  const calls205 = hits.filter(
    (x) => x.kind === "call" && x.call === TARGET_FUNC
  );

  const loads0 = hits.filter(
    (x) => x.kind === "i64.load" && x.offset === 0
  );

  const loads8 = hits.filter(
    (x) => x.kind === "i64.load" && x.offset === 8
  );

  const loads496 = hits.filter(
    (x) => x.kind === "i64.load" && x.offset === 496
  );

  const loads368 = hits.filter(
    (x) => x.kind === "i64.load" && x.offset === 368
  );

  console.log(`CALL ${TARGET_FUNC} inside Function 205 : ${calls205.length}`);
  console.log(`i64.load offset=0                 : ${loads0.length}`);
  console.log(`i64.load offset=8                 : ${loads8.length}`);
  console.log(`i64.load offset=368               : ${loads368.length}`);
  console.log(`i64.load offset=496               : ${loads496.length}`);

  console.log();
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log("FOUR INVOCATION ARGUMENT MAP");
  console.log("============================================================");

  for (const c of CALLS) {
    console.log();
    console.log(`${c.name} @ ${hex(c.pc)}`);
    console.log(`  arg0 = ${c.arg0}`);
    console.log(`  arg1 = ${c.arg1}`);
    console.log(`  L1   = ${c.arg1}`);
    console.log(`  L6   = MEM64[L1+0]`);
    console.log(`  L12  = MEM64[L1+8]`);
  }

  console.log();
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log("TARGET DATAFLOW");
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log("L6  = MEM64[L1 + 0]");
  console.log("L12 = MEM64[L1 + 8]");
  console.log();
  console.log("CALL87 @ 0x1242b:");
  console.log("  result = L12 × (L6 << 1)");
  console.log("  L10    = low64(result)");
  console.log("  L29    = L10 << 1");
  console.log();
  console.log("L13 = MEM64[L2 + 496]");
  console.log();
  console.log("CALL87 @ 0x126b4:");
  console.log("  arg0 = L2 - 64");
  console.log("  arg1 = L13");
  console.log("  arg2 = 0");
  console.log("  arg3 = L29");
  console.log("  arg4 = 0");

  console.log();
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log("CONCLUSION OF THIS PASS");
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(
    "This pass does NOT invent runtime memory values."
  );
  console.log(
    "It establishes the exact four invocation address families and"
  );
  console.log(
    "the Function 205 memory locations that must be traced next."
  );

  const unknowns = hits.filter((x) => x.kind.startsWith("UNKNOWN"));

  console.log();
  console.log(`Unknown opcode bytes retained : ${unknowns.length}`);

  if (unknowns.length) {
    console.log();
    console.log("First unknowns:");
    for (const u of unknowns.slice(0, 20)) {
      console.log(`  ${hex(u.pc)}  ${u.kind}`);
    }
  }
}

export async function run() {
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log("FUNCTION 2094 → FUNCTION 205 FOUR-WAY TRACE v1");
  console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
  console.log("============================================================");

  if (!fs.existsSync(WASM)) {
    throw new Error(`WASM not found: ${WASM}`);
  }

  const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

  console.log(`WASM size : ${buf.length.toLocaleString()}`);

  const { functions } = buildFunctionMap(buf);

  const fn = functions.find((x) => x.realIndex === TARGET_FUNC);

  if (!fn) {
    throw new Error(`Function ${TARGET_FUNC} not found`);
  }

  console.log(`Function ${TARGET_FUNC} body # : ${fn.bodyIndex}`);
  console.log(`Code start              : ${hex(fn.codeStart)}`);
  console.log(`Body end                : ${hex(fn.bodyEnd)}`);

  scanFunction205(buf, fn);

  console.log();
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log("DONE");
  console.log("============================================================");
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceFunction2094Global0V2.ts

import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_FUNC = 2094;

function readULEB(buf: Buffer, off: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = off;

  while (p < buf.length) {
    const b = buf[p++];

    value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value, next: p };
    }

    shift += 7;

    if (shift > 35) {
      throw new Error(`ULEB too long @ 0x${off.toString(16)}`);
    }
  }

  throw new Error(`Unexpected EOF @ 0x${off.toString(16)}`);
}

function readSLEB(buf: Buffer, off: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = off;
  let byte = 0;

  while (p < buf.length) {
    byte = buf[p++];

    value |= (byte & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) {
      if (shift < 32 && (byte & 0x40)) {
        value |= ~0 << shift;
      }

      return { value: value | 0, next: p };
    }

    if (shift > 35) {
      throw new Error(`SLEB too long @ 0x${off.toString(16)}`);
    }
  }

  throw new Error(`Unexpected EOF @ 0x${off.toString(16)}`);
}

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function findCodeSection(buf: Buffer) {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < buf.length) {
    const id = buf[p++];

    const size = readULEB(buf, p);
    const payloadStart = size.next;
    const payloadEnd = payloadStart + size.value;

    if (id === 10) {
      return {
        payloadStart,
        payloadEnd,
      };
    }

    p = payloadEnd;
  }

  throw new Error("Code section not found");
}

function buildFunctionMap(buf: Buffer) {
  const code = findCodeSection(buf);

  let p = code.payloadStart;

  const countInfo = readULEB(buf, p);
  const count = countInfo.value;
  p = countInfo.next;

  /*
   * Previously independently verified:
   *
   * Function bodies begin at real function index 83.
   * 9398 bodies traverse exactly to code-section end.
   */
  const FIRST_REAL_FUNC = 83;

  const functions: {
    bodyIndex: number;
    realIndex: number;
    sizeField: number;
    codeStart: number;
    bodyEnd: number;
    bodySize: number;
  }[] = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    const sizeField = p;

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(buf, p);
    const codeStart = sizeInfo.next;
    const bodyEnd = codeStart + sizeInfo.value;

    functions.push({
      bodyIndex: i,
      realIndex: FIRST_REAL_FUNC + i,
      sizeField,
      codeStart,
      bodyEnd,
      bodySize: sizeInfo.value,
    });

    p = bodyEnd;
  }

  if (p !== code.payloadEnd) {
    throw new Error(
      `CODE SECTION MISMATCH\n` +
      `parser = ${hex(p)}\n` +
      `section = ${hex(code.payloadEnd)}`
    );
  }

  return {
    code,
    functions,
  };
}

/*
 * Decode a small set of instructions.
 *
 * We only need enough decoding to recognize:
 *
 *   local.get 7
 *   i32.const N
 *   i32.add
 *   i32.sub
 *   i64.store
 *   i32.store
 *
 * This is deliberately a producer scanner, not a full WASM emulator.
 */

type Inst =
  | {
      pc: number;
      op: "local.get" | "local.set" | "local.tee";
      local: number;
    }
  | {
      pc: number;
      op: "i32.const";
      value: number;
    }
  | {
      pc: number;
      op: "i32.add" | "i32.sub";
    }
  | {
      pc: number;
      op: "i64.store" | "i32.store";
      offset: number;
    }
  | {
      pc: number;
      op: "call";
      target: number;
    }
  | {
      pc: number;
      op: "global.get" | "global.set";
      global: number;
    }
  | {
      pc: number;
      op: "unknown";
      byte: number;
    };

function decodeRelevant(buf: Buffer, start: number, end: number): Inst[] {
  const out: Inst[] = [];

  let p = start;

  while (p < end) {
    const pc = p;
    const op = buf[p++];

    /*
     * local.get / local.set / local.tee
     */
    if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
      const x = readULEB(buf, p);
      p = x.next;

      out.push({
        pc,
        op:
          op === 0x20
            ? "local.get"
            : op === 0x21
              ? "local.set"
              : "local.tee",
        local: x.value,
      });

      continue;
    }

    /*
     * i32.const
     */
    if (op === 0x41) {
      const x = readSLEB(buf, p);
      p = x.next;

      out.push({
        pc,
        op: "i32.const",
        value: x.value,
      });

      continue;
    }

    /*
     * global.get / global.set
     */
    if (op === 0x23 || op === 0x24) {
      const x = readULEB(buf, p);
      p = x.next;

      out.push({
        pc,
        op: op === 0x23 ? "global.get" : "global.set",
        global: x.value,
      });

      continue;
    }

    /*
     * call
     */
    if (op === 0x10) {
      const x = readULEB(buf, p);
      p = x.next;

      out.push({
        pc,
        op: "call",
        target: x.value,
      });

      continue;
    }

    /*
     * i32.add / i32.sub
     */
    if (op === 0x6a) {
      out.push({
        pc,
        op: "i32.add",
      });
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x6b) {
      out.push({
        pc,
        op: "i32.sub",
      });
      continue;
    }

    /*
     * i64.store = 0x37
     *
     * IMPORTANT:
     * 0x38 is NOT i64.store.
     * Earlier raw scans showed false positives caused by
     * 0x38 occurring inside immediates.
     */
    if (op === 0x37) {
      const align = readULEB(buf, p);
      p = align.next;

      const offset = readULEB(buf, p);
      p = offset.next;

      out.push({
        pc,
        op: "i64.store",
        offset: offset.value,
      });

      continue;
    }

    /*
     * i32.store = 0x36
     */
    if (op === 0x36) {
      const align = readULEB(buf, p);
      p = align.next;

      const offset = readULEB(buf, p);
      p = offset.next;

      out.push({
        pc,
        op: "i32.store",
        offset: offset.value,
      });

      continue;
    }

    /*
     * Simple no-immediate instructions.
     *
     * We don't need their semantic meaning for this scan.
     */
    if (
      op === 0x00 ||
      op === 0x01 ||
      op === 0x0b ||
      op === 0x0f ||
      op === 0x1a ||
      op === 0x1b ||
      op === 0x45 ||
      op === 0x46 ||
      op === 0x47 ||
      op === 0x48 ||
      op === 0x49 ||
      op === 0x4a ||
      op === 0x4b ||
      op === 0x4c ||
      op === 0x4d ||
      op === 0x4e ||
      op === 0x4f ||
      op === 0x50 ||
      op === 0x51 ||
      op === 0x52 ||
      op === 0x53 ||
      op === 0x54 ||
      op === 0x55 ||
      op === 0x56 ||
      op === 0x57 ||
      op === 0x58 ||
      op === 0x59 ||
      op === 0x5a ||
      op === 0x67 ||
      op === 0x68 ||
      op === 0x69 ||
      op === 0x6a ||
      op === 0x6b ||
      op === 0x6c ||
      op === 0x6d ||
      op === 0x6e ||
      op === 0x6f ||
      op === 0x70 ||
      op === 0x71 ||
      op === 0x72 ||
      op === 0x73 ||
      op === 0x74 ||
      op === 0x75 ||
      op === 0x76 ||
      op === 0x77 ||
      op === 0x78 ||
      op === 0x79 ||
      op === 0x7a ||
      op === 0x7b ||
      op === 0x7c ||
      op === 0x7d ||
      op === 0x7e ||
      op === 0x7f ||
      op === 0x80 ||
      op === 0x81 ||
      op === 0x82 ||
      op === 0x83 ||
      op === 0x84 ||
      op === 0x85 ||
      op === 0x86 ||
      op === 0x87 ||
      op === 0x88 ||
      op === 0x89 ||
      op === 0x8a ||
      op === 0xa7 ||
      op === 0xac ||
      op === 0xad
    ) {
      continue;
    }

    /*
     * block / loop / if
     */
    if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
      p++;
      continue;
    }

    /*
     * br / br_if
     */
    if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
      const x = readULEB(buf, p);
      p = x.next;
      continue;
    }

    /*
     * br_table
     */
    if (op === 0x0e) {
      const n = readULEB(buf, p);
      p = n.next;

      for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
        const x = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = x.next;
      }

      const d = readULEB(buf, p);
      p = d.next;
      continue;
    }

    /*
     * Unknown opcode.
     *
     * Keep scanning one byte at a time.
     * This is safe for our target pattern because the producer
     * sequences we seek are already known and verified.
     */
    out.push({
      pc,
      op: "unknown",
      byte: op,
    });
  }

  return out;
}

function printWindow(insts: Inst[], index: number, radius = 8) {
  const a = Math.max(0, index - radius);
  const b = Math.min(insts.length, index + radius + 1);

  for (let i = a; i < b; i++) {
    const x = insts[i];

    let s = `${hex(x.pc)} `;

    if (x.op === "local.get") {
      s += `local.get ${x.local}`;
    } else if (x.op === "local.set") {
      s += `local.set ${x.local}`;
    } else if (x.op === "local.tee") {
      s += `local.tee ${x.local}`;
    } else if (x.op === "i32.const") {
      s += `i32.const ${x.value}`;
    } else if (x.op === "i32.add") {
      s += "i32.add";
    } else if (x.op === "i32.sub") {
      s += "i32.sub";
    } else if (x.op === "i64.store") {
      s += `i64.store offset=${x.offset}`;
    } else if (x.op === "i32.store") {
      s += `i32.store offset=${x.offset}`;
    } else if (x.op === "call") {
      s += `call ${x.target}`;
    } else if (x.op === "global.get") {
      s += `global.get ${x.global}`;
    } else if (x.op === "global.set") {
      s += `global.set ${x.global}`;
  } else if (x.op === "unknown") {
  s += `UNKNOWN 0x${x.byte.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
} else {
  s += "UNKNOWN";
}

    console.log(
      `${i === index ? ">>" : "  "} ${s}`
    );
  }
}

/*
 * Detect:
 *
 *   local.get 7
 *   i32.const K
 *   i32.add
 *   ...
 *   i64.store
 *
 * We translate K into an address relative to G.
 *
 * Because:
 *
 *   L7 = G - 464
 *
 * therefore:
 *
 *   L7 + K = G + (K - 464)
 */
function findL7RelativeStores(insts: Inst[]) {
  const TARGETS = new Map<number, string>([
    [-336, "CALL #1 L1  (G-336)"],
    [-328, "CALL #1 L1+8 (G-328)"],

    [-208, "CALL #2 L1  (G-208)"],
    [-200, "CALL #2 L1+8 (G-200)"],

    [-528, "CALL #3 L1  (G-528)"],
    [-520, "CALL #3 L1+8 (G-520)"],

    [-464, "CALL #4 L1  (G-464)"],
    [-456, "CALL #4 L1+8 (G-456)"],
  ]);

  const results: {
    pc: number;
    k: number;
    gOffset: number;
    target: string;
    store: string;
  }[] = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < insts.length - 3; i++) {
    const a = insts[i];
    const b = insts[i + 1];
    const c = insts[i + 2];

    if (
      a.op === "local.get" &&
      a.local === 7 &&
      b.op === "i32.const" &&
      c.op === "i32.add"
    ) {
      const k = b.value;
      const gOffset = k - 464;

      const target = TARGETS.get(gOffset);

      if (!target) continue;

      /*
       * Search a small forward window for the store.
       *
       * We intentionally don't cross a call or another address
       * construction.
       */
      for (
        let j = i + 3;
        j < Math.min(i + 10, insts.length);
        j++
      ) {
        const x = insts[j];

        if (x.op === "call") break;

        if (x.op === "i64.store") {
          results.push({
            pc: x.pc,
            k,
            gOffset,
            target,
            store: "i64.store",
          });

          break;
        }

        if (x.op === "i32.store") {
          results.push({
            pc: x.pc,
            k,
            gOffset,
            target,
            store: "i32.store",
          });

          break;
        }

        /*
         * A second address construction means the first expression
         * may no longer be the active address.
         */
        if (
          x.op === "local.get" ||
          x.op === "local.set" ||
          x.op === "local.tee"
        ) {
          /*
           * Don't immediately abort because local.get may simply
           * supply the value being stored.
           */
        }
      }
    }
  }

  return results;
}

  console.log();
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log("KNOWN CALL 205 INVOCATIONS");
  console.log("============================================================");

  const rows = [
    ["#1", "0x000969de", "G-208", "G-336"],
    ["#2", "0x00096aa1", "G-272", "G-208"],
    ["#3", "0x00096aad", "G-208", "G-528"],
    ["#4", "0x00096ab4", "G-528", "G-464"],
  ];

  for (const r of rows) {
    console.log(
      `${r[0].padEnd(4)} @ ${r[1]}  arg0=${r[2].padEnd(7)} arg1=${r[3]}`
    );
  }
}
