}

export async function run() {
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log("MEMORY PRODUCER TRACE v2");
  console.log("FUNCTION 2094 → FOUR CALL205 INVOCATIONS");
  console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
  console.log("============================================================");

  if (!fs.existsSync(WASM)) {
    throw new Error(`WASM not found: ${WASM}`);
  }

  const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

  console.log(`WASM size : ${buf.toLocaleString()} bytes`);

  const { functions } = buildFunctionMap(buf);

  const fn = functions.find(
    (x) => x.realIndex === TARGET_FUNC
  );

  if (!fn) {
    throw new Error(`Function ${TARGET_FUNC} not found`);
  }

  console.log();
  console.log(`Function       : ${fn.realIndex}`);
  console.log(`Body #         : ${fn.bodyIndex}`);
  console.log(`Size field     : ${hex(fn.sizeField)}`);
  console.log(`Code start     : ${hex(fn.codeStart)}`);
  console.log(`Body end       : ${hex(fn.bodyEnd)}`);
  console.log(`Code bytes     : ${fn.bodyEnd - fn.codeStart}`);

  printKnownCall205Map();
  printTargetAddressMap();

  console.log();
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log("DECODING RELEVANT INSTRUCTIONS");
  console.log("============================================================");

  const insts = decodeRelevant(
    buf,
    fn.codeStart,
    fn.bodyEnd
  );

  console.log(`Decoded records : ${insts.length}`);

  const stores = insts.filter(
    (x) =>
      x.op === "i64.store" ||
      x.op === "i32.store"
  );

  console.log(`Store records   : ${stores.length}`);

  console.log();
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log("L7-RELATIVE TARGET PRODUCERS");
  console.log("============================================================");

  const results = findL7RelativeStores(insts);

  if (results.length === 0) {
    console.log("No direct L7-relative producer pattern found.");
  } else {
    console.log(`Matches : ${results.length}`);
    console.log();

    for (const r of results) {
      console.log(
        `${hex(r.pc)}  ${r.store.padEnd(12)} ` +
        `L7${r.k >= 0 ? "+" : ""}${r.k} ` +
        `= G${r.gOffset >= 0 ? "+" : ""}${r.gOffset} ` +
        `→ ${r.target}`
      );
    }
  }

  console.log();
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log("RAW STORE OFFSETS IN FUNCTION 2094");
  console.log("============================================================");

  for (const x of stores) {
    if (
      x.op === "i64.store" ||
      x.op === "i32.store"
    ) {
      console.log(
        `${hex(x.pc)}  ${x.op.padEnd(12)} offset=${x.offset}`
      );
    }
  }

  console.log();
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log("CALL 205 LOCATIONS");
  console.log("============================================================");

  const calls205 = insts.filter(
    (x) =>
      x.op === "call" &&
      x.target === 205
  );

  console.log(`CALL 205 count : ${calls205.length}`);

  for (const x of calls205) {
    console.log(`  ${hex(x.pc)}  call 205`);
  }

  console.log();
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log("IMPORTANT");
  console.log("============================================================");

  console.log(
    "A producer is reported only when the address expression"
  );
  console.log(
    "can be mapped directly from L7 = G-464."
  );

  console.log();
  console.log(
    "If a target is written through another local, the next pass"
  );
  console.log(
    "must trace that local backward before assigning ownership."
  );

  console.log();
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log("DONE");
  console.log("============================================================");
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceFunction2094Locals67V1.ts
====================================================================================================
import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const CODE_START = 0x00006e7d;
const CODE_END   = 0x00324ef5;

const IMPORT_COUNT = 83;
const TARGET_FUNC = 2094;

const TARGET_LOCALS = [6, 7];

function hex(n: number, w = 8) {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(w, "0");
}

function readULEB(b: Buffer, p: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let q = p;

    while (true) {
        const x = b[q++];

        value += (x & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((x & 0x80) === 0)
            break;

        shift += 7;
    }

    return { value, next: q };
}

function readSLEB(b: Buffer, p: number) {
    let value = 0n;
    let shift = 0n;
    let q = p;
    let x = 0;

    while (true) {
        x = b[q++];
        value |= BigInt(x & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((x & 0x80) === 0)
            break;

        shift += 7n;

        if (shift > 70n)
            throw new Error("SLEB overflow");
    }

    if (shift < 64n && (x & 0x40)) {
        value |= (-1n) << (shift + 7n);
    }

    return { value, next: q };
}

function typeName(x: number) {
    switch (x) {
        case 0x7f: return "i32";
        case 0x7e: return "i64";
        case 0x7d: return "f32";
        case 0x7c: return "f64";
        case 0x7b: return "v128";
        default: return hex(x, 2);
    }
}

function parseBodies(b: Buffer) {
    let p = CODE_START;

    const count = readULEB(b, p);
    p = count.next;

    const map: any[] = [];

    for (let body = 0; body < count.value; body++) {
        const sizeField = p;

        const s = readULEB(b, p);
        p = s.next;

        const codeStart = p;
        const bodyEnd = codeStart + s.value;

        map.push({
            func: IMPORT_COUNT + body,
            body,
            sizeField,
            codeStart,
            bodyEnd,
        });

        p = bodyEnd;
    }

    if (p !== CODE_END) {
        throw new Error(
            `Code boundary mismatch: ${hex(p)} != ${hex(CODE_END)}`
        );
    }

    return map;
}

function main() {
    const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" FUNCTION 2094 LOCALS 6/7 DATAFLOW INSPECTOR V1");
    console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log();

    console.log(`WASM size : ${b.length.toLocaleString()}`);

    const bodies = parseBodies(b);

    const fn = bodies.find(x => x.func === TARGET_FUNC);

    if (!fn)
        throw new Error("Function 2094 not found");

    console.log(`Function : ${TARGET_FUNC}`);
    console.log(`Body #   : ${fn.body}`);
    console.log(`Code     : ${hex(fn.codeStart)} .. ${hex(fn.bodyEnd)}`);
    console.log();

    let p = fn.codeStart;

    /*
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     * LOCAL DECLARATIONS
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     */

    const groupCount = readULEB(b, p);
    p = groupCount.next;

    const declared: {
        first: number;
        count: number;
        type: number;
    }[] = [];

    let localIndex = 0;

    for (let g = 0; g < groupCount.value; g++) {
        const c = readULEB(b, p);
        p = c.next;

        const type = b[p++];

        declared.push({
            first: localIndex,
            count: c.value,
            type,
        });

        localIndex += c.value;
    }

    console.log("DECLARED LOCALS");
    for (const d of declared) {
        console.log(
            `  local ${d.first} .. ${d.first + d.count - 1}` +
            `  count=${d.count}` +
            `  type=${typeName(d.type)}`
        );
    }

    console.log();
    console.log(
        `Declared local count : ${localIndex}`
    );

    /*
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     * IMPORTANT:
     *
     * Function parameters are NOT part of the local declaration
     * vector. Therefore a local index can be a parameter even though
     * it does not appear in the declaration section.
     *
     * We obtain the function signature from the Type Section.
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     */

    const TYPE_SECTION_START = 0x08;

    p = TYPE_SECTION_START;

    if (b[p++] !== 0x01)
        throw new Error("Type section mismatch");

    const typePayload = readULEB(b, p);
    p = typePayload.next;

    const typeEnd = p + typePayload.value;

    const typeCount = readULEB(b, p);
    p = typeCount.next;

    const types: {
        params: string[];
        results: string[];
    }[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < typeCount.value; i++) {
        const form = b[p++];

        if (form !== 0x60)
            throw new Error(
                `Unsupported type form ${hex(form, 2)}`
            );

        const pc = readULEB(b, p);
        p = pc.next;

        const params: string[] = [];

        for (let j = 0; j < pc.value; j++) {
            params.push(typeName(b[p++]));
        }

        const rc = readULEB(b, p);
        p = rc.next;

        const results: string[] = [];

        for (let j = 0; j < rc.value; j++) {
            results.push(typeName(b[p++]));
        }

        types.push({ params, results });
    }

    if (p !== typeEnd)
        throw new Error("Type section boundary mismatch");

    /*
     * FUNCTION SECTION
     */

    p = 0x000005ae;

    if (b[p++] !== 0x03)
        throw new Error("Function section mismatch");

    const fnPayload = readULEB(b, p);
    p = fnPayload.next;

    const fnEnd = p + fnPayload.value;

    const fnCount = readULEB(b, p);
    p = fnCount.next;

    let targetTypeIndex = -1;

    for (let i = 0; i < fnCount.value; i++) {
        const r = readULEB(b, p);
        p = r.next;

        const realFunc = IMPORT_COUNT + i;

        if (realFunc === TARGET_FUNC)
            targetTypeIndex = r.value;
    }

    if (p !== fnEnd)
        throw new Error("Function section boundary mismatch");

    if (targetTypeIndex < 0)
        throw new Error("Function 2094 type index not found");

    const sig = types[targetTypeIndex];

    console.log();
    console.log("FUNCTION SIGNATURE");
    console.log(
        `  type index : ${targetTypeIndex}`
    );
    console.log(
        `  (${sig.params.join(", ")}) -> (${sig.results.join(", ")})`
    );

    console.log();

    /*
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     * INSTRUCTION START
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     */

    p = fn.codeStart;

    const groups = readULEB(b, p);
    p = groups.next;

    for (let g = 0; g < groups.value; g++) {
        const c = readULEB(b, p);
        p = c.next;
        p++;
    }

    const instructionStart = p;

    console.log(
        `Instruction start : ${hex(instructionStart)}`
    );
    console.log();

    /*
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     * DATAFLOW SEARCH
     *
     * We only care about:
     *
     *   local.set 6
     *   local.tee 6
     *   local.get 6
     *
     *   local.set 7
     *   local.tee 7
     *   local.get 7
     *
     * Everything else is decoded only far enough to move over
     * immediates safely.
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     */

    const hits: any[] = [];

    function record(pc: number, kind: string, local: number) {
        hits.push({
            pc,
            kind,
            local,
        });
    }

    p = instructionStart;

    while (p < fn.bodyEnd) {
        const pc = p;
        const op = b[p++];

        /*
         * Structured control.
         */
        if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
            p++;
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x05 || op === 0x0b) {
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
            const r = readULEB(b, p);
            p = r.next;
            continue;
        }

        /*
         * local.get/set/tee
         */
        if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
            const r = readULEB(b, p);
            p = r.next;

            if (TARGET_LOCALS.includes(r.value)) {
                const kind =
                    op === 0x20
                        ? "local.get"
                        : op === 0x21
                            ? "local.set"
                            : "local.tee";

                record(pc, kind, r.value);
            }

            continue;
        }

        /*
         * global.get/set
         */
        if (op === 0x23 || op === 0x24) {
            const r = readULEB(b, p);
            p = r.next;
            continue;
        }

        /*
         * Memory loads.
         *
         * memarg = alignment + offset
         */
        if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x35) {
            const a = readULEB(b, p);
            p = a.next;

            const o = readULEB(b, p);
            p = o.next;

            continue;
        }

        /*
         * Memory stores.
         */
        if (op >= 0x36 && op <= 0x3e) {
            const a = readULEB(b, p);
            p = a.next;

            const o = readULEB(b, p);
            p = o.next;

            continue;
        }

        /*
         * Constants.
         */
        if (op === 0x41) {
            const r = readSLEB(b, p);
            p = r.next;
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x42) {
            const r = readSLEB(b, p);
            p = r.next;
            continue;
        }

        /*
         * Call.
         */
        if (op === 0x10) {
            const r = readULEB(b, p);
            p = r.next;

            continue;
        }

        /*
         * br_table
         */
        if (op === 0x0e) {
            const n = readULEB(b, p);
            p = n.next;

            for (let i = 0; i < n.value + 1; i++) {
                const r = readULEB(b, p);
                p = r.next;
            }

            continue;
        }

        /*
         * 0xfc extended instructions.
         */
        if (op === 0xfc) {
            const sub = readULEB(b, p);
            p = sub.next;

            switch (sub.value) {
                case 0x08: {
                    const a = readULEB(b, p);
                    p = a.next;
                    p++;
                    break;
                }

                case 0x09: {
                    const a = readULEB(b, p);
                    p = a.next;
                    break;
                }

                case 0x0a:
                    p += 2;
                    break;

                case 0x0b:
                    p += 1;
                    break;

                case 0x0c: {
                    const a = readULEB(b, p);
                    p = a.next;
                    p++;
                    break;
                }

                case 0x0d: {
                    const a = readULEB(b, p);
                    p = a.next;
                    break;
                }

                case 0x0e:
                    p += 2;
                    break;

                case 0x0f:
                case 0x10:
                case 0x11: {
                    const a = readULEB(b, p);
                    p = a.next;
                    break;
                }

                default:
                    break;
            }

            continue;
        }

        /*
         * All remaining single-byte instructions have no immediates
         * relevant to local 6/7.
         */
    }

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("LOCAL 6 / LOCAL 7 REFERENCES");
    console.log("============================================================");

    for (const h of hits) {
        console.log(
            `${hex(h.pc)}  ${h.kind.padEnd(10)} local${h.local}`
        );
    }

    console.log();

    for (const local of TARGET_LOCALS) {
        const list = hits.filter(x => x.local === local);

        console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
        console.log(`LOCAL ${local}`);
        console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

        console.log(
            `Total references : ${list.length}`
        );

        const gets = list.filter(x => x.kind === "local.get");
        const sets = list.filter(x => x.kind === "local.set");
        const tees = list.filter(x => x.kind === "local.tee");

        console.log(`  local.get : ${gets.length}`);
        console.log(`  local.set : ${sets.length}`);
        console.log(`  local.tee : ${tees.length}`);

        if (sets.length === 0 && tees.length === 0) {
            console.log();
            console.log(
                `RESULT: local${local} has NO assignment in Function 2094.`
            );

            if (local < sig.params.length) {
                console.log(
                    `Therefore local${local} is FUNCTION PARAMETER #${local}.`
                );

                console.log(
                    `Parameter type : ${sig.params[local]}`
                );
            } else {
                console.log(
                    `local${local} is not a parameter and has no assignment.`
                );
            }
        }

        console.log();
    }

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("TARGET CALL LOCATIONS");
    console.log("============================================================");

    const callTargets = [
        0x000969de,
        0x00096aa1,
        0x00096aad,
        0x00096ab4,
    ];

    for (const pc of callTargets) {
        console.log(hex(pc));
    }

    console.log();
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("DONE");
    console.log("============================================================");
}

main();

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceFunction2094MemoryOriginsV5.ts

import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const START = 0x00095e62;
const END   = 0x00096da8;

const bytes = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function hx(n: number): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function uleb(pos: number) {
    let v = 0;
    let s = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = bytes[pos++];

        v += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;

        if (!(b & 0x80))
            return { value: v, next: pos };

        s += 7;
    }
}

function sleb32(pos: number) {
    let v = 0;
    let s = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = bytes[pos++];

        v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;

        if (!(b & 0x80)) {

            if (s < 32 && (b & 0x40))
                v |= (-1 << (s + 7));

            return {
                value: v | 0,
                next: pos
            };
        }

        s += 7;
    }
}

type I = {
    pc: number;
    op: string;
    text: string;
    local?: number;
    imm?: number;
    offset?: number;
};

function decode(): I[] {

    const out: I[] = [];

    let p = START;

    while (p < END) {

        const pc = p;
        const b = bytes[p++];

        try {

            if (b === 0x20) {
                const x = uleb(p);
                p = x.next;

                out.push({
                    pc,
                    op: "local.get",
                    local: x.value,
                    text: `local.get ${x.value}`
                });

                continue;
            }

            if (b === 0x21) {
                const x = uleb(p);
                p = x.next;

                out.push({
                    pc,
                    op: "local.set",
                    local: x.value,
                    text: `local.set ${x.value}`
                });

                continue;
            }

            if (b === 0x22) {
                const x = uleb(p);
                p = x.next;

                out.push({
                    pc,
                    op: "local.tee",
                    local: x.value,
                    text: `local.tee ${x.value}`
                });

                continue;
            }

            if (b === 0x23) {
                const x = uleb(p);
                p = x.next;

                out.push({
                    pc,
                    op: "global.get",
                    text: `global.get ${x.value}`
                });

                continue;
            }

            if (b === 0x24) {
                const x = uleb(p);
                p = x.next;

                out.push({
                    pc,
                    op: "global.set",
                    text: `global.set ${x.value}`
                });

                continue;
            }

            if (b === 0x41) {
                const x = sleb32(p);
                p = x.next;

                out.push({
                    pc,
                    op: "i32.const",
                    imm: x.value,
                    text: `i32.const ${x.value}`
                });

                continue;
            }

            if (b === 0x42) {

                let q = p;

                while (true) {
                    const z = bytes[q++];

                    if (!(z & 0x80))
                        break;
                }

                p = q;

                out.push({
                    pc,
                    op: "i64.const",
                    text: "i64.const"
                });

                continue;
            }

            if (b === 0x28) {

                const a = uleb(p);
                p = a.next;

                const o = uleb(p);
                p = o.next;

                out.push({
                    pc,
                    op: "i32.load",
                    offset: o.value,
                    text: `i32.load offset=${o.value}`
                });

                continue;
            }

            if (b === 0x29) {

                const a = uleb(p);
                p = a.next;

                const o = uleb(p);
                p = o.next;

                out.push({
                    pc,
                    op: "i64.load",
                    offset: o.value,
                    text: `i64.load offset=${o.value}`
                });

                continue;
            }

            if (b === 0x31) {

                const a = uleb(p);
                p = a.next;

                const o = uleb(p);
                p = o.next;

                out.push({
                    pc,
                    op: "i64.load8_u",
                    offset: o.value,
                    text: `i64.load8_u offset=${o.value}`
                });

                continue;
            }

            if (b === 0x36) {

                const a = uleb(p);
                p = a.next;

                const o = uleb(p);
                p = o.next;

                out.push({
                    pc,
                    op: "i32.store",
                    offset: o.value,
                    text: `i32.store offset=${o.value}`
                });

                continue;
            }

            if (b === 0x37) {

                const a = uleb(p);
                p = a.next;

                const o = uleb(p);
                p = o.next;

                out.push({
                    pc,
                    op: "i64.store",
                    offset: o.value,
                    text: `i64.store offset=${o.value}`
                });

                continue;
            }

            const simple: Record<number, string> = {

                0x1a: "drop",
                0x1b: "select",

                0x6a: "i32.add",
                0x6b: "i32.sub",
                0x74: "i32.shl",
                0x75: "i32.shr_s",
                0x76: "i32.shr_u",

                0x7c: "i64.add",
                0x7d: "i64.sub",
                0x7e: "i64.mul",
                0x83: "i64.and",
                0x84: "i64.or",
                0x85: "i64.xor",
                0x86: "i64.shl",
                0x88: "i64.shr_u",

                0x45: "i32.eqz",
                0x46: "i32.eq",
                0x47: "i32.ne",
                0x48: "i32.lt_s",
                0x49: "i32.lt_u",
                0x4a: "i32.gt_s",
                0x4b: "i32.gt_u",
            };

            if (simple[b]) {

                out.push({
                    pc,
                    op: simple[b],
                    text: simple[b]
                });

                continue;
            }

            if (b === 0x10) {

                const x = uleb(p);
                p = x.next;

                out.push({
                    pc,
                    op: "call",
                    imm: x.value,
                    text: `call ${x.value}`
                });

                continue;
            }

            if (b === 0x0c || b === 0x0d) {

                const x = uleb(p);
                p = x.next;

                out.push({
                    pc,
                    op: b === 0x0c ? "br" : "br_if",
                    imm: x.value,
                    text:
                        `${b === 0x0c ? "br" : "br_if"} ${x.value}`
                });

                continue;
            }

            if (b === 0x02 || b === 0x03 || b === 0x04) {

                const x = sleb32(p);
                p = x.next;

                out.push({
                    pc,
                    op:
                        b === 0x02
                            ? "block"
                            : b === 0x03
                            ? "loop"
                            : "if",
                    text:
                        b === 0x02
                            ? "block"
                            : b === 0x03
                            ? "loop"
                            : "if"
                });

                continue;
            }

            if (b === 0x05) {

                out.push({
                    pc,
                    op: "else",
                    text: "else"
                });

                continue;
            }

            if (b === 0x0b) {

                out.push({
                    pc,
                    op: "end",
                    text: "end"
                });

                continue;
            }

            out.push({
                pc,
                op: "unknown",
                text:
                    `UNKNOWN 0x${b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
            });

        } catch {

            out.push({
                pc,
                op: "decode_error",
                text: "DECODE_ERROR"
            });

            break;
        }
    }

    return out;
}


/* ============================================================ */
/* SYMBOLIC EXPRESSIONS                                         */
/* ============================================================ */

type Expr =
    | { kind: "unknown"; text: string }
    | { kind: "const"; value: number }
    | { kind: "local"; n: number }
    | { kind: "global"; n: number }
    | { kind: "add"; a: Expr; b: Expr }
    | { kind: "sub"; a: Expr; b: Expr }
    | { kind: "shl"; a: Expr; bits: number }
    | { kind: "load"; addr: Expr; offset: number }
    | { kind: "load8"; addr: Expr; offset: number }
    | { kind: "select"; a: Expr; b: Expr; c: Expr };

function C(v: number): Expr {
    return {
        kind: "const",
        value: v
    };
}

function L(n: number): Expr {
    return {
        kind: "local",
        n
    };
}

function G(n: number): Expr {
    return {
        kind: "global",
        n
    };
}

function U(text: string): Expr {
    return {
        kind: "unknown",
        text
    };
}

function add(a: Expr, b: Expr): Expr {
    return {
        kind: "add",
        a,
        b
    };
}

function sub(a: Expr, b: Expr): Expr {
    return {
        kind: "sub",
        a,
        b
    };
}

function shl(a: Expr, bits: number): Expr {
    return {
        kind: "shl",
        a,
        bits
    };
}

function expr(e: Expr): string {

    switch (e.kind) {

        case "unknown":
            return e.text;

        case "const":
            return `${e.value}`;

        case "local":
            return `L${e.n}`;

        case "global":
            return `G${e.n}`;

        case "add":
            return `(${expr(e.a)} + ${expr(e.b)})`;

        case "sub":
            return `(${expr(e.a)} - ${expr(e.b)})`;

        case "shl":
            return `(${expr(e.a)} << ${e.bits})`;

        case "load":
            return `MEM64[${expr(e.addr)}+${e.offset}]`;

        case "load8":
            return `MEM8[${expr(e.addr)}+${e.offset}]`;

        case "select":
            return `select(${expr(e.a)},${expr(e.b)},${expr(e.c)})`;
    }
}


/* ============================================================ */
/* NORMALIZATION                                                */
/* ============================================================ */

function normalize(e: Expr): string {

    const s = expr(e);

    const patterns: Array<[RegExp, string]> = [

        [
            /^\(L7 \+ 256\)$/,
            "G-208"
        ],

        [
            /^\(L7 \+ 192\)$/,
            "G-272"
        ],

        [
            /^\(L7 \+ 128\)$/,
            "G-336"
        ],

        [
            /^\(L7 - 64\)$/,
            "G-528"
        ],

        [
            /^\(L7 \+ 256\) \+ \(L0 << 3\)$/,
            "G-208 + i*8"
        ],

        [
            /^\(L7 \+ 192\) \+ \(L0 << 3\)$/,
            "G-272 + i*8"
        ],

        [
            /^\(L7 \+ 128\) \+ \(L0 << 3\)$/,
            "G-336 + i*8"
        ],

        [
            /^\(L7 - 64\) \+ \(L0 << 3\)$/,
            "G-528 + i*8"
        ],
    ];

    for (const [r, v] of patterns) {

        if (r.test(s))
            return v;
    }

    return s;
}


/* ============================================================ */
/* SYMBOLIC STACK                                               */
/* ============================================================ */

const ins = decode();

const stack: Expr[] = [];

const locals = new Map<number, Expr>();

function pop(): Expr {

    if (!stack.length)
        return U("STACK_UNDERFLOW");

    return stack.pop()!;
}

function push(x: Expr) {
    stack.push(x);
}


/* ------------------------------------------------------------ */
/* IMPORTANT: CALL SIGNATURES                                    */
/* ------------------------------------------------------------ */

/*
 * Function 205:
 *
 *   (i32, i64, i64, i64, i64) -> void
 *
 * Therefore call 205 consumes exactly five stack values.
 *
 * We retain the consumed arguments so the call can be printed.
 */

const call205Args: Array<{
    pc: number;
    args: Expr[];
}> = [];


/*
 * Other calls are NOT given fabricated signatures.
 *
 * If we encounter one, the safest behavior is to poison only
 * the current symbolic stack rather than pretending that it
 * returned no value.
 */

function poisonStack(reason: string) {

    stack.length = 0;

    stack.push(
        U(`STACK_RESET(${reason})`)
    );
}


/* ------------------------------------------------------------ */
/* STORES                                                        */
/* ------------------------------------------------------------ */

const stores: Array<{
    pc: number;
    address: Expr;
    value: Expr;
}> = [];


/* ============================================================ */
/* WALK                                                          */
/* ============================================================ */

for (let i = 0; i < ins.length; i++) {

    const x = ins[i];

    switch (x.op) {

        case "local.get":

            push(
                locals.get(x.local!) ??
                L(x.local!)
            );

            break;


        case "local.set": {

            const v = pop();

            locals.set(
                x.local!,
                v
            );

            break;
        }


        case "local.tee": {

            const v =
                stack.length
                    ? stack[stack.length - 1]
                    : U("STACK_UNDERFLOW");

            locals.set(
                x.local!,
                v
            );

            break;
        }


        case "global.get":

            push(G(0));

            break;


        case "global.set":

            pop();

            break;


        case "i32.const":

            push(C(x.imm!));

            break;


        case "i32.add": {

            const b = pop();
            const a = pop();

            push(add(a, b));

            break;
        }


        case "i32.sub": {

            const b = pop();
            const a = pop();

            push(sub(a, b));

            break;
        }


        case "i32.shl": {

            const b = pop();
            const a = pop();

            if (b.kind === "const")
                push(shl(a, b.value));
            else
                push(
                    U(
                        `${expr(a)} << ${expr(b)}`
                    )
                );

            break;
        }


        case "i64.load8_u": {

            const a = pop();

            push({
                kind: "load8",
                addr: a,
                offset: x.offset ?? 0
            });

            break;
        }


        case "i64.load": {

            const a = pop();

            push({
                kind: "load",
                addr: a,
                offset: x.offset ?? 0
            });

            break;
        }


        case "select": {

            /*
             * WebAssembly select:
             *
             *   val1 val2 condition
             *
             * => one value
             */

            const condition = pop();
            const val2 = pop();
            const val1 = pop();

            push({
                kind: "select",
                a: val1,
                b: val2,
                c: condition
            });

            break;
        }


        case "i64.add": {

            const b = pop();
            const a = pop();

            push(add(a, b));

            break;
        }


        case "i64.sub": {

            const b = pop();
            const a = pop();

            push(sub(a, b));

            break;
        }


        case "i64.mul": {

            const b = pop();
            const a = pop();

            push(
                U(
                    `MUL64(${expr(a)},${expr(b)})`
                )
            );

            break;
        }


        case "drop":

            pop();

            break;


        case "i64.store": {

            const value = pop();
            const address = pop();

            stores.push({
                pc: x.pc,
                address,
                value
            });

            break;
        }


        case "i32.store": {

            const value = pop();
            const address = pop();

            stores.push({
                pc: x.pc,
                address,
                value
            });

            break;
        }


        case "call": {

            const target = x.imm!;

            if (target === 205) {

                /*
                 * Stack order:
                 *
                 * arg0 arg1 arg2 arg3 arg4
                 *
                 * The last pushed value is arg4.
                 */

                const args: Expr[] = [];

                for (let k = 0; k < 5; k++)
                    args.unshift(pop());

                call205Args.push({
                    pc: x.pc,
                    args
                });

                /*
                 * Function 205 returns void.
                 * Therefore it leaves no result on stack.
                 */

                break;
            }


            /*
             * Unknown call signature.
             *
             * Do not let unknown stack effects contaminate all
             * subsequent memory analysis.
             */

            poisonStack(
                `CALL_${target}_UNKNOWN_SIGNATURE`
            );

            break;
        }


        default:

            /*
             * Control-flow instructions and unsupported operations
             * are intentionally not allowed to fabricate symbolic
             * values.
             */

            break;
    }
}

    console.log(

    );

    console.log(
        `  VALUE   : ${expr(s.value)}`
    );

    console.log("");
}

import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_FUNC = 2094;

const TARGET_STORES = [
    0x0009670d,
    0x000967c8,
    0x00096883,
    0x00096baf,
];

const bytes = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function u32(pos: number): number {
    return (
        bytes[pos] |
        (bytes[pos + 1] << 8) |
        (bytes[pos + 2] << 16) |
        (bytes[pos + 3] << 24)
    ) >>> 0;
}

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = bytes[pos++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function hex(n: number): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function readSLEB64(pos: number): { value: bigint; next: number } {
    let result = 0n;
    let shift = 0n;

    while (true) {
        const b = bytes[pos++];
        result |= BigInt(b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            if (shift < 63n && (b & 0x40))
                result |= (-1n) << (shift + 7n);

            return { value: result, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7n;
    }
}

function readSLEB32(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = bytes[pos++];
        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
                result |= (-1 << (shift + 7));

            return { value: result | 0, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function findFunction205Body(): {
    bodyStart: number;
    bodyEnd: number;
} {
    const codeSectionStart = 0x00006e78;
    const payload = 0x00006e7d;
    const codeSectionEnd = 0x00324ef5;

    let p = payload;

    const count = readULEB(p);
    p = count.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
        const size = readULEB(p);
        const sizeField = p;

        const codeStart = size.next;
        const bodyEnd = codeStart + size.value;

        if (i === 2011) {
            if (codeStart !== 0x00095e62) {
                throw new Error(
                    `Function 2094 body mismatch: ${hex(codeStart)}`
                );
            }

            return {
                bodyStart: codeStart,
                bodyEnd,
            };
        }

        p = bodyEnd;
    }

    throw new Error("Function 2094 not found");
}

type Instr = {
    pc: number;
    end: number;
    text: string;
    kind: string;
    local?: number;
    offset?: number;
    immediate?: bigint | number;
};

function decodeRange(start: number, end: number): Instr[] {
    const out: Instr[] = [];
    let p = start;

    while (p < end) {
        const pc = p;
        const op = bytes[p++];

        try {
            // local.get
            if (op === 0x20) {
                const x = readULEB(p);
                p = x.next;

                out.push({
                    pc,
                    end: p,
                    text: `local.get ${x.value}`,
                    kind: "local.get",
                    local: x.value,
                });
                continue;
            }

            // local.set
            if (op === 0x21) {
                const x = readULEB(p);
                p = x.next;

                out.push({
                    pc,
                    end: p,
                    text: `local.set ${x.value}`,
                    kind: "local.set",
                    local: x.value,
                });
                continue;
            }

            // local.tee
            if (op === 0x22) {
                const x = readULEB(p);
                p = x.next;

                out.push({
                    pc,
                    end: p,
                    text: `local.tee ${x.value}`,
                    kind: "local.tee",
                    local: x.value,
                });
                continue;
            }

            // i32.const
            if (op === 0x41) {
                const x = readSLEB32(p);
                p = x.next;

                out.push({
                    pc,
                    end: p,
                    text: `i32.const ${x.value}`,
                    kind: "i32.const",
                    immediate: x.value,
                });
                continue;
            }

            // i64.const
            if (op === 0x42) {
                const x = readSLEB64(p);
                p = x.next;

                out.push({
                    pc,
                    end: p,
                    text: `i64.const ${x.value}`,
                    kind: "i64.const",
                    immediate: x.value,
                });
                continue;
            }

            // global.get
            if (op === 0x23) {
                const x = readULEB(p);
                p = x.next;

                out.push({
                    pc,
                    end: p,
                    text: `global.get ${x.value}`,
                    kind: "global.get",
                });
                continue;
            }

            // global.set
            if (op === 0x24) {
                const x = readULEB(p);
                p = x.next;

                out.push({
                    pc,
                    end: p,
                    text: `global.set ${x.value}`,
                    kind: "global.set",
                });
                continue;
            }

            // i32.add
            if (op === 0x6a) {
                out.push({
                    pc,
                    end: p,
                    text: "i32.add",
                    kind: "i32.add",
                });
                continue;
            }

            // i32.sub
            if (op === 0x6b) {
                out.push({
                    pc,
                    end: p,
                    text: "i32.sub",
                    kind: "i32.sub",
                });
                continue;
            }

            // i32.and
            if (op === 0x71) {
                out.push({
                    pc,
                    end: p,
                    text: "i32.and",
                    kind: "i32.and",
                });
                continue;
            }

            // i32.shl
            if (op === 0x74) {
                out.push({
                    pc,
                    end: p,
                    text: "i32.shl",
                    kind: "i32.shl",
                });
                continue;
            }

            // i32.shr_u
            if (op === 0x76) {
                out.push({
                    pc,
                    end: p,
                    text: "i32.shr_u",
                    kind: "i32.shr_u",
                });
                continue;
            }

            // i64.add
            if (op === 0x7c) {
                out.push({
                    pc,
                    end: p,
                    text: "i64.add",
                    kind: "i64.add",
                });
                continue;
            }

            // i64.sub
            if (op === 0x7d) {
                out.push({
                    pc,
                    end: p,
                    text: "i64.sub",
                    kind: "i64.sub",
                });
                continue;
            }

            // i64.mul
            if (op === 0x7e) {
                out.push({
                    pc,
                    end: p,
                    text: "i64.mul",
                    kind: "i64.mul",
                });
                continue;
            }

            // i64.and
            if (op === 0x83) {
                out.push({
                    pc,
                    end: p,
                    text: "i64.and",
                    kind: "i64.and",
                });
                continue;
            }

            // i64.or
            if (op === 0x84) {
                out.push({
                    pc,
                    end: p,
                    text: "i64.or",
                    kind: "i64.or",
                });
                continue;
            }

            // i64.shl
            if (op === 0x86) {
                out.push({
                    pc,
                    end: p,
                    text: "i64.shl",
                    kind: "i64.shl",
                });
                continue;
            }

            // i64.shr_u
            if (op === 0x88) {
                out.push({
                    pc,
                    end: p,
                    text: "i64.shr_u",
                    kind: "i64.shr_u",
                });
                continue;
            }

            // i64.load / i32.load
            if (op === 0x29 || op === 0x28) {
                const align = readULEB(p);
                p = align.next;

                const off = readULEB(p);
                p = off.next;

                out.push({
                    pc,
                    end: p,
                    text:
                        op === 0x29
                            ? `i64.load offset=${off.value}`
                            : `i32.load offset=${off.value}`,
                    kind:
                        op === 0x29
                            ? "i64.load"
                            : "i32.load",
                    offset: off.value,
                });

                continue;
            }

            // i64.store / i32.store
            if (op === 0x37 || op === 0x36) {
                const align = readULEB(p);
                p = align.next;

                const off = readULEB(p);
                p = off.next;

                out.push({
                    pc,
                    end: p,
                    text:
                        op === 0x37
                            ? `i64.store offset=${off.value}`
                            : `i32.store offset=${off.value}`,
                    kind:
                        op === 0x37
                            ? "i64.store"
                            : "i32.store",
                    offset: off.value,
                });

                continue;
            }

            // call
            if (op === 0x10) {
                const x = readULEB(p);
                p = x.next;

                out.push({
                    pc,
                    end: p,
                    text: `call ${x.value}`,
                    kind: "call",
                });

                continue;
            }

            // block / loop / if / else / end
            if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
                p++;

                out.push({
                    pc,
                    end: p,
                    text:
                        op === 0x02
                            ? "block"
                            : op === 0x03
                            ? "loop"
                            : "if",
                    kind: "control",
                });

                continue;
            }

            if (op === 0x05) {
                out.push({
                    pc,
                    end: p,
                    text: "else",
                    kind: "control",
                });
                continue;
            }

            if (op === 0x0b) {
                out.push({
                    pc,
                    end: p,
                    text: "end",
                    kind: "control",
                });
                continue;
            }

            // comparison operators commonly encountered
            if (
                op === 0x45 ||
                op === 0x46 ||
                op === 0x47 ||
                op === 0x48 ||
                op === 0x49 ||
                op === 0x4a ||
                op === 0x4b ||
                op === 0x50
            ) {
                out.push({
                    pc,
                    end: p,
                    text: `compare 0x${op.toString(16)}`,
                    kind: "compare",
                });
                continue;
            }

            // unknown: do not guess immediate length
            out.push({
                pc,
                end: p,
                text: `UNKNOWN 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`,
                kind: "unknown",
            });

        } catch {
            out.push({
                pc,
                end: p,
                text: `DECODE_ERROR 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`,
                kind: "unknown",
            });

            break;
        }
    }

    return out;
}

function dumpMemory(pc: number, radius: number): void {
    const a = Math.max(0, pc - radius);
    const b = Math.min(bytes.length, pc + radius);

    console.log(
        `RAW ${hex(a)} .. ${hex(b)}`
    );

    for (let p = a; p < b; p += 16) {
        const lineEnd = Math.min(b, p + 16);

        let s = `${hex(p)}  `;

        for (let q = p; q < lineEnd; q++) {
            s += bytes[q].toString(16).padStart(2, "0") + " ";
        }

        console.log(s);
    }
}

function printBackward(
    instructions: Instr[],
    targetIndex: number
): void {
    const from = Math.max(0, targetIndex - 18);

    console.log("");
    console.log(
        `BACKWARD WINDOW: ${hex(instructions[from].pc)} .. ${hex(instructions[targetIndex].pc)}`
    );

    for (let i = from; i <= targetIndex; i++) {
        const x = instructions[i];

        const marker =
            i === targetIndex
                ? "  <=== TARGET STORE"
                : "";

        console.log(
            `${hex(x.pc)}  ${x.text}${marker}`
        );
    }

    console.log("");
}

        x => x.pc === target

    console.log("");

    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

        console.log("NOT FOUND IN DECODER");
        dumpMemory(target, 48);
        continue;

    const store = instructions[idx];

    console.log(`Instruction : ${store.text}`);
    console.log(`Instruction index : ${idx}`);

    console.log("RAW BYTES:");
    dumpMemory(target, 48);

    // Look backward for the closest local.set/tee/get sequence.
    console.log("");

    let found = 0;

    for (
        let i = idx - 1;
        i >= 0 && found < 12;
        i--
    ) {
        const x = instructions[i];

        if (
            x.kind === "local.get" ||
            x.kind === "local.set" ||
            x.kind === "local.tee"
        ) {
            console.log(
                `  ${hex(x.pc)}  ${x.text}`
            );
            found++;
        }
    }

    if (found === 0)
        console.log("  none");

    console.log("");
    console.log("NEAREST CONSTANTS:");

    found = 0;

    for (
        let i = idx - 1;
        i >= 0 && found < 12;
        i--
    ) {
        const x = instructions[i];

        if (
            x.kind === "i32.const" ||
            x.kind === "i64.const"
        ) {
            console.log(
                `  ${hex(x.pc)}  ${x.text}`
            );
            found++;
        }
    }

    if (found === 0)
        console.log("  none");
}

import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const BODY_START = 0x00095e62;
const BODY_END   = 0x00096da8;

const TARGETS = [
  0x0009670d,
  0x000967c8,
  0x00096883,
  0x00096baf,
];

const bytes = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function hex(n: number): string {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function uleb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let v = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = bytes[pos++];
    v |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
      return { value: v >>> 0, next: pos };

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function sleb32(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let v = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = bytes[pos++];
    v |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
        v |= (-1 << (shift + 7));

      return { value: v | 0, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function sleb64(pos: number): { value: bigint; next: number } {
  let v = 0n;
  let shift = 0n;

  while (true) {
    const b = bytes[pos++];

    v |= BigInt(b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      if (shift < 64n && (b & 0x40))
        v |= (-1n) << (shift + 7n);

      return { value: v, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7n;
  }
}

type Instr = {
  pc: number;
  end: number;
  op: string;
  text: string;
  local?: number;
  offset?: number;
  imm?: number | bigint;
};

function decode(start: number, end: number): Instr[] {
  const out: Instr[] = [];
  let p = start;

  while (p < end) {
    const pc = p;
    const b = bytes[p++];

    try {

      // local.get
      if (b === 0x20) {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        out.push({
          pc,
          end: p,
          op: "local.get",
          local: x.value,
          text: `local.get ${x.value}`,
        });

        continue;
      }

      // local.set
      if (b === 0x21) {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        out.push({
          pc,
          end: p,
          op: "local.set",
          local: x.value,
          text: `local.set ${x.value}`,
        });

        continue;
      }

      // local.tee
      if (b === 0x22) {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        out.push({
          pc,
          end: p,
          op: "local.tee",
          local: x.value,
          text: `local.tee ${x.value}`,
        });

        continue;
      }

      // i32.const
      if (b === 0x41) {
        const x = sleb32(p);
        p = x.next;

        out.push({
          pc,
          end: p,
          op: "i32.const",
          imm: x.value,
          text: `i32.const ${x.value}`,
        });

        continue;
      }

      // i64.const
      if (b === 0x42) {
        const x = sleb64(p);
        p = x.next;

        out.push({
          pc,
          end: p,
          op: "i64.const",
          imm: x.value,
          text: `i64.const ${x.value}`,
        });

        continue;
      }

      // i64.load
      if (b === 0x29) {
        const a = uleb(p);
        p = a.next;

        const o = uleb(p);
        p = o.next;

        out.push({
          pc,
          end: p,
          op: "i64.load",
          offset: o.value,
          text: `i64.load offset=${o.value}`,
        });

        continue;
      }

      // i32.load
      if (b === 0x28) {
        const a = uleb(p);
        p = a.next;

        const o = uleb(p);
        p = o.next;

        out.push({
          pc,
          end: p,
          op: "i32.load",
          offset: o.value,
          text: `i32.load offset=${o.value}`,
        });

        continue;
      }

      // i64.store
      if (b === 0x37) {
        const a = uleb(p);
        p = a.next;

        const o = uleb(p);
        p = o.next;

        out.push({
          pc,
          end: p,
          op: "i64.store",
          offset: o.value,
          text: `i64.store offset=${o.value}`,
        });

        continue;
      }

      // i32.store
      if (b === 0x36) {
        const a = uleb(p);
        p = a.next;

        const o = uleb(p);
        p = o.next;

        out.push({
          pc,
          end: p,
          op: "i32.store",
          offset: o.value,
          text: `i32.store offset=${o.value}`,
        });

        continue;
      }

      // i32.add
      if (b === 0x6a) {
        out.push({
          pc,
          end: p,
          op: "i32.add",
          text: "i32.add",
        });

        continue;
      }

      // i32.sub
      if (b === 0x6b) {
        out.push({
          pc,
          end: p,
          op: "i32.sub",
          text: "i32.sub",
        });

        continue;
      }

      // i32.shl
      if (b === 0x74) {
        out.push({
          pc,
          end: p,
          op: "i32.shl",
          text: "i32.shl",
        });

        continue;
      }

      // i32.shr_u
      if (b === 0x76) {
        out.push({
          pc,
          end: p,
          op: "i32.shr_u",
          text: "i32.shr_u",
        });

        continue;
      }

      // i64.add
      if (b === 0x7c) {
        out.push({
          pc,
          end: p,
          op: "i64.add",
          text: "i64.add",
        });

        continue;
      }

      // i64.sub
      if (b === 0x7d) {
        out.push({
          pc,
          end: p,
          op: "i64.sub",
          text: "i64.sub",
        });

        continue;
      }

      // i64.mul
      if (b === 0x7e) {
        out.push({
          pc,
          end: p,
          op: "i64.mul",
          text: "i64.mul",
        });

        continue;
      }

      // i64.and
      if (b === 0x83) {
        out.push({
          pc,
          end: p,
          op: "i64.and",
          text: "i64.and",
        });

        continue;
      }

      // i64.or
      if (b === 0x84) {
        out.push({
          pc,
          end: p,
          op: "i64.or",
          text: "i64.or",
        });

        continue;
      }

      // i64.shl
      if (b === 0x86) {
        out.push({
          pc,
          end: p,
          op: "i64.shl",
          text: "i64.shl",
        });

        continue;
      }

      // i64.shr_u
      if (b === 0x88) {
        out.push({
          pc,
          end: p,
          op: "i64.shr_u",
          text: "i64.shr_u",
        });

        continue;
      }

      // global.get
      if (b === 0x23) {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        out.push({
          pc,
          end: p,
          op: "global.get",
          text: `global.get ${x.value}`,
        });

        continue;
      }

      // global.set
      if (b === 0x24) {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        out.push({
          pc,
          end: p,
          op: "global.set",
          text: `global.set ${x.value}`,
        });

        continue;
      }

      // call
      if (b === 0x10) {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        out.push({
          pc,
          end: p,
          op: "call",
          text: `call ${x.value}`,
        });

        continue;
      }

      // drop
      if (b === 0x1a) {
        out.push({
          pc,
          end: p,
          op: "drop",
          text: "drop",
        });

        continue;
      }

      // comparisons
      if (
        b === 0x45 ||
        b === 0x46 ||
        b === 0x47 ||
        b === 0x48 ||
        b === 0x49 ||
        b === 0x4a ||
        b === 0x4b ||
        b === 0x50
      ) {
        out.push({
          pc,
          end: p,
          op: `cmp_${b.toString(16)}`,
          text: `compare 0x${b.toString(16)}`,
        });

        continue;
      }

      // control instructions
      if (b === 0x02 || b === 0x03 || b === 0x04) {
        const x = sleb32(p);
        p = x.next;

        out.push({
          pc,
          end: p,
          op: "control",
          text:
            b === 0x02
              ? "block"
              : b === 0x03
              ? "loop"
              : "if",
        });

        continue;
      }

      if (b === 0x05) {
        out.push({
          pc,
          end: p,
          op: "control",
          text: "else",
        });

        continue;
      }

      if (b === 0x0b) {
        out.push({
          pc,
          end: p,
          op: "control",
          text: "end",
        });

        continue;
      }

      // Unknown: do NOT invent its length.
      out.push({
        pc,
        end: p,
        op: "unknown",
        text: `UNKNOWN 0x${b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`,
      });

    } catch {
      out.push({
        pc,
        end: p,
        op: "decode_error",
        text: `DECODE_ERROR 0x${b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`,
      });

      break;
    }
  }

  return out;
}

function previousLocalMutation(
  ins: Instr[],
  index: number,
  local: number
): number {
  for (let i = index - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    const x = ins[i];

    if (
      (x.op === "local.tee" || x.op === "local.set") &&
      x.local === local
    ) {
      return i;
    }
  }

  return -1;
}

function printWindow(
  ins: Instr[],
  center: number,
  radius: number
): void {
  const a = Math.max(0, center - radius);
  const b = Math.min(ins.length - 1, center + radius);

  for (let i = a; i <= b; i++) {
    const x = ins[i];

    console.log(
      `${hex(x.pc)}  ${x.text}`
    );
  }
}

function findPreviousLocalGets(
  ins: Instr[],
  index: number,
  local: number,
  count: number
): number[] {
  const result: number[] = [];

  for (let i = index - 1; i >= 0 && result.length < count; i--) {
    const x = ins[i];

    if (
      (x.op === "local.get" ||
       x.op === "local.set" ||
       x.op === "local.tee") &&
      x.local === local
    ) {
      result.push(i);
    }
  }

  return result;
}

const instructions = decode(BODY_START, BODY_END);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 2094 STORE VALUE + ADDRESS PRODUCER TRACE v4");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");
console.log(`WASM size : ${bytes.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`Function  : 2094`);
console.log(`Body      : ${hex(BODY_START)} .. ${hex(BODY_END)}`);
console.log(`Decoded   : ${instructions.length}`);
console.log("");

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TARGETS");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const target of TARGETS) {
  const idx = instructions.findIndex(
    x => x.pc === target
  );

  console.log("");
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
  console.log(`STORE @ ${hex(target)}`);
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  if (idx < 0) {
    console.log("TARGET NOT FOUND");
    continue;
  }

  console.log(
    `Instruction index : ${idx}`
  );

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // Find the two immediately relevant local.get operations
  // preceding the store.
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  const gets: number[] = [];

  for (
    let i = idx - 1;
    i >= 0 && gets.length < 8;
    i--
  ) {
    if (instructions[i].op === "local.get") {
      gets.push(i);
    }
  }

  console.log("");
  console.log("IMMEDIATE LOCAL.GETS:");

  for (const i of gets) {
    const x = instructions[i];

    console.log(
      `  ${hex(x.pc)}  ${x.text}`
    );
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // The target stores are known to use local 6 / 7 / 8.
  // Find latest mutation of those locals.
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  for (const local of [6, 7, 8]) {
    const producer = previousLocalMutation(
      instructions,
      idx,
      local
    );

    console.log("");

    if (producer < 0) {
      console.log(
        `LOCAL ${local}: no previous mutation found`
      );
      continue;
    }

    const p = instructions[producer];

    console.log(
      `LOCAL ${local} PRODUCER @ ${hex(p.pc)}`
    );

    console.log(
      `  ${p.text}`
    );

    console.log("");
    console.log(
      `  BACKWARD WINDOW FOR LOCAL ${local}:`
    );

    printWindow(
      instructions,
      producer,
      10
    );

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // If local.tee, inspect the local.get operands immediately
    // preceding the tee.
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (p.op === "local.tee") {
      console.log("");
      console.log(
        `  PRECEDING LOCAL REFERENCES FOR L${local}:`
      );

      const refs = findPreviousLocalGets(
        instructions,
        producer,
        local,
        4
      );

      for (const r of refs) {
        console.log(
          `    ${hex(instructions[r].pc)}  ${instructions[r].text}`
        );
      }
    }
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // Full compact window immediately before target.
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  console.log("");
  console.log("STORE BACKWARD WINDOW:");

  printWindow(
    instructions,
    idx,
    16
  );
}
